### [1] [Nitsum: Serving Tiered LLM Requests with Adaptive Tensor Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05467)
*Vikranth Srivatsa,Zijian He,Pu Guo,Dongming Li,Yiying Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Nitsum分布式LLM服务系统，将张量并行作为运行时控制面，通过联合优化TP等级、预填充/解码GPU分配和请求调度，在多租户分层SLO场景下将SLO合规吞吐量相比SOTA提升至多5.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务日益呈现多租户特征，同一部署需在固定GPU预算下同时处理延迟敏感的交互式请求和宽松的后台任务，形成分层SLO场景。由于工作负载混合、请求长度和负载强度动态变化，现有系统主要优化请求级控制而保持执行配置静态，难以适应多层级竞争，无法最大化满足TTFT和TPOT目标的总体吞吐量。

Method: 提出Nitsum分布式LLM服务系统，核心创新是将张量并行（TP）作为一等运行时控制面而非静态部署选择。系统联合优化三个维度：TP等级、预填充与解码阶段的GPU划分比例、以及请求调度策略。为实现频繁TP适应的实用性，引入TP感知的权重复用技术和快速KV迁移机制。

Result: 在真实工作负载追踪和针对性微基准测试上，Nitsum将SLO合规吞吐量（同时满足TTFT和TPOT目标的请求）相比最先进系统提升至多5.3倍，验证了动态TP适应策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将张量并行从静态部署配置转变为动态运行时控制面，Nitsum成功解决了多租户分层SLO场景下的资源竞争问题，显著提升了系统整体服务质量和资源利用效率。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly multi-tenant: the same deployment must handle latency-critical interactive requests and more relaxed background workloads under a fixed GPU budget. This creates a tiered-SLO setting where maximizing overall goodput (requests that satisfy both TTFT and TPOT targets) is challenging because workload mix, request lengths, and load intensity vary over time. Existing systems mainly optimize request-level controls (e.g., queuing and batching) while keeping execution configuration largely static, which limits adaptation under multi-tier contention.
  We present Nitsum, a distributed LLM serving system that treats tensor parallelism (TP) as a first-class runtime control surface rather than a static deployment choice. Nitsum jointly optimizes TP level, prefill/decode GPU split, and request scheduling. To make frequent TP adaptation practical, Nitsum introduces TP-aware weight reuse and fast KV migration. Experiments on real traces and targeted microbenchmarks show that Nitsum improves SLO-compliant goodput over SoTA by up to 5.3 times.

</details>


### [2] [EdgeServing: Deadline-Aware Multi-DNN Serving at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05527)
*Jiahe Cao,Xiaomeng Li,Qiang Liu,Tao Han,Ning Zhang,Weisong Shi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了EdgeServing系统，通过时间片GPU共享和早退机制实现边缘设备上多DNN模型的截止时间感知服务，显著降低了SLO违规率和P95延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着边缘计算的发展，在单个共享GPU上服务多个深度神经网络模型已成为常见但具有挑战性的场景。现有调度器依赖局部启发式方法，无法捕捉调度决策对全局队列尾延迟的影响，而GPU空间共享方法则牺牲了延迟的可预测性。

Method: EdgeServing采用时间分割GPU共享与早退推理机制以提高推理可预测性，引入稳定性评分来量化每个候选调度决策对未来队列状态的影响。运行时，系统协同选择模型、退出点和批次大小，以最小化预测的系统级SLO影响。

Result: 在多个硬件平台上的实验结果表明，EdgeServing在SLO违规率和P95延迟方面均持续优于代表性基线方法。早退机制在严格延迟约束下扩展了调度动作空间，使系统性能得以提升。

Conclusion: EdgeServing成功解决了边缘设备上多DNN模型服务的调度难题，通过全局优化调度决策实现了更好的延迟性能和SLO保障，为边缘计算场景下的模型服务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: As edge computing expands, serving multiple deep neural network (DNN) models on a single shared GPU has become a common yet challenging scenario, where each scheduling decision affects the tail latency of all concurrent queues. Existing schedulers rely on local heuristics and fail to capture this global impact, while GPU spatial-sharing approaches sacrifice latency predictability. In this paper, we propose EdgeServing, a deadline-aware multi-DNN serving system for edge devices. EdgeServing adopts time-division GPU sharing with early-exit inference for high inference predictability, and introduces a stability score to quantify how each candidate scheduling decision impacts the future queue status. At runtime, it cohesively selects the model, exit point, and batch size to minimize predicted system-wide SLO impact. Experimental results on multiple hardware platforms show that EdgeServing consistently outperforms representative baselines in both SLO violation ratio and P95 latency, enabled by early-exit mechanism, which expands the scheduling action space under tight latency constraints.

</details>


### [3] [A Scalable Digital Twin Framework for Energy Optimization in Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05581)
*Raphael Hendrigo de Souza Gonçalves,Wendel Marcos dos Santos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种可扩展的数字孪生框架，用于数据中心的能源优化。该框架集成了物联网数据采集、云计算和LSTM机器学习模型，实验证明能有效降低能耗并提升PUE指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据中心规模的扩大，其能源消耗问题日益严峻。为了实现可持续运营并降低成本，迫切需要一种能够实时监控、精准预测并智能管理能源的可扩展解决方案。

Method: 构建了一个集成物联网数据采集、云计算和机器学习技术的数字孪生框架。开发了一个受控的小规模数据中心环境，监测功耗、温度和计算工作负载等变量，并采用长短期记忆网络（LSTM）模型预测能源需求以辅助运营决策。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在能源效率方面取得了显著改善，具体表现为电力消耗的降低和电源使用效率（PUE）的提升。虽然在受限环境中进行评估，但验证了其在降低能耗方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的数字孪生框架证明了其作为数据中心管理方案的潜力，具有可扩展性和成本效益的特点，为数据中心实现智能化和可持续化的能源管理提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: This study proposes a scalable Digital Twin framework for energy optimization in data centers.The framework integrates IoT-based data acquisition, cloud computing, and machine learning techniques to enable real-time monitoring, forecasting, and intelligent energy management. A controlled small-scale data center environment was developed to monitor variables such as power consumption, temperature, and computational workload. Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) models were employed to predict energy demand and support operational decision-making. Experimental results demonstrated improvements in energy efficiency, including reductions in power consumption and enhancements in Power Usage Effectiveness (PUE). Despite being evaluated in a constrained environment, the proposed framework demonstrates strong potential as a scalable and cost-effective solution for sustainable data center management.

</details>


### [4] [Irminsul: MLA-Native Position-Independent Caching for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05696)
*Bole Ma,Jan Eitzinger,Harald Köstler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Irminsul系统，通过内容寻址缓存解决智能体LLM工作负载中因位置偏移导致的prefix缓存失效问题，在MLA架构模型上实现最高83%的token恢复率和63%的预填充能耗节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体LLM工作负载在每个轮次中会将完全相同的token置于偏移位置，导致prefix缓存在首个分歧字节处失效，造成缓存命中率下降和时间到首token（TTFT）严重飙升（10-16秒）。现有位置无关缓存系统需要在全维度键上校正RoPE，这带来了由GQA而非缓存本身引入的架构成本。

Method: 提出Irminsul系统，扩展SGLang的radix cache，采用基于内容哈希的键控机制处理CDC分块片段，并引入δ-旋转规则处理k_r分量。该方法利用Multi-Head Latent Attention（MLA）架构中KV行分解为位置无关的c_KV和64维k_r的结构特性，实现内容寻址缓存。

Result: 在三个原生MLA-MoE部署（DeepSeek-V2-Lite、Kimi Moonlight-16B-A3B、JoyAI-Flash）上进行评估，所有三个模型均保持输出一致性。在智能体流量场景下，相比精确前缀匹配，Irminsul恢复高达约83%的提示token，每次缓存命中节省63%的预填充能耗。

Conclusion: 内容寻址缓存应作为服务栈中的一等原语存在，而非对前缀匹配的修补方案。该方法在MLA架构中具有天然适配性，而非仅作为GQA的权宜之计。

Abstract: Agentic LLM workloads put bit-identical tokens at shifted positions every turn, voiding prefix caches at the first byte of divergence. Operators report cache-hit regressions ranging from moderate slowdowns to severe TTFT spikes of 10-16s on unchanged content. Prior position-independent caching systems correct RoPE on the full $d_K$-dimensional key, an architectural cost imposed by GQA, not by caching itself. Multi-Head Latent Attention, deployed at scale in DeepSeek-V2/V3/R1, Kimi-K2/Moonlight, GLM-5, and Mistral Large 3, factors each KV row into a position-free $c_{KV}$ and a 64-dim $k_r$ correctable in closed form; this structure motivates content-addressed caching as a natural fit rather than a GQA workaround. We present Irminsul, which extends SGLang's radix cache with content-hash keying over CDC-chunked segments and a $δ$-rotation rule for $k_r$. We evaluate three native MLA-MoE deployments - DeepSeek-V2-Lite (16B/2.4B), Kimi Moonlight-16B-A3B, and JoyAI-Flash (48B/3B) - with output-consistency on all three and recovery measured on the two endpoints; Irminsul recovers up to ~83% of prompt tokens above exact-prefix on agentic traffic while delivering 63% prefill energy savings per cache hit. We argue that content-addressed caching belongs in the serving stack as a first-class primitive, not a retrofit over prefix matching.

</details>


### [5] [LLM-Enhanced Deep Reinforcement Learning for Task Offloading in Collaborative Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05727)
*Hao Guo,Kaixiang Xv,Ziwu Ge,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了LeDRL框架，通过结合轻量级LLM与自注意力增强的DRL，解决了协作边缘计算中任务卸载的实时决策问题，在任务成功率上提升了17%以上，并在Jetson边缘设备上验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作边缘计算需要动态任务卸载决策来维持低延迟和高可靠性，尤其是在节点故障不可预测的情况下。然而，现有的深度强化学习（DRL）方法存在样本效率低和易陷入局部最优的问题，而大语言模型（LLM）则难以满足实时决策的需求。

Method: 提出LeDRL混合决策框架，将轻量级LLM与自注意力增强的DRL相结合。该框架构建结构化、上下文感知的提示（Prompts）以获取高层策略先验，并通过基于自注意力的对齐模块进行上下文感知的策略优化。同时，引入反思性评估器从历史轨迹中提取语义反馈，指导未来的提示生成。

Result: 在多种网络规模下的广泛实验表明，LeDRL在任务成功率、收敛速度和实时响应性方面均优于基线方法，任务成功率提升超过17%。此外，在基于Jetson的边缘设备原型系统CoEdgeSys上部署，验证了其在资源约束下的鲁棒性和可行性。

Conclusion: LeDRL有效地结合了LLM的语义理解能力和DRL的实时决策优势，克服了各自的局限性，为协作边缘计算中的动态任务卸载提供了一个高效、鲁棒且可实际部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Collaborative edge computing uses edge nodes in different locations to execute tasks, necessitating dynamic task offloading decisions to maintain low latency and high reliability, especially under unpredictable node failures. Although deep reinforcement learning (DRL) and large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for task offloading, DRL often suffers from high sample inefficiency and local optima, whereas LLMs struggle with real-time decision-making. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{LeDRL}, a hybrid decision framework that couples a \emph{lightweight LLM} with self-attention-enhanced DRL for real-time task offloading. LeDRL constructs structured, context-aware prompts capturing node status, task semantics, and link dynamics to derive high-level strategy priors. These are selectively processed by a self-attention-based alignment module for context-aware policy optimization. A reflective evaluator distills semantic feedback from past trajectories to guide future prompts, enabling more informative and temporally generalizable LLM queries. Extensive experiments show that LeDRL outperforms baselines in task success rate, convergence speed, and real-time responsiveness across diverse network scales, achieving over 17\% improvement in success rate. Furthermore, we deploy LeDRL on Jetson-based edge devices using our prototype system \textit{CoEdgeSys}, demonstrating its robustness and feasibility under resource constraints. Our code is available at:https://github.com/GalleyG5/LeDRL.git.

</details>


### [6] [A Privacy-Preserving Machine Learning Framework for Edge Intelligence: An Empirical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05751)
*Quoc Lap Trieu,Bahman Javadi,Jim Basilakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对边缘智能提出了隐私保护机器学习框架，对比分析了差分隐私(DP)、安全多方计算(SMC)和全同态加密(FHE)三种方法在准确率、响应时间和能耗方面的性能差异，并揭示了它们在隐私-效用-可提取性权衡上的不同特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着边缘智能在智慧医疗、制造业和关键基础设施等领域的广泛应用，在保持系统效率的同时确保数据隐私成为一个日益严峻的挑战。现有隐私保护方法在实际边缘部署中的性能特征和权衡关系尚不清晰，亟需系统性的评估研究。

Method: 提出了一个专为边缘智能应用定制的隐私保护机器学习框架，包含四层系统架构以及训练和推理算法。重点研究了差分隐私(DP)、安全多方计算(SMC)和全同态加密(FHE)三种主流方法，通过实际实现和基于大规模追踪的仿真实验，评估了这些方法对模型准确率、响应时间和能耗等关键性能指标的影响。

Result: DP在吞吐量和延迟方面接近明文基准，但准确率随模型复杂度下降（AlexNet下降达35%，LeNet在FordA上低于18%）。SMC性能由通信主导，网络带宽和轮复杂度决定端到端延迟（链路容量从250Mbps增至500Mbps可降低AlexNet约30%延迟）。FHE对模型结构和数值精度位宽高度敏感，响应时间约为DP的1000倍。DP通过降低攻击者在黑盒模型窃取中的数据效率来改变隐私-效用-可提取性前沿，而SMC和FHE在推理过程中保护输入和参数，但需要补充输出控制以实现对模型提取的类似抵抗能力。

Conclusion: 研究结果为边缘计算场景中隐私、性能和资源效率之间的权衡提供了关键见解，为实践者根据具体应用需求选择合适的隐私保护技术提供了指导，并指出了不同方法在安全性、效率和可部署性方面的互补特性。

Abstract: As Edge Intelligence (EI) becomes increasingly prevalent in domains such as smart healthcare, manufacturing, and critical infrastructure, ensuring data privacy while maintaining system efficiency is a growing challenge. This paper presents a new privacy-preserving machine learning (PPML) framework tailored for EI applications, including a four-layer system architecture and training and inference algorithms. We focus on three leading approaches: Differential Privacy (DP), Secure Multi-party Computation (SMC), and Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE), and assess their impact on key performance metrics, including model accuracy, response time, and energy consumption. Results from real implementation and extensive trace-based simulations of inference tasks show that DP generally preserves throughput and latency close to plaintext baselines, while accuracy drops with model complexity (up to 35 percent on AlexNet and under 18 percent on LeNet for FordA). SMC performance is driven by communication; network bandwidth and round complexity determine end-to-end latency. For AlexNet, increasing link capacity from 250 Mbps to 500 Mbps reduces latency by about 30 percent. FHE is highly sensitive to model structure and numerical precision bit width, with tighter parameters imposing substantial compute overhead; we observe roughly a 1000 times increase in response time compared to DP. Beyond efficiency, DP shifts the privacy-utility-extractability frontier by reducing the attacker's data efficiency in black-box model stealing, whereas SMC and FHE, while protecting inputs and parameters during inference, require complementary output controls to achieve similar resistance to extraction. These findings provide critical insights into the trade-offs between privacy, performance, and resource efficiency in edge computing scenarios.

</details>


### [7] [Relay Buffer Independent Communication over Pooled HBM for Efficient MoE Inference on Ascend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06055)
*Tianlun Hu,Tiancheng Hu,Shengsheng Litang,Sheng Wang,Xiaoming Bao,Yuxing Li,Wei Wang,Zhongzhe Hu,Lijun Li,Hongwei Sun,Jingbin Zhou\\*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向Ascend系统的无中继缓冲区MoE通信设计，通过直接将数据放置到目标专家窗口并直接读取远程专家窗口，消除了大部分中间中继和重排序缓冲区，有效降低了dispatch和combine的延迟，提升了MoE推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家模型（MoE）推理过程中，跨设备的token交换规模巨大，导致dispatch和combine成为预填充和解码阶段的主要性能瓶颈。除网络传输外，路由驱动的布局变换、临时中继和输出恢复等操作也带来显著开销。现有MoE通信路径通常以缓冲区为中心，需要在集合传输周围使用显式的进程间中继和重排序缓冲区，这增加了系统复杂度和延迟。

Method: 提出了一种无中继缓冲区的通信设计，核心是围绕直接放置到目标专家窗口和直接从远程专家窗口读取来重组dispatch和combine操作。该设计基于全局池化高带宽内存和对称内存分配，仅保留轻量级控制状态（包括计数、偏移和同步元数据），消除了大部分中间中继和重排序缓冲区。具体实现了两种调度策略：针对吞吐量导向执行的prefill调度（具有更丰富的规划状态），以及针对延迟敏感执行的紧凑decode调度。

Result: 在基于Ascend的MoE工作负载实验中，该设计在预填充和解码两个场景下均降低了dispatch和combine延迟。在服务层面，实现改善了首令牌时间（TTFT），保持了具有竞争力的每输出令牌时间（TPOT），并在实际延迟约束下扩大了可行的调度空间。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在具有全局可寻址设备内存的平台上，减少专家执行周围的中间缓冲和输出恢复是加速MoE推理的有效方向。该设计为后续MoE系统优化提供了新的思路，特别是在内存架构支持全局寻址的硬件平台上。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference requires large-scale token exchange across devices, making dispatch and combine major bottlenecks in both prefill and decode. Beyond network transfer, routing-driven layout transformation, temporary relay, and output restoration can add substantial overhead. Existing MoE communication paths are often buffer-centric, using explicit inter-process relay and reordering buffers around collective transfer. This report presents a relay-buffer-free communication design for MoE inference acceleration on Ascend systems. The design reorganizes dispatch and combine around direct placement into destination expert windows and direct reading from remote expert windows. Built on globally pooled high-bandwidth memory and symmetric-memory allocation, it removes most intermediate relay and reordering buffers while retaining only lightweight control state, including counts, offsets, and synchronization metadata. We instantiate the design as two schedules for the main phases of MoE inference: a prefill schedule with richer planning state for throughput-oriented execution, and a compact decode schedule for latency-sensitive execution. Experiments on Ascend-based MoE workloads show reduced dispatch and combine latency in both settings. At the serving level, the implementation improves time to first token (TTFT), preserves competitive time per output token (TPOT), and enlarges the feasible scheduling space under practical latency constraints. These results indicate that, on platforms with globally addressable device memory, reducing intermediate buffering and output restoration around expert execution is an effective direction for accelerating MoE inference.

</details>


### [8] [FalconGEMM: Surpassing Hardware Peaks with Lower-Complexity Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06057)
*Honglin Zhu,Jiaping Cao,Jiang Shao,Siyuan Feng,Qian Qiu,Peng Chen,Xu Zhang,Yixian Zhou,Man Lung Yiu,Guang Ji,Minwen Deng,Wenxi Zhu,Jintao Meng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了FalconGEMM，一个跨平台框架，通过自动化部署、优化和选择低复杂度矩阵乘法算法（LCMAs），在多种硬件上实现了超越现有GEMM库和LCMA竞争者的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低复杂度矩阵乘法算法（LCMAs）在理论上具有降低计算复杂度的潜力，特别适合大规模深度学习模型（如LLM）的训练和推理。然而，现有方法难以在不同硬件平台上高效部署和优化，缺乏自动化的策略选择机制，使得LCMAs的实际应用受限。

Method: 提出了FalconGEMM框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 部署模块，通过代码生成实现跨硬件和输入配置的可移植执行；(2) 执行模块，采用组并行优化技术最大化片上数据复用、利用并行资源并减少带宽开销；(3) 决策模块，利用轻量级分析性能模型，根据矩阵形状和硬件配置文件选择最优策略。

Result: 在GPU（H20、A100）和CPU（ARM、x86）架构上的LLM工作负载评估中，FalconGEMM相比cuBLAS、CUTLASS、Intel MKL等GEMM库性能提升7.59%-17.85%，相比AlphaTensor等LCMA竞争者性能提升12.41%-55.61%，成功实现了峰值突破性能。

Conclusion: FalconGEMM框架有效地将低复杂度矩阵乘法算法的理论优势转化为实际生产力，实现了跨现代异构硬件环境的生产级部署，为深度学习特别是大语言模型的训练和推理提供了显著的性能加速。

Abstract: Peak breaking Matrix Multiplication is a promising technique to improve the performance of DL, especially in LLM training and inference. We present FalconGEMM, a cross-platform framework that automates the deployment, optimization, and selection of Lower-Complexity Matrix Multiplication Algorithms (LCMAs) across diverse hardware. There are three key innovations: (1) a Deployment Module that enables portable execution across various hardware and input configurations through code generation; (2) an Execution Module with Group-Parallel Optimizations that maximizes on-chip data reuse, utilizes parallel resources, and reduces bandwidth overhead; and (3) a Decision Module featuring a lightweight analytical performance model to select the optimal strategy based on matrix shapes and hardware profiles. Extensive evaluation is conducted on LLM workloads across GPU (H20, A100) and CPU (ARM, x86) architectures with multiple data types. FalconGEMM succeeds in delivering peak breaking performance and outperforms GEMM libraries (e.g., cuBLAS, CUTLASS, Intel MKL, etc) by 7.59%-17.85% and LCMA competitors like AlphaTensor by 12.41%-55.61%. Our framework makes the theoretical promise of LCMAs practical for production deployment across the heterogeneous landscape of modern hardware.

</details>


### [9] [Tackling the Data-Parallel Load Balancing Bottleneck in LLM Serving: Practical Online Routing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06113)
*Tianci Bu,Yuan Lyu,Zixi Chen,Chendong Song,Hong Liang,Tsepten Gurung,Yuwei Fan,Yinyu Ye,Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了BalanceRoute算法族，旨在解决大规模LLM服务中数据并行负载均衡瓶颈问题，通过在线路由策略显著降低负载不均衡并提升服务吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在采用张量并行（TP）或专家并行（EP）的大规模LLM服务中，数据并行（DP）负载均衡已成为首要瓶颈。由于同步屏障的存在，每个解码步骤的延迟由负载最重的工作节点决定，即使是轻微的持续负载不均衡也会累积造成大量计算浪费。该问题在LLM解码场景中尤为棘手：请求分配具有粘性（KV缓存迁移成本高）、单请求负载随时间增长、请求到达非平稳，且路由器需在亚100毫秒的解码预算内对数百个请求和数十个工作节点做出决策。

Method: 提出BalanceRoute算法族，包含两个变体：(1) BR-0：无需预测基础设施，采用分段线性F-score评估函数，刻画填充安全边界与溢出包络之间的尖锐不对称性，通过两阶段分解使单步成本与毫秒级调度兼容；(2) BR-H：在BR-0基础上引入短常数视界H和轻量级终止分类器接口，将F-score扩展为视界折扣形式。

Result: 在144节点NPU集群上部署BalanceRoute，基于私有生产轨迹和公开Azure-2024轨迹与vLLM基线进行对比评估。两种工作负载下，BalanceRoute均显著降低了平均DP不均衡度，并提升了端到端服务吞吐量。

Conclusion: BalanceRoute提供了一套实用的在线路由解决方案，有效应对LLM解码场景下DP负载均衡的独特挑战，在真实生产环境中展现出显著的性能提升，为大规模LLM服务系统优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Data-parallel (DP) load balancing has emerged as a first-order bottleneck in large-scale LLM serving. When a model is sharded across devices via tensor parallelism (TP) or expert parallelism (EP) and replicated across many DP workers, every decode step ends in a synchronization barrier whose latency is set by the most heavily loaded worker; even modest persistent imbalance across DP workers compounds, step after step, into a substantial fraction of wasted compute. The problem is hard for reasons specific to LLM decoding: assignments are sticky (migrating KV caches has a high cost), per-request loads grow over time, arrivals are non-stationary, and the router must decide within a sub-100\,ms decode budget over hundreds of waiting requests and tens of workers. We present \textbf{BalanceRoute}, a family of practical online routing algorithms that target this bottleneck. The first, \textbf{BR-0}, requires no prediction infrastructure and uses a piecewise-linear F-score that captures the sharp asymmetry between admissions that fill safe margin and those that overflow into the envelope; a two-stage decomposition keeps per-step cost compatible with millisecond-scale scheduling. The second, \textbf{BR-H}, generalizes BR-0 with a short, constant lookahead $H$ and a lightweight termination-classifier interface, extending the F-score to a horizon-discounted form. We deploy BalanceRoute on a 144-NPU cluster and evaluate against vLLM baselines on both a proprietary production trace and the public Azure-2024 trace. Across both workloads, BalanceRoute substantially reduces average DP imbalance and improves end-to-end serving throughput.

</details>


### [10] [CCL-Bench 1.0: A Trace-Based Benchmark for LLM Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06544)
*Eric Ding,Byungsoo Oh,Bhaskar Kataria,Kaiwen Guo,Jelena Gvero,Abhishek Vijaya Kumar,Arjun Devraj,Lindsey Bowen,Atharv Sonwane,Emaad Manzoor,Rachee Singh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了CCL-Bench，一种基于跟踪数据的基准测试工具，通过记录可复用的证据（执行轨迹、YAML工作负载卡、启动脚本）来计算细粒度的计算、内存和通信效率指标，解决了现有基准测试无法解释配置性能差异的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的大语言模型基础设施评估基准测试（如"工作负载X在硬件Y和软件Z上最快"）依赖于复杂的配置空间，涵盖硬件加速器、互连带宽、软件框架、并行化策略和通信库。现有基准测试仅发布少量端到端数值，无法解释为何某配置优于另一配置，缺乏诊断性信息。

Method: 提出CCL-Bench，一种基于跟踪数据的基准测试方法。每个数据点包含三个核心组件：执行轨迹、YAML工作负载卡和启动脚本。开发了社区可扩展的工具包，基于这些证据计算细粒度的计算、内存和通信效率指标，支持社区扩展和复用。

Result: 使用CCL-Bench揭示了三个关键发现：（i）更高的计算-通信重叠可能伴随更长的训练步骤时间，暴露低效的并行化选择；（ii）对于中小型工作负载，TPU互连带宽翻倍带来的端到端步骤时间提升显著高于GPU互连带宽翻倍；（iii）在相同硬件上，某一训练框架的最优配置可能比同类框架的最优配置慢达3倍。

Conclusion: CCL-Bench通过提供细粒度的可复用证据和分析工具，克服了现有基准测试的局限性，能够支持更深入的配置分析和性能诊断，为大语言模型基础设施的评估和优化提供了更有效的工具。

Abstract: Evaluative claims about LLM infrastructure -- ``workload X is fastest on hardware Y with software Z'' -- depend on a complex configuration space spanning hardware accelerators, interconnect bandwidth, software frameworks, parallelism plans, and communication libraries. Current infrastructure evaluation benchmarks publish a small set of end-to-end numbers that do not explain why one configuration outperforms another. We present CCL-Bench, a trace-based benchmark that addresses the limitations of existing benchmarks by recording reusable evidence for every ML workload. Each contributed data point in CCL-Bench packages an execution trace, a YAML workload card, and the launch scripts. We have developed a community-extensible toolkit to compute fine-grained compute, memory, and communication efficiency metrics from this evidence. Using CCL-Bench, we surface three claims that summary-statistic benchmarks cannot support: (i) higher compute-communication overlap can coincide with longer training step time and reveal inefficient parallelization choices, (ii) doubling TPU interconnect bandwidth yields a much higher end-to-end improvement in step time than doubling GPU interconnect bandwidth on small and medium workloads, and (iii) the best-tuned configuration on one training framework can run up to 3$\times$ slower than the best-tuned configuration on a peer framework on identical hardware.

</details>

*Paul Eichler,Tom Baumeister,Mouhammad Sakr,Mahboubeh Kalateh Dowlati,Marcus Völp,Swen Jacobs*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TACO工具套件，用于容错和基于阈值的分布式算法的开发与自动验证，实现了多种模型检测方法并进行了实验性能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错和基于阈值的分布式算法在分布式系统中具有重要作用，但其验证过程复杂且具有挑战性。需要一个模块化、可扩展且文档完善的工具框架来支持这类算法的开发和自动验证。

Method: 实现了三种针对阈值自动机的可判定片段的模型检测方法，以及两种超越可判定片段的半决策过程。TACO采用模块化、可扩展的设计架构，并提供完善的文档支持。

Result: 成功开发了TACO工具套件，实现了多种验证方法，并通过实验评估了这些算法的性能表现，证明了工具的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: TACO为阈值自动机的算法开发和验证提供了一个功能强大、模块化且可扩展的框架，填补了该领域工具支持的空白，为后续研究和工具开发奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present TACO, a toolsuite for the development and automatic verification of fault-tolerant and threshold-based distributed algorithms. Our toolsuite implements three approaches for model checking threshold automata in different decidable fragments known from the literature and two semi-decision procedures going beyond these decidable fragments. Moreover, TACO is a modular, extensible, and well-documented framework for developing algorithms and tools for threshold automata. We present important features, give an overview of the implemented algorithms, and evaluate their performance experimentally.

</details>


### [12] [ResiHP: Taming LLM Training Failures with Dynamic Hybrid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06374)
*Tenghui Ma,Jihu Guo,Wei Gao,Sitian Lu,Zhisheng Ye,Hanjing Wang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ResiHP系统，通过工作负载感知的故障检测器和动态调度器，解决混合并行大模型训练中因硬件故障导致的性能偏差问题，训练吞吐量相比现有系统提升1.04-4.39倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM训练中，硬件故障导致设备间性能偏差，降低整体训练效率。现有弹性系统忽视了数据集序列长度可变性和混合并行下的设备性能偏差问题，导致：(1)序列长度变化引起的迭代时间波动触发虚假故障检测；(2)故障缓解方式低效，增加不必要的检测开销。

Method: 提出ResiHP弹性训练系统，包含两个核心组件：(1)故障检测器（Detector）：采用工作负载感知的执行时间预测器，在轻量级在线检测的同时将真实故障与迭代时间波动区分开；(2)调度器（Scheduler）：动态调整并行组大小、模型分区和工作负载调度策略，优化故障情况下的训练效率。

Result: 在256-GPU集群上的实验表明，ResiHP在多种故障场景下相比最先进的弹性训练系统，训练吞吐量提升1.04-4.39倍。

Conclusion: ResiHP通过准确识别故障和细粒度自适应策略，有效解决了混合并行训练中的性能偏差问题，显著提升了故障场景下的训练效率，为大规模LLM训练提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Hybrid parallelism underpins large-scale LLM training across tens of thousands of GPUs. At such scale, hardware failures on individual devices lead to performance skew across devices, diminishing overall training efficiency. Existing resilient systems overlook sequence length variability in datasets and device performance skew under hybrid parallelism. As a result, (1) iteration time fluctuations induced by sequence length variability can trigger spurious fail-slow detections, and (2) failures are mitigated through individual adaptations in hybrid parallelism, leading to unnecessary detection overhead and inefficient resilient training. To respond, this paper presents ResiHP, a resilient system that enables robust failure detection and fine-grained adaptation for hybrid parallel training. First, we develop a Detector to accurately identify failures. In particular, it employs a workload-aware execution time predictor that disentangles failures from iteration time fluctuations while remaining lightweight for online detection. Second, we design a Scheduler that dynamically adapts parallelism group sizes, model partitioning, and workload scheduling policies to improve training efficiency under failures. Experiments show that ResiHP improves training throughput by 1.04-4.39$\times$ compared with state-of-the-art resilient training systems under diverse failure scenarios in a 256-GPU cluster.

</details>


### [13] [ADELIA: Automatic Differentiation for Efficient Laplace Inference Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06392)
*Afif Boudaoud,Lisa Gaedke-Merzhäuser,Alexandros Nikolaos Ziogas,Vincent Maillou,Alexandru Calotoiu,Marcin Copik,Håvard Rue,Mathieu Luisier,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ADELIA，首个利用反向自动微分(AD)的高性能INLA实现。通过多GPU并行和稀疏结构利用，在梯度计算上实现了4.2-7.9倍加速，显著降低了大规模时空贝叶斯推断的计算时间和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有INLA实现使用中心有限差分(FD)近似导数，需要2d+1次评估，计算量和能耗随超参数维度d线性增长，限制了固定预算下的求解时间。虽然反向自动微分(AD)的梯度计算复杂度独立于d，但将其高效应用于INLA的结构化稀疏核仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出ADELIA方法，这是首个支持自动微分的INLA实现。核心技术包括：1) 利用反向模式自动微分计算精确梯度；2) 设计结构感知的多GPU反向传播算法；3) 充分利用模型的稀疏性结构优化计算。

Result: 在10个基准模型上评估（包括真实空气污染监测案例），实现每个梯度4.2-7.9倍加速；在高达190万潜在变量的生产规模模型上可靠收敛，而FD方法表现不佳；即使在16-32 GPU上扩展以匹配ADELIA的运行时间，FD仍消耗5-8倍更多能量。

Conclusion: ADELIA成功解决了INLA中自动微分应用的关键挑战，为大规模时空贝叶斯推断提供了高效、节能的解决方案，在计算速度、收敛性和能耗方面均显著优于传统有限差分方法。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal Bayesian inference drives environmental and health sciences using latent Gaussian models. Integrated Nested Laplace Approximations (INLA) enable inference for these models at HPC scale but rely on derivative-based optimization over $d$ hyperparameters. State-of-the-art INLA implementations approximate derivatives via central finite differences (FD), requiring $2d{+}1$ evaluations. These evaluations are embarrassingly parallel, but total work and energy grow with $d$, limiting time-to-solution under fixed budgets. Reverse-mode automatic differentiation (AD) computes exact gradients independently of $d$, but its efficient application to INLA's structured-sparse kernels is an open challenge. We present ADELIA, the first AD-enabled INLA implementation with a structure-exploiting multi-GPU backward pass leveraging model sparsity. We evaluate ADELIA on ten benchmark models, including real-world air-pollution monitoring. We achieve $4.2$--$7.9\times$ per-gradient speedups and reliable convergence on production-scale models with up to 1.9M latent variables, where FD struggles. Even when scaled to 16--32 GPUs to match ADELIA's wall-clock time, FD consumes $5$--$8\times$ more energy.

</details>


### [14] [ROSE: Rollout On Serving GPUs via Cooperative Elasticity for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06534)
*Wei Gao,Yuheng Zhao,Dilxat Muhtar,Dakai An,Xuchun Shang,Tianyuan Wu,Lunxi Cao,Shaopan Xiong,Weixun Wang,Ju Huang,Teng Ma,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Bo Zheng,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了ROSE系统，通过协同弹性机制，安全地复用服务集群中的闲置GPU资源来加速智能体强化学习的轨迹推演，在不影响服务SLO的前提下，实现了1.20-3.31倍的端到端吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体强化学习（Agentic RL）在执行多步推理和工具使用时，受限于长尾的推演过程和多轮环境交互，导致静态GPU资源供给效率低下：过度供给造成资源浪费，供给不足则引发资源竞争并拖慢训练。作者观察到生产环境中的服务集群通常存在大量闲置的GPU计算和内存资源，由此提出利用这些闲置资源来缓解瓶颈。

Method: 提出了ROSE系统，这是一种协同弹性后训练系统，包含三个核心组件：(1) SLO安全的协同服务执行器，通过高效的GPU内存和计算共享来提升推演吞吐量，同时保障服务等级目标；(2) 跨集群权重传输引擎，利用权重分片和稀疏性技术实现跨集群快速权重同步；(3) 弹性推演调度器，动态配置协同资源容量，并在专用推演GPU和机会性服务GPU之间调度轨迹推演任务。

Result: 在多种模型规模和集群规模下的实验表明，与最先进的资源固定和弹性基线相比，ROSE将平均端到端吞吐量提升了1.20至3.31倍。

Conclusion: 协同弹性是一种有效的资源优化策略，能够在保障服务性能的前提下，通过安全复用服务集群的闲置GPU资源显著提升智能体强化学习的训练效率。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a key driver for improving the multi-step reasoning and tool-use capabilities of LLMs. However, its efficiency is bottlenecked by long-tail rollouts with multi-turn environment interactions, making static GPU provisioning a poor fit: overprovisioning wastes GPUs on stragglers, while underprovisioning increases contention and slows training.
  We observe that production serving clusters routinely leave substantial GPU compute and memory headroom. Based on this observation, we argue for cooperative elasticity: opportunistically repurposing underutilized serving GPUs to execute rollouts. Realizing cooperative elasticity is non-trivial because it must preserve serving Service Level Objectives (SLOs) under bursty traffic and minimize communication overhead. To address these challenges, we present ROSE, a cooperative, resource-elastic post-training system that safely harvests idle compute and memory on serving GPUs to accelerate agentic RL rollouts. ROSE consists of three components: (1) an SLO-safe co-serving executor that improves rollout throughput while preserving serving SLOs through efficient GPU memory and compute sharing; (2) a cross-cluster weight transfer engine that leverages weight shards and sparsity for fast weight synchronization across clusters; and (3) an elastic rollout scheduler that dynamically provisions cooperative capacity and routes trajectory rollouts across dedicated rollout GPUs and opportunistic serving GPUs. Experiments across multiple model sizes and cluster scales show that ROSE improves average end-to-end throughput by 1.20-3.31 x compared with state-of-the-art resource-fixed and elastic baselines.

</details>

### [15] [An Open-Source Flow for Single-Phase, Edge-Triggered to Two-Phase, Non-Overlapping Clocking Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05374)
*Paolo Pedroso,Lee-Way Wang,Matthew Guthaus*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个集成于OpenROAD流程的全自动两相时钟设计流程，通过将触发器RTL自动转换为两相锁存器设计，相比复用器方案，门控时钟版本平均降低29.2%功耗并减少50%锁存器数量，且在基准设计时序违例的情况下利用时间借用成功实现时序收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两相时钟在时序裕量和时钟灵活性方面具有显著优势，但严格的不重叠约束和180°相位分离要求导致RTL实现与时序收敛极其复杂，缺乏自动化支持使其在实际应用中极少被采用。

Method: 提出首个集成于OpenROAD Flow Scripts的全自动两相时钟流程：利用Yosys技术映射将触发器RTL转换为锁存器设计，通过ABC重定时优化，执行双时钟树综合、两相正确性验证，并完成从RTL到GDS的全流程物理设计。实现了时钟门控与复用器两种锁存器变体。

Result: 时钟门控变体相比复用器变体平均降低29.2%功耗、减少50%锁存器数量；两种变体均与触发器基准对比，在一个触发器设计时序失败的案例中，利用两相时钟的时间借用特性成功实现时序收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作通过全自动化流程消除了两相时钟设计的主要障碍，证明了其在功耗与时序收敛方面的实用价值，为两相时钟在现代设计流程中的广泛应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Two-phase clocking offers significant advantages in timing margin and clock flexibility, yet its adoption remains limited due to the absence of automation in modern design flows. Managing strict non-overlap and 180$^\circ$ phase separation introduces complexity in RTL implementation and timing closure, leaving two-phase clocking rare in practice. This paper presents the first fully automated two-phase clocking flow integrated into OpenROAD Flow Scripts (ORFS). Our methodology automatically transforms flip-flop-based RTL into two-phase latch-based designs using Yosys technology mapping, ABC retiming, dual clock tree synthesis, two-phase correctness validation, and full physical design from RTL-to-GDS. We implement clock-gated and recirculation mux variants, where clock-gated achieves an average 29.2\% power reduction and 50\% latch count reduction over recirculation mux. Both variants are compared against flip-flop baselines, demonstrating timing closure through time borrowing on a design that failed timing with flip-flops.

</details>


### [16] [Beyond Static Policies: Exploring Dynamic Policy Selection for Single-Thread Performance Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05471)
*Yanxin Zhang,Ian McDougall,Junnan Li,Shayne Wadle,Vikas Singh,Karthikeyan Sankaralingam*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模模拟分析发现，不同程序执行阶段适合不同的策略组合。静态策略相比理想情况平均有1.54%的IPC损失，而动态策略选择机制可将损失降低13.6倍至0.11%，为单线程性能优化提供了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十多年处理器设计均采用单一静态策略（如缓存替换和预取策略），本文旨在探究不同工作负载或同一负载的不同执行阶段是否需要不同的策略组合，以打破静态设计的局限性。

Method: 使用ChampSim模拟器，对49个基准程序划分为490个执行阶段（每阶段2000万条指令），系统评估多种缓存替换和预取策略组合的性能表现，并与传统静态策略处理器及理想预言机进行对比。

Result: 最佳静态策略仅在19.18%的执行阶段达到最优，平均IPC损失为1.54%；17.35%的阶段损失超过2.5%。能够在两种策略间动态切换的处理器可将平均IPC损失降至0.11%（降低13.6倍），并在52.65%的时间内匹配预言机性能。

Conclusion: 动态策略选择机制相比传统静态策略具有显著性能优势，可有效减少IPC损失并释放单线程性能潜力，是未来处理器设计的重要研究方向。

Abstract: For over a decade, processor design has focused on implementing sophisticated policies for various components of the out-of-order pipeline, including cache replacement and prefetching. The prevailing design philosophy has been to build processors with a single, static selection of policies across these different mechanisms. This paper investigates a fundamental question: do different workloads, or even different execution phases within the same workload, benefit from different policy combinations? We present a comprehensive analysis exploring whether a hypothetical processor capable of dynamically selecting from multiple policies could significantly outperform traditional static-policy processors. Using ChampSim-based simulation across 49 benchmarks segmented into 490 execution phases of 20M instructions each, we evaluate performance across multiple policy combinations for cache replacement and prefetching. Our findings reveal that significant performance headroom exists: the best static policy achieves optimal performance for only 19.18\% of execution phases and incurs a mean IPC loss of 1.54\% compared to an oracle. Moreover, 85 phases (17.35\%), spanning 14 of the 49 applications, exhibit more than 2.5\% IPC loss relative to the oracle. Furthermore, we demonstrate that a processor capable of dynamically switching between two carefully chosen policies can achieve a 13.6$\times$ reduction in mean IPC loss (from 1.54\% to 0.11\%) and match oracle performance 52.65\% of the time. These results suggest that dynamic policy selection represents a promising avenue for unlocking single-thread performance improvements that have become increasingly difficult to achieve.

</details>


### [17] [DICE: Enabling Efficient General-Purpose SIMT Execution with Statically Scheduled Coarse-Grained Reconfigurable Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05496)
*Jiayi Wang,Ang Da Lu,Zhichen Zeng,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: DICE用粗粒度可重构阵列（CGRA）替代GPU的SIMD后端，通过空间流水线执行减少68%的寄存器堆访问，在保持性能相当的同时实现1.77-1.90倍能效提升和42%-46%功耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU虽然通过SIMT编程模型主导大规模并行计算，但其底层SIMD执行机制存在显著的能效开销，主要源于频繁的寄存器堆访问和复杂的控制逻辑。这些开销限制了GPU的能效表现。

Method: 提出DICE架构，用静态调度的粗粒度可重构阵列（CGRA）替代SIMD后端。核心创新包括：(1) 将活跃线程以流水线方式分发到CGRA fabric上，使数据在处理单元间直接流动；(2) 引入p-graph概念处理运行时动态性（如可变延迟内存加载和数据依赖控制流），通过将动态依赖边划分到不同CGRA配置来保持静态调度；(3) 关键优化：双缓冲配置内存隐藏重配置延迟、编译时p-graph展开提升资源利用率、时序内存合并单元（TMCU）合并连续流水线线程的内存请求。

Result: 在Rodinia基准测试上的评估表明：DICE平均减少68%的寄存器堆访问；在相同计算和内存资源下，CGRA处理器相比NVIDIA Turing流式多处理器实现1.77-1.90倍动态能效提升和42.0%-45.9%的平均功耗降低，完整DICE系统性能与Turing GPU基线相当。

Conclusion: 空间流水线执行可以在不牺牲性能的情况下实现显著的能效提升，证明了用CGRA替代SIMD后端是提升GPU能效的有效途径。

Abstract: While GPUs dominate massively parallel computing through the single-instruction, multiple-thread (SIMT) programming model, their underlying single-instruction, multiple-data (SIMD) execution incurs substantial energy overhead from frequent register file (RF) accesses and complex control logic. We present DICE, a novel architecture that addresses these inefficiencies by replacing the SIMD backend with minimal-overhead, statically scheduled coarse-grained reconfigurable arrays (CGRAs). Unlike SIMD units that execute warps of threads in lockstep, DICE dispatches active threads in a pipelined manner onto the CGRA fabric, where data flow directly between processing elements (PEs), reducing RF accesses for intermediate values. To handle operations with runtime dynamism, such as variable-latency memory loads and data-dependent control flow, while preserving static scheduling, DICE compiles programs into "p-graphs" by partitioning dynamic dependence edges across separate CGRA configurations. DICE further introduces several key optimizations: double-buffered configuration memory to hide reconfiguration latency, compile-time p-graph unrolling to enhance resource utilization, and a temporal memory coalescing unit (TMCU) to merge memory requests from consecutive, pipelined threads. Evaluations on Rodinia benchmarks in Accel-sim demonstrate that DICE reduces register file accesses by 68% on average. With equivalent computation and memory resources, DICE's CGRA Processors (CPs) achieve a geometric mean of 1.77-1.90x dynamic energy efficiency and 42.0%-45.9% average power reduction compared to the modeled NVIDIA Turing Streaming Multiprocessors (SMs), while the full DICE system achieves performance comparable to the modeled Turing GPU baselines. DICE demonstrates that spatial pipeline execution can deliver substantial energy savings without sacrificing performance.

</details>


### [18] [Accelerating MoE with Dynamic In-Switch Computing on Multi-GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05607)
*Qijun Zhang,Chen Zhang,Zhuoshan Zhou,Haibo Wang,Zhe Zhou,Zhipeng Tu,Guangyu Sun,Zhiyao Xie,Yijia Diao,Zhigang Ji,Jingwen Leng,Guanghui He,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出DySHARP，一种动态交换机内计算方案，通过动态多播寻址和以Token为核心的内核融合，解决MoE专家并行中大量冗余的GPU间通信问题，相比现有最优方案最高实现1.79倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流大模型广泛采用混合专家模型以降低计算开销，但在专家并行中存在频繁的GPU间通信瓶颈。研究发现MoE中存在大量冗余的GPU间数据传输，理论上可通过交换机内计算解决。然而现有方案NVLink SHARP仅支持静态规则通信模式，无法处理MoE中动态不规则通信模式，存在功能缺失。

Method: 提出DySHARP整体动态交换机内计算解决方案，包含两大核心：1) 动态多播寻址技术，协同设计ISA、架构和运行时，作为NVLS的动态扩展以减少冗余流量；2) 以Token为核心的内核融合技术，深度融合分发-计算-组合流水线，解决因流量减少方向不对称性导致的加速效果转化问题。

Result: 相比最先进的解决方案，DySHARP实现了最高1.79倍的加速效果。通过动态多播寻址减少了冗余流量，并通过内核融合成功将流量减少转化为实际加速性能。

Conclusion: DySHARP成功填补了NVLS在动态通信支持方面的功能空白，为MoE模型的高效专家并行提供了可行的通信优化方案，证明了交换机内计算在处理动态不规则通信模式方面的有效性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has been adopted by many leading large models to reduce computational requirements. However, frequent inter-GPU communication in MoE expert parallelism (EP) becomes a performance challenge. We observe substantial redundant inter-GPU data transfers in MoE that can be potentially addressed by in-switch computing. Unfortunately, the existing solution, NVLink SHARP (NVLS), can only support static collectives with regular patterns, incapable of dynamic communication with irregular patterns in MoE. To bridge the functionality gap, we propose DySHARP, an integral dynamic in-switch computing solution to accelerate MoE, encompassing both communication primitives and communication-aware scheduling: 1) Dynamic multimem addressing co-designs ISA, architecture, and runtime, as a dynamic extension to NVLS, reducing redundant traffic. However, the resulting traffic reduction is inherently asymmetric between two directions, preventing it from directly translating into speedup. 2) Token-centric kernel fusion deeply fuses the dispatch-computation-combine pipeline, resolving this asymmetry to translate traffic reduction into actual speedup. Compared with the state-of-the-art solution, DySHARP achieves up to 1.79$\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [19] [Towards Compute-Aware In-Switch Computing for LLMs Tensor-Parallelism on Multi-GPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05628)
*Chen Zhang,Qijun Zhang,Zhuoshan Zhou,Yijia Diao,Haibo Wang,Zhe Zhou,Zhipeng Tu,Zhiyao Li,Guangyu Sun,Zhuoran Song,Zhigang Ji,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CAIS，首个计算感知的交换机内计算框架，通过统一通信模式与计算内存语义需求，解决了张量并行中通信与计算隔离导致的资源利用率低问题，实现了显著的LLM训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模LLM推理与训练中的张量并行（TP）引入了频繁的集合通信操作，主导了GPU间的通信开销。虽然以NVLink SHARP（NVLS）为代表的交换机内计算技术通过减少冗余数据传输加速了集合通信，但其以通信为中心的设计理念导致了通信模式与LLM计算核心的内存语义需求不匹配。这种不匹配将计算与通信阶段隔离，导致多GPU系统中资源利用不足且重叠受限。

Method: 提出CAIS框架，包含三项核心技术：（1）计算感知的ISA和微架构扩展，以支持计算感知的交换机内计算；（2）合并感知的线程块（TB）协调机制，以提高时间对齐性从而实现高效的请求合并；（3）图级数据流优化器，以实现紧密的跨内核重叠。

Result: 在LLM工作负载上的评估表明，CAIS相比最先进的NVLS方案实现了1.38倍的平均端到端训练加速，相比未使用NVLS但采用T3（SOTA计算-通信重叠方案）实现了1.61倍的加速。

Conclusion: CAIS通过将通信模式与计算的内存语义需求对齐，有效解决了现有交换机内计算技术存在的通信-计算隔离问题，在多GPU系统上显著加速了张量并行，为大规模LLM训练与推理提供了一种高效解决方案。

Abstract: Tensor parallelism (TP) in large-scale LLM inference and training introduces frequent collective operations that dominate inter-GPU communication. While in-switch computing, exemplified by NVLink SHARP (NVLS), accelerates collective operations by reducing redundant data transfer, its communication-centric design philosophy introduces the mismatch between its communication mode and the memory semantic requirement of LLM's computation kernel. Such a mismatch isolates the compute and communication phases, resulting in underutilized resources and limited overlap in multi-GPU systems. To address the limitation, we propose CAIS, the first Compute-Aware In-Switch computing framework that aligns communication modes with computation's memory semantics requirement. CAIS consists of three integral techniques: (1) compute-aware ISA and microarchitecture extension to enable compute-aware in-switch computing. (2) merging-aware TB (Thread Block) coordination to improve the temporal alignment for efficient request merging. (3) graph-level dataflow optimizer to achieve a tight cross-kernel overlap. Evaluations on LLM workloads show that CAIS achieves 1.38$\times$ average end-to-end training speedup over the SOTA NVLS-enabled solution, and 1.61$\times$ over T3, the SOTA compute-communicate overlap solutions but do not leverage NVLS, demonstrating its effectiveness in accelerating TP on multi-GPU systems.

</details>


### [20] [TokenStack: A Heterogeneous HBM-PIM Architecture and Runtime for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05639)
*Zhuoran Li,Zhuohang Bian,Zihao Huang,Guangyu Sun,Yun Liang,Youwei Zhuo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: TokenStack提出了一种垂直异构的HBM-PIM架构，通过分离容量层和计算层，并利用基础裸片控制器管理数据移动，显著提升了大语言模型服务的吞吐量和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型服务受限于键值（KV）缓存。解码阶段每个新token都需要读取先前的KV状态，导致注意力机制成为带宽和容量密集型的内存任务。现有的HBM-PIM设计存在资源浪费：统一的HBM-PIM堆栈使所有层都承担PIM逻辑的开销，而专用PIM设计（如AttAcc）虽然恢复了容量，但缩减了GPU端可用的HBM带宽。实际上，只有热KV块受益于近存计算，而权重、激活和冷KV主要需要密集存储和GPU可见带宽。

Method: 提出TokenStack，一种面向KV中心LLM服务的垂直异构HBM-PIM架构。该架构利用HBM4的逻辑裸片衬底，将每个堆栈分离为密集容量层和启用PIM的计算层。使用逻辑基础裸片作为堆栈本地控制点，管理跨层数据移动而无需主机端开销。基础裸片控制器处理跨层DMA、分层地址转换、注意力端收集/广播协调以及迁移期间的在线量化。在硬件之上，TokenStack采用拓扑感知的KV放置、工作负载感知的驱逐和有界复制策略，将热KV保持在PIM计算附近，同时将较冷的状态移动到密集层。

Result: 基于四种模型的生产环境追踪数据，在完成的多QPS运行中，TokenStack相比AttAcc将几何平均token吞吐量提升了1.62倍，SLO合规服务容量提升了1.70倍，并将每个token的能耗降低了30-47%。

Conclusion: TokenStack通过垂直异构架构有效解决了LLM服务中KV缓存的瓶颈问题，在吞吐量、服务容量和能效方面均显著优于现有设计，为大语言模型的高效服务提供了可行的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving is now limited by the key-value (KV) cache. During decode, each new token rereads prior KV state, so attention becomes a bandwidth- and capacity-heavy memory task. HBM-PIM helps by moving attention closer to memory, but current stack organizations still waste resources. In practice, only hot KV blocks benefit from near-memory compute. Weights, activations, and cold KV mainly need dense storage and GPU-visible bandwidth. A uniform HBM-PIM stack makes all layers pay for PIM logic, while a dedicated-PIM design such as AttAcc recovers capacity but shrinks the HBM bandwidth left for GPU-side work. We propose TokenStack, a vertically heterogeneous HBM-PIM architecture for KV-centric LLM serving that leverages HBM4's logic-die substrate. TokenStack separates each stack into dense capacity layers and PIM-enabled compute layers, then uses the logic base die as a stack-local control point that manages cross-layer movement without host-side overhead. The base-die controller handles cross-layer DMA, layered address translation, attention-side gather/broadcast coordination, and inline quantization during migration. On top of this hardware, TokenStack uses topology-aware KV placement, workload-aware eviction, and bounded replication to keep hot KV near PIM compute while moving colder state to dense layers. Using production-derived traces across four models, completed multi-QPS runs show that TokenStack increases geometric-mean token throughput by 1.62x and SLO-compliant serving capacity by 1.70x over AttAcc, and reduces per-token energy by 30-47%.

</details>


### [21] [MoE-Hub: Taming Software Complexity for Seamless MoE Overlap with Hardware-Accelerated Communication on Multi-GPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05888)
*Zhuoshan Zhou,Chen Zhang,Shuyi Zhang,Qijun Zhang,Haibo Wang,Zhe Zhou,Zhipeng Tu,Guangyu Sun,Yijia Diao,Zhigang Ji,Jingwen Leng,Guanghui He,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出MoE-Hub，一种软硬件协同设计方案，通过引入目标无关的通信范式解决MoE架构中的GPU通信瓶颈，实现1.21x-1.98x的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家(MoE)架构在大语言模型扩展中至关重要，但在多GPU系统中，其扩展性严重受限于GPU间通信瓶颈。虽然通信与计算重叠是公认的优化方法，但现有的静态、以地址为中心的GPU通信模型与MoE动态、不规则的令牌到专家映射存在根本性的抽象不匹配，导致在数据传输前需要一个复杂的软件中介阶段来解析地址，限制了性能和软件灵活性。

Method: 提出MoE-Hub软硬件协同设计方案，引入目标无关(destination-agnostic)的通信范式。该方案将数据传输与地址管理解耦，允许生产者在路由后仅使用逻辑目标地址立即发送数据，而地址分配和数据流编排由GPU hub中的轻量级硬件透明处理。通过硬件加速整个通信控制平面，实现无缝透明的通信与计算重叠。

Result: 实验评估表明，MoE-Hub相较于最先进的系统，在每层加速比达到1.40x-3.08x，端到端加速比达到1.21x-1.98x。

Conclusion: MoE-Hub成功解决了MoE架构中动态令牌映射与静态GPU通信模型之间的抽象不匹配问题，通过软硬件协同设计实现了通信控制平面的硬件加速，有效消除了GPU间通信瓶颈，为大规模MoE模型的扩展提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is crucial for scaling large language models, but its scalability is severely limited by inter-GPU communication bottlenecks in multi-GPU systems. Although overlapping communication with computation is a widely recognized optimization, its effective deployment still remains challenging, both in terms of performance and programmability. In this work, we identify the root cause as a fundamental abstraction mismatch between MoE's dynamic, irregular token-to-expert mapping and the static, address-centric communication model of modern GPUs, which necessitates a complex software mediation phase to resolve addresses before data transfers, limiting performance and software flexibility. To resolve this, we propose MoE-Hub, a hardware-software co-design that introduces a destination-agnostic communication paradigm. MoE-Hub decouples data transmission from address management, allowing producers to send data immediately after routing using only a logical destination, while address allocation and data-flow orchestration are handled transparently by lightweight hardware in the GPU hub. By hardware-accelerating the entire communication control plane, MoE-Hub enables seamless and transparent overlap. Our evaluation shows that MoE-Hub achieves 1.40x-3.08x per-layer and 1.21x-1.98x end-to-end speedup over state-of-the-art systems.

</details>


### [22] [LLM-Driven Design Space Exploration of FPGA-based Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05920)
*Vinamra Sharma,Xingjian Fu,Jude Haris,José Cano*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了SECDA-DSE框架，将大语言模型（LLM）集成到SECDA生态系统中，通过推理引导的探索和强化微调机制，实现了FPGA加速器设计空间探索的自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现代AI工作负载设计FPGA加速器需要遍历庞大且复杂的硬件设计空间（包括架构参数、数据流策略和存储层级），导致设计过程耗时且资源密集。虽然SECDA方法支持快速硬件-软件协同设计，但识别最优加速器配置仍需大量人工努力和领域专业知识。

Method: 提出了SECDA-DSE框架，包含用于生成加速器配置的结构化DSE探索器，以及利用检索增强生成和思维链提示进行推理引导探索的LLM栈，并引入反馈循环实现强化微调以持续改进设计。

Result: 通过基于高层次综合的评估验证了SECDA-DSE的可行性，生成的加速器设计在Zynq-7000 FPGA上满足综合时序和资源约束。

Conclusion: SECDA-DSE成功实现了FPGA加速器设计空间探索的自动化，显著降低了设计过程中的人工干预和领域专业知识需求，为AI加速器的快速设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Designing field-programmable gate array (FPGA)-based accelerators for modern artificial intelligence workloads requires navigating a large and complex hardware design space encompassing architectural parameters, dataflow strategies, and memory hierarchies, making the process time-consuming and resource-intensive. While the SECDA methodology enables rapid hardware-software co-design of accelerators through SystemC simulation and FPGA execution, identifying optimal accelerator configurations still requires substantial manual effort and domain expertise. This work presents SECDA-DSE, a framework that integrates Large Language Models (LLMs) into the SECDA ecosystem, comprising tools built around SECDA to automate the design space exploration (DSE) of FPGA-based accelerators. SECDA-DSE combines a structured DSE Explorer for generating accelerator configurations with an LLM Stack that performs reasoning-guided exploration using retrieval-augmented generation and chain-of-thought prompting, alongside a feedback loop that enables reinforced fine-tuning for continuous improvement. We demonstrate the feasibility of SECDA-DSE through an initial high-level synthesis based evaluation of a generated accelerator design that meets synthesis timing and resource constraints on an Zynq-7000 FPGA.

</details>


### [23] [A virtually connected probabilistic computer as a solver for higher-order, densely connected, or reconfigurable combinatorial optimisation problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06037)
*Amy J. Searle,Harry Youel,Fredrik Hasselgren,Annika Möslein,Ramy Aboushelbaya,Marko von der Leyen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于虚拟连接的光子概率计算架构，无需传统硬件所需的拓扑变换（如嵌入、稀疏化和二次化），通过贪心图着色算法实现硬件并行化。仿真预测该架构在求解Erdos-Renyi图自旋玻璃基态问题时，速度比数字退火单元快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非常规计算方法（如专用硬件求解NP难问题）常需将问题几何结构转换为适应硬件拓扑的形式，导致解质量下降。为解决这一限制，作者探索了虚拟连接硬件在避免拓扑变换需求方面的潜力。

Method: 采用基于高速光子量子随机数生成器的概率计算架构，利用虚拟硬件连接；结合贪心图着色算法实现硬件并行化；通过仿真模拟硬件性能，对比展示了二次化或稀疏化带来的问题规模膨胀。

Result: 光子概率计算机在Erdos-Renyi图自旋玻璃基态近似问题上，预测比近期报道的数字退火单元的求解时间快数个数量级。虚拟连接架构有效规避了物理连接硬件的拓扑限制问题。

Conclusion: 虚拟连接的光子概率计算架构为求解受几何限制的NP难问题提供了高效替代方案，展现了超越现有数字退火技术的潜力。

Abstract: Recently, there has been growing interest in unconventional computing as an approach for solving NP-hard problems, by developing dedicated hardware to find solutions more efficiently than conventional CPUs. In many of these approaches, however, certain problem geometries must be transformed into forms that are more amenable to the available hardware topology through techniques such as embedding, sparsification, and quadratisation, leading to a deterioration in solution quality. A probabilistic computing architecture based on high speed photonic quantum random number generators was recently proposed which utilises virtual hardware connections (Aboushelbaya et al., 2025), circumventing the necessity for such procedures. Here, we discuss the applicability of virtually connected hardware for running heuristic solving methods to solve a selection of problems, which due to their geometry, would suffer from topological hardware restrictions. We also employ greedy graph colouring algorithms for hardware parallelisation, allowing favourable scaling for desirable solution qualities. To emphasise the difficulty in solving these problems on physically connected hardware, we demonstrate the increase in problem size that would occur with quadratisation or sparsification. Using simulations to emulate hardware, we predict that a photonic probabilistic computer would outperform the time to solution recently reported for digital annealing units, on the ground state approximation of Erdos-Renyi graph spin-glasses, by orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [24] [XtraMAC: An Efficient MAC Architecture for Mixed-Precision LLM Inference on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06052)
*Feng Yu,Hongshi Tan,Yao Chen,Weng-Fai Wong,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了XtraMAC，一种支持整数、浮点和混合精度运算的统一MAC架构，在FPGA上实现了1.4-2.0倍的更高计算密度，降低了27-51%的资源消耗，并为混合精度LLM工作负载提供了高达1.9倍的能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）中混合精度量化的广泛采用，对能够在混合数据类型间高效执行乘累加（MAC）运算并在运行时动态切换数据类型的硬件提出了需求。然而，现有的FPGA MAC解决方案存在固定数据类型设计、空间或时间资源共享效率低下以及对混合精度执行支持不足等局限性，导致DSP资源利用不足，限制了并行性和吞吐量。

Method: 提出XtraMAC，一种新颖的MAC架构，在单一数据类型自适应微架构中统一了整数、浮点和混合精度运算。该架构将所有支持的MAC格式分解为共享的整数尾数乘积，并配备轻量级的符号和指数处理，实现了动态操作数打包和高效的DSP资源共享，在所有数据类型中保持恒定延迟和一个周期的启动间隔。

Result: 在AMD Xilinx U55c FPGA上评估，XtraMAC实现了1.4-2.0倍的更高计算密度，将每次操作的LUT、FF和DSP消耗减少了27-51%，并在代表性混合精度LLM工作负载上提供了高达1.9倍的能效提升和1.2倍的加速。

Conclusion: XtraMAC通过统一的数据类型自适应架构有效解决了现有FPGA MAC方案在混合精度支持方面的局限性，显著提升了资源利用率和能效，为LLM的高效硬件加速提供了有效的解决方案，且实现已开源。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of mixed-precision quantization in large language models (LLMs) has created demand for hardware that can efficiently perform multiply-accumulate (MAC) operations across mixed datatypes and switch datatypes at runtime. Existing FPGA-based MAC solutions fall short due to limitations in fixed-datatype design, inefficient spatial or temporal resource sharing, and poor support for mixed-precision execution. These limitations collectively lead to under-utilization of DSP resources, limiting achievable parallelism and throughput. In this work, we present XtraMAC, a novel MAC architecture that unifies integer, floating-point, and mixed-precision operations within a single, datatype-adaptive microarchitecture. XtraMAC decomposes all supported MAC formats into a shared integer mantissa product with lightweight sign and exponent handling, enabling dynamic operand packing and efficient DSP resource sharing with constant latency and initiation interval of one across all datatypes. Evaluated on an AMD Xilinx U55c FPGA, XtraMAC achieves 1.4-2.0x higher compute density, reduces per-operation LUT, FF, and DSP consumption by 27-51%, and delivers up to 1.9x greater energy efficiency and 1.2x speedup on representative mixed-precision LLM workloads. The implementation of XtraMAC is open-sourced at https://github.com/Xtra-Computing/XtraMAC.

</details>


### [25] [PoTAcc: A Pipeline for End-to-End Acceleration of Power-of-Two Quantized DNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06082)
*Rappy Saha,Jude Haris,Nicolas Bohm Agostini,David Kaeli,José Cano*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了PoTAcc，一个用于在资源受限边缘设备上加速和评估PoT量化DNN的开源端到端流水线，在FPGA平台上实现了3.6倍加速和78%能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PoT量化能显著减少DNN模型大小并将乘法替换为位移操作，但在资源受限边缘设备上的性能表现尚不明确。现有通用边缘CPU/GPU缺乏位移操作的优化后端支持，而定制硬件加速器虽然能更好利用PoT量化，但现有推理框架支持有限，且不同PoT量化策略对硬件设计、性能和能效的影响尚未系统探索。

Method: 提出PoTAcc开源端到端流水线，通过TensorFlow Lite实现PoT量化模型的无缝准备和部署。设计了针对三种PoT量化方法的基于位移的处理单元（shift-PE）加速器，并在两个FPGA平台（PYNQ-Z2和Kria）上实现。支持异构平台包括纯CPU系统和混合CPU-FPGA系统。

Result: 在CNN和Transformer架构的多种模型上评估了精度、性能、能效和资源利用率。CPU-加速器设计在PYNQ-Z2和Kria板上相比纯CPU执行实现了高达3.6倍加速和78%的能耗降低。

Conclusion: PoTAcc成功填补了PoT量化DNN在边缘设备部署方面的空白，证明了定制shift-PE加速器在PoT量化模型上的有效性，为资源受限环境下的神经网络推理提供了实用的开源解决方案。

Abstract: Power-of-two (PoT) quantization significantly reduces the size of deep neural networks (DNNs) and replaces multiplications with bit-shift operations for inference. Prior work has shown that PoT-quantized DNNs can preserve accuracy for tasks such as image classification; however, their performance on resource-constrained edge devices remains insufficiently understood. While general-purpose edge CPUs and GPUs do not provide optimized backends for bit-shift operations, custom hardware accelerators can better exploit PoT quantization by implementing dedicated shift-based processing elements. However, deploying PoT-quantized models on such accelerators is challenging due to limited support in existing inference frameworks. In addition, the impact of different PoT quantization strategies on hardware design, performance, and energy efficiency during full inference has not been systematically explored.
  To address these challenges, we propose PoTAcc, an open-source end-to-end pipeline for accelerating and evaluating PoT-quantized DNNs on resource-constrained edge devices. PoTAcc enables seamless preparation and deployment of PoT-quantized models via TensorFlow Lite (TFLite) across heterogeneous platforms, including CPU-only systems and hybrid CPU-FPGA systems with custom accelerators. We design shift-based processing element (shift-PE) accelerators for three PoT quantization methods and implement them on two FPGA platforms. We evaluate accuracy, performance, energy efficiency, and resource utilization across a range of models, including CNNs and Transformer-based architectures. Results show that our CPU-accelerator design achieves up to 3.6x speedup and 78% energy reduction compared to CPU-only execution for PoT-quantized DNNs on PYNQ-Z2 and Kria boards. The code will be publicly released at https://github.com/gicLAB/PoTAcc

</details>

### [26] [Are Flat Minima an Illusion?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05209)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“平坦极小值提升泛化”的主流观点，指出锐度随参数化变化因而不是因果因素。作者提出“弱度”（网络功能在参数空间中的完成体积）作为泛化的真正驱动因素，并在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上验证其预测能力优于锐度和简单性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流观点认为损失景观中的平坦区域有助于泛化，锐度感知最小化（SAM）等方法由此发展。然而，函数保持的重参数化可使Hessian特征值增大两个数量级而不改变任何预测，说明权重空间的几何结构可以被任意构造。作者因此质疑锐度作为泛化因果因素的合理性，转而寻找一个参数化不变的替代解释。

Method: 1. 理论分析：引入“弱度”概念（网络功能在参数空间中兼容的完成体积），证明其在重参数化下不变且在可交换需求下具有极小极大最优性，并解释PAC-Bayes界有效的原因是其与弱度相关。
2. 实证检验：在MNIST上观察大批量泛化优势随训练数据量增加而消失（从+1.6%降至+0.02%），表明锐度是混淆因素而非原因。
3. 头对头比较：训练100个同架构同训练流程的网络，计算弱度、锐度、简单性与泛化差距的相关系数。

Result: 1. 理论结果：弱度是重参数化不变的，定义为网络“做什么”而非“如何参数化”；证明其在可交换需求下的极小极大最优性；PAC-Bayes界因与弱度相关而有效。
2. 实验结果（MNIST）：弱度预测泛化（ρ=+0.374, p=0.00012），锐度反相关（ρ=-0.226），简单性无预测力（p=0.848）。
3. 实验结果（Fashion-MNIST）：弱度预测泛化（ρ=+0.384, p=8.15×10⁻⁵），简单性在此数据集有一定预测力。
4. 关键发现：大批量泛化优势随数据量增加而消失；简单性依赖数据集，弱度则不变。

Conclusion: 平坦极小值并非泛化的根本原因。锐度随参数化变化而丧失因果解释力；简单性依赖数据集特性。弱度作为重参数化不变的功能性度量，才是泛化性能的真正驱动因素。这一发现对理解神经网络泛化机制和改进训练算法（如超越SAM）具有重要启示。

Abstract: Neural networks that land in flat regions of the loss landscape tend to generalise better than those in sharp regions. Sharpness-Aware Minimisation exploits this to improve generalisation. But function-preserving reparameterisation can inflate the Hessian of any minimum by two orders of magnitude without changing a single prediction. If the geometry of weight space can be manufactured from nothing, it cannot be the cause of anything. In other words, flat is simple and simplicity depends on encoding. Here I show that the actual driver is weakness, the volume of completions compatible with the learned function in the learner's embodied language. Weakness is reparameterisation-invariant because it is defined over what the network \emph{does}, not how it is parameterised. I prove weakness is minimax-optimal under exchangeable demands, and that PAC-Bayes bounds work because they correlate with it. On MNIST, the large-batch generalisation advantage \emph{vanishes} as training data grows, from $+1.6\%$ at $n = 2{,}000$ to $+0.02\%$ at $n = 60{,}000$. A quantity whose predictive power depends on how much data you have is not a cause but a confounder. I run head-to-heads on 100 networks with identical architecture and training. For MNIST weakness predicts generalisation ($ρ= +0.374$, $p = 0.00012$), sharpness anticorrelates ($ρ= -0.226$) and simplicity predicts nothing ($p = 0.848$). For Fashion-MNIST ($ρ= +0.384$, $p = 8.15 \times 10^{-5}$), though simplicity is at least somewhat predictive there. Simplicity is dataset dependent, whereas weakness is invariant. Flat minima were never the answer.

</details>


### [27] [Nationwide EHR-Based Chronic Rhinosinusitis Prediction Using Demographic-Stratified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05213)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Justina Varghese,Akshay R Prabhakar,Sebastian Guadarrama-Sistos-Vazquez,Jiefu Chen,Masayoshi Takashima,Omar G. Ahmed,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用全国性纵向电子健康档案（EHR）数据，通过混合特征选择管道和人口分层模型，实现了慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）的早期预测，整体AUC达到0.8461，为初级护理中的早期分诊和转诊优先级排序提供了支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）是一种常见的异质性炎症性疾病，症状与过敏性鼻炎等常见疾病重叠，早期识别困难。现有预测研究多依赖单一机构队列，降低了人群层面的普适性。

Method: 研究利用All of Us研究计划的全国性纵向EHR数据，采用两年的诊断前病史进行CRS诊断预测。为解决EHR数据的极端稀疏性和高维度问题，实施了混合特征选择管道，将约11万个候选代码压缩为100个可解释特征。为捕捉人口异质性，训练了六个性别和生命阶段分层的模型，并进行亚组特异性超参数调优。

Result: 该框架整体AUC达到0.8461，相较于最佳基线提升了0.0168。

Conclusion: 常规收集的EHR数据可支持具有人群代表性的CRS风险分层，有助于初级护理中的早期分诊和转诊优先级排序。

Abstract: Chronic rhinosinusitis (CRS) is a common heterogeneous inflammatory disorder that causes substantial morbidity and healthcare costs. CRS is difficult to identify early from routine encounters, as symptom presentations overlap with common conditions such as allergic rhinitis, and heterogeneous phenotypes further obscure risk patterns. Prior predictive studies often rely on single-institutional cohorts , which reduce population-level generalizability. To overcome this, we leveraged nationwide longitudinal EHR data from the \textit{All of Us} Research Program to predict CRS diagnosis using two years of pre-diagnostic history. To address extreme feature sparsity and dimensionality in coded EHR data, we implemented a hybrid feature-selection pipeline that combines prevalence-based statistical screening with model-based importance ranking, compressing approximately 110,000 candidate codes into 100 interpretable features. To capture demographic heterogeneity, we trained demographic stratified models across six adult sex and life-stage subgroups with subgroup-specific hyperparameter tuning. Our framework achieved an overall AUC of 0.8461, improving discrimination by 0.0168 over the best baseline. These results demonstrate that routinely collected EHR data may support population-representative CRS risk stratification and inform earlier triage and referral prioritization in primary care.

</details>


### [28] [SAT: Sequential Agent Tuning for Coordinator Free Plug and Play Multi-LLM Training with Monotonic Improvement Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05216)
*Yi Xie,Yangyang Xu,Yi Fan,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无协调器的序列智能体调优(SAT)方法，通过分散式训练多个小型LLM来匹配或超越单个大模型，实现了单调改进和可证明的即插即用不变性，3个4B智能体在AIME基准上超越了32B大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型虽然性能强大但部署成本过高。现有研究探索使用多个小型高效LLM团队来匹配或超越单个大模型，但联合更新多个智能体会引入复合分布偏移，导致训练过程中的协调和稳定性问题。

Method: 提出序列智能体调优(SAT)范式：将团队表示为因子化策略，采用智能体间的块坐标更新实现可扩展的分散式训练。开发了序列感知的在策略优势估计器和每智能体KL信任区域来隔离占用漂移，无需中央控制器。

Result: 三个4B智能体(总计12B参数)在AIME24/25基准上平均超越Qwen3-32B达3.9%。验证即插即用理论：替换两个8B智能体后，综合得分提升10.4%。

Conclusion: SAT框架理论上保证了单调改进和即插即用不变性（可单独升级智能体而无需重新训练整个团队），实证表明小型智能体团队可以有效替代大型模型，为高效部署LLM提供了新途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with a large number of parameters achieve strong performance but are often prohibitively expensive to deploy. Recent work explores using teams of smaller, more efficient LLMs that collectively match or even outperform a single large model. However, jointly updating multiple agents introduces compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and stability during training difficult. We address this by introducing Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT), a coordinator-free training paradigm. SAT represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Specifically, we develop a sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, coupled with per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift. Theoretically, this framework provides two critical guarantees. First, it ensures monotonic improvement, stabilizing the training process. Second, it establishes provable plug-and-play invariance: any agent can be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the rest of the team, with a formal guarantee that the performance bound improves. Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses the much larger Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9\% on average. We validate our plug-and-play theory by swapping in two 8B agents, which boosts the composite score by 10.4\%. We provide code and appendix of proof at https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS

</details>


### [29] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks with Learnable Loss Balancing and Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05217)
*Reza Pirayeshshirazinezhad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自监督物理信息神经网络框架，通过可学习的混合神经元自适应平衡物理损失与数据损失，结合迁移学习策略，在数据稀缺条件下实现了高精度的科学机器学习预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理信息神经网络（PINN）通常依赖固定或启发式的权重来平衡物理残差和数据损失，这在数据稀缺场景下需要大量手动调参且难以达到最优效果。作者旨在开发一种能够自适应调整物理与数据项相对贡献的框架，以提高训练稳定性和泛化能力，解决数据稀缺问题。

Method: 提出了一种自监督PINN框架，引入了“可学习混合神经元”（learnable blending neuron），该机制根据物理残差和数据损失的不确定性动态调整二者的相对权重，从而实现自适应平衡，无需手动调参。此外，集成了迁移学习策略，重用相关领域的表示并适应于数据有限的新物理系统。

Result: 在液态金属微型散热器传热预测任务上验证了该框架，仅使用87个CFD数据点，自适应PINN实现了低于8%的误差。该方法在性能上优于浅层神经网络、核方法以及纯物理基准模型。

Conclusion: 该框架为在神经网络中自适应嵌入物理知识提供了一种通用方案，为流体动力学、材料建模等科学领域的各种数据稀缺问题提供了鲁棒且可复现的解决途径。

Abstract: We propose a self-supervised physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework that adaptively balances physics-based and data-driven supervision for scientific machine learning under data scarcity. Unlike prior PINNs that rely on fixed or heuristic weighting of physics residuals and data loss, our approach introduces a learnable blending neuron that dynamically adjusts the relative contribution of each term based on their uncertainties. This mechanism enables stable training and improved generalization without manual tuning. To further enhance efficiency, we integrate a transfer learning strategy that reuses representations from related domains and adapts them to new physical systems with limited data. We validate the framework for the prediction of heat transfer in liquid-metal miniature heat sinks using only 87 CFD datapoints, where the adaptive PINN achieves an error <8%, outperforming shallow neural networks, kernel methods, and physics-only baselines. Our framework provides a general recipe for embedding physics adaptively into neural networks, offering a robust and reproducible approach for data-scarce problems across various scientific domains, including fluid dynamics and material modeling.

</details>


### [30] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的严格联系，提出了视界约束Rashomon集框架，通过引入Lyapunov加权度量，使决策质量提升18-34%，为安全关键混沌域中的机器学习部署提供了原则性指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测多重性和混沌动力学是机器学习中的两个基本挑战，尽管它们在概念上存在联系，但一直以来独立演化。在混沌系统中，模型多重性如何随预测视界演变这一关键问题尚未得到研究，现有针对静态预测任务的Rashomon集理论无法直接适用。

Method: 提出视界约束Rashomon集理论框架，刻画混沌系统中模型多重性随预测视界的演化；证明有效Rashomon集以最大Lyapunov指数确定的速率随预测时间呈指数收缩；引入Lyapunov加权度量以提供更紧的预测分歧界限；开发决策对齐选择算法，基于下游效用而非仅依赖预测精度在近优模型中进行选择。

Result: 在合成混沌系统（Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky）和实际应用（风电、交通、天气）上的大量实验表明，该框架在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，将决策质量提高了18-34%。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的首个严格联系，揭示了混沌如何从根本上改变预测等价性的本质，为在安全关键混沌领域部署机器学习提供了有原则的指导。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

</details>


### [31] [Sparse Prefix Caching for Hybrid and Recurrent LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05219)
*Mikhail Shirokikh,Sergey Nikolenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对状态空间模型（SSM）的稀疏前缀缓存方法，通过在关键位置存储检查点而非密集缓存所有token，在保持精确输出的同时显著提升了缓存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前缀缓存系统主要针对自回归大语言模型设计，假设需要密集的每token键值重用。然而状态空间模型改变了问题结构：循环层可以从单个存储状态恢复，而不需要整个token历史。这种不对称性在无重用和密集缓存之间开启了一个新的设计空间。

Method: 将稀疏前缀缓存形式化为在重叠深度分布下的检查点放置问题，开发了一个精确的O(NM)动态规划算法。该方法在稀疏的检查点位置存储精确的循环状态，当缓存命中时，从最深存储的检查点恢复并精确重计算剩余后缀。

Result: 在QuALITY和System Prompts数据集上，分布感知的检查点放置方法在测量的层组帕累托前沿上优于所有固定预算基线，匹配或超越最强的启发式方法（块缓存），同时通常使用更少的检查点。在低检查点预算且重叠分布最不均匀的情况下，增益最大。

Conclusion: 该方法特别适用于多个请求共享实质性但不完全相同前缀的场景，保持了精确输出，不改变循环计算本身或需要新的循环更新内核，适用于可以精确提取和恢复隐藏状态的循环/SSM层，并且对于混合模型可以与现有的KV缓存压缩技术结合使用。

Abstract: Prefix caching is a key latency optimization for autoregressive LLM serving, yet existing systems assume dense per-token key/value reuse. State-space models change the structure of the problem: a recurrent layer can resume from a single stored state rather than requiring the entire token history. This asymmetry opens a new design point between no reuse and dense caching: store exact recurrent states at a sparse set of checkpoint positions and, on a cache hit, resume from the deepest stored checkpoint and recompute the remaining suffix exactly.
  We formalize sparse prefix caching as checkpoint placement under a distribution over overlap depths, yielding an exact O(NM) dynamic program. For use cases where requests share a non-trivial prefix (e.g. asking different questions about a single long document), we show that our method consistently improves the Pareto frontier traced by standard heuristics on real-world data. Across QuALITY and System Prompts, distribution-aware placement dominates every fixed-budget baseline on the measured layer-group Pareto frontier and matches or outperforms the strongest heuristic (block caching) while typically using substantially fewer checkpoints, with the largest gains at low checkpoint budgets where the overlap distribution is most non-uniform. The method is most relevant when many requests share a substantial but not identical prefix within a retained cache entry. It preserves exact outputs, does not change the recurrent computation itself or require new recurrent update kernels, applies to recurrent/SSM layers whose hidden state can be extracted and restored exactly, and for hybrid models can be combined with existing KV-cache compression techniques.

</details>


### [32] [A Note on TurboQuant and the Earlier DRIVE/EDEN Line of Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18555)
*Ran Ben-Basat,Yaniv Ben-Itzhak,Gal Mendelson,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文阐明TurboQuant是EDEN的特例或次优变体，实验表明EDEN在所有设置下均优于TurboQuant。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清近期TurboQuant工作与早期DRIVE（NeurIPS 2021）和EDEN（ICML 2022）方案之间的关系，纠正对量化方法的误解。

Method: 通过理论分析比较三种量化方法：1）将TurboQuant_mse作为EDEN的特例（固定S=1）进行分析；2）分析TurboQuant_prod的两步量化结构；3）比较两种方法在随机旋转、Lloyd-Max算法等方面的相似性。

Result: TurboQuant_mse是EDEN的特例但使用了次优参数；TurboQuant_prod存在三个次优问题；实验证明优化后的EDEN在精度上全面超越TurboQuant，2-bit EDEN甚至优于3-bit TurboQuant_prod。

Conclusion: TurboQuant工作主要复现了EDEN的已有结果，但采用了次优参数选择。研究者在选择量化方法时应优先考虑EDEN而非TurboQuant。

Abstract: This note clarifies the relationship between the recent TurboQuant work and the earlier DRIVE (NeurIPS 2021) and EDEN (ICML 2022) schemes. DRIVE is a 1-bit quantizer that EDEN extended to any $b>0$ bits per coordinate; we refer to them collectively as EDEN.
  First, TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ is a special case of EDEN obtained by fixing EDEN's scalar scale parameter to $S=1$. EDEN supports both biased and unbiased quantization, each optimized by a different $S$ (chosen via methods described in the EDEN works). The fixed choice $S=1$ used by TurboQuant is generally suboptimal, although the optimal $S$ for biased EDEN converges to $1$ as the dimension grows; accordingly TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ approaches EDEN's behavior for large $d$.
  Second, TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$ combines a biased $(b-1)$-bit EDEN step with an unbiased 1-bit QJL quantization of the residual. It is suboptimal in three ways: (1) its $(b-1)$-bit step uses the suboptimal $S=1$; (2) its 1-bit unbiased residual quantization has worse MSE than (unbiased) 1-bit EDEN; (3) chaining a biased $(b-1)$-bit step with a 1-bit unbiased residual step is inferior to unbiasedly quantizing the input directly with $b$-bit EDEN.
  Third, some of the analysis in the TurboQuant work mirrors that of the EDEN works: both exploit the connection between random rotations and the shifted Beta distribution, use the Lloyd-Max algorithm, and note that Randomized Hadamard Transforms can replace uniform random rotations.
  Experiments support these claims: biased EDEN (with optimized $S$) is more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$, and unbiased EDEN is markedly more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$, often by more than a bit (e.g., 2-bit EDEN beats 3-bit TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$). We also repeat all accuracy experiments from the TurboQuant paper, showing that EDEN outperforms it in every setup we have tried.

</details>


### [33] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了概念引导的理论框架，证明了标准行为消除方法是LEACE方法的特例，提出了LEACE-Switch和MidSteer两种方法，后者在各种任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管引导中间表示已成为控制生成模型的有效策略，特别是在部署后对齐和安全设置中，但目前缺乏全面的理论框架，需要建立概念引导的系统性理论基础。

Method: 首先建立引导与仿射概念擦除之间的联系，证明消除不期望行为的标准方法是LEACE（一种闭式仿射擦除方法）的特例；然后制定概念切换的理论框架LEACE-Switch，并在此基础上提出MidSteer（最小扰动概念引导），这是一种更通用的仿射框架，放宽了假设条件并实现了定向的最小扰动变换。

Result: MidSteer在多种任务、模态和架构中表现优异，包括视觉扩散模型和大语言模型。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了概念引导的理论框架，将现有方法统一在理论体系下，并提出了性能更优的通用方法。

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [34] [Data-Driven Variational Basis Learning Beyond Neural Networks: A Non-Neural Framework for Adaptive Basis Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05221)
*Andrew Kiruluta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种非神经网络的DVBL框架，通过变分优化直接从数据中学习基函数，实现了数据自适应且保持数学可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典表示系统（如傅里叶级数、小波）缺乏对高维数据经验结构的内在适应性，而神经网络虽能从数据中学习特征，但其分层非线性参数化牺牲了可解释性、对基结构的显式控制和数学透明性。因此需要一种既能数据自适应又保持数学可解释性的表示学习方法。

Method: 提出数据驱动变分基学习（DVBL）框架，将基原子作为主要优化变量，与样本特定系数及潜在的线性演化算子联合学习。采用交替最小化算法进行优化，并整合流形和动态正则化。建立最小化子存在性，证明算法的块下降性质，给出系数恢复和基可识别性条件。

Result: 实现了数据自适应的基展开，同时保持显式、可解释和可进行严格分析的特性。无需神经网络架构即可整合流形和动态正则化。

Conclusion: DVBL框架为表示学习提供了一种非神经网络的替代方案，在保持数学透明性和可解释性的同时实现了数据自适应性，相比经典字典学习、谱方法和深度表示学习具有概念上的新颖性。

Abstract: Classical representation systems such as Fourier series, wavelets, and fixed dictionaries provide analytically tractable basis expansions, but they are not intrinsically adapted to the empirical structure of modern high-dimensional data. Neural networks overcome this limitation by learning features from data, yet they do so through layered nonlinear parameterizations that often sacrifice interpretability, explicit control over basis structure, and mathematical transparency. In this manuscript we develop a non-neural alternative that learns basis functions directly from data through variational optimization. The proposed framework, termed Data Driven Variational Basis Learning (DVBL), treats basis atoms as primary optimization variables and learns them jointly with sample-specific coefficients and, when appropriate, a latent linear evolution operator. This yields a data-adaptive basis expansion that remains explicit, interpretable, and amenable to rigorous analysis. We formulate the model, establish existence of minimizers, prove blockwise descent properties for an alternating minimization algorithm, give conditions for coefficient recovery and basis identifiability, and show how manifold and dynamical regularization can be integrated without invoking neural architectures. We also discuss the conceptual novelty of the framework relative to classical dictionary learning, spectral methods, Koopman operator methods, and deep representation learning.

</details>


### [35] [Quantizing With Randomized Hadamard Transforms: Efficient Heuristic Now Proven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06014)
*Ran Ben-Basat,William Kuszmaul,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了两次随机Hadamard变换（RHT）在单坐标边缘分布上可近似均匀随机旋转（URR），三次RHT可满足向量量化的弱相关性需求，并提出了根据输入矩动态调整RHT次数的线性时间方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀随机旋转（URR）是现代量化方法中常用的预处理步骤，但在实践中常被随机Hadamard变换（RHT）替代以实现快速计算。然而，单次RHT在最坏情况下性能不佳，需要研究多少次RHT组合能渐近匹配URR的性能。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明两次RHT后，归一化旋转向量的每个固定坐标的边缘分布在Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离下与标准高斯分布偏差为O(d^{-1/2})；2. 将界限应用于DRIVE和QUIC-FL压缩方案分析；3. 证明三次RHT可实现衰减的坐标协方差，满足向量量化需求；4. 提出线性时间O(d)的输入矩检验方法，动态调整运行时RHT次数。

Result: 两次RHT可使单坐标边缘分布渐近匹配URR，在DRIVE和QUIC-FL方案中达到与URR渐近相同的性能；三次RHT可确保固定有界多维VQ码本在使用时与URR期望误差相同（仅差一个随维度消失的附加项）；动态矩检验方法可在实际非对抗性输入上提升性能。

Conclusion: 两次RHT足以满足单坐标边缘分布收敛需求的压缩方案；三次RHT可满足向量量化对坐标间弱相关性的要求；实际应用中可通过线性时间矩检验动态调整RHT次数以优化性能，在保证理论保证的同时提高效率。

Abstract: Uniform random rotations (URRs) are a common preprocessing step in modern quantization approaches used for gradient compression, inference acceleration, KV-cache compression, model weight quantization, and approximate nearest-neighbor search in vector databases. In practice, URRs are often replaced by randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which preserve orthogonality while admitting fast implementations. The remaining issue is the performance for worst-case inputs.
