With a URR, each coordinate is individually distributed as a shifted beta distribution, which converges to a Gaussian distribution in high dimensions. Generally, one RHT is not suitable in the worst case, as individual coordinates can be far from these distributions. We show that after composing two RHTs on any $d$-sized input vector, the marginal distribution of every fixed coordinate of the normalized rotated vector is within $O(d^{-1/2})$ of a standard Gaussian both in Kolmogorov distance and in $1$-Wasserstein distance. We then plug these bounds into the analyses of modern compression schemes, namely DRIVE and QUIC-FL, and show that two RHTs achieve performance that asymptotically matches URRs.
  However, we show that two RHTs may not be sufficient for Vector Quantization (VQ), which often requires weak correlation across fixed-size blocks of coordinates (as opposed to only marginal distribution convergence for single coordinates). We prove that a composition of three RHTs leads to decaying coordinate covariance. This ensures that any fixed, bounded, multi-dimensional VQ codebook optimized for URRs has the same expected error when using three RHTs, up to an additive term that vanishes with the dimension.
  Finally, because practical inputs are rarely adversarial, we propose a linear-time ${O}(d)$ check on the input's moments to dynamically adapt the number of RHTs used at runtime to improve performance.

</details>


### [36] [Adaptive Computation Depth via Learned Token Routing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05222)
*Ahmed Abdelmuniem Abdalla Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Token-Selective Attention (TSA)，一种通过学习的逐token门控机制来跳过Transformer中不必要计算的方法。该方法在不修改基础架构且仅增加1.7%参数的情况下，能够减少14-23%的计算量，同时保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Transformer架构对所有token应用相同数量的层，而忽略了不同token的处理难度差异。这种“一刀切”的方式导致了计算资源的浪费，因为并非所有token都需要相同深度的处理。

Method: TSA在连续的Transformer块之间的残差连接上引入了一个轻量级的两层MLP门控机制。该门控为每个token生成一个连续的“停止概率”，决定其是否跳过当前层的更新。该机制是端到端可微分的，无需更改基础架构，参数开销仅为1.7%。

Result: 在字符级语言建模任务上，TSA在Tiny-Shakespeare和enwik8数据集上节省了14-23%的token-layer操作（TLOps），且质量损失小于0.5%。即使在没有深度正则化的情况下（λ=0），仅靠任务损失梯度就能驱动路由器跳过20%的操作。在效率匹配的情况下，TSA的验证损失比早期退出方法低0.7%。

Conclusion: TSA证明了可以通过纯数据驱动的方式学习到与难度成正比的路由策略，无需显式的深度压力。该方法不仅实现了理论上的计算节省，还能直接转化为实际推理时的加速，为高效Transformer设计提供了一种简单有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply the same number of layers to every token regardless of contextual difficulty. We present Token-Selective Attention (TSA), a learned per-token gate on residual updates between consecutive transformer blocks. Each gate is a lightweight two-layer multi-layer perceptron (MLP) that produces a continuous halting probability, making the mechanism end-to-end differentiable with 1.7% parameter overhead and no changes to the base architecture. Notably, TSA learns difficulty-proportional routing without any explicit depth pressure: even at $λ=0$ (no depth regularisation), the task-loss gradient alone drives the router to skip 20% of token-layer operations. On character-level language modeling, TSA saved 14-23% of token-layer operations (TLOps) across Tiny-Shakespeare and enwik8 at <0.5% quality loss. At matched efficiency, TSA achieved 0.7% lower validation loss than early exit, and the learned routing transfers directly to inference-time sparse execution for real wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [37] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对稀疏自编码器（SAE）中组合式激活引导的不稳定性问题，提出了一个几何分析框架。研究发现，在高维稀疏锥流形和过完备字典设定下，存在一个组合崩溃阈值，ReLU整流机制会引发类似棘轮效应的干扰累积，从而限制了基于并集的引导方法的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）已广泛用于解耦Transformer中的特征叠加，并支持通过激活引导进行精确控制。然而，关于同时激活多个语义潜变量的组合式引导，其理论基础尚不完善。主流的线性表示假设往往忽略了在过完备字典中出现的非线性干扰效应，因此需要深入研究组合引导的理论边界与稳定性问题。

Method: 作者提出了一个几何分析框架来研究特征并集的不稳定性。具体方法包括：1. 将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形；2. 在球形字典模型下，推导出以信号锥的高斯平均宽度（统计维度）为特征的渐近组合崩溃阈值；3. 分析在高偏置状态下，ReLU整流如何将微观相关性引起的方差波动转化为系统性漂移，揭示干扰增长的棘轮效应机制；4. 在CLEVR数据集上提取结构化语义特征进行实验验证。

Result: 理论分析推导出了组合崩溃阈值的解析形式，表明特征并集的稳定性受限于几何约束。实验结果证实了预测的缩放趋势：与随机基线相比，CLEVR中提取的具有层级相关性的语义特征会加速向崩溃状态过渡。ReLU整流导致的系统性漂移会使干扰在组合过程中持续累积。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了基于并集的引导方法在可扩展性上存在固有的几何限制。结果表明，简单的线性叠加无法有效管理组合过程中的干扰累积。这启发了未来需要设计显式管理干扰的组合机制，以超越朴素的线性叠加假设，从而实现更稳定、可扩展的多特征引导。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

</details>


### [38] [CLAD: A Clustered Label-Agnostic Federated Learning Framework for Joint Anomaly Detection and Attack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06571)
*Iason Ofeidis,Nikos Papadis,Randeep Bhatia,Leandros Tassiulas,TV Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CLAD框架，通过结合聚类联邦学习（CFL）和双模式微架构（DM²A），同时解决了物联网安全中的设备异构性和标签稀缺两大瓶颈，在80%客户端无标签场景下检测性能相对提升30%，通信成本减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网和工业物联网的快速扩展创造了大规模、异构的攻击面，传统网络安全机制面临挑战。现有联邦学习方法在处理多样化设备行为时泛化能力不足，且无法有效利用现实边缘环境中大量未标记数据，存在设备异构性和标签稀缺两大核心问题。

Method: 提出CLAD框架，整合聚类联邦学习（CFL）与双模式微架构（DM²A）。DM²A包含共享编码器和两个分支，实现无监督异常检测和有监督攻击分类的联合学习，从而利用有标签和无标签客户端的数据。聚类组件动态聚合具有相似流量模式的设备，防止全局模型发散。

Result: 在80%客户端无标签的场景下，CLAD相比最先进基线方法实现了30%的相对检测性能提升，同时通信成本降低一半。

Conclusion: CLAD框架成功解决了物联网入侵检测系统中的设备异构性和标签稀缺问题，通过充分利用有标签和未标签数据，在显著降低通信成本的同时大幅提升了检测性能，为物联网安全提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and Industrial IoT (IIoT) has created a massive, heterogeneous attack surface that challenges traditional network security mechanisms. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative to centralized Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS), standard approaches struggle to generalize across diverse device behaviors and typically fail to utilize the vast amounts of unlabeled data present in realistic edge environments. To bridge these gaps, we propose CLAD, a holistic framework that seamlessly incorporates Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) with a novel Dual-Mode Micro-Architecture ($\text{DM}^2\text{A}$). This unified approach simultaneously tackles the two primary bottlenecks of IoT security: device heterogeneity and label scarcity. The $\text{DM}^2\text{A}$ component features a shared encoder followed by two branches, enabling joint unsupervised anomaly detection and supervised attack classification; this allows the framework to harvest intelligence from both labeled and unlabeled clients. Concurrently, the clustering component dynamically groups devices with congruent traffic patterns, preventing global model divergence. By carefully combining these elements, CLAD ensures that no data is discarded and distinct operational patterns are preserved. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that this integrated approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 30% relative improvement in detection performance in scenarios with 80% unlabeled clients, with only half the communication cost.

</details>


### [39] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了不可学习样本（UEs）在预训练-微调范式下的有效性问题，发现预训练权重的加载与冻结会显著削弱现有UE方法的保护效果，并据此提出了浅层语义伪装（SSC）方法以在语义有效子空间内生成扰动，成功在多种训练范式中保持了数据的不可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不可学习样本研究主要评估从头训练场景，但在广泛采用的预训练-微调范式下的行为尚未被探索。由于个人数据在模型训练中的未授权使用已成为日益严重的隐私威胁，研究UEs在实际训练范式下的有效性至关重要。

Method: 通过语义过滤理论解释现象：UEs倾向于诱导模型过拟合非语义噪声，而预训练范式下冻结的浅层保留了数据语义，过滤了干扰信息。基于此提出分层欺骗策略——浅层语义伪装（SSC），将生成过程限制在语义有效子空间内，以绕过预训练权重引入的语义抑制。

Result: 实验表明，加载和冻结预训练权重会显著削弱现有UE方法的有效性。所提出的SSC方法在包括浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练（SF-Pretrain）等挑战性训练范式中，能够一致地保持数据的不可学习性。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了基于预训练的不可学习学习领域的空白，证明了通过将扰动生成限制在语义有效子空间内，可以有效对抗预训练权重带来的语义保护机制，为实际应用场景下的数据隐私保护提供了新思路。

Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

</details>


### [40] [Recursive Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06639)
*Apurva Gandhi,Satyaki Chakraborty,Xiangjun Wang,Aviral Kumar,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了递归智能体优化（RAO），一种强化学习方法，用于训练能够递归地生成子任务并委托给自身新实例的智能体，实现了更好的训练效率和更强的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决现有智能体在处理长上下文和复杂任务时的局限性，使其能够通过分治策略在推理时进行扩展，从而应对超出模型上下文窗口或比训练任务更难的问题。

Method: 提出递归智能体优化（RAO），这是一种强化学习方法，用于训练递归智能体。该智能体能够生成子任务并递归地委托给自身的新实例，通过学习何时以及如何进行委托和通信，实现推理时的自然扩展。

Result: 实验表明，经RAO训练的递归智能体具有更高的训练效率，能够处理超出模型上下文窗口的任务，能泛化至比训练时更难的任务，且相比单智能体系统减少了实际运行时间。

Conclusion: RAO成功实现了递归智能体的训练，使其具备推理时扩展能力，在效率、泛化性和上下文处理能力上均优于传统单智能体系统。

Abstract: We introduce Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO), a reinforcement learning approach for training recursive agents: agents that can spawn and delegate sub-tasks to new instantiations of themselves recursively. Recursive agents implement an inference-time scaling algorithm that naturally allows agents to scale to longer contexts and generalize to more difficult problems via divide-and-conquer. RAO provides a method to train models to best take advantage of such recursive inference, teaching agents when and how to delegate and communicate. We find that recursive agents trained in this way enjoy better training efficiency, can scale to tasks that go beyond the model's context window, generalize to tasks much harder than the ones the agent was trained on, and can enjoy reduced wall-clock time compared to single-agent systems.

</details>


### [41] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MACS，一种免训练推理框架，通过熵加权负载机制和动态模态自适应容量机制解决MoE多模态大语言模型在专家并行推理中的掉队效应问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE多模态大语言模型在专家并行推理中存在显著的效率瓶颈，即掉队效应。现有基于token计数的负载均衡方法无法解决多模态场景下的两个独特挑战：（1）信息异质性，大量冗余视觉token被同等对待；（2）模态动态性，不同任务中视觉与文本比例变化导致资源错配。

Method: 提出MACS（模态感知容量扩展）框架，包含两个核心机制：熵加权负载机制，通过量化视觉token的语义价值解决信息异质性问题；动态模态自适应容量机制，根据输入的实时模态组成分配专家资源。该框架无需训练即可部署。

Result: 实验结果表明，MACS在多种多模态基准测试上显著优于现有方法，有效解决了MoE MLLMs在专家并行推理中的效率问题。

Conclusion: MACS为MoE多模态大语言模型的高效部署提供了新颖且鲁棒的解决方案，能够有效缓解专家并行推理中的掉队效应，实现更优的负载均衡。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [42] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将结果监督内化为过程监督的强化学习新方法，通过自动识别、纠正和复用失败的推理轨迹来提取过程级学习信号，实现了仅依赖结果监督的细粒度策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在推理任务中面临的核心挑战是：结果级监督信号稀疏，难以将其转化为指导中间推理步骤的细粒度学习信号。现有方法要么依赖序列级结果奖励导致信用分配困难，要么依赖昂贵且难以扩展的外部过程监督。

Method: 提出了“监督内化”方法，将结果监督内化为过程监督。模型通过识别、纠正和复用失败的推理轨迹，自动提取过程级学习信号，从而在仅有结果监督的情况下实现细粒度的策略优化。

Result: 该方法能够使模型在强化学习过程中持续生成和优化自身的内部过程监督，为强化学习推理中的细粒度信用分配开辟了一条区别于外部过程监督的新路径。

Conclusion: 通过将结果监督内化为过程监督，本文提出的训练范式解决了稀疏奖励下的信用分配难题，为强化学习推理提供了一种可扩展且高效的细粒度优化方案。

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [43] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPT框架，将数据筛选重构为在线重加权问题，通过动态调整样本权重而非离线预处理来提升大语言模型的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据筛选方法（如数据选择和混合）采用离线范式，与训练过程分离，导致工程开销大、对模型/任务变化脆弱，且通过硬过滤或重采样改变数据规模会牺牲多样性并损害泛化能力。

Method: 提出ADAPT（自适应数据重加权）框架，将数据筛选重新定义为在线重加权问题。该方法在训练过程中通过损失加权动态调整样本重要性，利用基于相似度的质量信号引导自适应的逐样本学习率，且不改变训练样本数量。

Result: 在指令微调和大规模预训练实验中，ADAPT持续优于离线选择/混合方法和先前的在线方法，在同等FLOPs下实现了更强的跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将数据筛选作为在线重加权问题而非离线预处理，能够有效解决现有方法的局限性，ADAPT作为隐式课程学习器，随着模型演化逐步将焦点从粗粒度模式转向细粒度语义区分，显著提升了模型性能。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

</details>


### [44] [OpenG2G: A Simulation Platform for AI Datacenter-Grid Runtime Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05519)
*Jae-Won Chung,Zhirui Liang,Yanyong Mao,Jiasi Chen,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Vladimir Dvorkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文构建了OpenG2G仿真平台，用于研究AI数据中心与电网之间的实时协调问题，支持多种控制范式的比较和数据中心灵活性量化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI计算需求的快速增长和数据中心的大规模建设给电网带来了巨大的容量和可靠性挑战，导致新建数据中心面临多年的并网延迟，成为AI发展的瓶颈。为了缓解这一压力，数据中心开始提供快速功率灵活性以响应电网信号，但缺乏有效的工具来理解大型数据中心对电网的影响以及设计有效的协调策略。

Method: 构建OpenG2G仿真平台，采用模块化和可扩展的架构设计，包含三个核心组件：（1）由生产级AI服务真实测量数据驱动的数据中心后端；（2）基于高保真电网模拟器的电网后端；（3）连接两者的通用控制器接口。该平台支持用户实现和比较多种控制范式，包括经典控制器、优化控制器和基于学习的控制器。

Result: OpenG2G能够回答广泛的协调问题，允许用户量化AI模型和部署选择如何影响数据中心的灵活性和协调结果。通过真实的电网场景和AI工作负载演示验证了平台的实用性。

Conclusion: OpenG2G为理解和协调AI数据中心与电网之间的复杂交互提供了有效的仿真工具，有助于设计更好的协调策略来缓解AI增长对电网的压力，促进数据中心的快速部署和电网的稳定运行。

Abstract: AI's growing compute demand and new datacenter buildouts present major capacity and reliability challenges for the electricity grid, leading to multi-year interconnection delays for new datacenters and bottlenecking AI growth. To ease this strain, datacenters increasingly offer rapid power flexibility in response to grid signals, where the datacenter can increase or decrease its power consumption by adapting its workload in real time.
  In order to understand the impact of large datacenters on the grid and to facilitate the design of effective coordination strategies, we build OpenG2G, a simulation platform for AI datacenter-grid runtime coordination. We show that OpenG2G is capable of answering a wide range of coordination questions by allowing users to implement and compare various control paradigms (including classic, optimization, and learning-based controllers), and quantify how AI model and deployment choices affect datacenter flexibility and coordination outcomes. This versatility is enabled by OpenG2G's modular and extensible architecture: a datacenter backend driven by real measurements of production-grade AI services, a grid backend built on high-fidelity grid simulators, and a generic controller interface that closes the loop between them. We describe the design of OpenG2G and demonstrate its usefulness through realistic grid scenarios and AI workloads.

</details>


### [45] [Evolutionary fine tuning of quantized convolution-based deep learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05228)
*Marcin Pietroń*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于进化策略的量化优化方法，通过迭代调整少量权重值来改善深度学习模型量化后的精度，实验证明该方法能快速提升量化模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在IoT、移动设备和实时系统中的应用受限于其复杂度和内存大小。量化是主流的模型压缩技术，但传统的最近邻量化方法无法保证最优精度，因此需要更高效的量化优化方法。

Method: 采用进化策略作为优化方法，在每次迭代中改变小比例权重的值，将其转移到不同的量化状态，替代传统的最近邻舍入方法。

Result: 在VGG、ResNet图像分类与检测任务以及自编码器架构上进行的实验表明，该方法能够快速提升量化模型的精度。

Conclusion: 提出的进化策略方法配合合适的算子和参数集，可以有效改善量化模型的精度，为深度学习模型在资源受限设备上的部署提供了更好的量化方案。

Abstract: Deep learning models are the most efficient models in many machine learning tasks. The main disadvantage when using them in IoT, mobile devices, independent autonomous or real-time systems is their complexity and memory size. Therefore, much research has concentrated on compression techniques of deep learning architectures. One of the most popular technique is quantization. In most of the works, the quantization is done based on the nearest neighbour quantization technique. This work focuses on improving the quantization efficiency in pretrained and quantized models. This approach has the potential to improve the final accuracy of quantized models. The main postulate of the work is that final quantization states of the network based on nearest neighbour rounding does not guarantee optimal accuracy. In the presented work, the evolution strategy is used as an optimization approach. The evolution in each iteration changes the values of the small percentage of weights. It shifts theirs values to different quantization states. The work shows that proposed evolution with an appropriate set of operators and parameters can fast improve the accuracy of the quantized models. The results are presented for popular architectures such as VGG and Resnet for image classification and detection. Additionally, simulations were carried out for the autoencoder architecture.

</details>


### [46] [Attribution-Guided Continual Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05285)
*Yazheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,Rui Xu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于归因引导的持续微调框架，通过评估参数重要性来调节梯度更新，有效缓解了大语言模型在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在持续学习序列任务时普遍存在灾难性遗忘问题。现有的缓解方法（如数据回放、参数冻结或正则化）缺乏对模型内部知识分布的语义感知，无法准确区分应保留或更新的参数，导致优化效果受限。

Method: 提出归因引导的持续微调框架。该方法在每个Transformer层中估计任务特定的逐元素参数重要性分数，并利用这些分数调节梯度更新：对先前任务重要的参数进行较小的更新，而对相关性较低的参数保持可塑性以学习新任务。

Result: 在持续学习基准测试中，该方法始终优于基线方法，在保持新任务竞争性性能的同时，实现了对旧任务更好的保留能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入语义感知的参数重要性评估机制，该方法有效解决了持续学习中参数更新的选择性问题，为大语言模型的持续学习提供了一种更精细、更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from catastrophic forgetting in continual learning: after learning new tasks sequentially, they perform worse on earlier tasks. Existing methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting by data replay, parameter freezing, or regularization. However, these methods lack semantic awareness of internal knowledge distribution in LLMs. As a result, they cannot distinguish parameters that should be preserved or updated. We propose an attribution-guided continual fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our method estimates task-specific, element-wise parameter importance in each Transformer layer and uses these scores to modulate gradients. Parameters important to previous tasks receive smaller updates, while less relevant ones remain plastic for learning new tasks. Experiments on continual learning benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms baselines, achieving better retention of old tasks while maintaining competitive performance on new tasks.

</details>


### [47] [Graph Normalization: Fast Binarizing Dynamics for Differentiable MWIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05330)
*Laurent Guigues*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了图归一化（GN），一种用于近似求解NP难最大权重独立集（MWIS）问题的可微分动态系统。该方法收敛性有保证、速度快，并在大规模基准测试中表现优异，为需要可微分“硬”决策的深度学习架构开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大权重独立集（MWIS）问题涵盖了许多组合挑战（如最优分配、调度、集合打包和MAP推断），但该问题是NP难的。现有的方法（如置信传播）缺乏收敛性保证。因此，迫切需要一种既有理论保证（收敛到最优解）又高效、可微分的方法，以便将其集成到需要端到端学习的深度学习架构中。

Method: 提出了图归一化（GN），一种基于图的动态系统。
1. **优化视角**：采用精确的Majorization-Minimization（MM）步骤实现快速拟牛顿下降，系统地改进MWIS松弛原目标。
2. **博弈论视角**：证明了GN与非线性进化博弈的复制子动力学等价。顶点为进入独立集而竞争，遵循费希尔自然选择基本定理，平均适应度（即MWIS目标）严格递增。
3. **理论推导**：建立了MIS与倾斜单纯形上二次形式局部极小值之间的双射关系（加权扩展的Motzkin-Straus定理）。
4. **应用**：对于分配问题，GN表现为Sinkhorn算法的变体，自然收敛到硬分配。

Result: 1. **理论结果**：证明了GN总是收敛到最大独立集的二元指示器。
2. **实验性能**：作为最先进的Bregman-Sinkhorn松弛MWIS求解器的快速二值化引擎，在多达100万条边的真实世界基准测试中，GN在CPU上仅需数秒即可识别出与已知最佳结果误差在1%以内的解。

Conclusion: GN框架为需要约束下可微分“硬”决策的深度学习架构（如结构化稀疏注意力、动态网络剪枝、混合专家模型）提供了新思路，并能推广至计算机视觉、计算生物学和资源分配等领域的约束优化端到端学习。

Abstract: We introduce Graph Normalization (GN), a principled dynamical system on graphs that serves as a differentiable approximation engine for the NP-hard Maximum Weight Independent Set (MWIS) problem. MWIS encompasses many combinatorial challenges, including optimal assignment, scheduling, set packing, and MAP inference in discrete Markov Random Fields. Unlike Belief Propagation, we prove GN always converges to a binary indicator of a Maximum Independent Set. GN realizes a fast quasi-Newton descent through an exact Majorization-Minimization step, systematically improving the MWIS relaxed primal objective. We establish an equivalence between GN and the Replicator Dynamics of a nonlinear evolutionary game, where vertices compete for inclusion in an independent set. While a non-potential game, the GN game follows Fisher's Fundamental Theorem of Natural Selection, where the average fitness equals the MWIS primal objective and strictly increases. This connection leads to a weighted extension of the Motzkin-Straus theorem, showing MISes are in bijection with the local minima of a quadratic form over a tilted simplex. For the Assignment Problem, GN acts as a variant of the Sinkhorn algorithm that naturally converges to a hard assignment while generalizing to arbitrary constraint graphs. We demonstrate GN's performance as a fast binarization engine for the state-of-the-art Bregman-Sinkhorn relaxed MWIS solver. On real-world benchmarks with up to 1M edges, GN identifies solutions within 1% of the best known results in seconds on a CPU. GN opens new avenues for deep learning architectures requiring differentiable, "hard" decisions under constraints, with applications in structured sparse attention, dynamic network pruning, and Mixture-of-Experts. Beyond core AI, the GN framework enables end-to-end learning of constrained optimization in computer vision, computational biology, and resource allocation.

</details>


### [48] [Feature Starvation as Geometric Instability in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05341)
*Faris Chaudhry,Keisuke Yano,Anthea Monod*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应弹性网络稀疏自编码器（AEN-SAEs），通过结合ℓ₂结构项和自适应ℓ₁重加权，从理论上解决了标准SAEs的特征匮乏和收缩偏差问题，无需启发式方法即可在LLMs上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的ℓ₁正则化稀疏自编码器存在特征匮乏（神经元死亡）和收缩偏差问题，现有解决方案依赖计算昂贵的启发式重采样和不可微的硬掩码方法。作者认为特征匮乏并非数据多样性不足的经验产物，而是过完备字典的基本优化几何病理：ℓ₁诱导的稀疏编码映射不稳定，且与浅层摊销编码器存在根本性错位。

Method: 提出自适应弹性网络稀疏自编码器（AEN-SAEs），这是一种基于经典稀疏回归的完全可微架构。该方法结合了ℓ₂结构项（强制强凸性和Lipschitz稳定性）与自适应ℓ₁重加权（消除收缩偏差并抑制虚假特征），从而联合控制诱导多面体几何的曲率和相互作用结构。

Result: 理论上证明AEN-SAEs产生Lipschitz连续的稀疏编码映射，并在温和假设下恢复全局特征支持。实验方面，在合成环境和大语言模型（Pythia 70M、Llama 3.1 8B）上，AEN-SAEs在无需辅助启发式方法的情况下缓解了特征匮乏问题，同时保持了具有竞争力的重建能力。

Conclusion: AEN-SAEs成功解决了标准SAEs的结构不稳定性问题，提供了一种理论扎实、完全可微的解决方案，在消除启发式方法依赖的同时保持了模型性能，为LLMs的可解释性研究提供了更可靠的特征解缠方法。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are used to disentangle the dense, polysemantic internal representations of large language models (LLMs) into interpretable, monosemantic concepts. However, standard $\ell_1$-regularized SAEs suffer from feature starvation (dead neurons) and shrinkage bias, often requiring computationally expensive heuristic resampling and nondifferentiable hard-masking methods to bypass these challenges. We argue that feature starvation is not merely an empirical artifact of poor data diversity, but a fundamental optimization-geometric pathology of overcomplete dictionaries: the $\ell_1$-induced sparse coding map is unstable and fundamentally misaligned with shallow, amortized encoders. To address this structural instability, we introduce adaptive elastic net SAEs (AEN-SAEs), a fully differentiable architecture grounded in classical sparse regression. AEN-SAEs combine an $\ell_2$ structural term that enforces strong convexity and Lipschitz stability with adaptive $\ell_1$ reweighting that eliminates shrinkage bias and suppresses spurious features, thereby jointly controlling the curvature and interaction structure of the induced polyhedral geometry. Theoretically, we show that AEN-SAEs yield a Lipschitz-continuous sparse coding map and recover the global feature support under mild assumptions. Empirically, across synthetic settings and LLMs (Pythia 70M, Llama 3.1 8B), AEN-SAEs mitigate feature starvation without auxiliary heuristics while maintaining competitive reconstruction abilities.

</details>


### [49] [A Multi-Head Attention Approach for SLA Compliance Monitoring in Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05354)
*Omanshu Thapliyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将数据中心托管合同中的SLA规则编码为结构化JSON对象的框架，通过训练多头Transformer模型提前30分钟预测违规行为，并生成面向财务、运营和合规三个视角的结构化预测视图，从而实现主动式SLA管理并降低违约罚款风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的数据中心托管服务等级协议（SLA）监控采用被动响应模式，仅在违规发生后才检测到违约，这限制了补救机会并可能导致违约罚款。现有的监控方法缺乏预测能力，无法提前预警潜在的SLA违规事件。

Method: 该方法首先将SLA规则编码为结构化JSON对象以自动生成训练数据。然后训练一个针对每个客户的多头Transformer模型，其中每个注意力头专门针对一条SLA规则进行学习，捕获违规发生前30分钟的时间依赖关系。推理阶段将结构化预测事件转换为三个角色特定的视图：暴露信用责任的财务模式、展示风险评分和推荐干预措施的运营模式，以及用于审计的合规模式。

Result: 通过将模型架构直接与合同义务对齐，该框架能够成功预测SLA违规行为，提前30分钟发出预警。系统能够生成针对不同角色的结构化输出，包括财务责任评估、运营风险评分与干预建议，以及合规审计所需的不可变遥测签名。

Conclusion: 该框架使运营人员能够提前预判SLA违规，优先采取纠正措施，从而有效减少财务处罚。通过将模型架构与合同条款直接对应，实现了更具可解释性和实用性的SLA主动管理系统。

Abstract: Service level agreements (SLAs) in data center colocation contracts define precise thresholds for power, temperature, and humidity, with tiered violation penalties expressed as credits against monthly recurring charges. Traditional reactive monitoring detects breaches only after they occur, limiting remediation opportunities. We present a framework that encodes SLA rules as structured JSON objects to generate training data without manual annotation. We train a per-customer multi-head transformer model in which each attention head specializes in one SLA rule, learning temporal dependencies that precede violations by 30 minutes. Post-training, the inference service emits structured prediction events transformed into three role-specific views: finance schemas exposing credit liability, operations schemas surfacing risk scores and recommended interventions, and compliance schemas bundling predictions with immutable telemetry signatures for audit. By aligning model architecture directly with contractual obligations, this framework enables operators to anticipate SLA breaches, prioritize corrective actions, and minimize financial penalties.

</details>


### [50] [Balancing Stability and Plasticity in Sequentially Trained Early-Exiting Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05358)
*Alaa Zniber,Ouassim Karrakchou,Mounir Ghogho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对早退神经网络中顺序训练导致的灾难性遗忘问题，提出了两种基于持续学习思想的方法（EWC和LwF），有效保护了已有出口的知识，提升了整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，早退神经网络通常采用顺序训练的方式，即向共享骨干网络逐步添加新的出口分类器。然而，这种训练方式会导致新引入的出口干扰先前已学习好的出口，导致早期分类器性能退化。这种“可塑性-稳定性”困境促使作者寻求解决方案。

Method: 作者提出了两种替代方法来应对顺序训练中的干扰问题，分别对应持续学习中的不同策略：
1. **基于参数约束的方法**：利用弹性权重巩固（Elastic Weight Consolidation, EWC）技术，识别并保护对先前训练出口至关重要的参数，在学习新出口时限制这些参数的改变。
2. **基于输出分布保持的方法**：利用无遗忘学习（Learning without Forgetting, LwF）技术，在适应新出口的同时，保持早期出口的输出分布不变。
这两种方法分别从参数层面和输出层面解决了稳定性与可塑性的权衡问题。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上的实验表明，所提出的方法一致地提升了早退网络的性能。与现有的顺序训练方法相比，该方法实现了更高的准确率，并在低计算预算下获得了显著的性能加速。

Conclusion: 本文成功将持续学习中的稳定性-可塑性权衡理论应用于早退神经网络的顺序训练场景，提出的两种方法均能有效缓解新出口对旧出口的干扰，为构建高效、自适应的推理网络提供了有效的训练范式。

Abstract: Early-exiting neural networks enable adaptive inference by allowing inputs to exit at intermediate classifiers, reducing computation for easy samples while maintaining high accuracy. In practice, exits can be trained sequentially by incrementally adding them to a shared backbone; however, this sequential training can cause newly introduced exits to interfere with previously learned ones, degrading the performance of earlier classifiers. We address this problem by retaining the knowledge embedded in existing exits while allowing new ones to specialize. We propose two alternative approaches that operate at different levels of the model. The first constrains learning by protecting parameters that are important for previously trained exits, while the second preserves the output distributions of earlier exits as the network adapts. These alternatives directly reflect the stability-plasticity trade-off studied in continual learning. Accordingly, we leverage \textit{Elastic Weight Consolidation} to constrain critical weights and \textit{Learning without Forgetting} to preserve output distributions. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that our approaches consistently improve early-exit performance, achieving higher accuracy over existing sequential training methods and significant performance speedups at low computational budgets.

</details>


### [51] [COPYCOP: Ownership Verification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05360)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CopyCop算法，用于识别经过架构差异、权重差异、维度差异以及对抗性变换处理后的抄袭图神经网络(GNN)。该算法在14个数据集和5种GNN架构上验证了其准确性和鲁棒性，并提供了理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印和指纹方法难以识别经过变换处理的抄袭GNN模型。攻击者可能训练一个GNN专门模仿另一个GNN的节点嵌入，并通过变换输出嵌入来掩盖抄袭关系，且两个GNN可能具有不同的架构、权重和嵌入维度，这给检测带来了严峻挑战。

Method: 提出CopyCop算法，该算法能够在GNN架构不同、权重不同、嵌入维度不同，且输出嵌入经过对抗性变换的情况下，检测两个GNN是否存在抄袭关系。算法提供了理论保证，确保检测的有效性。

Result: 在14个数据集和5种GNN架构上的实验表明，CopyCop能够准确识别抄袭GNN，并对广泛的对抗性攻击和变换具有鲁棒性。与现有的水印和指纹方法相比，CopyCop在更严格的条件下仍能有效工作。

Conclusion: CopyCop为GNN所有权验证提供了一种有效的解决方案，能够在对抗性设置下成功识别抄袭模型。该工作填补了现有GNN保护方法在应对复杂抄袭场景方面的空白，具有重要的理论和实践价值。

Abstract: Given two GNNs that output node embeddings, how can we determine if they were trained independently? An adversary could have trained one GNN specifically to mimic the other GNN's embeddings. To obscure this relationship between the GNNs, the adversarial GNN might then transform its output embeddings. The two GNNs could have different architectures, weights, and embedding dimensions, and the adversary can transform the embeddings. Despite these stringent conditions, our algorithm (named CopyCop) can identify such copycat GNNs, unlike existing watermarking and fingerprinting methods. We also provide theoretical guarantees for CopyCop. Finally, experiments on 14 datasets and 5 GNN architectures demonstrate that CopyCop is accurate and robust against a broad class of adversarial attacks and transformations. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CopyCop-Graph-Ownership-Verification-8143/README.md

</details>


### [52] [SPADE: Faster Drug Discovery by Learning from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05370)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Ben Fauber,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SPADE算法，用于从头发现高亲和力、高选择性的配体分子，平均仅需40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体，显著提升了样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现过程中，只有不到5%的候选配体能通过早期筛选，且针对缺乏先验数据的新颖蛋白质，需要从头开始迭代筛选，这要求在尽可能少的测试次数内找到足够的高质量配体。

Method: 提出了一种名为SPADE的新颖配体选择算法，能够从稀疏数据中学习，以极低的测试成本高效筛选候选药物分子。

Result: SPADE平均仅需40次测试即可发现10个高质量配体；在一对一对比中，在更多蛋白质上超越了深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，样本效率中位数提升7%-32%；评分速度比最接近的竞争对手快10倍。

Conclusion: SPADE算法在药物发现任务中表现出卓越的样本效率和计算效率，显著优于现有深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，为新蛋白质靶点的快速药物筛选提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Drug discovery seeks molecules (ligands) that bind strongly and selectively to a target protein. However, fewer than 5% of candidate ligands pass the bar for even the early stages of drug discovery. Furthermore, we want methods that work for novel proteins for which we have no prior data. Starting from scratch, we have to iteratively select and test candidate ligands such that we find enough ligands of the desired quality in as few tests as possible. Our proposed algorithm, named SPADE, introduces a novel approach to ligand selection that requires only 40 tests on average to find 10 high-quality ligands. In one-vs-one comparisons, SPADE outperforms deep learning and Bayesian optimization methods on more proteins, achieving median improvements of 7%-32% in sample efficiency. SPADE is also 10x faster than its closest competitor at scoring candidate drugs. Dataset and code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md

</details>


### [53] [Neural Co-state Policies: Structuring Hidden States in Recurrent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05373)
*David Leeftink,Max Hinne,Marcel van Gerven*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了循环神经网络隐状态与庞特里亚金极小值原理（PMP）协状态之间的形式化联系，通过引入PMP导出的协状态损失来显式构建网络内部动力学，提升了部分可观测强化学习任务的性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境下，基于记忆的循环策略虽然能够编码历史信息，但其内部动力学是不透明的黑盒，缺乏可解释性。作者希望找到循环网络隐状态与最优控制理论之间的形式化联系，以设计更具原则性和鲁棒性的控制策略。

Method: 证明了标准循环架构的潜在表示直接映射到PMP协状态，使读出层可解释为执行哈密顿量最小化。提出了一种PMP导出的协状态损失函数，显式地构建网络的内部动力学结构，使其符合最优控制原理。

Result: 在部分可观测的DMControl任务上，该方法性能匹配或超越基线方法，且对零样本分布外传感器掩蔽具有鲁棒性。实验验证了隐状态与协状态之间的对齐关系。

Conclusion: 通过将循环网络重新框架化为受极小值原理支配的动态过程，为设计鲁棒的连续控制策略提供了原则性的方法，同时增强了网络内部动力学的可解释性。

Abstract: A key capability of intelligent agents is operating under partial observability: reasoning and acting effectively despite missing or incomplete state observations. While recurrent (memory-based) policies learned via reinforcement learning address this by encoding history into latent state representations, their internal dynamics remain uninterpretable black boxes. This paper establishes a formal link between these hidden states and the Pontryagin minimum principle (PMP) from optimal control. We demonstrate that for standard recurrent architectures, latent representations map directly to PMP co-states, which allows the readout layer to be interpreted as performing Hamiltonian minimization. Because standard reward maximization does not naturally discover this alignment, we introduce a PMP-derived co-state loss to explicitly structure the internal dynamics. Empirically, this approach matches or improves performance on partially observable DMControl tasks, and is robust against zero-shot out-of-distribution sensor masking. By framing recurrent networks as dynamic processes governed by the minimum principle, we provide a principled approach to designing robust continuous control policies.

</details>


### [54] [Conditional Diffusion Under Linear Constraints: Langevin Mixing and Information-Theoretic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05387)
*Ahmad Aghapour,Erhan Bayraktar,Asaf Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型在线性逆问题（如图像修复和超分辨率）中的零样本条件采样问题。通过得分函数的正切-法向分解，证明了用无条件正切得分替代条件正切得分的误差由观测与未观测分量之间的条件互信息上界界定，并提出了一种改进的采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于投影的采样器在反向扩散过程中仅通过测量一致性来校正观测分量，但测量一致性本身无法确定可行集上的概率质量分布，导致条件样本产生偏差。针对这一问题，本文旨在为线性逆问题中的零样本条件采样提供理论分析和改进方法。

Method: 通过得分函数的正切-法向分解进行分析。对于高斯噪声，观测方向的得分完全由测量确定，仅正切条件得分未知。证明了用无条件正切得分替代该得分的误差由观测与未观测分量之间的维度无关条件互信息上界界定。基于此理论，提出了投影Langevin初始化结合引导反向去噪的采样方法。

Result: 理论结果表明，无条件正切得分替代条件正切得分的误差被维度无关的条件互信息上界所约束，给出了初始化误差和路径得分匹配误差的信息论分解。在图像修复和超分辨率实验中，所提出的方法优于强投影基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文为扩散模型在线性逆问题中的零样本条件采样提供了理论分析框架，揭示了测量一致性与条件分布之间的关系。提出的投影Langevin初始化和引导反向去噪方法在实验中取得了更优的性能，为相关应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We study zero-shot conditional sampling with pretrained diffusion models for linear inverse problems, including inpainting and super-resolution. In these problems, the observation determines only part of the unknown signal. The remaining degrees of freedom must be sampled according to the correct conditional data distribution. Existing projection-based samplers enforce measurement consistency by correcting the observed component during reverse diffusion. However, measurement consistency alone does not determine how probability mass should be distributed along the feasible set, and this can lead to biased conditional samples.
  We analyze this issue through a normal--tangent decomposition of the score function. For Gaussian noising, the observed-direction score is exactly determined by the measurement; only the tangent conditional score is unknown. We prove that the error from replacing this score by the unconditional tangent score is upper bounded by a dimension-free conditional mutual information between observed and unobserved components. This gives an information-theoretic decomposition into initialization and pathwise score-mismatch errors. Motivated by the theory, we propose a projected-Langevin initialization followed by guided reverse denoising, which outperforms a strong projection-based baseline in inpainting and super-resolution experiments.

</details>


### [55] [Two-Stage Learned Decomposition for Scalable Routing on Multigraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05389)
*Filip Rydin,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani,Balázs Kulcsár*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种节点-边缘策略分解（NEPF）方法，通过将路由策略分解为节点排序和边缘选择两个阶段，有效解决了多重图VRP的可扩展性问题，在保证解质量的同时大幅提升了训练和推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经组合优化方法大多局限于欧几里得空间或简单图结构，难以处理具有平行边（代表不同权衡选项，如距离与时间）的多重图VRP。少数针对多重图的方法则面临严重的可扩展性问题，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出节点-边缘策略分解（NEPF）框架，将路由策略分解为两个阶段：节点排序阶段和边缘选择阶段。为此引入了预编码边缘聚合方案、边缘阶段的非自回归架构，以及分层强化学习方法来联合训练这两个阶段。

Result: 在六种VRP变体上的实验表明，NEPF在解质量方面匹配或超越现有最先进方法，同时在训练和推理速度上显著更快，有效解决了可扩展性问题。

Conclusion: NEPF方法成功地将多重图VRP的复杂策略分解为可处理的子问题，通过非自回归架构和分层训练实现了高效学习，为复杂图结构下的组合优化问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Most neural methods for Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are limited to Euclidean settings or simple graphs. In this work, we instead consider multigraphs, where parallel edges represent distinct travel options with varying trade-offs (e.g., distance vs time). Few methods are designed for such formulations and those that do exist face major scalability issues. We mitigate these scalability issues via a Node-Edge Policy Factorization (NEPF) approach, which splits the routing policy into a node permutation stage and an edge selection stage. To enable the decomposition, we introduce a pre-encoding edge aggregation scheme and a non-autoregressive architecture for the edge stage, as well as a hierarchical reinforcement learning method to train the stages jointly. Our experiments across six VRP variants demonstrate that NEPF matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art in terms of solution quality, while being significantly faster in training and inference.

</details>


### [56] [Differentiable Parameter Optimization for DAEs with State-Dependent Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05395)
*Ion Matei,Maksym Zhenirovskyy,Anthony Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对带状态依赖事件的微分代数方程(DAE)，提出了两种梯度计算策略用于参数优化，包括自动微分仿真法和显式离散伴随法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带状态依赖事件的DAE系统在参数学习方面面临挑战，因为代数变量隐式定义、事件时间依赖参数、重置映射引入不连续性，导致基于梯度的参数学习困难。

Method: 将学习问题建模为带DAE动力学、代数约束、监护方程和重置映射的约束最小二乘问题，开发了两种梯度计算策略：(1)自动微分仿真法：在向量场内求解代数变量，利用隐函数定理微分代数求解，通过分段可微积分处理事件；(2)显式离散伴随法：将前向仿真表示为事件分割残差系统，通过求解光滑段和事件残差的拉格朗日乘子计算梯度。

Result: 两种方法都能为前向仿真选择的事件路径提供梯度，在固定事件排序和横截监护穿越条件下有效；伴随方法中的残差项是等式约束而非启发式惩罚项。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了带事件DAE系统的可微参数优化问题，两种互补方法在梯度解释、事件时间处理、实现复杂性和局部有效性方面各有特点，为相关系统参数学习提供了理论和方法支持。

Abstract: Differential-algebraic equations (DAEs) with state-dependent events arise in systems whose continuous dynamics are constrained by algebraic equations and interrupted by mode changes, switching logic, impacts, or state reinitializations. Gradient-based parameter learning for such systems is challenging because algebraic variables are implicitly defined, event times depend on the parameters, and reset maps introduce discontinuities. This paper studies differentiable parameter optimization for semi-explicit DAEs with events. We formulate the learning problem as a constrained least-squares problem with DAE dynamics, algebraic constraints, guard equations, and reset maps. We then develop two complementary gradient-computation strategies. The first is an automatic-differentiation-through-simulation method that solves algebraic variables inside the vector field, differentiates the algebraic solve using the implicit function theorem, and handles events through segmented differentiable integration. The second is an explicit discrete-adjoint method that represents the forward simulation as an event-split residual system and computes gradients by solving for the Lagrange multipliers of smooth-segment and event residuals. The formulation clarifies that residual terms in the adjoint method are equality constraints, not heuristic penalties. We compare the two approaches in terms of gradient interpretation, event-time handling, implementation complexity, and local validity. Both methods provide gradients for the event path selected by the forward simulation and are valid under fixed event ordering and transversal guard crossings.

</details>


### [57] [Information Theoretic Adversarial Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05415)
*Yiwei Zhang,Jeremiah Birrell,Reza Ebrahimi,Rouzbeh Behnia,Jason Pacheco,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了WARDEN，一种面向大语言模型的分布鲁棒对抗训练框架，通过动态重加权对抗样本，在保持模型实用性的同时显著降低了攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在安全对齐方面取得了进展，但仍易受对抗性提示攻击。现有的对抗训练方法计算成本高昂且难以扩展，而近期的连续对抗训练方法（如CAT和CAPO）虽有所改进，但仍需进一步提升鲁棒性与效率。

Method: 提出WARDEN框架，在经验训练分布周围构建f-散度模糊集，通过动态重加权对抗样本优化最坏情况下的对抗损失。利用凸对偶公式，在KL散度下目标函数简化为log-sum-exp形式，通过动态参数控制重加权强度，形成一类新的信息论目标函数。

Result: 在多个大语言模型和攻击设置下，WARDEN显著降低了攻击成功率，其计算成本和模型实用性与CAT、CAPO及MixAT等基线方法相当。

Conclusion: WARDEN为大语言模型提供了一种实用的、可扩展的鲁棒对齐方法，在有效提升模型对抗鲁棒性的同时，保持了模型的原有性能和较低的计算开销。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to adversarial prompting despite advances in alignment and safety, often exhibiting harmful behaviors under novel attack strategies. While adversarial training can improve robustness, existing approaches are computationally expensive and difficult to scale. Recent continuous adversarial training methods, such as Continuous adversarial training (CAT) and Continuous Adversarial Preference Optimization (CAPO), address this challenge by leveraging gradient-based perturbations in the embedding space, enabling more efficient and expressive attacks. Building on this paradigm, we propose WARDEN, a distributionally robust adversarial training framework for LLMs that dynamically reweights adversarial examples through an f -divergence ambiguity set around the empirical training distribution. Our method optimizes the worst-case adversarial loss within a divergence ball around the empirical data distribution, automatically emphasizing harder adversarial examples. Using the convex dual formulation, the objective reduces to a log-sum-exp form under the KL divergence, with a dynamical parameter controlling the strength of reweighting. This study leads to a new class of information-theoretic objectives that significantly reduce attack success rates while maintaining model utility. Across multiple LLMs and attack settings, WARDEN substantially reduces attack success rates with computational and utility costs comparable to CAT-, CAPO-, and MixAT-based baselines, making it a practical approach for scalable robust alignment.

</details>


### [58] [Active Learning for Conditional Generative Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05435)
*Alexander DeLise,Nick Dexter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于提示词条件生成模型的生成式压缩感知，证明了提示词匹配的Christoffel采样具有近最优恢复保证，而提示词不匹配会带来明确的兼容性惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式压缩感知将预训练生成器的值域作为非线性模型，用于从有限测量中恢复结构化信号。现有工作未充分探讨条件生成模型中提示词的作用，特别是在欠采样傅里叶测量图像恢复任务中，提示词在采样设计和恢复模型定义中的双重角色需要被区分和理解。

Method: 提出了一个条件生成式压缩感知框架，区分提示词的两个角色：用于设计采样分布的提示词和用于定义恢复模型的提示词。针对ReLU和Lipschitz条件生成器，分析了提示词匹配的Christoffel采样，推导了稳定恢复界限，并量化了提示词不匹配时的兼容性惩罚。在Stable Diffusion模型上进行了实验验证。

Result: 证明了提示词匹配的Christoffel采样保持与现有近最优生成式压缩感知理论相同的Christoffel复杂度常数，而提示词不匹配会引入明确的兼容性惩罚项。实验表明提示词能够有意义地重塑Christoffel采样分布并影响图像恢复质量。

Conclusion: 提示词应被视为设计变量，在感知、近似和恢复过程中具有不同的效果。提示词匹配对于实现最优恢复至关重要，这为条件生成模型在压缩感知中的应用提供了理论指导和实践启示。

Abstract: Generative compressed sensing uses the range of a pretrained generator as a nonlinear model for recovering structured signals from limited measurements. We study a conditional version of this problem for image recovery from subsampled Fourier measurements using prompt-conditioned generative models. Our framework separates two roles of conditioning: the prompt used to design the sampling distribution and the prompt used to define the recovery model. For ReLU and Lipschitz conditional generators, we prove stable recovery bounds showing that prompt-matched Christoffel sampling retains the same Christoffel complexity constant as existing near-optimal generative compressed sensing theory, while prompt mismatch incurs an explicit compatibility penalty. Experiments with Stable Diffusion show that prompts meaningfully reshape Christoffel sampling distributions and influence image recovery. Overall, our results suggest that prompts should be treated as design variables with distinct effects on sensing, approximation, and recovery.

</details>


### [59] [On Semantic Loss Fine-Tuning Approach for Preventing Model Collapse in Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05438)
*Pratik Deshmukh,Atirek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer模型在因果推理任务的标准微调中会发生灾难性坍缩，提出基于图逻辑约束和动态lambda调度的语义损失函数来防止该问题，并在大规模实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现Transformer模型在因果推理任务上进行标准微调时会发生灾难性的模型坍缩，模型会学习到平凡解（如总是预测“是”或“否”），导致虽然表面准确率很高，但实际上并未掌握因果推理能力。

Method: 提出了一种语义损失函数，结合了基于图的逻辑约束和动态lambda调度机制。该方法在微调过程中引入结构化的语义约束，防止模型学习到平凡解。研究使用了Gemma 270M模型，在传递性和d-分离任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 在传递性任务上达到70.4%准确率，d-分离任务上达68.6%，相比坍缩基线提升42.7%。对抗性评估中语义模型达67-70%准确率，而坍缩模型仅为43-71%。实验基于200,000+评估样本和五种模型变体。未采用语义损失时坍缩率达100%。

Conclusion: 语义损失函数对于Transformer模型实现稳定的因果推理能力是必要且不可或缺的，而非可选项。该方法有效防止了模型坍缩问题，使模型能够做出稳定的、依赖上下文的预测。

Abstract: Standard fine-tuning of transformer models on causal reasoning tasks leads to catastrophic model collapse, where models learn trivial solutions such as always predicting "Yes" or "No" regardless of input structure. We demonstrate that fine-tuning Gemma 270M on transitivity and d-separation tasks without semantic loss results in 100% collapse rate, with models achieving misleadingly high accuracy (73.9%) while learning no causal reasoning. We propose a semantic loss function with graph-based logical constraints and dynamic lambda scheduling that prevents this collapse. Our approach achieves 70.4% accuracy on transitivity tasks and 68.6% on d-separation tasks with stable, context-dependent predictions, representing a 42.7% improvement over collapsed baselines. Adversarial evaluation on 1,000 structural reasoning samples shows semantic models achieve 67-70% accuracy while collapsed models fail catastrophically at 43-71%. We validate our findings through comprehensive benchmarking on 200,000+ evaluation samples across five model variants, demonstrating that semantic loss is essential and not optional, for stable causal reasoning in transformers.

</details>


### [60] [Robustness of Graph Self-Supervised Learning to Real-World Noise: A Case Study on Text-Driven Biomedical Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05463)
*Othmane Kabal,Mounira Harzallah,Fabrice Guillet,Hideaki Takeda,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对文本驱动噪声知识图谱上的图自监督学习（GSSL）进行了全面评估，发现特征重建任务对噪声更具鲁棒性，而双向关系型消息传递GNN架构更适合处理噪声图，提出的NATD-GSSL框架相比预训练语言模型基线提升达7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图自监督学习研究假设输入是干净、人工策划的图，然而NLP技术使得大规模从文本自动抽取知识图谱成为可能，这为GSSL带来了新机遇但也引入了真实世界的噪声。先前的鲁棒性研究主要依赖合成扰动，对这种真实文本驱动噪声缺乏探索。

Method: 提出噪声感知文本驱动图GSSL框架（NATD-GSSL），整合自动图构建、图优化和GSSL三部分。采用双图协议进行评估：对比从MedMentions衍生的噪声图与干净的统一医学语言系统（UMLS）参考图，通过共享金标准进行对齐。

Result: 不同预训练任务和GNN架构的鲁棒性存在显著差异：关系重建对噪声高度敏感且受益于清晰的模式定义，特征重建则显著更鲁棒，能达到与干净图设置相当的性能；对比学习目标受噪声影响较小但强烈依赖于与下游任务的对齐程度。双向关系消息传递设计更适合噪声文本驱动图，单向设计在干净图上表现最佳。

Conclusion: NATD-GSSL为在真实世界噪声图上应用GSSL提供了实用指导，证明了架构和任务选择对噪声鲁棒性的关键影响，在无监督术语类型任务上相比预训练语言模型基线获得最高7%的性能提升。

Abstract: Graph Self-Supervised Learning (GSSL) offers a powerful paradigm for learning graph representations without labeled data. However, existing work assumes clean, manually curated graphs. Recent advances in NLP enable the large-scale automatic extraction of knowledge graphs from text, opening new opportunities for GSSL while introducing substantial real-world noise. This type of noise remains largely unexplored, as prior robustness studies typically rely on synthetic perturbations. To address this gap, we present the first comprehensive evaluation of GSSL methods on text-driven graphs for unsupervised term typing. We introduce Noise-Aware Text-Driven Graph GSSL (NATD-GSSL), a unified framework that combines automatic graph construction, graph refinement, and GSSL. Our evaluation follows a dual-graph protocol that contrasts a noisy graph derived from MedMentions with a clean Unified Medical Language System (UMLS) reference graph, aligned through a shared gold standard. Our results reveal variability in robustness across both pretext tasks and Graph Neural Network (GNN) architectures. Relation reconstruction is highly sensitive to noise and benefits from well-defined schemas, whereas feature reconstruction is considerably more robust, achieving performance comparable to clean-graph settings. Contrastive objectives are generally less affected by noise but depend strongly on alignment with downstream tasks. GNN architecture also plays a critical role: bidirectional relational message-passing designs are better suited to noisy, text-driven graphs, while unidirectional relational ones perform best on clean graphs. Overall, NATD-GSSL provides practical guidance for applying GSSL to real-world, noisy graphs and achieves up to a 7\% improvement over pretrained language model baselines. All code and benchmarks are publicly available at https://github.com/OthmaneKabal/MC2GAE.

</details>


### [61] [A Unified Benchmark for Evaluating Knowledge Graph Construction Methods and Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05476)
*Othmane Kabal,Mounira Harzallah,Fabrice Guillet,Hideaki Takeda,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个双重目的基准测试，用于联合评估GNN在噪声文本图谱上的性能以及图谱构建方法在下游任务中的有效性，并提供了标准化、可复现、可扩展的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从文本自动构建的知识图谱虽然应用广泛，但存在噪声、碎片化和语义不一致性等问题，影响图神经网络（GNN）在下游任务中的表现。然而，评估其性能和鲁棒性仍然困难，因为往往不清楚观察到的结果源于学习模型还是构建图谱本身的质量。

Method: 在生物医学领域，基于单一文本语料库构建基准，包含使用不同抽取方法自动构建的两个图谱，以及一个由专家策划的高质量参考图谱（作为性能上限）。该设计通过半监督节点分类任务，实现对构建方法的可控比较和对GNN鲁棒性的系统性评估，并提供标准化、可复现、可扩展的评估框架。

Result: 该基准能够联合评估GNN在噪声文本图谱上的性能及图谱构建方法的有效性，支持对图谱构建方法的对比分析以及对GNN鲁棒性的系统性评价，同时便于集成新的图谱抽取方法和学习模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的基准与框架为评估文本构建知识图谱的质量及GNN在噪声图谱上的鲁棒性提供了有效工具，促进了该领域的标准化和进一步研究。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs automatically constructed from text are increasingly used in real-world applications. However, their inherent noise, fragmentation, and semantic inconsistencies significantly affect the performance of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) on downstream tasks. Assessing their performance and robustness remains difficult, as it is often unclear whether observed results stem from the learning model or from the quality of the constructed graph itself. In this work, we introduce a dual-purpose benchmark designed to jointly evaluate (i) the performance of GNNs on noisy, text-derived graphs and (ii) the effectiveness of graph construction methods on a downstream task. The benchmark is built in the biomedical domain from a single textual corpus and includes two automatically constructed graphs generated using different extraction methods, alongside a high-quality reference graph curated by experts that serves as an upper performance bound. This design enables controlled comparison of construction methods and systematic evaluation of GNN robustness through semi-supervised node classification. We further provide a standardized, reproducible, and extensible evaluation framework, facilitating the integration of new graph extraction methods and learning models.

</details>


### [62] [GRALIS: A Unified Canonical Framework for Linear Attribution Methods via Riesz Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05480)
*Raimondo Fanale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRALIS，一个统一的数学框架，为深度神经网络的可解释性归因方法建立了表示理论，首次实现了SHAP、IG、LIME等方法的形式化统一，并满足了13.5/14条公理化属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主要XAI归因方法（如GradCAM、SHAP、LIME、Integrated Gradients）基于不同的理论基础，缺乏形式化的可比性。不同方法之间难以统一评估，且单个方法无法同时满足完整性、敏感性、局部性等多重理论保证。因此，亟需一个统一的数学框架来建立归因方法的表示理论，实现方法间的形式化比较和理论保证。

Method: 提出了GRALIS（Gradient-Riesz Averaged Locally-Integrated Shapley）数学框架。核心理论基于Riesz表示定理，证明了在L^2(Q,μ)空间上，每个加性、线性、连续的归因泛函都存在唯一的规范表示(Q, w, Δ)。该框架包含七个形式化定理：(T1)必要的规范形式、(T2)精确完整性、(T3)蒙特卡罗收敛O(1/√m)+O(1/k)、(T4)精确Shapley交互值、(T5)Hoeffding ANOVA分解、(T6)Sobol敏感性泛化、(T7)具有最小方差权重的多尺度扩展(MS-GRALIS)。通过Mobius变换建立GRALIS-SIV对应关系。该方法类包含SHAP、IG、LIME和线性化GradCAM，但排除非线性泛函如标准GradCAM或注意力图。

Result: 理论层面：GRALIS满足13.5/14条公理化属性，显著优于现有方法（SHAP、LIME等仅满足2.5-6/14条），同时实现了完整性、敏感性、局部性、k阶交互和最优多尺度聚合。实验层面：在BreaKHis数据集（1,187张组织学图像，DenseNet-121模型）上进行初步验证，结果显示恶性类别的删除忠诚度AUC提升0.015，类别条件一致性达96%，SAL=0.762±0.109，稀疏性指数为0.39。

Conclusion: GRALIS首次建立了一个统一的数学理论框架，实现了主要XAI归因方法的形式化统一和理论保证，证明了其在公理化属性上的显著优势。初步实验验证了其在医学图像分析中的有效性和可靠性，为归因方法的理论研究和实际应用提供了坚实基础。与基线XAI方法的扩展比较将在后续论文中详述。

Abstract: The main XAI attribution methods for deep neural networks -- GradCAM, SHAP, LIME, Integrated Gradients -- operate on separate theoretical foundations and are not formally comparable. We present GRALIS (Gradient-Riesz Averaged Locally-Integrated Shapley), a mathematical framework establishing a representation theory for attributions: every additive, linear, and continuous attribution functional on L^2(Q,mu) admits a unique canonical representation (Q, w, Delta), proved necessary by the Riesz Representation Theorem. This class encompasses SHAP, IG, LIME and linearized GradCAM, but excludes nonlinear functionals such as standard GradCAM or attention maps. Seven formal theorems provide simultaneous guarantees absent in any individual method: (T1) necessary canonical form; (T2) exact completeness; (T3) Monte Carlo convergence O(1/sqrt(m))+O(1/k); (T4) exact Shapley Interaction Values; (T5) Hoeffding ANOVA decomposition; (T6) Sobol sensitivity generalization; (T7) multi-scale extension (MS-GRALIS) with minimum-variance weights. An algebraic appendix justifies the GRALIS-SIV correspondence via the Mobius transform without circularity. GRALIS satisfies 13.5/14 axiomatic properties vs. 2.5-6/14 for individual methods, including completeness, sensitivity, locality, order-k interactions and optimal multi-scale aggregation simultaneously. Preliminary validation on BreaKHis (1,187 histology images, DenseNet-121) reports deletion faithfulness AUC +0.015 (malignant), 96% class-conditional consistency, SAL = 0.762+/-0.109 and sparsity index 0.39. Extended comparison with baseline XAI methods is planned for a companion paper.

</details>


### [63] [Approximate Next Policy Sampling: Replacing Conservative Target Policy Updates in Deep RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05481)
*Dillon Sandhu,Ronald Parr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了近似下一策略采样（ANPS）方法，通过调整训练数据分布而非限制策略更新来解决强化学习中的“鸡生蛋，蛋生鸡”问题，并基于此实现了稳定价值近似策略迭代（SV-API）及其PPO变体（SV-PPO），在保持安全性的同时实现了更大的策略更新步长和更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中存在一个经典的“鸡生蛋，蛋生鸡”问题：为了安全地改进策略，价值函数需要在更新后策略的状态访问分布上准确，但该分布未知且无法采样。传统的保守更新方法通过缩小策略更新步长来解决此问题，但代价是降低了学习效率。本文旨在探索一种替代方案，通过修改训练分布而非约束策略更新来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出了近似下一策略采样（ANPS）方法，要求训练数据分布近似下一策略的分布。基于此，设计了稳定价值近似策略迭代（SV-API）算法：在近似策略迭代循环中保持目标策略固定，使用迭代更新的行为策略收集相关经验，仅在满足收敛条件时才提交新策略。将该框架应用于PPO得到稳定价值PPO（SV-PPO）算法。

Result: SV-PPO在高维离散（Atari）和连续控制基准测试中匹配或超越原始PPO的性能，同时能够执行更大的目标策略更新。实验证明当满足特定稳定性标准时，更新保证安全；否则，安全性不低于标准近似策略迭代。

Conclusion: ANPS作为解决强化学习经典挑战的新方案具有可行性，通过修改训练分布而非限制策略更新，能够在保证安全性的前提下实现更大的策略更新步长和更好的性能，为强化学习算法设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: We revisit a classic "chicken-and-egg" problem in reinforcement learning: to safely improve a policy, the value function must be accurate on the state-visitation distribution of the updated policy. That distribution over states is unknown and cannot be sampled for the purposes of training the value function. Conservative updates solve this problem, but at the cost of shrinking the policy update. This paper explores an alternative solution, Approximate Next Policy Sampling (ANPS), which addresses the problem by modifying the training distribution rather than constraining the policy update. ANPS is satisfied if the distribution of the training data approximates that of the next policy. To demonstrate the feasibility and efficacy of ANPS, we introduce Stable Value Approximate Policy Iteration (SV-API). SV-API modifies the standard approximate policy iteration loop to hold the target policy fixed while an iteratively updated behavioral policy gathers relevant experience. It only commits to a new policy once a convergence criterion has been met. If certain stability criteria are met, the update is guaranteed to be safe; otherwise, it remains no less safe than standard approximate policy iteration. Applying SV-API to PPO yields Stable Value PPO (SV-PPO), which matches or improves performance on high-dimensional discrete (Atari) and continuous control benchmarks while executing substantially larger target policy updates. These results demonstrate the viability of ANPS as a new solution to this classic challenge in RL.

</details>


### [64] [A Robust Foundation Model for Conservation Laws: Injecting Context into Flux Neural Operators via Recurrent Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05488)
*Taeyoung Kim,Joon-Hyuk Ko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超网络架构的方法，通过引入ViT上下文注入机制增强Flux Neural Operator，使其能够在未知控制方程或PDE系数的情况下推断并求解守恒定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标准神经算子方法在处理守恒系统时可能缺乏鲁棒性和泛化能力，且通常需要已知控制方程或PDE系数。为了实现在未知方程情况下对守恒定律的推断和求解，需要一种能够从有限时间窗口中提取解动态并生成上下文条件神经算子的方法。

Method: 该方法将模型构建为超网络架构：首先从有限时间窗口中提取解动态，使用循环Vision Transformer (ViT)对其进行编码，然后生成上下文条件神经算子的参数。该架构将经典有限体积法(FVM)与神经算子相结合，实现了基于上下文的动态参数生成。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在保持Flux NO相对于标准神经算子的鲁棒性、泛化能力和长时间预测优势的同时，能够在广泛的守恒系统范围内（包括未见过的通量情况）提供可靠的数值解。模型成功在无需显式访问控制方程或PDE系数的情况下推断和求解守恒定律。

Conclusion: 所提出的架构成功实现了在未知控制方程条件下的守恒定律求解，兼具数值方法的可靠性和神经算子的学习能力，为处理未知物理系统的建模和预测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We propose an architecture that augments the Flux Neural Operator (Flux NO), which combines the classical finite volume method (FVM) with neural operators, with ViT-based context injection. Our model is formulated as a hypernetwork: it extracts solution dynamics over a finite temporal window, encodes them with a recurrent Vision Transformer, and generates the parameters of a context-conditioned neural operator. This enables the model to infer and solve conservation laws without explicit access to the governing equation or PDE coefficients. Experimentally, we show that the proposed method preserves the robustness, generalization ability, and long-time prediction advantages of Flux NO over standard neural operators, while delivering reliable numerical solutions across a broad range of conservative systems, including previously unseen fluxes. Our code is available at https://github.com/xx257xx/CONTEXT_FLUX_NO.

</details>


### [65] [MEMOA: Massive Mixtures of Online Agents via Mean-Field Decentralized Nash Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05492)
*Xuwei Yang,David B. Emerson,Fatemeh Tavakoli,Anastasis Kratsios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模联邦学习的可扩展性问题，提出了一种基于平均场（mean-field）的去中心化策略，通过最小最大化准则优化最弱智能体性能，证明了该策略在大群体极限下收敛于纳什最优集中策略，实验验证了其优于贪婪基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模人工智能时代，联邦学习面临计算和通信成本随智能体数量增加而无法有效扩展的挑战，亟需一种既能保持性能又能降低计算与通信开销的去中心化解决方案。

Method: 提出一种去中心化智能体策略，每个智能体仅依赖自身状态和系统平均场（mean-field）进行自主决策；采用最弱客户端/最小最大化准则刻画最优性，最小化群体中表现最差智能体的在线遗憾；引入在线加权机制优化服务器端混合预测，同时改善平均预测和最弱客户端预测性能。

Result: 推导出封闭形式的唯一最优去中心化策略；证明该策略在大群体极限下渐近收敛于纳什最优集中策略；数值实验验证了理论保证，表明该策略通常优于自然的贪婪去中心化基线方法。

Conclusion: 本研究成功解决了联邦学习在大规模场景下的可扩展性难题，所提出的去中心化策略不仅具有理论最优性保证，且在实际性能上超越传统方法，为大规模AI系统的分布式训练提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: In the modern age of large-scale AI, federated learning has become an increasingly important tool for training large populations of AI agents; however, its computational and communication costs can rapidly fail to scale with the number of agents. This is precisely where decentralized agentic strategies shine: each agent acts autonomously, using only its own state together with a minimal summary of the ensemble, namely the mean-field. We derive the unique optimal decentralized policy in closed form. Optimality is characterized through a worst-client/minimax criterion: minimizing the under-performer regret, namely the maximal online cost incurred by the weakest agent in the ensemble. We further prove that the resulting decentralized policy asymptotically converges, in the large-population limit, to the Nash-optimal centralized policy, whose direct computation is not scalable. We use an online weighting mechanism to optimize the server-computed mixture of client predictions, thereby improving the mean prediction in addition to the previously optimized weakest-client prediction. Numerical experiments verify our theoretical guarantees and demonstrate that our decentralized policy typically outperforms natural greedy decentralized baselines.

</details>


### [66] [Shortcut Solutions Learned by Transformers Impair Continual Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05495)
*William T. Redman,Erik C. Johnson,Brian Robinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将LEGO框架扩展至持续学习场景，对比发现循环结构的ALBERT模型比前馈结构的BERT模型表现更优：ALBERT学习到了类似For循环的解决方案，具有更好的持续学习能力和前向迁移能力，而BERT则陷入了限制泛化的捷径解。不过，在需要跨经验组合的任务中，两类模型均告失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别和利用跨领域的共同特征是人类类比推理和持续学习能力的关键。尽管近期对Transformer模型的组合推理能力研究较多，但关于其如何利用表征来学习新相关经验的系统性研究尚存空白。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究不同Transformer架构在持续学习场景下的能力差异。

Method: 将此前开发的"学习等式和群运算"（LEGO）框架扩展至持续学习设置（称为"continual LEGO"）。在此实验范式下，系统性对比研究前馈Transformer模型（以BERT为代表）和循环Transformer模型（以ALBERT为代表）的持续学习表现。

Result: 1. BERT学习的是捷径解（shortcut solutions），限制了其泛化能力，阻碍了向前迁移；2. ALBERT学习到了类似For循环的解决方案，表现出更好的持续学习性能；3. 在需要跨经验组合的持续学习设置中，两种模型都失败了；4. 通过结合跨经验数据的训练策略可以挽救ALBERT的性能下降，但对BERT无效，因为初始训练使其有害的捷径解变得根深蒂固。

Conclusion: 循环结构的ALBERT模型具有更适合持续学习的归纳偏置，架构与计算解决方案之间的相互作用值得未来进一步研究。研究结果表明Transformer架构的选择对持续学习能力有重要影响，为开发更适合持续学习的现代模型提供了启示。

Abstract: Identifying and exploiting common features across domains is at the heart of the human ability to make analogies, and is believed to be crucial for the ability to continually learn. To do this successfully, general and flexible computational strategies must be developed. While the extent to which Transformer neural network models can perform compositional reasoning has been the subject of intensive recent investigation, little work has been done to systematically understand how well these models can leverage their representations to learn new, related experiences. To address this gap, we expand the previously developed Learning Equality and Group Operations (LEGO) framework to a continual learning (CL) setting ("continual LEGO"). Using this continual LEGO experimental paradigm, we study the capability of feedforward and recurrent Transformer models to perform CL. We find that BERT, a canonical feedforward Transformer model, learns shortcut solutions that limits its ability to generalize and prevents strong forward transfer to new experiences. In contrast, we find evidence supporting the hypothesis that ALBERT, a recurrent version of BERT, learns a For loop-esque solution, which leads to better CL performance. When applying BERT and ALBERT models to a CL setting that requires composition across experiences, we find that both model families fail. Our investigation suggests that ALBERT models can have their performance drop rescued by use of training strategies that combine data across experiences, but this is not true for BERT models, where a detrimental shortcut solution becomes entrenched with initial training. Our results demonstrate that the recurrent ALBERT model may have an inductive bias better suited for CL and motivate future investigation of the interplay between Transformer architecture and computational solutions that emerge in modern models and tasks.

</details>


### [67] [Online Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05497)
*Yuheng Lai,Garvesh Raskutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OLCP及OLCP-Hedge算法，通过结合在线适应性与协变量依赖的局部化方法，解决了在线学习和时间序列场景下传统保形预测因数据不可交换性而失效的问题，实现了更高效的异质性不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的保形预测要求数据满足可交换性，但在线学习和时间序列场景下该假设不成立。现有的在线保形方法（如自适应保形推断ACI）虽能实现长期有效性，但在协变量异质性下因依赖全局校准而效率低下。

Method: 提出在线局部化保形预测（OLCP），将在线适应性与协变量依赖的局部化相结合；进一步提出OLCP-Hedge算法，利用受约束在线凸优化框架将带宽选择转化为在线专家聚合问题，以降低对局部化带宽的敏感性。

Result: 为两种算法提供了覆盖保证；通过仿真和真实数据实验证明，所提方法在保持长期有效覆盖的同时，能产生比现有基线更窄的预测集。

Conclusion: OLCP和OLCP-Hedge成功解决了在线环境下协变量异质性导致的预测效率问题，在保证统计有效性的前提下显著提升了预测精度，为非平稳场景下的不确定性量化提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is a framework that provides valid uncertainty quantification for general models with exchangeable data. However, in the online learning and time-series settings, exchangeability is not satisfied. Existing online conformal methods, such as adaptive conformal inference (ACI), can achieve long-run validity, yet they remain inefficient under covariate heterogeneity because they rely on global calibration. We propose \emph{Online Localized Conformal Prediction (OLCP)}, which combines online adaptation with covariate-dependent localization to better reflect heterogeneity. To reduce sensitivity to the localization bandwidth, we further develop \emph{OLCP-Hedge}, which performs bandwidth selection as an online expert aggregation problem using a constrained online convex optimization framework. Importantly, we provide coverage guarantees for both algorithms and demonstrate through simulations and real-data experiments that the proposed methods attain valid long-run coverage with narrower prediction sets than existing baselines.

</details>


### [68] [Non-Myopic Active Feature Acquisition via Pathwise Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05511)
*Linus Aronsson,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了NM-PPG方法，通过连续松弛和直通式展开方案解决主动特征获取中的高方差和训练部署不一致问题，性能优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主动特征获取（AFA）问题中，特征获取成本高昂，学习器需要自适应地决定获取哪些特征值以及何时停止获取并进行预测。现有方法通常采用分数函数策略梯度，存在高方差问题，且训练与部署阶段存在不一致性。

Method: 将AFA建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），引入获取过程的连续松弛，使得路径梯度能够通过完整的获取轨迹进行传播，避免了标准分数函数策略梯度的高方差问题。提出直通式展开方案，在前向传播中遵循硬特征获取，在反向传播中通过相应的软松弛进行反向传播。使用熵正则化和分阶段温度锐化来稳定优化过程。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，NM-PPG方法在性能上优于当前最先进的AFA基线方法。

Conclusion: NM-PPG成功地将非近视获取策略与端到端优化相结合，通过连续松弛和直通式展开方案有效解决了方差和训练部署不一致问题，为主动特征获取提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Active feature acquisition (AFA) considers prediction problems in which features are costly to obtain and the learner adaptively decides which feature values to acquire for each instance and when to stop and predict. AFA can be formulated as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), which naturally admits a sequential decision-making perspective. In this paper, we present non-myopic pathwise policy gradients (NM-PPG), a new AFA method built around this formulation. We introduce a continuous relaxation of the acquisition process that enables pathwise gradients through the full acquisition trajectory, avoiding the high variance of standard score-function policy gradients while allowing end-to-end optimization of a non-myopic acquisition policy. To better align training with deployment, we further develop a straight-through rollout scheme that follows hard feature acquisitions in the forward pass while backpropagating through the corresponding soft relaxation in the backward pass. We stabilize optimization with entropy regularization and staged temperature sharpening. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that NM-PPG yields superior performance relative to state-of-the-art AFA baselines.

</details>


### [69] [Bayesian Rain Field Reconstruction using Commercial Microwave Links and Diffusion Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05520)
*Badr Moufad,Albina Ilina,Hai Victor Habi,Salem Lahlou,Yazid Janati,Hagit Messer,Eric Moulines*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用扩散模型作为空间先验的贝叶斯反演框架，用于解决商用微波链路（CMLs）降雨场重建问题，无需特定训练即可实现高保真度的地面降雨场重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用微波链路（CMLs）虽然具有密集的空间覆盖能力，但其测量值为路径积分信号，导致准确的地面降雨场重建面临挑战。现有方法通常将CMLs过度简化为点传感器，忽略了信号衰减与降雨之间的线积分关系，在非均匀降水条件下性能显著下降。

Method: 将降雨场重建建模为一个以扩散模型（Diffusion Models）作为高保真空间先验的贝叶斯反演问题。该方法证明了扩散模型相比截断高斯过程能更好地保留关键降雨统计数据。通过该框架，可利用即插即用（Plug-and-Play）、序列蒙特卡洛（Sequential Monte Carlo）和副本交换（Replica Exchange）等多种方法实现无需训练的后验采样。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比现有的基于CML的重建基线方法取得了持续的改进，能够更准确地重建非均匀降水场。

Conclusion: 将扩散模型引入贝叶斯反演框架为CML降雨感知提供了一种有效的新范式，不仅解决了路径积分测量的固有难题，还通过免训练的后验采样方法实现了性能提升，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Commercial Microwave Links (CMLs) offer dense spatial coverage for rainfall sensing but produce path-integrated measurements that make accurate ground-level reconstruction challenging. Existing methods typically oversimplify CMLs as point sensors and neglect line integration relating rainfall to signal attenuation, resulting in degraded performance under heterogeneous precipitation. In this work, we view rain field reconstruction as a Bayesian inverse problem with Diffusion Models (DMs) as high-fidelity spatial priors. We show that diffusion models better preserve key rainfall statistics compared to censored Gaussian processes. Framing rainfall estimation as a Bayesian inverse problem with a DM prior enables training-free posterior sampling using a broad family of methods, including Plug-and-Play, Sequential Monte Carlo, and Replica Exchange methods. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over established CML-based reconstruction baselines.

</details>


### [70] [MOSAIC: Module Discovery via Sparse Additive Identifiable Causal Learning for Scientific Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05524)
*Shicheng Fan,Nour Elhendawy,Jianle Sun,Ke Fang,Kun Zhang,Yihang Wang,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOSAIC模型，一种稀疏时序VAE，通过支持恢复将观测变量的可解释性迁移至可识别的潜变量空间，实现了科学时间序列中潜变量模块的发现与解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果表征学习(CRL)虽能提供潜变量的可识别性保证，但并不保证可解释性，潜变量语义通常需事后通过已知真值因子对齐来赋予；而在科学时间序列中，底层机制未知，发现可解释结构是主要目标。由于科学观测（如残基距离、气候指数）本身具有语义（对应命名的物理量），作者希望将观测变量的可解释性转移至可识别的潜空间中。

Method: 提出MOSAIC（Module discovery via Sparse Additive Identifiable Causal learning），一种稀疏时序变分自编码器(VAE)。该方法整合了时序CRL的可识别性与观测变量上的支持恢复：通过机制调节的时序变化识别潜变量，利用加性解码器为每个潜变量恢复一组稀疏关联的观测变量，从而实现模块级可解释性。理论上证明了在一般光滑混合函数下，ANOVA主效应支持是可识别的，并为可处理的稀疏加性变体提供了有限样本恢复保证。

Result: 在RNA分子动力学、太阳风、ENSO气候、Tennessee Eastman过程及合成托卡马克基准等多个真实与合成科学时间序列数据集上，MOSAIC成功恢复了与领域知识一致的变量分组，验证了其发现潜变量模块的能力。理论层面证明了ANOVA主效应支持在一般光滑混合函数下的可识别性，并提供了有限样本恢复保证。

Conclusion: MOSAIC有效弥合了因果表征学习中可识别性与可解释性之间的鸿沟，通过将观测变量的语义传递给潜变量，实现了科学时间序列中可解释的潜机制发现，为科学探索提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Causal representation learning (CRL) seeks to recover latent variables with identifiability guarantees, typically up to permutation and component-wise reparameterization under appropriate assumptions. However, identifiability does not imply interpretability: latent semantics are typically assigned post hoc by alignment with known ground-truth factors. This limitation is particularly acute in scientific time series, where underlying mechanisms are unknown and discovering interpretable structure is a primary goal. In contrast, scientific observations (such as residue-pair distances, climate indices, or process sensors) are inherently semantic, as they correspond to named physical quantities. This raises a key question: can the interpretability of observations be transferred to the identifiable latent space? We propose MOSAIC (Module discovery via Sparse Additive Identifiable Causal learning), a sparse temporal VAE that integrates temporal CRL identifiability with support recovery over observed variables. MOSAIC identifies latent variables via regime-conditioned temporal variation, and recovers for each latent a sparse set of associated observations through an additive decoder, yielding module-level interpretability. We show that ANOVA main-effect supports are identifiable under general smooth mixing functions, and provide finite-sample recovery guarantees for a tractable sparse-additive variant. Empirically, MOSAIC recovers domain-consistent variable groups across RNA molecular dynamics, solar wind, ENSO climate, the Tennessee Eastman process, and a synthetic tokamak benchmark, enabling interpretable discovery of latent mechanisms in scientific time series.

</details>


### [71] [Energy Generative Modeling: A Lyapunov-based Energy Matching Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05530)
*Yixuan Wang,Wenqian Xue,Warren E. Dixon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将基于静态标量能量函数的生成模型中的训练和采样统一为Wasserstein空间上的密度传输问题（非线性控制问题），提供了Langevin采样的有限步停止准则，证明了确定性梯度流不存在Lyapunov证书，并展示了训练能量的加性组合保持Gibbs不变测度和闭环Lyapunov证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的静态标量能量生成模型通常将训练和采样视为独立的过程，缺乏统一的理论框架。作者旨在通过非线性控制理论的视角，将这两个阶段统一在同一个动力学框架下，从而引入更丰富的理论工具（如Lyapunov函数、Barrier函数等）来分析和改进生成模型。

Method: 作者将生成建模问题重新表述为Wasserstein空间上的密度传输问题，并将其建模为非线性控制问题，其中KL散度作为Lyapunov函数。训练和采样被视为此主动力学的两个实例，仅初始条件不同。基于此框架，作者利用非线性控制理论工具进行了理论分析。

Result: 作者证明了两个解析结果：首先，由于Lyapunov证书是渐近的，作者推导出了Langevin采样的有限步停止准则，并证明了在同一能量景观上的确定性梯度流不存在Lyapunov证书。其次，作者证明了训练标量能量的加性组合仍然保持显式的Gibbs不变测度，并继承闭环Lyapunov证书。合成分布上的实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将静态标量能量生成模型与非线性控制理论工具箱联系起来，不仅提供了对现有模型的深入理解（如停止准则），还为未来的研究开辟了新方向，如利用Barrier函数进行约束生成和利用收缩度量加速采样。

Abstract: Generative models based on static scalar energy functions represent an emerging paradigm in which a single time independent potential drives sample generation through its gradient field, eliminating the need for time conditioning entirely. We unify the training and sampling phases of this paradigm, conventionally treated as separate procedures, within a single framework: density transport on the Wasserstein space, cast as a nonlinear control problem in which the Kullback Leibler (KL) divergence serves as a Lyapunov function. Training and sampling are then two instances of this same master dynamics, differing only in initial condition. Within this autonomous framework we develop two analytic results. First, since the Lyapunov certificate is asymptotic, we derive a finite step stopping criterion for Langevin sampling and prove that no Lyapunov certificate exists for the deterministic gradient flow on the same energy landscape. Second, the reformulation brings the toolkit of nonlinear control theory to bear on static scalar energy generative modeling, that is, we show that additive composition of trained scalar energies retains an explicit Gibbs invariant measure and inherits the closed-loop Lyapunov certificate. Beyond these immediate results, this reformulation bridges static scalar energy generative models with the full toolkit of nonlinear control theory, opening the door to barrier functions for constrained generation and contraction metrics for accelerated sampling. Experiments on synthetic distributions validate the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [72] [Adversarial Graph Neural Network Benchmarks: Towards Practical and Fair Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05534)
*Tran Gia Bao Ngo,Zulfikar Alom,Federico Errica,Murat Kantarcioglu,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora*

Main category: cs.LG
