TL;DR: 本文对GNN对抗攻击与防御方法进行了大规模基准测试，发现实验设置的不一致性导致结论模糊甚至矛盾。通过45.3万次实验证明，目标节点选择和模型训练过程等被忽视因素严重影响攻击效果评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GNN对抗学习领域缺乏公平、稳健和标准化的评估协议，不同研究采用不同的实验设置，导致研究结果模糊且可能相互矛盾，阻碍了对GNN鲁棒性的真实理解。

Method: 构建统一框架，对6个流行图数据集上的7种攻击方法和8种防御方法进行全面再评估，涵盖投毒和规避两种场景，总计进行超过45.3万次实验。

Result: 在公平稳健的评估程序下，对抗攻击性能表现出显著差异；先前被忽视的因素（如目标节点选择和被攻击模型的训练过程）对攻击效果产生深远影响，甚至完全扭曲性能认知。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在对抗图机器学习中采用标准化评估协议的紧迫性，呼吁社区建立公平、稳健的实验标准。

Abstract: Adversarial learning and the robustness of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are topics of widespread interest in the machine learning community, as documented by the number of adversarial attacks and defenses designed for these purposes. While a rigorous evaluation of these adversarial methods is necessary to understand the robustness of GNNs in real-world applications, we posit that many works in the literature do not share the same experimental settings, leading to ambiguous and potentially contradictory scientific conclusions. In this benchmark, we demonstrate the importance of adopting fair, robust, and standardized evaluation protocols in adversarial GNN research. We perform a comprehensive re-evaluation of seven widely used attacks and eight recent defenses under both poisoning and evasion scenarios, across six popular graph datasets. Our study spans over 453,000 experiments conducted within a unified framework. We observe substantial differences in adversarial attack performance when evaluated under a fair and robust procedure. Our findings reveal that previously overlooked factors, such as target node selection and the training process of the attacked model, have a profound impact on attack effectiveness, to the extent of completely distorting performance insights. These results underscore the urgent need for standardized evaluations in adversarial graph machine learning.

</details>


### [73] [Towards Scalable One-Step Generative Modeling for Autoregressive Dynamical System Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05540)
*Tianyue Yang,Xiao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MeLISA是一种无隐空间的像素空间自回归生成代理模型，通过单次模型评估生成预报块，结合窗口一致性目标与时间增量一致性损失，在保持推理效率的同时，显著提升了短期预报精度和长期统计特性保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子自回归模型在湍流区域容易发生漂移，而扩散模型和隐空间生成代理虽能表示随机转移，却需要多步去噪、精细设计噪声调度或辅助压缩模型。研究旨在构建一种既具备高效推理能力，又能准确保持长期轨迹统计结构的代理模型。

Method: 提出MeanFlow长期不变时空一致性自回归模型（MeLISA），定义分块随机转移核，每次推理仅需单次模型评估。引入窗口一致性MeanFlow目标，从部分观测时间窗口学习条件时空生成；结合时间增量一致性损失，约束多滞后有限增量以捕获时间相关结构。采用紧凑UNet与可扩展DiT作为骨干网络。

Result: 在256×256扩展二维Kolmogorov流和192×192湍流通道流切片基准上，MeLISA在短期预报精度与长期统计指标（能谱、湍流动能、混合率相关动力学）上均优于神经算子基线，推理速度可比甚至更快。紧凑3.7-5.7M参数版本参数效率优异，DiT版本可扩展至150M参数。

Conclusion: MeLISA成功兼顾推理效率与长期统计准确性，为高维物理动力学提供了一个无需隐空间编码器和迭代扩散求解器的高效生成代理建模方案。

Abstract: Fast surrogate modeling for high-dimensional physical dynamics requires more than low short-term error: useful models must roll out efficiently while preserving the statistical structure of long trajectories. Neural operators provide inexpensive autoregressive forecasts but can drift in turbulent regimes, whereas rolling diffusion and latent generative surrogates can represent stochastic transitions at the cost of multi-step denoising, noise-schedule design, or auxiliary compression models. We propose MeanFlow Long-term Invariant Spatiotemporal Consistency Autoregressive Models (MeLISA), a latent-free autoregressive generative surrogate built on pixel-space MeanFlow. MeLISA defines a blockwise stochastic transition kernel that generates each forecast block with a single model evaluation, avoiding latent encoders and iterative diffusion solvers at inference time. To stabilize long-horizon rollouts, MeLISA combines a Window-Consistency MeanFlow objective that learns conditional spatiotemporal generation from partially observed temporal windows with a Time Increment Consistency loss that constrains multi-lag finite increments and targets temporal-correlation structure. We evaluate MeLISA with compact UNet and scalable DiT backbones on two high-resolution benchmarks, extended 2D Kolmogorov flow at $256 \times 256$ and turbulent channel-flow slice at $192 \times 192$. MeLISA outperforms neural-operator baselines on short-term forecasting accuracy and long-horizon statistical metrics, including energy spectra, turbulent kinetic energy, and mixing-rate-related dynamics, while achieving inference speeds comparable to, and in some cases faster than, neural operators. Compact 3.7-5.7M-parameter variants already deliver strong parameter efficiency, and DiT variants provide a scalable path up to 150M parameters. Overall, MeLISA benefits both rollout efficiency and long-horizon statistical accuracy.

</details>


### [74] [Adaptive Q-Chunking for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05544)
*Nandiraju Gireesh,Yuanliang Ju,He Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应Q-Chunking (AQC)方法，通过相对优势比较和折扣因子归一化解决动作块大小选择问题，在离线与在线RL任务及大规模VLA模型中取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多步离线到在线RL方法使用固定块大小，但不同场景需要不同粒度：接触事件需要短块实现反应控制，自由空间运动则需要长块优化信用分配，固定策略无法兼顾。

Method: 训练多个块大小的批评器，通过相对于各视界基线的优势值进行选择，并采用折扣因子归一化。该准则在无真实信号时将错误答案转化为无偏的近随机选择，在存在优势尺度时具有判别力。

Result: 理论上证明了优势选择器的抗噪性界限及自适应分块相对于任何固定块大小的价值优势。在OGBench和Robomimic上达到SOTA离线与在线成功率，应用于大规模VLA模型显著提升RoboCasa-GR1任务性能。

Conclusion: AQC成功解决了折扣尺度不匹配导致的块大小选择崩溃问题，实现了状态条件下的自适应动作块大小选择，为离线到在线RL及序列动作预测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning with action chunking eliminates multi-step off-policy bias and enables temporally coherent exploration, but all existing methods use a fixed chunk size across every state. This is suboptimal: near contact events the agent needs short chunks for reactive control, while during free-space motion long chunks provide better credit assignment. The natural solution is to train critics for several chunk sizes and select the best one at each state, but naive comparison of learned critic values systematically collapses to the shortest chunk due to discount-scale mismatch, and degrades to noise in low-value states. We propose Adaptive Q-Chunking (AQC), which resolves both failures by comparing the advantage of each chunk size relative to a per-horizon baseline, normalized by the discount factor. This criterion converts biased wrong answers into unbiased near-random choices when no genuine signal exists, and becomes discriminative when a particular scale enables better planning. We prove theoretical bounds on the advantage selector's noise immunity and on the value dominance of adaptive chunking over any fixed chunk size. We demonstrate that AQC achieves state-of-the-art offline and online success rates on OGBench and Robomimic, and can be applied to enhance the performance of large-scale VLA models that predict action sequences, significantly boosting performance on RoboCasa-GR1 tasks.

</details>


### [75] [FedeKD: Energy-Based Gating for Robust Federated Knowledge Distillation under Heterogeneous Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05553)
*Quang-Huy Nguyen,Jiaqi Wang,Wei-shinn Ku*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedeKD框架，通过能量门控机制实现样本级信任估计，在无需公共数据集的情况下有效缓解联邦学习中的负迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在异构环境中常因数据分布差异和非对称模型设计导致负迁移；现有联邦知识蒸馏方法依赖公共数据集或假设知识可靠性均一，限制了其实际鲁棒性。

Method: 提出FedeKD框架：每个客户端维护高容量私有模型进行本地学习，以及轻量级共享代理模型进行跨客户端知识交换；服务器聚合代理模型形成全局代理指导私有模型更新；核心是能量门控机制，将任务特定的私有-代理模型分歧转换为样本级信任权重，实现样本级的知识传递加权。

Result: 在六个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，FedeKD在异构环境下显著减少负迁移，同时保持强预测性能。

Conclusion: FedeKD通过样本级信任估计和能量门控机制，在不依赖公共数据的前提下有效提升了联邦知识蒸馏在异构环境中的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) operates in heterogeneous environments, where variations in data distributions and asymmetric model design often result in negative transfer. While federated knowledge distillation (FKD) avoids direct model parameter sharing, existing methods typically rely on public datasets or assume that transferred knowledge is uniformly reliable, which limits their robustness in practice. This paper presents FedeKD, a reliability-aware FKD framework that makes sample-wise trust estimation an explicit component of knowledge transfer, without relying on additional public data. Each client maintains a high-capacity private model for local learning and a lightweight shared proxy model for cross-client knowledge exchange. During training, proxy models are aggregated on the server to form a global proxy, which is then used to guide updates of the private models. At the core of FedeKD is an energy-based gating mechanism that converts task-specific private-proxy disagreement into sample-wise trust weights for backward distillation. This mechanism enables sample-wise weighting of knowledge transfer, where the proxy model contributes more to reliable samples while down-weighting unreliable ones. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that FedeKD significantly reduces negative transfer under heterogeneous settings while maintaining strong predictive performance.

</details>


### [76] [Accelerating LMO-Based Optimization via Implicit Gradient Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05577)
*Won-Jun Jang,Si-Hyeon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LMO-IGT方法，利用隐式梯度传输在保持单次梯度计算开销的前提下，将随机LMO优化器的收敛复杂度从O(ε^{-4})提升至O(ε^{-3.5})，其中Muon-IGT表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于线性最小化预言机（LMO）的优化器（如Lion和Muon）虽然表现优异，但方差缩减方法会带来额外的梯度计算开销；同时，LMO方法在无约束和约束形式下的理论理解分散，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出LMO-IGT方法，通过在传输点评估随机梯度来利用隐式梯度传输（IGT）；引入统一的随机LMO优化框架及新的平稳性度量——正则化支撑函数（RSF），将梯度范数和Frank-Wolfe间隙统一起来。

Result: 理论分析表明：标准随机LMO复杂度为O(ε^{-4})，方差缩减LMO为O(ε^{-3})但需额外梯度计算，而LMO-IGT仅需单次随机梯度即可达到O(ε^{-3.5})；实验中LMO-IGT在几乎无额外开销下持续优于随机LMO，其中Muon-IGT性能最优。

Conclusion: 隐式梯度传输为现代LMO优化提供了一种有效且实用的加速机制，在保持低计算开销的同时显著提升了收敛速度。

Abstract: Recent optimizers such as Lion and Muon have demonstrated strong empirical performance by normalizing gradient momentum via linear minimization oracles (LMOs). While variance reduction has been explored to accelerate LMO-based methods, it typically incurs substantial computational overhead due to additional gradient evaluations. At the same time, the theoretical understanding of LMO-based methods remains fragmented across unconstrained and constrained formulations. Motivated by these limitations, we propose \emph{LMO-IGT}, a new class of stochastic LMO-based methods leveraging implicit gradient transport (IGT). We further introduce a unified framework for stochastic LMO-based optimization together with a new stationarity measure, the \emph{regularized support function} (RSF), which bridges gradient-norm and Frank--Wolfe-gap notions within a common framework. By evaluating stochastic gradients at transported points, LMO-IGT accelerates convergence while retaining the single-gradient-per-iteration structure of standard stochastic LMO. Our analysis establishes that stochastic LMO achieves an iteration complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-4})$, variance-reduced LMO achieves $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3})$ at the cost of additional gradient evaluations, and LMO-IGT achieves $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3.5})$ using only a single stochastic gradient per iteration. Empirically, LMO-IGT consistently improves over stochastic LMO counterparts with negligible overhead. Among its instantiations, Muon-IGT achieves the strongest overall performance across evaluated settings, demonstrating that IGT provides an effective and practical acceleration mechanism for modern LMO-based optimization.

</details>


### [77] [AeroJEPA: Learning Semantic Latent Representations for Scalable 3D Aerodynamic Field Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05586)
*Francisco Giral,Abhijeet Vishwasrao,Andrea Arroyo Ramo,Mahmoud Golestanian,Federica Tonti,Adrian Lozano-Duran,Steven L. Brunton,Sergio Hoyas,Hector Gomez,Soledad Le Clainche,Ricardo Vinuesa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AeroJEPA，一种基于联合嵌入预测架构的气动代理模型，解决了现有方法在高分辨率3D流场上的扩展性差和潜表示缺乏物理意义的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的气动代理模型面临两大瓶颈：一是难以扩展到现实3D气动问题中极大规模的流场数据；二是学习到的潜表示难以直接用于分析和设计优化。

Method: 提出AeroJEPA框架，不直接预测完整流场，而是预测流场的潜表示。该方法从几何和工况的上下文潜表示预测目标潜表示，并通过连续隐式解码器可选地重建流场，从而将潜预测与空间分辨率解耦，并引导潜空间按语义组织。

Result: 在HiLiftAeroML（高保真附面层大流场）和SuperWing（跨声速机翼族大规模泛化）两个数据集上验证。结果表明：作为连续代理模型具有竞争力，能自然扩展到高分辨率输出，且学习到的潜表示编码了未直接监督的几何和气动量。潜空间支持受控插值、线性探测、概念向量运算及约束设计优化实验。

Conclusion: 预测式潜学习是实现可扩展且具有设计意义的气动代理建模的有前景方向。

Abstract: Aerodynamic surrogate models are increasingly used to replace repeated high-fidelity CFD evaluations in many-query design settings, but current approaches still face two important limitations: they often scale poorly to the very large fields arising in realistic 3D aerodynamics, and they rarely produce latent representations that are directly useful for analysis and design. We introduce AeroJEPA, a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for aerodynamic field modeling that addresses both issues. Rather than predicting the full flow field directly from geometry, AeroJEPA predicts a target latent representation of the flow from a context latent representation of the geometry and operating conditions, and optionally reconstructs the field through a continuous implicit decoder. This formulation decouples latent prediction from field resolution while encouraging the latent space to organize semantically. We evaluate AeroJEPA on two complementary datasets: HiLiftAeroML, which stresses the method in a high-fidelity regime with extremely large boundary-layer fields, and SuperWing, which tests large-scale generalization and latent-space optimization over a broad family of transonic wings. Across these benchmarks, AeroJEPA is competitive as a continuous surrogate for aerodynamic fields, scales naturally to high-resolution outputs, and learns context and predicted latents that encode geometry and aerodynamic quantities not used directly as supervision. We further show that the resulting latent space supports controlled interpolation, linear probing, concept-vector arithmetic, and a constrained design latent-optimization experiment. These results suggest that predictive latent learning is a promising direction for scalable and design-meaningful aerodynamic surrogate modeling.

</details>


### [78] [When Can Voting Help, Hurt, or Change Course? Exact Structure of Binary Test-Time Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05592)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推翻了多数投票改进预测器性能的单调性传统认知，引入“带符号投票签名”概念，证明了完整的奇数预算投票曲线与该签名等价，为理解和分析投票行为的各种现象提供了统一的数学框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典固定能力理论认为多数投票对随机预测器的改进是单调的：当准确率高于多数阈值时投票有益，低于时有害。作者发现该结论根本性地不完整，旨在揭示在更一般的可交换性假设下，投票行为存在复杂的非单调现象，需要建立新的理论框架来准确刻画投票的真实规律。

Method: 采用de Finetti表示定理，将可交换的重复正确性建模为由潜在正确率分布决定的随机过程。引入核心概念“带符号投票签名”（signed voting signature），即在二项分布方差尺度上记录高于/低于多数阈值的潜在质量差。理论证明了完整奇数预算投票曲线的增量即为符号Hausdorff矩，建立曲线与签名的一一对应关系。

Result: 证明了完整奇数预算投票曲线与带符号投票签名等价；发现简单潜在混合分布即可产生非单调投票曲线，甚至在构造实例中产生无限次趋势翻转；揭示了签名可确定曲线，但曲线无法唯一确定潜在分布定律（分支对称性不可识别性）；区分了两种估计机制：直接成功概率信息可恢复完整签名，而固定深度标签仅能恢复有限前缀。

Conclusion: 多数投票的表现由潜在正确率分布的特定结构决定而非简单的准确性指标。所提出的签名理论统一解释了投票曲线的形状现象、不可识别性、可实现性和端点变化率等关键性质，为理解和优化黑盒预测器的投票干预提供了精确的数学基础。

Abstract: Majority voting is one of the few black-box interventions that can improve a fixed stochastic predictor: repeated access can be cheaper than changing a high-capability model. Classical fixed-competence theory makes this intervention look monotone -- more votes help above the majority threshold and hurt below it. We show that this picture is fundamentally incomplete. Under the de Finetti representation for exchangeable repeated correctness, voting is governed by a latent distribution of per-example correctness probabilities. Even simple latent mixtures can generate sharply different voting curves, including nonmonotone behavior and, in an explicit construction, infinitely many trend changes. The full latent law determines the curve, but the curve does not determine the law. The exact object recovered by voting is a signed voting signature: at each binomial variance scale, it records excess latent mass above rather than below the majority threshold. Our main theorem proves that the complete odd-budget curve and this signature are equivalent: the curve increments are signed Hausdorff moments, and the full curve recovers the signature uniquely. This viewpoint explains shape phenomena, branch-symmetric nonidentifiability, realizability, variation, and endpoint rates. It also separates estimation regimes: direct per-example success-probability information targets the full signature, whereas fixed-depth grouped labels reveal only a finite prefix.

</details>


### [79] [Optimal Contextual Pricing under Agnostic Non-Lipschitz Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05609)
*Jianyu Xu,Yu-Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing算法，解决了非Lipschitz需求曲线下的上下文动态定价问题，将遗憾上界从$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$改进至最优的$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平滑需求定价算法依赖的跨上下文插值论证在面对非Lipschitz需求曲线（含任意跳跃和原子点）时失效，先前最佳方法仅能达到$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$遗憾，与已知下界存在差距。

Method: 提出Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing算法，该多项式时间算法结合了三个核心机制：随机化参数估计、保守残差网格探测（conservative residual-grid probing），以及基于置信度的单步重定向（confidence-based one-step redirection）。

Result: 算法达到$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$遗憾上界，在随机良好条件上下文场景下，该结果匹配Kleinberg和Leighton (2003)提出的下界（仅差对数因子），改进了Xu和Wang (2022)的先前上界。

Conclusion: 在具有不可知非Lipschitz噪声分布的线性估值上下文定价问题中，本研究填补了长期存在的遗憾差距，实现了理论最优结果。

Abstract: We study contextual dynamic pricing with linear valuations and bounded-support agnostic noise, whose induced demand curve may be non-Lipschitz with arbitrary jumps and atoms. Such discontinuities break the cross-context interpolation arguments used by smooth-demand pricing algorithms, while the best previous method achieved only $\tilde O(T^{3/4})$ regret. We propose Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing, a polynomial-time algorithm that combines randomized parameter estimation, conservative residual-grid probing, and confidence-based one-step redirection. Our algorithm achieves $\tilde O(T^{2/3})$ optimal regret, matching the known lower bounds of Kleinberg and Leighton (2003) up to logarithmic factors and improving over the previous upper bound of Xu and Wang (2022). Under stochastic well-conditioned contexts, this closes the long-existing open regret gap in linear-valuation contextual pricing under agnostic non-Lipschitz noise distribution.

</details>


### [80] [LLMSpace: Carbon Footprint Modeling for Large Language Model Inference on LEO Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05615)
*Lei Jiang,Adrian Ildefonso,Daniel Loveless,Fan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLMSpace，首个针对低地球轨道（LEO）卫星上大语言模型推理的碳排放建模框架，揭示了空间LLM推理在碳足迹、延迟、硬件设计与寿命之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）的大规模推理导致能源需求激增，引发能源与碳排放危机。虽然有研究提出利用太阳能驱动的LEO卫星分担地面算力以缓解能耗，但卫星制造、发射排放及抗辐射硬件需求使其全生命周期碳足迹尚不明确，亟需量化分析框架。

Method: 构建LLMSpace框架，联合建模运行碳排放与隐含碳排放，涵盖卫星外围子系统、抗辐射加速器与存储器，并引入LLM特有的负载特征（如prefill-decode阶段与token生成），基于真实的卫星与GPU配置进行系统级碳建模。

Result: 利用LLMSpace对实际卫星与GPU配置建模，揭示了空间LLM推理在碳排放、推理延迟、硬件设计参数与卫星运行寿命之间的关键权衡关系，为可持续空间计算提供了定量依据。

Conclusion: LLMSpace为评估和优化AI赋能卫星上LLM推理的环境影响提供了首个系统化工具，支持在碳足迹与性能间做出更明智的设计决策，推动可持续的空间计算发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) impose rapidly growing energy demands, creating an emerging energy and carbon crisis driven by large-scale inference. Solar-powered, AI-enabled low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites have been proposed to mitigate terrestrial electricity consumption, but their lifecycle carbon footprint remains poorly understood due to launch emissions, satellite manufacturing, and radiation-hardened hardware requirements. This paper presents \textit{LLMSpace}, the first carbon modeling framework for LLM inference on AI-enabled LEO satellites. LLMSpace jointly models operational and embodied carbon, peripheral subsystems, radiation-hardened accelerators and memories, and LLM-specific workload characteristics such as prefill-decode behavior and token generation. Using realistic satellite and GPU configurations, LLMSpace reveals key trade-offs among carbon footprint, inference latency, hardware design, and operational lifetime for sustainable space-based LLM inference. Source code: https://github.com/UnchartedRLab/LLMSpace.

</details>


### [81] [Region-adaptable retrieval of coastal biogeochemical parameters from near-surface hyperspectral remote sensing reflectance using physics-aware meta-learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05623)
*Yiqing Guo,Nagur R. C. Cherukuru,Eric A. Lehmann,S. L. Kesav Unnithan,Tim J. Malthus,Gemma Kerrisk,Xiubin Qi,Faisal Islam,Tisham Dhar,Mark J. Doubell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段物理感知元学习框架，用于从近表面遥感反射率观测中反演海岸水生生物地球化学参数，在澳大利亚五个地理差异显著的站点验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱原位传感在反演水生生物地球化学参数方面具有潜力，但由于环境条件和生物地球化学的区域差异导致BGC范围和生物光学特性不同，遥感反射率与BGC参数之间的关系在不同水体间变化显著，使得跨水体的反演算法泛化仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出两阶段物理感知元学习框架：第一阶段利用生物光学前向模型，基于具有澳大利亚海岸水体广泛代表性的原位生物光学光谱库生成大型合成数据集，用于预训练区域无关的元学习基础模型以学习基本物理关系；第二阶段使用本地样本对预训练基础模型进行特定区域微调。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）BGC参数及其对应的高光谱Rrs特征在实验站点间呈现明显区域差异；（2）合成数据集在参数分布和参数间相关性方面具有物理合理性并与真实样本高度一致；（3）所提方法在BGC反演中优于五个基准模型；（4）原位测量与模型预测的BGC参数时间序列在幅度和时间动态上均显示出良好一致性。

Conclusion: 该两阶段物理感知元学习框架通过结合物理先验知识迁移学习，有效解决了海岸水体BGC参数反演的区域泛化问题，为跨区域水质监测提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Hyperspectral in situ sensing has shown promise in retrieving aquatic biogeochemical (BGC) parameters, such as total suspended solids, dissolved organic carbon, and total chlorophyll-a, for cost-effective monitoring of coastal water quality. However, generalising such retrieval algorithms across water bodies remains challenging, as the relationship between remote sensing reflectance (Rrs) and BGC parameters can vary considerably from one region to another due to regional distinctions in environmental conditions and biogeochemistry that lead to different BGC ranges and bio-optical properties. In this study, we propose a two-stage physics-aware meta-learning framework for retrieving coastal BGC parameters from near-surface Rrs observations. In the first stage, a bio-optical forward model is used to generate a large synthetic dataset based on an in situ bio-optical spectral library with broad representativeness of Australian coastal waters. This dataset is then used to pretrain a region-agnostic base model with meta-learning, allowing the model to learn fundamental physical relationships. In the second stage, the pretrained base model is fine-tuned for specific regions with local samples. We collected in situ hyperspectral Rrs and BGC measurements from five geographically distinct sites in Australian coastal waters. Our experimental results suggest: (1) the BGC parameters and their corresponding hyperspectral Rrs signatures exhibited clear regional distinctions among the experimental sites; (2) the synthetic dataset was physically plausible and closely aligned with real-world samples in both parameter distributions and inter-parameter correlations; (3) the proposed approach outperformed five benchmark models in BGC retrieval; and (4) time series of in situ measured and model-predicted BGC parameters showed good agreement in both magnitude and temporal dynamics.

</details>


### [82] [Scaling Pretrained Representations Enables Label-Free Out-of-Distribution Detection Without Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05638)
*Brett Barkley,Preston Culbertson,David Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现代预训练模型的冻结表示已具备足够的几何结构，可直接用于无标签的分布外（OOD）检测，无需专门微调；随着骨干网络规模增大，不同检测器的性能差距消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在输入偏离训练分布时无法可靠地发出信号，且传统观点认为有效的OOD检测通常需要类别条件建模或通过监督微调获得专用模型。本文旨在验证现代预训练模型的冻结表示是否已具备足够的几何结构来实现准确的无标签OOD检测。

Method: 在涵盖视觉和语言的59个骨干网络-任务配对上，比较两种互补的无标签检测器：基于未标记潜在表示拟合的全局Mahalanobis估计器，以及在相同特征上操作的轻量级、基于扩散的局部典型性估计器ReSCOPED。通过表示缩放实验分析检测性能变化。

Result: 尽管检测机制不同，两种检测器表现出一致的依赖规模的模式：随着表示质量的提升，局部和全局检测器的绝对性能均有所提高；随着骨干网络规模的增大，两类检测器在视觉和语言任务上的性能差距消失。这表明无标签OOD检测强烈依赖于冻结预训练骨干网络所暴露的几何结构。

Conclusion: 无标签OOD检测主要取决于冻结预训练骨干网络所呈现的几何特性；随着骨干网络规模增大，检测器选择的重要性降低，可直接在冻结模型上高效部署OOD检测，无需专门的微调或专用模型。

Abstract: Models trained with deep learning often fail to signal when inputs fall outside their training data manifold, leading to unreliable predictions under distribution shift. Prior work suggests that effective out-of-distribution (OOD) detection often requires class-conditional modeling or specialized models obtained through supervised fine-tuning. We revisit this assumption in modern pretrained models and show that their frozen representations already encode sufficient geometric structure for accurate label-free OOD detection. Across 59 backbone-task pairings spanning vision and language, we compare two complementary label-free detectors: a global Mahalanobis estimator fit on unlabeled latent representations, and ReSCOPED, a lightweight, diffusion-based typicality estimator operating on the same features at a local level. Despite their different detection mechanisms, representation scaling reveals a consistent regime-dependent pattern: both local and global detectors' absolute performance improves with better representation quality, and performance gaps between the two detectors disappear across both language and vision tasks as representations scale. These results suggest that label-free OOD detection depends strongly on the geometry exposed by frozen pretrained backbones, reducing the importance of detector choice as backbone scale increases and enabling efficient deployment directly on frozen models.

</details>


### [83] [Information-Preserving Domain Transfer with Unlabeled Data in Misspecified Simulation-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05652)
*Joon Jang,Eunho Jeong,Kyu Sung Choi,Hyeonjin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SPIN框架，通过保留参数相关信息的域迁移方法解决仿真推断（SBI）在模型错误设定下的可靠性问题，利用未标注真实数据提升后验推断的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的仿真推断（SBI）方法在模型错误设定（即真实数据与仿真器生成数据不匹配）情况下可靠性会下降。现有使用未标注真实数据的方法通常只对齐边缘分布，但这并不能直接保留后验推断所需的参数相关信息。

Method: 提出SPIN框架，利用未标注、未配对的真实观测数据进行域迁移。在训练阶段，通过将仿真观测数据转换到真实域再转换回仿真域，利用原始仿真标签来鼓励保留参数相关的互信息。在测试阶段，学习到的真实到仿真映射将真实观测转换到仿真域进行后验推断。

Result: 在受控合成实验和物理真实世界基准测试中，SPIN提升了真实世界的后验推断性能。随着模型错误设定程度的增加，SPIN的改进效果更加明显。

Conclusion: SPIN成功解决了SBI在模型错误设定下的挑战，通过保留参数相关信息的域迁移机制，在无需真实参数标签或配对观测数据的情况下，显著提升了真实世界场景中的后验推断质量。

Abstract: Simulation-based inference (SBI) provides amortized Bayesian parameter inference from simulator-generated data without requiring explicit likelihood evaluation. Its reliability can degrade under model misspecification, where real-world observations are not well represented by the simulator used for training. Existing methods using unlabeled real-world data often align simulated and real-world data distributions, but marginal alignment alone does not directly preserve parameter-relevant information needed for posterior inference. We propose SPIN, an SBI framework with parameter-relevant information-preserving domain transfer using unlabeled, unpaired real-world observations. During training, SPIN translates labeled simulator observations toward the real-world domain and back to the simulator domain, using the original simulator labels to encourage domain transfer that preserves parameter-relevant mutual information. At test time, the learned real-to-simulator transport maps real-world observations into the simulator domain for posterior inference, without requiring real-world parameter labels or paired real--simulator observations. Across controlled synthetic and physical real-world benchmarks, SPIN improves real-world posterior inference, with the improvement becoming clearer as misspecification increases.

</details>


### [84] [Structural Correspondence and Universal Approximation in Diagonal plus Low-Rank Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05659)
*Ying Chen,Aoxi Li,Jihun Kim,Javad Lavaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DLoR（对角加低秩）的结构，仅通过在低秩层中增加一个极简的稀疏对角分量，就能在保持参数高效的同时实现通用近似能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习架构为降低计算成本广泛采用低秩结构（如LoRA），但其理论表达能力保障研究不足，现有理论通常依赖于预训练基矩阵、ReLU激活函数或不可验证的奇异性条件等严格假设。本文旨在探索无预训练先验下低秩神经网络的表达极限与突破方法。

Method: 首先证明纯秩1层虽能精确插值任意标量数据集但在函数逼近时会崩溃。随后引入统一的结构对应框架，提出对角加低秩（DLoR）结构，通过加性分解（权衡网络宽度）或乘性分解（权衡网络深度）来精确重构任意满秩变换，并利用渐近泰勒余项追踪技术进行理论证明。

Result: 证明了DLoR神经网络对一般激活函数能够完全恢复通用近似定理，并发现乘性深度在参数-表达性扩展方面优于加性宽度，证明了稠密矩阵和特定激活函数并非通用表达性的拓扑前提。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过最小化地增加稀疏对角分量即可克服低秩流形的表达瓶颈，无需牺牲参数效率，也无需依赖稠密矩阵或特定激活函数，为参数高效的神经网络设计提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: The massive computational costs of scaling modern deep learning architectures have driven the widespread use of parameter-efficient low-rank structures, such as LoRA and low-rank factorization. However, theoretical guarantees for their expressive power are less explored, often relying on restrictive priors like a pretrained base matrix, ReLU activations or non-verifiable singularity conditions. We first investigate the limits of neural networks constrained strictly to low-rank manifolds without pretrained dense priors. We demonstrate a theoretical paradox: while purely rank-1 layers can exactly interpolate arbitrary scalar datasets, they collapse for function approximations. To overcome this bottleneck without surrendering parameter efficiency, we introduce a unified \textit{Structural Correspondence} framework. We prove that augmenting low-rank layers with only a minimal sparse diagonal component, say a Diagonal plus Low-Rank (DLoR) structure, is sufficient to reach Universal Approximation. We show that any full-rank transformation can be exactly reconstructed using these DLoR components by trading off network width (additive decomposition) or depth (multiplicative decomposition). By tracking asymptotic Taylor remainders, we prove that DLoR neural networks fully restore the Universal Approximation Theorem for general activation functions. Finally, we establish that multiplicative depth provides superior parameter-to-expressivity scaling compared to additive width. Our results show that dense matrices and specific activation functions are not topological prerequisites for universal expressivity.

</details>


### [85] [Distributionally Robust Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05660)
*Yufeng Yang,Fangning Zhuo,Ziyi Chen,Heng Huang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了分布鲁棒多目标优化框架（DR-MOO），并设计了两种多梯度下降算法，将样本复杂度从$\mathcal{O}(ε^{-12})$显著提升至$\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$，有效解决了数据分布偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多目标优化（MOO）方法未显式考虑数据中的分布偏移问题。在实际应用需要多准则学习的场景下，数据分布的变化会影响模型的鲁棒性和泛化性能，因此需要一种能够处理最坏情况分布的多目标优化方法。

Method: 1. 提出分布鲁棒多目标优化（DR-MOO）框架，最小化各目标在最坏情况分布下的损失；2. 定义DR-MOO的Pareto型解概念；3. 基于拉格朗日对偶重构，首先设计双循环多梯度下降算法（MGDA），内循环估计对偶变量；4. 引入梯度裁剪技术处理广义平滑和有偏梯度估计，消除双重采样需求，提出单循环双裁剪MGDA算法。

Result: 1. 双循环MGDA达到ε-Pareto平稳点的总样本复杂度为$\mathcal{O}(ε^{-12})$；2. 单循环双裁剪MGDA将样本复杂度显著提升至$\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$；3. 理论适用于非凸设置，且不需要目标函数或梯度有界的假设；4. 实验表明该方法与最先进的MGDA基准方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 本文成功将分布鲁棒优化引入多目标优化领域，提出了DR-MOO框架及配套算法。通过理论创新和算法设计，在保证理论严谨性的同时大幅提升了计算效率，为处理存在分布偏移的多目标学习问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) has received growing attention in applications that require learning under multiple criteria. However, the existing MOO formulations do not explicitly account for distributional shifts in the data. We introduce distributionally robust multi-objective optimization (DR-MOO), which minimizes multiple objectives under their respective worst-case distributions. We propose Pareto-type solution concepts for DR-MOO and develop multi-gradient descent algorithms (MGDA) with provable guarantees. Leveraging a Lagrangian dual reformulation, we first design a double-loop MGDA that uses an inner loop to estimate dual variables and achieves a total sample complexity $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-12})$ for reaching an $ε$-Pareto-stationary point. To further improve efficiency, we incorporate gradient clipping to handle generalized-smooth and biased gradient estimates, removing the need for double sampling. This yields a single-loop double-clip MGDA with substantially improved sample complexity $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$. Our theory applies to the nonconvex setting and does not require bounded objectives or gradients. Experiments demonstrate that our methods are competitive with state-of-the-art MGDA baselines.

</details>


### [86] [Temporal Functional Circuits: From Spline Plots to Faithful Explanations in KAN Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05685)
*Naveen Mysore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了时间功能电路框架，通过门控残差KAN将可学习的边函数转化为可解释的时间序列预测解释，在保持竞争力的预测性能同时提供了MLP无法提供的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN网络虽然在每条连接上具有显式的可学习边函数，具备机制化解释的潜力，但这些边函数仍处于潜在可视化状态，缺乏对时间序列预测的忠实、基于时间的解释方法。

Method: 提出门控残差KAN架构，将预测分解为线性基和稀疏激活的KAN修正；通过输出感知归因将每条边映射到输入滞后；根据学习到的激活范围对边进行排序；通过边级干预（置零和样条移除）验证忠实度。

Result: 在复杂度递增的四个合成机制中，门控随信号复杂度增加而逐渐打开；在机制切换信号上，门控KAN的MSE比纯线性模型低59%；在八个基准测试中，门控架构与线性、注意力和MLP方法性能相当，同时提供可解释的边函数。

Conclusion: 移除学习的B样条组件而保留基SiLU项会降低预测性能，证明样条形状本身携带预测价值；该框架成功将KAN边函数转化为忠实的时间解释，在可解释性和预测性能之间取得了有效平衡。

Abstract: Unlike MLPs, Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) expose explicit learnable edge functions on every connection, enabling mechanistic explanation in time-series forecasting. This paper introduces Temporal Functional Circuits, a framework that transforms KAN edge functions from latent visualizations into faithful, temporally grounded explanations. Built on a gated residual KAN that decomposes forecasts into a linear base and a sparsely activated KAN correction, the framework (i) maps each edge to input lags via output-aware attribution, (ii) ranks edges by learned activation range, and (iii) validates faithfulness through edge-level interventions including zeroing and spline removal. Removing the learned B-spline component while retaining the base SiLU term degrades forecasts, providing evidence that the spline shape itself carries predictive value beyond the base activation. On four synthetic regimes of increasing complexity, the learned gate opens progressively wider as signal complexity grows. On regime-switching signals, gated KAN achieves 59% lower MSE than linear-only models. Across eight benchmarks, the gated architecture is competitive with linear, attention, and MLP alternatives, while providing interpretable edge functions that MLP-based corrections cannot offer.

</details>


### [87] [Budgeted Attention Allocation: Cost-Conditioned Compute Control for Efficient Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05697)
*Amrit Nidhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Budgeted Attention Allocation的单调头门控机制，使单个Transformer模型能够根据注意力预算灵活调整推理成本与质量，在多个数据集上实现了可测量的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer模型通常在训练后只暴露一个推理成本，而实际部署的系统往往需要多个成本-质量的操作点来满足不同场景的需求。

Method: 提出了Budgeted Attention Allocation机制，这是一种基于请求注意力预算调节的单调头门控机制。采用密集热启动保证稳定性，并通过硬门控适应将软成本控制转换为实测的单线程CPU速度，还结合了预算化结构剪枝技术。

Result: 在合成序列任务上，单个预算化模型在0.303注意力成本时达到99.7%准确率，在0.504成本时达到100%准确率。在AG News上，BERT-Mini预算化门控在预算0.50时达到87.6%准确率和1.20倍加速。在DBpedia14上，预算0.50时达到97.4%准确率，优于密集全注意力的96.6%。

Conclusion: 贡献不在于普遍优势，而是提供了一个可重现的可行性研究：展示了如何通过单个可控检查点在不同预算间权衡注意力成本与准确率，并能转换为小规模CPU基准上的实测结构加速。

Abstract: Transformers usually expose one inference cost per trained model, while deployed systems often need multiple cost-quality operating points. We study Budgeted Attention Allocation, a monotone head-gating mechanism conditioned on a requested attention budget. Dense warm-starting is important for stability: on a robust synthetic sequence task, one budgeted model reaches 99.7% accuracy at 0.303 estimated attention cost and 100.0% accuracy at 0.504 cost. On held-out AG News with a custom word-level transformer, hard-gate adaptation turns soft cost control into measured single-thread CPU speed, reaching 82.1% accuracy with 1.28x speedup at budget 0.50. In pretrained BERT-Mini AG News, budgeted structural pruning reaches 87.6% accuracy with 1.20x speedup at budget 0.50; a validation-ranked zero-shot dense post-hoc structural baseline reaches 86.1%, and one recovery epoch raises that per-budget specialist to 87.9%. On DBpedia14, BERT-Mini budgeted gates reach 97.4% at exact budget 0.50 versus 96.6% for dense full attention. Static fixed-budget gates and recovered dense specialists remain strong. The contribution is therefore not universal dominance, but a reproducible feasibility study of one controllable checkpoint across budgets that can trade attention cost for accuracy and be converted into measured structural speedups on small CPU benchmarks.

</details>


### [88] [On the Blessing of Pre-training in Weak-to-Strong Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05710)
*Wei Yao,Wang Zhaoyang,Gengze Xu,Chen Qian,Dongrui Liu,Ziqiao Wang,Yong Liu,Yunbei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明预训练是弱到强泛化（W2SG）现象出现的必要前提，揭示了W2SG能力通过相变机制随预训练进程涌现的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有W2SG研究表明强模型可以超越弱监督者，但预训练在其中的决定性作用在理论和实证方面均未被充分探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在高维单指数模型框架下形式化W2SG问题，使用尖峰高斯数据建模，将预训练形式化为谱初始化步骤；通过理论分析证明预训练提供的几何热启动将模型置于具有扰动强凸几何特性的"有效区域"；使用合成仿真和大语言模型数百个中间预训练检查点进行大规模评估验证。

Result: 理论证明了在随机初始化下学习失败的前提下，W2SG在预训练提供的有效区域内是可实现的；推导出了严格的一般化界，刻画了初始性能提升后因弱监督者偏差而出现饱和瓶颈的优化动态；实证验证了所有理论假设，并证明W2SG不是固有能力，而是通过相变随预训练进程涌现。

Conclusion: 预训练是W2SG现象出现的本质前提，为理解强模型如何超越弱监督者提供了理论基础，同时为实际应用中预训练策略的设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: The paradigm of Weak-to-Strong Generalization (W2SG) suggests that a pre-trained strong model can surpass its weak supervisor, yet the decisive role of pre-training remains theoretically and empirically under-explored. In this work, we identify pre-training as the essential prerequisite for the emergence of W2SG. Theoretically, we formalize the W2SG problem within a high-dimensional single-index model framework using spiked Gaussian data, modeling pre-training as a spectral initialization step. Building upon prior impossibility results regarding the failure of learning under random initialization, we prove that W2SG is achievable when pre-training provides a geometric warm start that places the model within an "effective region" characterized by a perturbed strong-convexity geometry. Within this region, we derive a rigorous generalization bound that naturally captures the optimization dynamics: an initial performance improvement followed by a saturation bottleneck dictated by the weak supervisor's bias. Empirically, we first validate all our assumptions and theoretical insights through controlled synthetic simulations. Finally, through a massive-scale evaluation of hundreds of intermediate pre-training checkpoints from large language models, we demonstrate that W2SG is not an innate capability but emerges via a phase transition tightly coupled with the progression of pre-training.

</details>


### [89] [Enabling Federated Inference via Unsupervised Consensus Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05718)
*Yui Hashimoto,Takayuki Nishio,Yuichi Kitagawa,Takahito Tanimura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CE-FI框架，通过共识嵌入层和协作输出层实现了在保护隐私前提下的跨模型协作推理，无需共享模型参数或原始输入，仅使用无标签数据即可训练，在图像分类任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作推理框架通常需要共享输入数据、模型参数或公共编码器，这限制了其在隐私敏感或跨组织场景中的应用。需要在保护隐私的前提下实现独立部署模型间的协作推理。

Method: 提出Consensus Embedding-based Federated Inference (CE-FI)框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Consensus Embedding (CE)层，将异构中间表示映射到公共嵌入空间；(2) Cooperative Output (CO)层，基于嵌入生成预测。两个层仅使用共享的无标签数据训练，不假设公共编码器存在。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100图像分类基准上，在多种非独立同分布条件下，CE-FI持续优于独立推理，性能与需要更强共享假设的传统方法相当。在文本和时间序列任务上验证了框架的适用性，但性能依赖于集成策略。分析发现表示对齐是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: CE-FI成功实现了隐私保护下的跨模型协作推理，无需共享敏感信息或假设公共编码器，仅依赖无标签数据即可完成协作训练，在图像分类任务上取得显著成效，为分布式环境中的模型协作提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cooperative inference across independently deployed machine learning models is increasingly desirable in distributed environments, as there is a growing need to leverage multiple models while keeping their data and model parameters private. However, existing cooperative frameworks typically rely on sharing input data, model parameters, or a common encoder, which limits their applicability in privacy-sensitive or cross-organizational settings. To address this challenge, we propose Consensus Embedding-based Federated Inference (CE-FI), a framework that enables pretrained models to cooperate at inference time without sharing model parameters or raw inputs and without assuming a common encoder. CE-FI introduces two components: a Consensus Embedding (CE) layer that maps heterogeneous intermediate representations into a common embedding space, and a Cooperative Output (CO) layer that produces predictions from these embeddings. Both layers are trained using shared unlabeled data only, so the cooperative stage does not require additional labeled data. Experiments on image classification benchmarks -- CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 -- under diverse non-IID conditions show that CE-FI consistently outperforms solo inference and performs comparably to conventional methods that require stronger sharing assumptions. Additional evaluations on text and time-series tasks indicate applicability beyond image classification, although performance depends on the ensemble strategy. Further analysis identifies representation alignment as the primary bottleneck.

</details>


### [90] [WARP: A Benchmark for Primal-Dual Warm-Starting of Interior-Point Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05728)
*Dhruv Suri,Helgi Hilmarsson,Shourya Bose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有AC-OPF机器学习热启动方法评估基线的错误，证明了仅预测原始变量的方法无法减少迭代次数，并提出预测完整原始-对偶-障碍状态的方法可实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献声称机器学习预测原始变量热启动可减少30-46%迭代次数，但本文发现这些结果基于不恰当的评估基线。IPOPT求解器的默认启动点实际上是障碍函数中心性的近优解，而先前工作错误地与平启动点进行对比，因此需要纠正评估基线并重新审视热启动方法的有效性。

Method: 本文首先建立正确的评估基线，通过理论分析揭示内点法的几何性质：原始变量预测精度与收敛速度负相关，且仅提供原始最优解会导致求解器发散。通过Oracle实验验证完整的原始-对偶-障碍状态的加速效果。最后提出WARP方法：一种拓扑条件的编码-处理-解码交互网络，在异构约束图上预测完整的内点状态(x̂, λ̂, ẑ, μ̂)，并原生支持N-1 contingencies拓扑变化。

Result: 实验表明：在正确的基线下，仅预测原始变量的热启动方法无法减少求解器迭代次数；完整的原始-对偶-障碍状态(x*, λ*, z*, μ*)可将IPOPT迭代从23次减少到3次，实现85%的减少；WARP方法实现76%的迭代减少，且无需重新训练即可适应N-1 contingencies拓扑变化。

Conclusion: 现有仅预测原始变量的热启动方法存在根本性局限，必须预测完整的原始-对偶-障碍状态才能实现显著加速。本文发布的数据集、评估协议和WARP方法为该领域的严格评估建立了新标准，同时证明了拓扑感知方法在电力系统优化中的有效性。

Abstract: Solving AC Optimal Power Flow (AC-OPF) is of central importance in electricity market operations, where interior-point methods (IPMs) such as IPOPT are the standard solvers. A growing body of work uses machine learning to predict primal warm-start iterates, reporting iteration reductions of 30-46\%. We show that these reported gains rest on an inappropriate evaluation baseline: prior methods benchmark against the flat start $V_m = 1, V_a = 0$, whereas the solver's actual default - the variable-bound midpoint $(l+u)/2$ - is near-optimal for log-barrier centrality. Against this corrected baseline, no primal-only warm-start method reduces solver iterations. We trace the failure to a geometric property of interior-point methods: primal prediction accuracy is anticorrelated with convergence speed, and providing the ground-truth optimal solution $x^*$ without dual variables causes the solver to diverge. Oracle experiments establish that the complete primal-dual-barrier state $(x^*, λ^*, z^*, μ^*)$ reduces IPOPT iterations from 23 to 3 - an 85\% reduction that is structurally inaccessible to primal-only methods. To enable rigorous evaluation of warm-start methods on this task, we release a benchmark suite comprising dual-labeled AC-OPF datasets with IPOPT-extracted solutions, a corrected evaluation protocol, and WARP - a topology-conditioned encode-process-decode interaction network that predicts the full interior-point state $(\hat{x}, \hatλ, \hat{z}, \hatμ)$ on the heterogeneous constraint graph. WARP achieves a 76\% reduction in IPOPT iterations while natively accommodating N-1 contingency topology variations without retraining.

</details>


### [91] [CRAFT: Forgetting-Aware Intervention-Based Adaptation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05732)
*Md Anwar Hossen,Fatema Siddika,Juan Pablo Munoz,Tanya Roosta,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRAFT框架，通过在隐藏表示上学习低秩干预而非更新模型权重，利用统一的KL散度目标进行路由、正则化和合并，有效缓解了大语言模型持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过微调获取新能力时，持续适应往往导致灾难性遗忘，即在学习新任务时遗忘旧知识。现有的基于权重更新的方法难以平衡新旧任务性能。

Method: CRAFT框架包含三个阶段：(1)基于输出分布散度将任务路由至相似任务组；(2)使用Kullback-Leibler (KL) 散度对模型进行微调，以该组先验状态为基准，直接控制遗忘并决定收敛；(3)利用相同的KL信号将更新任务的干预合并到共享表示中。该设计通过单一的KL目标统一了路由、正则化和合并过程，且不更新模型权重，仅学习隐藏表示上的低秩干预。

Result: 在多个基准测试和不同模型规模上，CRAFT相比强力的LoRA方法提升了整体性能，显著减少了遗忘，并且对任务顺序具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在表示空间控制适应过程并以输出空间散度为指导，为LLM的持续学习提供了一种可扩展且有原则的方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can acquire new capabilities through fine-tuning, but continual adaptation often leads to catastrophic forgetting. We propose CRAFT, a continual learning framework that avoids updating model weights by instead learning low-rank interventions on hidden representations. CRAFT proceeds in three stages: it first routes each task to a group of similar tasks based on output-distribution divergence; it then fine-tunes the model using a Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence against the group's prior state, which directly controls forgetting and determines convergence; finally, it merges interventions for the updated task into the shared representation using the same KL signal. This design unifies routing, regularization, and merging through a single KL-based objective. CRAFT improves overall performance and reduces forgetting compared to strong LoRA-based approaches across multiple benchmarks and model scales, while remaining robust to task ordering. These results suggest that controlling adaptation in representation space, guided by output-space divergence, provides a scalable and principled approach to continual learning in LLMs.

</details>


### [92] [CoMemNet: Contrastive Sampling with Memory Replay Network for Continual Traffic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05738)
*Mei Wu,Wenchao Weng,Wenxin Su,Wenjie Tang,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoMemNet，一种用于交通预测的双分支持续学习框架，通过动态对比采样器和轻量级时序记忆缓冲区解决了流式交通网络中图结构持续演化带来的灾难性遗忘问题，在三个大规模真实数据集上达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非欧几里得图时空建模方法大多依赖静态的底层图结构，无法有效捕获流式交通网络中持续扩展和演化的模式，存在灾难性遗忘和内存爆炸等问题。

Method: 提出双分支持续学习框架CoMemNet：1）快速收敛的在线分支负责主要预测任务；2）动量更新的目标分支利用Wasserstein距离特征创建动态对比采样器(DC Sampler)，选择具有显著动态网络特征变化的节点集进行训练；3）主干网络集成轻量级节点自适应时序记忆缓冲区(TMRB-N)，通过记忆回放巩固旧知识。

Result: CoMemNet在三个大规模真实世界数据集（包括两个新策划的开源数据集）上实现了最先进的预测性能。

Conclusion: CoMemNet有效解决了流式交通网络预测中的灾难性遗忘和内存爆炸问题，为动态演化的时空图学习提供了一个简单高效的持续学习解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, the integration of non-topological space modeling with temporal learning methods has emerged as an effective approach for capturing spatio-temporal information in non-Euclidean graphs. However, most existing methods rely on static underlying graph structures, which are inadequate for capturing the continuously expanding and evolving patterns in streaming traffic networks. To address this challenge, we propose a simple yet efficient dual-branch continual learning framework for traffic prediction, named CoMemNet. The fast-converging Online branch undertakes the primary prediction tasks, while the momentum-updated Target branch extracts historical information using Wasserstein Distance features to create a Dynamic Contrastive Sampler (DC Sampler). This sampler selects a node set with significant dynamic network feature changes for training, effectively mitigating the issue of catastrophic forgetting. Additionally, the backbone incorporates a lightweight Node-Adaptive Temporal Memory Buffer (TMRB-N) to consolidate old knowledge through memory replay and address the risk of memory explosion. Finally, we provide two newly curated open-source datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that CoMemNet achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across all three large-scale real-world datasets. The code is available at: https://github.com/meiwu5/CoMemNet.

</details>


### [93] [Multi-Dimensional Behavioral Evaluation of Agentic Stock Prediction Systems Using LLM Judges with Closed-Loop Reinforcement Learning Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05739)
*Mohammad Al Ridhawi,Mahtab Haj Ali,Hussein Al Osman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM评判的行为评估框架，通过细粒度的行为维度评分来诊断和优化智能体股票预测系统的决策过程，显著提升了预测准确性和风险调整收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体股票预测系统做出一系列相互依赖的决策（如机制检测、路径路由、强化学习控制），但这些单个决策的质量被MAPE或方向准确性等聚合指标所掩盖，缺乏对决策过程的细粒度评估手段。

Method: 提出了行为评估框架：1）在每个自主决策点记录行为轨迹，按五天周期分组；2）使用三个LLM评判者（GPT 5.4、Claude 4.6 Opus、Gemini 3.1 Pro）沿六个领域特定维度（机制检测、路由、适应性、风险校准、策略一致性、错误恢复）进行评分；3）通过扰动验证确保评分的维度特异性；4）将缺陷评分转换为信用分配惩罚项，加入到Soft Actor-Critic (SAC)奖励函数中进行微调。

Result: 在420个周期的扰动验证中，目标维度评分下降1.6-2.4分，非目标维度仅下降0.32分，模型间一致性达Krippendorff's α=0.85。行为评分与20天夏普比率相关系数ρ=0.72。在2017-2025测试期间，一日MAPE从0.61%降至0.54%（相对降低11.5%，p<0.001），方向准确性从71%提升至74%，夏普比率提升18%（95%置信区间[8.2%, 27.4%]），改进主要集中在高波动性周期。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了对智能体股票预测系统决策过程的细粒度行为评估，并通过针对性的奖励函数优化显著改善了系统性能，特别是在原系统表现不佳的高波动环境下效果突出。为智能体金融系统的评估和优化提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: Agentic stock prediction systems make sequences of interdependent decisions (regime detection, pathway routing, reinforcement learning control) whose individual quality is hidden by aggregate metrics such as mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) or directional accuracy. We present a behavioral evaluation framework that addresses this gap. Behavioral traces logged at every autonomous decision point are grouped into five-day episodes and scored along six domain-specific dimensions (regime detection, routing, adaptation, risk calibration, strategy coherence, error recovery) by an ensemble of three large language model (LLM) judges (GPT 5.4, Claude 4.6 Opus, Gemini 3.1 Pro). Perturbation-based validation on 420 episodes yields targeted score drops of $-1.6$ to $-2.4$ on intended dimensions versus an average of $-0.32$ on the remaining five, with cross-model agreement up to Krippendorff's $α= 0.85$. The composite behavioral score, used here only for cross-episode reporting, correlates at $ρ= 0.72$ with realized 20-day Sharpe ratio from offline backtesting. Closing the loop, the framework converts deficient per-dimension scores into a credit-assigned penalty term added to the Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) reward. Three short fine-tuning cycles, all confined to the validation period, produce on the held-out 2017-2025 test period a one-day MAPE reduction from 0.61% to 0.54% (an 11.5% relative reduction; $p<0.001$, Cohen's $d=0.31$), a directional accuracy increase from 71% to 74%, and an 18% Sharpe ratio improvement (95% bootstrap CI [8.2%, 27.4%]), with gains concentrated in high-volatility episodes where the original system was most behaviorally deficient. Results are from offline backtesting and do not address effects specific to live deployment.

</details>


### [94] [Weak-to-Strong Generalization is Nearly Inevitable (in Linear Models)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05742)
*Scott Geng,Dutch Hansen,Jerry Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了弱到强泛化现象在线性逻辑回归中几乎不可避免，且不依赖模型容量差异，挑战了现有理论认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，在使用弱教师反馈微调强学生模型时，学生模型不仅能超越教师，还能提升自身能力。现有研究主要集中在大语言模型，且普遍认为模型容量不匹配是该现象的核心机制。本文旨在探究这一现象是否存在于更基础的设定中，并验证模型容量差异是否为必要条件。

Method: 作者在标准线性逻辑回归设定下研究弱到强泛化现象。他们对数据分布做出温和假设，分析在仅使用弱教师反馈进行训练时，学生模型的泛化表现。关键在于，该设定中学生模型与教师模型具有相同的表达能力（线性模型），不存在模型容量差异。

Result: 证明了弱到强泛化现象在标准线性逻辑回归中确实发生，且在大多数学生-教师对中都存在这一现象。这表明弱到强泛化在该设定下几乎不可避免。更重要的是，该现象出现在学生和教师具有相同模型容量的情况下，证明模型容量不匹配并非弱到强泛化的必要条件。

Conclusion: 弱到强泛化现象比先前认为的更为普遍和基础，它不需要模型容量差异作为前提条件。这一发现挑战了当前理论界关于模型容量不匹配是弱到强泛化核心机制的主流观点，表明我们需要重新思考这一现象的本质机制，可能存在更基础的理论解释。

Abstract: Weak-to-strong generalization is a phenomenon in post-training whereby a strong student model, when finetuned solely with feedback from a weaker teacher, can not only surpass the teacher, but can improve upon its own capabilities. Recent work of Burns et al. (2023) demonstrated that this can occur in the setting of frontier language models, and subsequently there has been a flurry of both empirical work trying to exploit this phenomenon, as well as theoretical work attempting to understand it. In this work, we demonstrate that weak-to-strong generalization occurs in standard linear logistic regression, under mild distributional assumptions on the data. In fact, we show that this happens for most student-teacher pairs, suggesting that weak-to-strong generalization is in fact \emph{almost inevitable}, even in this basic setting. Notably, our setting does not require the student to be more expressive or have more model capacity in any way compared to the teacher, which runs contrary to the prevailing theoretical belief that a mismatch in model capacity is a central mechanism to weak-to-strong generalization.

</details>


### [95] [RVPO: Risk-Sensitive Alignment via Variance Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05750)
*Ivan Montero,Tomasz Jurczyk,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RVPO框架，通过惩罚奖励间方差解决多目标RLHF中的约束忽视问题，在医疗和科学推理任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无critic的RLHF方法使用算术平均聚合多目标奖励，存在约束忽视问题：某一目标的高分可以数值上抵消其他目标的关键失败（如安全性或格式），掩盖了对可靠多目标对齐至关重要的低性能"瓶颈"奖励。

Method: 提出Reward-Variance Policy Optimization (RVPO)风险敏感框架，在优势聚合过程中惩罚奖励间方差，将目标从"最大化总和"转变为"最大化一致性"。通过泰勒展开证明LogSumExp (SoftMin)算子可有效作为平滑方差惩罚项。

Result: 在基于评分标准的医学和科学推理任务上（最多17个LLM评判奖励信号，Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B）以及工具调用任务（基于规则的约束，Qwen2.5-1.5B/3B）上评估。RVPO在HealthBench上取得显著提升（14B模型：0.261 vs. GDPO的0.215，p < 0.001），在GPQA-Diamond上保持竞争性准确率且未出现其他多奖励方法的后期性能退化。

Conclusion: 方差正则化能够在不牺牲通用能力的情况下，缓解不同模型规模下的约束忽视问题，证明了奖励一致性优化在多目标对齐中的有效性。

Abstract: Current critic-less RLHF methods aggregate multi-objective rewards via an arithmetic mean, leaving them vulnerable to constraint neglect: high-magnitude success in one objective can numerically offset critical failures in others (e.g., safety or formatting), masking low-performing "bottleneck" rewards vital for reliable multi-objective alignment. We propose Reward-Variance Policy Optimization (RVPO), a risk-sensitive framework that penalizes inter-reward variance during advantage aggregation, shifting the objective from "maximize sum" to "maximize consistency." We show via Taylor expansion that a LogSumExp (SoftMin) operator effectively acts as a smooth variance penalty. We evaluate RVPO on rubric-based medical and scientific reasoning with up to 17 concurrent LLM-judged reward signals (Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B) and on tool-calling with rule-based constraints (Qwen2.5-1.5B/3B). By preventing the model from neglecting difficult constraints to exploit easier objectives, RVPO improves overall scores on HealthBench (0.261 vs. 0.215 for GDPO at 14B, $p < 0.001$) and maintains competitive accuracy on GPQA-Diamond without the late-stage degradation observed in other multi-reward methods, demonstrating that variance regularization mitigates constraint neglect across model scales without sacrificing general capabilities.

</details>


### [96] [Full-Spectrum Graph Neural Network: Expressive and Scalable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05759)
*Xiaohan Wang,Deyu Bo,Longlong Li,Kelin Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了全谱图神经网络（FSpecGNN），通过将信号提升到节点对域并使用双变量特征值对滤波器，突破了传统谱GNN的表达能力限制，实现了节点对信号的通用近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谱图神经网络虽然能够通用近似节点信号，但其表达能力受限于1维Weisfeiler-Lehman测试，无法实现对高阶信号的通用近似。为了突破这一理论界限，需要设计一种更具表达能力的谱图神经网络模型。

Method: 提出了FSpecGNN，这是经典谱GNN的二阶推广。该方法从两个维度推进了谱滤波：(1)将信号从节点域提升到节点对域；(2)将基于特征值的单变量谱滤波器扩展为基于特征值对的双变量滤波器。通过低秩近似将全谱卷积简化为多项式谱滤波器的组合，避免了显式的节点对级别计算，实现了可扩展的实现方式。

Result: 理论证明经典谱GNN是FSpecGNN的对角特例；FSpecGNN的表达能力上限为Local 2-GNN，同时能够通用近似节点对信号。实验验证了模型的表达能力，在异配性图基准测试中取得了优异性能。该方法特别适用于异配性图学习任务。

Conclusion: FSpecGNN成功突破了传统谱GNN的表达能力界限，在保持可扩展性的同时实现了对节点对信号的通用近似，为异配性图学习提供了有效的解决方案，在理论和实践层面都展现了重要价值。

Abstract: It is well established that spectral graph neural networks (GNNs) can universally approximate node signals; however, their expressive power remains bounded by the 1-dimensional Weisfeiler-Lehman test, which is mirrored in their lack of universality for higher-order signals. To go beyond this bound, we propose the Full-Spectrum GNN (FSpecGNN), a second-order generalization of classical spectral GNNs. FSpecGNN advances spectral filtering in two perspectives: (1) it lifts the signal from the node domain to the node-pair domain; and (2) it extends the univariate spectral filter over eigenvalues to a bivariate filter over eigenvalue pairs. We show that classical spectral GNNs arise as a diagonal special case of FSpecGNN, and prove that FSpecGNN can be at most as expressive as Local 2-GNN while universally approximating node-pair signals, the latter being particularly beneficial for heterophilic graph learning. Moreover, FSpecGNN admits scalable implementations that avoid explicit node-pair-level computations; combined with a low-rank approximation that reduces full-spectrum convolution to a combination of polynomial spectral filters, it enables learning on large graphs. Empirically, FSpecGNN validates the predicted expressivity and delivers strong performance on heterophilic benchmarks.

</details>


### [97] [Adaptive Selection of LoRA Components in Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05769)
*Myoungjun Kim,Sangwoo Park,Yoseob Han,Jin-Hyun Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AS-LoRA框架，通过层级自由度、轮次自适应性和曲率感知评分三维自适应策略，有效解决了差分隐私联邦学习中LoRA聚合误差问题，在多个基准数据集上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私联邦学习中，LoRA的乘积结构导致聚合误差，且DP噪声会进一步放大该误差，降低模型稳定性和准确性。现有方法对所有层和通信轮次采用统一的更新模式，忽略了LoRA两个因子间的结构不对称性和训练过程中的轮次动态变化。

Method: 提出AS-LoRA自适应框架，包含三个核心轴：（i）层级自由度——每层独立选择其活跃组件；（ii）轮次自适应性——选择策略随通信轮次动态更新；（iii）曲率感知评分——基于损失的二阶近似推导得出。该框架无额外隐私成本。

Result: 在GLUE、SQuAD、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet数据集上，在严格DP预算和非IID数据划分条件下，AS-LoRA相比联邦LoRA基线在GLUE上提升高达+7.5 pp，在MNLI-mm上提升+12.5 pp；相比SVD聚合方法聚合成本降低33-180倍，通信开销可忽略。

Conclusion: AS-LoRA理论上消除了层绑定调度的重建误差下界，加速收敛，隐式偏好平坦极小值。实验证明其在多种任务和数据分布下均能显著提升差分隐私联邦微调的性能，兼具高效性和实用性。

Abstract: Differentially private federated fine-tuning of large models with LoRA suffers from aggregation error caused by LoRA's multiplicative structure, which is further amplified by DP noise and degrades both stability and accuracy. Existing remedies apply a single update mode uniformly across all layers and all communication rounds (or alternate them on a fixed schedule), ignoring both the structural asymmetry between the two LoRA factors and the round-wise dynamics of training. We propose AS-LoRA, an adaptive framework defined by three axes (i) layer-wise freedom, in which each layer independently selects its active component, (ii) round-wise adaptivity, in which the selection updates over communication rounds, and (iii) a curvature-aware score derived from a second-order approximation of the loss. Theoretically, AS-LoRA eliminates the reconstruction-error floor of layer-tied schedules, accelerates convergence, implicitly biases solutions toward flatter minima, and incurs no additional privacy cost. Across GLUE, SQuAD, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet under strict DP budgets and non-IID partitions, AS-LoRA improves over the federated LoRA baselines by up to $+7.5$ pp on GLUE and $+12.5$ pp on MNLI-mm for example, while matching or exceeding SVD-based aggregation methods at $33\text{--}180 \times$ lower aggregation cost and with negligible communication overhead. Code for the proposed method is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/as_lora-F75F/.

</details>


### [98] [A Measure-Theoretic Finite-Sample Theory for Adaptive-Data Fitted Q-Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05791)
*Manuel Haussmann,Mustafa Mert Çelikok,Melih Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了面向一般可测Borel空间的拟合Q迭代（FQI）统一理论框架，首次给出了有限样本、自适应数据情形下的性能界与路径在线遗憾保证，为现代深度强化学习算法的形式化分析奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管无模型离策略深度强化学习在复杂非线性机器人系统控制中取得了启发式成功，但理论分析仍局限于表格或可线性化场景，理论与实践之间存在显著鸿沟。作者识别出导致该鸿沟的三种传统孤立：测度论MDP基础忽略学习误差源、确定性误差传播分析缺乏估计误差的有限样本分析、以及PAC泛化界仅适用于简化拓扑结构。

Method: 提出了FQI在一般可测Borel空间上的统一理论框架。通过将测度论概率与Banach空间中Bellman算子压缩性相结合，推导性能界；利用序贯Rademacher复杂度刻画策略依赖数据收集下的Bellman回归泛化误差；并将分析扩展至连续空间中的累积路径在线遗憾。

Result: 获得了FQI在自适应数据收集下的有限样本性能界；证明了序贯Rademacher复杂度在策略依赖数据收集下控制Bellman回归泛化；首次给出连续空间中FQI的累积路径在线遗憾保证。

Conclusion: 该工作为现代深度强化学习算法的形式化分析提供了必要的理论基础，有效弥合了启发式成功与现有理论局限之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning (RL) promises to revolutionize the control of complex nonlinear robotic systems, a profound gap persists between the heuristic success of model-free off-policy deep RL and the underlying theory, which remains largely confined to tabular or linearizable settings. We identify the cause of this gap as an emergent isolation of three traditions: (i) measure-theoretic MDP foundations on general spaces limit their analysis to exact dynamic programming and ignore all error sources of a learning process; (ii) deterministic error propagation analysis addresses the approximation error via concentrability coefficients without a finite-sample analysis of the estimation error; and (iii) PAC generalization bounds characterize the estimation errors of simplified topologies. We bridge these traditions with a unified theoretical framework for fitted Q-iteration (FQI) on general measurable Borel spaces. Our main result provides a finite-sample, adaptive-data performance bound by chaining measure-theoretic probability with Bellman-operator contraction in Banach spaces. We prove that sequential Rademacher complexity controls Bellman-regression generalization under policy-dependent data collection. We further extend this analysis to provide the first cumulative, pathwise online regret guarantee for FQI in continuous spaces. These results lay the necessary foundations for the formal analysis of many modern deep RL algorithms.

</details>


### [99] [Revealing Modular Gradient Noise Imbalance in LLMs: Calibrating Adam via Signal-to-Noise Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05794)
*Ziqing Wen,Zhouyang Liu,Jiahuan Wang,Ping Luo,Li Shen,Dongsheng Li,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MoLS方法，通过估计模块级信噪比(SNR)来自动调整Adam优化器的模块学习率，解决了大语言模型中模块异质性带来的优化挑战，无需人工调参即可实现更好的收敛和泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的模块异质性导致优化困难，现有自适应优化器（如Adam）无法显式处理模块级梯度异质性，导致收敛慢、性能次优或训练不稳定。现有方法依赖人工调整模块特定学习率，计算成本高且难以泛化。

Method: 首先分析Adam在高噪声模块中的噪声抑制行为，然后提出MoLS方法。MoLS通过估计模块级信噪比(SNR)来缩放Adam更新，实现自动化的模块级学习率分配，无需人工调参，且与内存高效训练算法兼容。

Result: 在多个LLM训练基准测试中，MoLS提高了收敛速度和泛化能力，性能可与精心调优的模块特定学习率相媲美，同时保持了与内存高效训练算法的兼容性。

Conclusion: MoLS为异构模块LLM训练提供了一种原理性的优化方法，通过信噪比引导的自适应学习率分配，在不增加人工调参负担的情况下实现了优异的训练性能。

Abstract: The impressive performance of large language models (LLMs) arises from their massive scale and heterogeneous module composition. However, this structural heterogeneity introduces additional optimization challenges. While adaptive optimizers such as Adam(W) provide per-parameter adaptivity, they do not explicitly account for module-level gradient heterogeneity, resulting in slower convergence, suboptimal performance, or training instability. Existing approaches typically rely on manually tuned module-specific learning rates or specific optimization strategies, which are computationally costly and difficult to generalize across tasks or models. To establish a more principled approach, we first analyze the noise-damping behavior of Adam in high-noise modules and introduce \textbf{Module-wise Learning Rate Scaling via SNR (MoLS)}. MoLS estimates module-level SNRs to scale Adam updates, allowing automated module-wise learning rate allocation without manual tuning. Empirical results through multiple LLM training benchmarks demonstrate that MoLS improves convergence speed and generalization, achieving performance comparable to carefully tuned module-specific learning rates, while remaining compatible with memory-efficient training algorithms.

</details>


### [100] [Reward Shaping and Action Masking for Compositional Tasks using Behavior Trees and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05795)
*Nicholas Potteiger,Ankita Samaddar,Taylor T. Johnson,Xenofon Koutsoukos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了掩码奖励行为树（MRBT）结构，结合LLM自动生成、SMT求解器验证和神经符号强化学习，解决了组合任务中子任务失败反应性和对象变化模块性的挑战，显著提升了训练效率和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的利用大语言模型（LLM）自动化强化学习（RL）中的奖励塑形和动作掩码的方法，未能完全解决对子任务失败的反应性以及对组合任务中变化对象的模块化问题。

Method: 提出了掩码奖励行为树（MRBT）这一符号结构，作为具有反应性和模块性的奖励与动作掩码函数；设计了MRBT模板并推导逻辑规范以构建和验证MRBT；开发了一个自动化流程：利用LLM生成鲁棒的MRBT，使用SMT求解器验证规范正确性，并通过神经符号RL循环训练智能体。

Result: 实验成功生成并优化了五个MRBT，相比基线方法和未使用动作掩码的MRBT，训练效率和任务成功率得到持续提升；验证了MRBT具有可迁移性、模块化和可验证性三大优势。

Conclusion: MRBT结构有效解决了组合任务中的反应性和模块化挑战，结合LLM与形式化验证的自动化流程能够显著提升强化学习在复杂任务中的表现，具有良好的可迁移性、模块性和可验证性。

Abstract: Decomposing complex tasks into a sequence of simpler subtasks can improve learning efficiency for an autonomous agent. Reinforcement learning (RL) can be used to optimize agent policies to complete subtasks, but requires well-defined subtask rewards and benefits from action masking. Recent work uses large language models (LLMs) to automate reward shaping and action masking, however none of them fully address reactivity to subtask failure and modularity to varying objects for compositional tasks. To overcome these challenges, we develop masking reward behavior tree (MRBT), a symbolic structure used as a reactive and modular reward and action mask function. We design an MRBT template and derive logical specifications to construct and verify MRBTs for a sequence of object-interaction subtasks. Further, we develop an automated pipeline that uses an LLM to generate MRBTs robust to varying task objects, an SMT-solver to verify correctness of specifications, and a neurosymbolic RL loop to train agents on compositional tasks. Experiments demonstrate successful generation and refinement of five MRBTs, consistently improving training efficiency and task success rates over baselines and MRBTs without action masking. We further highlight three advantages of MRBTs: transferability, modularity, and verifiability.

</details>


### [101] [Selective Rollout: Mid-Trajectory Termination for Multi-Sample Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05802)
*Zhiyuan Zhai,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GRPO训练中约40%零奖励方差组造成的计算浪费，提出基于组内前缀编辑距离的早停机制，在保持训练效果的同时提升10.7%的训练速度和2.5个百分点的测试成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体环境的GRPO训练中，每个提示需要采样一组并行轨迹计算优势值，但实践中约40%的组最终奖励相同导致零方差，这些组不产生梯度却消耗大量计算资源。现有方法在轨迹生成前后进行过滤，未利用生成过程中的中间信息。

Method: 提出一种单参数门控机制，在中间步骤监测组内部分轨迹的成对前缀编辑距离均值，当该距离低于阈值时（表明轨迹已收敛到相同动作序列），提前终止该组的生成过程，避免无效计算。

Result: 在ALFWorld上使用Qwen2.5-7B进行的60轮GRPO训练实验表明：该方法使训练时间减少10.7%（统计显著），50个未见任务的测试成功率提升2.5个百分点，成功率的提升源于减少了零优势梯度对批次训练的稀释效应。

Conclusion: 通过利用轨迹生成过程中的中间信息进行早停，该方法有效减少了GRPO训练中的计算浪费，同时通过减少零优势梯度稀释提升了模型泛化性能，实现了效率与效果的双重改善。

Abstract: Group-relative RL training (GRPO) samples a small group of parallel rollouts for every training prompt and uses their within-group reward spread to compute per-trajectory advantages. In agentic environments each rollout is a long multi-turn dialogue with one LLM call per step, so this multi-sample multiplier dominates the total training cost. When every rollout of a prompt ends with the same reward, the group has zero reward variance and contributes no gradient, so the extra rollouts add no information; such groups are common in practice (typically around 40% of all groups), so the wasted-compute fraction is substantial rather than marginal. Existing methods filter such groups at the prompt level, either after their rollouts are paid for or before any rollout begins, but both decide without using information that becomes available during the rollout itself. We instead ask whether the in-group divergence between the partial trajectories at an intermediate step can already predict that the group will be zero-variance: when the parallel rollouts have already converged on the same action prefix, the group is on track to produce a single reward, and we can stop early. We propose a one-parameter gate that stops a group when the mean pairwise prefix edit distance between its partial action sequences falls below a threshold. On a 60-iteration on-policy GRPO run on ALFWorld with Qwen2.5-7B, averaged over four random seeds, the gated arm finishes 10.7% faster in wall-clock (bootstrap 95% CI excludes 0) and shifts held-out success rate on 50 unseen tasks by +2.5 pp, with the held-out gain tracing to a measurable reduction in zero-advantage gradient-batch dilution. Code is available at https://github.com/zhiyuanZhai20/selective-rollout.

</details>


### [102] [Retrieval from Within: An Intrinsic Capability of Attention-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05806)
*Elad Hoffer,Yochai Blau,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出INTRA框架，证明注意力模型本身具备检索能力，通过统一检索与生成过程超越了传统RAG系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成(RAG)系统将检索和生成视为分离的模块，存在检索器-生成器不匹配的问题。本文探索基于注意力的编码器-解码器模型是否能够直接从其内部表示中进行检索，从而统一检索和生成过程。

Method: 提出INTRA（通过注意力的内在检索）框架，利用解码器注意力查询对预编码的证据块进行评分，然后直接将其作为生成的上下文重用。该设计通过重用预计算的编码器状态来分摊上下文编码成本，并消除了传统RAG流水线中的检索器-生成器不匹配问题。

Result: 在问答基准测试中，INTRA在证据召回率和端到端答案质量两个指标上均优于强工程化检索流水线。实验证明基于注意力的模型已经具备可以被激发的检索机制，而无需作为外部模块添加。

Conclusion: 注意力模型天然具备检索能力，可以通过适当框架设计加以利用。INTRA成功统一了检索和生成过程，为RAG系统设计提供了新思路，表明外部检索模块并非必要。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) typically treats retrieval and generation as separate systems. We ask whether an attention-based encoder-decoder can instead retrieve directly from its own internal representations. We introduce INTRA (INTrinsic Retrieval via Attention), a framework where decoder attention queries score pre-encoded evidence chunks that are then directly reused as context for generation. By construction, INTRA unifies retrieval and generation, eliminating the retriever-generator mismatch typical of RAG pipelines. This design also amortizes context encoding by reusing precomputed encoder states across queries. On question-answering benchmarks, INTRA outperforms strong engineered retrieval pipelines on both evidence recall and end-to-end answer quality. Our results demonstrate that attention-based models already possess a retrieval mechanism that can be elicited, rather than added as an external module.

</details>


### [103] [A Testable Certificate for Constant Collapse in Teacher-Guided VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05813)
*Zegu Zhang,Jianhua Peng,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对变分自编码器（VAE）中的后验坍塌问题，提出了一种可精确测量的诊断方法。通过对“输入无关的常数坍塌”这一具体失效模式进行分析，推导出了一个严格的阈值：当且仅当对齐损失低于教师互信息时，模型可以证明未发生此类坍塌。实验验证了该认证边界在训练监控与恢复中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后验坍塌通常通过KL散度过小或解码器过强等模糊症状进行定性诊断，缺乏明确的判定边界。作者希望建立一个数学上精确的阈值，将定性的失效模式转化为可测量的认证标准。

Method: 1. 定义“输入无关常数坍塌”这一具体失效模式，并证明最佳常数学生分布是数据集平均教师分布，其对齐成本等于教师互信息。
2. 提出认证标准：若潜在变量的原始见证在对齐损失上严格低于该互信息阈值，则可证明见证非输入常数。
3. 设计实验流程（CIFAR-100与Tiny-ImageNet-200），包括全训练、移除对齐驱动坍塌、以及从坍塌点重启恢复三阶段。

Result: 在CIFAR-100实验中，正常训练始终保持在认证边界安全侧；移除对齐后模型进入常数学生坍塌区域；从坍塌检查点重启并启用对齐可恢复认证。Tiny-ImageNet-200上三个独立搜索的教师均呈现一致的“预防—坍塌—挽救”模式。标准VAE基线及保持重建质量或后验可预测性的方法均无法通过该认证。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将一种后验坍塌失效模式从定性诊断转变为可测量的认证，提供了严格的数学阈值。虽然该保证范围较窄（仅认证匹配的非常数教师相关变化通过了潜在路径），但为检测和理解VAE的后验坍塌提供了新的理论工具与实证依据。

Abstract: Posterior collapse in variational autoencoders is often diagnosed by its symptoms: a small KL term, a strong decoder, or weak use of the latent code. These signals are useful, but they do not define a collapse boundary. We study a concrete failure mode, input-independent constant collapse, and show that this case admits an exact threshold. For any fixed nonconstant teacher distribution \(T(\cdot\mid x)\), the best constant student is the dataset-average teacher distribution, and its alignment cost is the teacher mutual information \(I_T(X;T)\). Therefore, if a strictly latent-only raw witness achieves alignment loss below this value, with a safety margin, the witness cannot be constant in the input.
  This identity turns a qualitative failure mode into a measurable one. In CIFAR-100 experiments with per-seed teacher search, full training stays on the certified side of the boundary, removing alignment drives the raw witness into the constant-student regime, and restarting from a collapsed checkpoint with alignment enabled restores the certificate. Tiny-ImageNet-200 fixed-target runs show the same prevention--collapse--rescue pattern across three independently searched teachers. Standard VAE-style baselines, including methods that preserve reconstruction quality or post-hoc predictability, remain negative under the raw certificate. The guarantee is intentionally narrow: it certifies that the matched nonconstant teacher-relative variation passes through the latent pathway, rather than claiming that all forms of posterior collapse have been ruled out.

</details>


### [104] [HCInfer: An Efficient Inference System via Error Compensation for Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05819)
*Shen Xu,Xiangwen Zhuge,Zhe Xu,Yingkun Hu,Zheng Yang,Yunhao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HCInfer异构推理系统，通过将残差补偿分支卸载至CPU、在GPU执行压缩主干网络，配合异步流水线和动态秩分配，在保持高吞吐量的同时显著恢复压缩模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型压缩部署方案存在精度严重下降或吞吐量瓶颈问题。近期误差补偿方法通过辅助LoRA分支恢复精度，但作者发现这些分支天然适合卸载：参数总量大，但每次推理仅访问少量补偿参数，为异构计算提供了机会。

Method: 提出HCInfer异构推理系统：(1)将残差补偿分支卸载至CPU内存，压缩主干网络在GPU执行；(2)设计异步补偿流水线隐藏补偿开销；(3)引入敏感性感知的动态秩分配策略最大化精度恢复。

Result: 实验结果表明，与压缩模型相比，HCInfer在下游任务上最高提升5.2%精度；与全精度模型相比，最高实现10.4倍加速。

Conclusion: HCInfer成功实现了精度恢复与推理速度的有效平衡，为大语言模型在消费级硬件上的部署提供了一种高效的异构计算解决方案。

Abstract: LLMs often struggle with memory-constrained deployment on consumer-grade hardware due to their massive parameter sizes. While existing solutions such as model compression and offloading improve deployment feasibility, they often suffer from substantial accuracy degradation or severe throughput bottlenecks. Recent error compensation methods recover accuracy through auxiliary LoRA-style branches, and we observe that these branches are inherently amenable to offloading: they require substantial parameter storage but access only a small subset of compensation parameters during each inference step. Motivated by this opportunity, we propose HCInfer, a heterogeneous inference system that offloads residual compensation to the CPU while executing the compressed backbone on the GPU, and further introduces an asynchronous compensation pipeline and sensitivity-aware dynamic rank allocation to hide compensation overhead and maximize accuracy recovery. Experimental results show that HCInfer achieves a maximum accuracy improvement of 5.2% on downstream tasks compared to compression model and sustaining a maximum speedup of 10.4x compared to full-precision model.

</details>


### [105] [MDN: Parallelizing Stepwise Momentum for Delta Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05838)
*Yulong Huang,Xiang Liu,Hongxiang Huang,Xiaopeng Lin,Zunchang Liu,Xiaowen Chu,Zeke Xie,Bojun Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Momentum DeltaNet (MDN)，通过将动量机制引入线性注意力模型的递归更新中，解决了现有方法信息衰减快和收敛次优的问题，在多个基准测试上超越了Mamba2和GDN等强基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性注意力模型（如Mamba2和GDN）将线性递归解释为闭式在线随机梯度下降（SGD），但朴素的SGD更新存在信息快速衰减和收敛次优的问题。虽然基于动量的优化器可以缓解这些问题，但在训练效率和效果之间难以兼顾。

Method: 作者开发了一种分块并行算法，通过几何重排序更新系数实现步进式动量规则；从动力系统视角，将基于动量的递归分析为引入复共轭特征值的二阶系统，并据此设计了稳定的门控约束；最终利用Triton内核实现了高效的训练。

Result: 在400M和1.3B参数模型上的大量实验表明，MDN在多样化下游评估基准上持续超越包括Transformer、Mamba2和GDN在内的强基线模型，且训练吞吐量与Mamba2和KDA等竞争性线性模型相当。

Conclusion: 通过动量机制和二阶系统分析，MDN成功解决了线性注意力模型的信息衰减和收敛问题，在保持训练效率的同时实现了性能提升，为长序列建模提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: Linear Attention (LA) offers a promising paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs) to long sequences by avoiding the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Recent LA models such as Mamba2 and GDN interpret linear recurrences as closed-form online stochastic gradient descent (SGD), but naive SGD updates suffer from rapid information decay and suboptimal convergence in optimization. While momentum-based optimizers provide a natural remedy, they pose challenges in simultaneously achieving training efficiency and effectiveness. To address this, we develop a chunkwise parallel algorithm for LA with a stepwise momentum rule by geometrically reordering the update coefficients. Further, from a dynamical systems perspective, we analyze the momentum-based recurrence as a second-order system that introduces complex conjugate eigenvalues. This analysis guides the design of stable gating constraints. The resulting model, Momentum DeltaNet (MDN), leverages Triton kernels to achieve comparable training throughput with competitive linear models such as Mamba2 and KDA. Extensive experiments on the 400M and 1.3B parameter models demonstrate consistent performance improvements over strong baselines, including Transformers, Mamba2 and GDN, across diverse downstream evaluation benchmarks. Code: https://github.com/HuuYuLong/MomentumDeltaNet .

</details>


### [106] [Hypothesis generation and updating in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05851)
*Hua-Dong Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在数字游戏中研究大语言模型（LLM）的假设推理能力，发现LLM的推理可用双参数贝叶斯模型描述，但存在系统性偏差（默认强采样偏向狭窄假设，思维模式偏向先验），且存在评估-生成差距，泛化能力不足，限制了其作为通用问题求解器的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用于问题求解，需在部分信息下进行假设生成与更新，但其推理机制及最优性尚不清晰。为填补这一空白，作者在可控的数字游戏场景中，探究LLM如何进行假设推断，并比较其与最优贝叶斯模型及人类行为的差异。

Method: 在数字游戏中，通过三种互补探测方式测量LLM的后验分布：后验预测、假设评估与假设生成。将LLM行为与最优贝叶斯模型及人类行为进行比较，并检验不同探测方式下后验表达的一致性。

Result: LLM行为通常可用双参数贝叶斯模型拟合，但存在系统性偏差：默认表现出强采样假设，隐含奥卡姆剃刀效应，偏向更窄的假设；思维模式下则更依赖先验。发现评估-生成差距：LLM在假设评估时选择更正确的假设，而在生成时倾向更简单、更规则化的假设。此外，LLM的贝叶斯偏差模式泛化能力有限，虽在观察样本上表现良好，但对假设域中未覆盖部分的泛化较差。

Conclusion: LLM在假设推理中表现出近似贝叶斯但有偏差的行为，存在采样假设偏差、评估-生成不一致及泛化局限。这揭示了LLM作为通用问题求解器，特别是在需要超越数据进行假设推断的科学推理任务中的局限性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly help people solve problems, from debugging code to repairing machinery. This process requires generating plausible hypotheses from partial descriptions, then updating them as more information arrives. Yet how LLMs perform this form of inference, and how close it is to optimal, remains unclear. We study this question in the number game, a controlled setting in which a learner infers the hypothesis supported by a few positive integers, such as $\{16, 8, 2, 64\}$: a rule like powers of 2 or an interval like numbers near 20. We measure the posterior over hypotheses using three complementary probes: posterior prediction, hypothesis evaluation, and hypothesis generation. We then compare LLM behavior with an optimal Bayesian model and human behavior, and test whether the same posterior is expressed across probes. LLMs are often well described by a two-parameter Bayesian fit, but with systematic offsets: by default they show a strong-sampling assumption that creates an implicit Occam's razor, favoring narrower hypotheses, while thinking mode shifts them toward greater prior reliance. We also find a robust evaluation--generation gap: LLMs select more correct hypotheses during hypothesis evaluation but generate simpler, more rule-like hypotheses. Finally, this Bayesian-with-bias pattern does not extrapolate. Models can behave as if they hold rule-like hypotheses over observed examples, yet generalize poorly to parts of the hypothesis domain not covered by those examples. Our results highlight a limitation of LLMs as general problem solvers, especially for scientific inference, where hypotheses must go beyond the data.

</details>


### [107] [Measuring Learning Progress via Gradient-Momentum Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05856)
*Samuel Blad,Martin Längkvist,Amy Loutfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了梯度动量耦合（GMC）信号，利用优化动力学区分有意义的模式和随机噪声，以改进强化学习中的好奇心驱动探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习进度衡量指标（如预测误差）无法有效区分有意义的学习模式与随机噪声，导致好奇心驱动探索在噪声环境下表现不佳。

Method: 提出梯度动量耦合（GMC）信号，通过计算每个样本梯度与历史梯度动量的逐参数归一化绝对乘积，利用动量对噪声和振荡的自然过滤特性，量化样本对持续学习的有用程度。

Result: 控制实验表明GMC具有噪声鲁棒性并能产生课程学习效应，信号优先选择学习速度快的任务而非难度大的任务；在MiniGrid实验中，将现有好奇心架构中的预测误差替换为GMC可提高对观测噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GMC信号为好奇心驱动的探索提供了一种更可靠的学习进度衡量方法，能够有效识别对学习有贡献的样本，提升了算法在噪声环境下的性能。

Abstract: Measuring learning progress is essential for curiosity-driven exploration in reinforcement learning, but widely used signals such as prediction error often fail to distinguish meaningful, learnable patterns from random noise. This paper proposes Gradient-Momentum Coupling (GMC), a signal derived from optimization dynamics that quantifies how useful each sample's gradient is for ongoing learning by measuring its per-parameter normalized absolute product with the momentum from previous gradients. By leveraging momentum's natural filtering of noise and oscillations, GMC identifies samples that contribute to ongoing parameter updates. Controlled experiments demonstrate noise robustness and emergent curriculum learning, with the signal prioritizing tasks by learning speed rather than difficulty.
