TL;DR: 本文提出BoostLLM框架，将提升范式应用于大语言模型微调，通过训练序列化PEFT适配器作为弱学习器，在低数据场景下的表格预测任务中达到或超越XGBoost和GPT-4o的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在表格预测任务中通过对结构化特征序列化处理进行适配，但在低数据场景下的性能仍不及梯度提升决策树。本文旨在探究传统的提升范式是否可以作为大语言模型微调的通用训练原则。

Method: 提出BoostLLM框架，将参数高效微调转化为多轮残差优化过程，通过训练序列化的PEFT适配器作为弱学习器。引入表格归纳偏置，将决策树路径作为第二输入视图与原始特征并行输入，其中路径视图在早期训练中充当结构化教师角色。

Result: BoostLLM在多个LLM骨干网络和数据集上持续优于标准微调方法，在不同样本数量下匹配或超越XGBoost，并以4B模型优于基于GPT-4o的方法。框架具有良好的可扩展性，与更强的树模型和扩展的提升轮数配合可获得额外性能提升。

Conclusion: 提升范式可以作为大语言模型微调的通用训练原则，特别适用于结构化数据的低数据场景，为LLM在表格预测任务中的应用提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently been adapted to tabular prediction by serializing structured features into natural language, but their performance in low-data regimes remains limited compared to gradient-boosted decision trees (GBDTs). In this work, we revisit the boosting paradigm, traditionally associated with tree ensembles, and ask whether it can be applied as a general training principle for LLM fine-tuning. We propose BoostLLM, a framework that transforms parameter-efficient fine-tuning into a multi-round residual optimization process by training sequential PEFT adapters as weak learners. To incorporate tabular inductive bias, BoostLLM integrates decision-tree paths as a second input view alongside raw features; analysis reveals that the path view acts as a structured teacher in early training steps before the model shifts toward feature-driven representations. Empirically, BoostLLM achieves consistent improvements over standard fine-tuning across multiple LLM backbones and datasets, matching or surpassing XGBoost across a wide range of shot counts and outperforming GPT-4o-based methods with a 4B model. We further show that the framework scales: pairing with stronger tree models and extended boosting horizons yields additional gains under appropriate stabilization. These results suggest that boosting can serve as a general training principle for LLM fine-tuning, particularly in low-data regimes for structured data.

</details>


### [145] [Listwise Policy Optimization: Group-based RLVR as Target-Projection on the LLM Response Simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06139)
*Yun Qu,Qi Wang,Yixiu Mao,Heming Zou,Yuhang Jiang,Yingyue Li,Wutong Xu,Lizhou Cai,Weijie Liu,Clive Bai,Kai Yang,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出列表策略优化（LPO）方法，通过显式目标投影揭示了基于组的策略梯度方法的几何结构，在多项推理任务中优于传统基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）方法普遍采用基于组的策略梯度，通过组相对优势信号更新策略。然而这些优化策略背后的几何结构尚不清晰，缺乏统一的理论框架来解释其工作机制和指导改进。

Method: 发现现有策略共享隐式定义响应单纯形上目标分布并通过一阶近似投影的几何结构；提出列表策略优化（LPO）框架，将邻近RL目标约束到响应单纯形，通过精确散度最小化显式执行目标投影，解耦投影步骤支持灵活的散度选择。

Result: 在多样化推理任务和大语言模型骨干网络上，LPO在匹配目标下持续优于典型策略梯度基线；提供的列表目标具有单调改进性，投影梯度具有有界、零和、自校正特性；内禀保持优化稳定性和响应多样性。

Conclusion: LPO成功解构了现有RLVR方法的隐式机制，建立了统一的理论框架，通过显式目标投影实现了性能提升与稳定性保持的平衡，为大语言模型后训练提供了新的优化范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard approach for large language models (LLMs) post-training to incentivize reasoning capacity. Among existing recipes, group-based policy gradient is prevalent, which samples a group of responses per prompt and updates the policy via group-relative advantage signals. This work reveals that these optimization strategies share a common geometric structure: each implicitly defines a target distribution on the response simplex and projects toward it via first-order approximation. Building on this insight, we propose Listwise Policy Optimization (LPO) to explicitly conduct the target-projection, which demystifies the implicit target by restricting the proximal RL objective to the response simplex, and then projects the policy via exact divergence minimization. This framework provides (i) monotonic improvement on the listwise objective with bounded, zero-sum, and self-correcting projection gradients, and (ii) flexibility in divergence selection with distinct structural properties through the decoupled projection step. On diverse reasoning tasks and LLM backbones, LPO consistently improves training performance over typical policy gradient baselines under matched targets, while intrinsically preserving optimization stability and response diversity.

</details>


### [146] [SymDrift: One-Shot Generative Modeling under Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06140)
*Samir Darouich,Vinh Tong,Lluís Pastor-Pérez,Tanja Bien,Loay Mualem,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SymDrift框架，通过坐标空间对称化漂移和G不变嵌入两种策略，解决了生成物理系统（如分子）时漂移模型面临的对称性挑战，实现了单步生成且性能优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成物理系统（如分子）需要学习在全局对称性（如三维旋转）下不变的分布。现有的等变扩散和流匹配模型虽然能有效融合这些不变性，但依赖计算成本高的多步采样。漂移模型作为高效替代方案虽能实现单步生成，但面临对称性特有的挑战：等变生成器通常无法产生与对称化目标分布相同的漂移场，而解决该问题需要对经验分布进行昂贵的对称化处理。

Method: 提出SymDrift框架，通过两种互补策略使漂移场本身具备对称性感知能力：（i）基于最优对齐的坐标空间对称化漂移；（ii）通过构造消除对称性歧义的G不变嵌入。该框架无需对经验分布进行昂贵的对称化处理。

Result: 在构象体和过渡态生成的标准基准测试上，SymDrift优于现有的单步方法，并与成本显著更高的多步方法保持竞争力。通过实现单步推理，SymDrift相比现有基线减少了高达40倍的计算开销。

Conclusion: SymDrift成功解决了漂移模型在对称性方面的挑战，实现了高效的单步生成，在保持性能竞争力的同时大幅降低了计算成本，为虚拟药物筛选和大规模反应网络探索等高通量应用提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative modeling of physical systems, such as molecules, requires learning distributions that are invariant under global symmetries, such as rotations in three-dimensional space. Equivariant diffusion and flow matching models can incorporate such invariances effectively, even when trained on a non-invariant empirical distribution, but they typically rely on costly multi-step sampling. Recently, drifting models have emerged as an efficient alternative, enabling single-step generation and achieving state-of-the-art performance in generative modeling tasks. However, we show that drifting models face a symmetry-specific challenge, since an equivariant generator does not generally produce the same drifting field as the one obtained from the symmetrized target distribution. Addressing this issue would require expensive symmetrization of the empirical distribution. To avoid this cost, we propose SymDrift, a framework that makes the drifting field itself symmetry-aware. We introduce two complementary strategies: (i) a symmetrized drift in coordinate space based on optimal alignment, and (ii) a $G$-invariant embedding that removes symmetry ambiguity by construction. Empirically, SymDrift outperforms existing one-shot methods on standard benchmarks for conformer and transition state generation, while remaining competitive with significantly more expensive multi-step approaches. By enabling one-shot inference, SymDrift reduces computational overhead by up to 40$\times$ compared to existing baselines, making it promising for high-throughput applications such as virtual drug screening and large-scale reaction network exploration.

</details>


### [147] [Matrix-Valued Optimism is Matrix-Valued Augmentation: Additive Hybrid Designs for Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06141)
*Jiayi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推广了增广拉格朗日方法与乐观原始-对偶方法在矩阵值校正参数下的等价性，提出了加性原理并据此设计了混合校正规则，在适度病态条件下性能优于单一方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增广拉格朗日方法通过添加约束相关的原始曲率来稳定等式约束优化，而乐观原始-对偶方法通过添加对偶记忆实现稳定。现有研究已证明这两种机制在标量参数下等价，本文旨在将此等价性扩展到矩阵值校正参数，以理解两者深层联系并指导算法设计。

Method: 证明了针对对称矩阵参数的加性原理：理想的原始轨迹仅依赖于校正矩阵的和，而与其在增广和乐观通道间的分配方式无关。基于此设计自由度，提出闭环混合规则：选择矩阵校正，在两通道间分配校正，并利用局部谱权重选择原始和对偶步长。

Result: 在具有可控约束雅可比条件的非线性等式约束问题上的实验表明：混合设计优于纯增广和纯乐观端点，紧密跟踪网格搜索混合预言机，并在轻度到中度病态条件下与一阶原始-对偶基线方法具有竞争力。实验也识别出了局限性：随着约束雅可比病态化，精确抵消需要越来越大的矩阵校正。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了增广拉格朗日校正与乐观原始-对偶校正之间的深层代数等价性，同时阐明了两者在有限步可行性方面的差异，为设计更优的混合算法提供了理论依据和实用规则。

Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian and optimistic primal--dual methods stabilize equality-constrained optimization through seemingly different mechanisms: the former adds constraint-dependent primal curvature, while the latter adds dual memory. Recent work has shown that these mechanisms are equivalent for scalar parameters. We extend this equivalence to matrix-valued correction. We prove an additivity principle: for symmetric matrix parameters, the ideal primal trajectory depends only on the summed correction matrix, not on how it is split between augmented and optimistic channels. This exposes a design freedom: algebraically equivalent decompositions can have different finite-step feasibility because augmented correction affects primal curvature, whereas optimistic correction affects the scale of the dual memory correction. We formulate the resulting step-size-limited design problem and derive a closed-form hybrid rule that selects a matrix correction, splits it between the two channels, and chooses primal and dual steps using local spectral weights. Experiments on nonlinear equality-constrained problems with controlled constraint-Jacobian conditioning show that the hybrid design improves over pure augmented and pure optimistic endpoints, closely tracks a grid-search hybrid oracle, and is competitive with first-order primal--dual baselines under mild-to-moderate ill-conditioning. The experiments also identify the expected limitation: exact cancellation requires increasingly large matrix corrections as the constraint Jacobian becomes ill-conditioned.

</details>


### [148] [Unifying Goal-Conditioned RL and Unsupervised Skill Learning via Control-Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06145)
*Alireza Modirshanechi,Benjamin Eysenbach,Peter Dayan,Eric Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了目标条件强化学习(GCRL)与互信息技能学习(MISL)的理论联系，证明不同GCRL公式虽本质上不等价，但都与MISL目标存在精确对应关系，为无监督预训练在下游任务中的有效性提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督预训练在目标条件强化学习中取得了显著进展，但其理论基础尚不明确。特别是互信息技能学习方法虽然能发现行为多样化的技能并用于下游目标达成，但理论上为何这些技能能够支持目标达成仍然是一个谜题。此外，GCRL和MISL都是涵盖性术语，不同方法使用不同的性能标准和多样性概念，这增加了理论分析的复杂性。

Method: 作者将GCRL和MISL统一为控制最大化问题的实例进行研究。首先识别三种典型的GCRL公式，分析它们之间的关系；然后通过"目标敏感性"这一共同解释框架连接不同公式；最后将MISL目标理解为技能敏感性度量，建立其与下游目标敏感性之间的界限关系，从而揭示MISL方法与GCRL任务之间的精确对应关系。

Result: 证明了三种典型GCRL公式本质上不等价，即使在相同环境中也可能产生不兼容的最优策略。然而它们共享一个共同解释：表现良好的目标条件策略是指其未来轨迹对所命令目标高度敏感的策略，具体敏感性概念由GCRL公式决定。关键发现是MISL目标受到公式特定的下游目标敏感性的界限约束，为每个GCRL公式都存在匹配的MISL目标，使更多样化的技能能够带来更大的下游目标敏感性。

Conclusion: 本研究为强化学习预训练建立了理论基础，阐明了为什么通过MISL学习的技能能够支持下游目标达成。研究结果具有重要的实践意义，能够指导用户在关注特定类别的下游任务时应选择哪种预训练目标，从而弥合了理论与实践之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Unsupervised pretraining has driven empirical advances in goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), but its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In particular, an influential class of methods, mutual information skill learning (MISL), discovers behaviorally diverse skills that can later be used for downstream goal-reaching. However, it remains a theoretical mystery why skills learned through MISL should support goal-reaching. A subtle challenge is that both GCRL and MISL are umbrella terms: different GCRL tasks use distinct criteria for measuring goal-reaching performance, while different MISL methods optimize distinct notions of behavioral diversity. We address this challenge and unify GCRL and MISL as instances of control maximization. We identify three canonical GCRL formulations and prove that they are fundamentally inequivalent: they can induce incompatible optimal policies even in the same environment. Nevertheless, they all share a common interpretation: a well-performing goal-conditioned policy is one whose future trajectory is highly sensitive to the commanded goal, with the precise notion of sensitivity determined by the GCRL formulation. Noting that MISL objectives can be understood as measures of skill-sensitivity akin to goal-sensitivity, we show that MISL objectives are bounded by formulation-specific downstream goal-sensitivities. These bounds establish a precise correspondence between MISL methods and downstream GCRL tasks: for every GCRL formulation, there exists a matching MISL objective for which more diverse skills afford greater downstream goal sensitivity. Our results thus lay a theoretical foundation for RL pretraining and have important practical implications, such as suggesting which pretraining objectives to use when a user cares about a specific class of downstream tasks.

</details>


### [149] [AdaGamma: State-Dependent Discounting for Temporal Adaptation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06149)
*Yaomin Wang,Jianting Pan,Ran Tian,Xiaoyang Li,Yu Zhang,Hengle Qin,Tianshu YU*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AdaGamma，一种用于状态依赖折扣因子的深度Actor-Critic方法，通过引入回报一致性目标来防止训练不稳定和TD-error崩塌，在连续控制基准测试和京东物流平台的在线A/B测试中均取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中的折扣因子同时控制有效规划视野和自举强度，但现有的深度强化学习方法通常在所有状态使用单一固定值，忽略了状态依赖折扣因子的潜在优势。虽然状态依赖折扣在概念上具有吸引力，但朴素的深度Actor-Critic实现会导致训练不稳定并退化为TD-error崩塌，这阻碍了其在实际应用中的使用。

Method: 提出了AdaGamma方法，这是一种实用的深度Actor-Critic算法，能够学习状态依赖的折扣函数，同时引入回报一致性目标来正则化诱导的备份结构，从而防止退化的目标操作。在理论层面，分析了状态依赖折扣诱导的Bellman算子，并在适当条件下建立了其基本适定性性质。该方法成功集成到SAC和PPO算法中。

Result: 在连续控制基准测试中，AdaGamma集成到SAC和PPO后均取得了持续的性能改进。更重要的是，在京东物流平台的在线A/B测试中实现了统计显著的性能增益，验证了该方法的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 状态依赖折扣因子在深度强化学习中可以有效应用，关键在于配合回报一致性目标来防止退化的目标操作。AdaGamma为状态依赖折扣提供了一种稳定、实用的解决方案，具有重要的理论和实践意义。

Abstract: The discount factor in reinforcement learning controls both the effective planning horizon and the strength of bootstrapping, yet most deep RL methods use a single fixed value across all states. While state-dependent discounting is conceptually appealing, naive deep actor--critic implementations can become unstable and degenerate toward TD-error collapse. We propose AdaGamma, a practical deep actor--critic method for state-dependent discounting that learns a state-dependent discount function together with a return-consistency objective to regularize the induced backup structure. On the theory side, we analyze the Bellman operator induced by state-dependent discounting and establish its basic well-posedness properties under suitable conditions. Empirically, AdaGamma integrates into both SAC and PPO, yielding consistent improvements on continuous-control benchmarks, and achieves statistically significant gains in an online A/B test on the JD Logistics platform. These results suggest that state-dependent discounting can be made effective in deep RL when coupled with a return-consistency objective that prevents degenerate target manipulation.

</details>


### [150] [Grokking or Glitching? How Low-Precision Drives Slingshot Loss Spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06152)
*Liu Hanqing,Jianjun Cao,Yuanze Li,Zijian Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究证明深度神经网络训练中出现的“Slingshot Mechanism”周期性损失尖峰现象源于浮点运算精度限制，提出了数值特征膨胀（NFI）机制来解释这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常将Slingshot现象归因于内在优化动力学，但其触发机制尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示该现象的根本原因，解释训练后期的异常参数增长和logit发散问题。

Method: 通过理论证明，分析浮点运算精度限制对梯度计算的影响。研究发现在高置信度阶段，正确类别logit与其他logit的差值超过吸收误差阈值，导致梯度计算出现系统性偏差，破坏了类别间梯度的零和约束。

Result: 证明了这种偏差在分类器层参数更新中形成系统性漂移，与特征形成正反馈循环，导致全局分类器均值和全局特征均值呈指数增长。这种数值特征膨胀（NFI）机制解释了Slingshot尖峰前的快速范数增长、梯度的重新出现以及随后的损失尖峰。

Conclusion: 该研究重新解释了Slingshot现象，将其定义为有限精度训练的数值动力学现象，为训练后期的异常参数增长和logit发散提供了可测试的解释。部分吸收可能不产生可见尖峰，但仍会破坏零和约束并驱动参数范数的快速增长。

Abstract: Deep neural networks exhibit periodic loss spikes during unregularized long-term training, a phenomenon known as the "Slingshot Mechanism." Existing work usually attributes this to intrinsic optimization dynamics, but its triggering mechanism remains unclear. This paper proves that this phenomenon is a result of floating-point arithmetic precision limits. As training enters a high-confidence stage, the difference between the correct-class logit and the other logits may exceed the absorption-error threshold. Then during backpropagation, the gradient of the correct class is rounded exactly to zero, while the gradients of the incorrect classes remain nonzero. This breaks the zero-sum constraint of gradients across classes and introduces a systematic drift in the parameter update of the classifier layer. We prove that this drift forms a positive feedback loop with the feature, causing the global classifier mean and the global feature mean to grow exponentially. We call this mechanism Numerical Feature Inflation (NFI). This mechanism explains the rapid norm growth before a Slingshot spike, the subsequent reappearance of gradients, and the resulting loss spike. We further show that NFI is not equivalent to an observed loss spike: in more practical tasks, partial absorption may not produce visible spikes, but it can still break the zero-sum constraint and drive rapid growth of parameter norms. Our results reinterpret Slingshot as a numerical dynamic of finite-precision training, and provide a testable explanation for abnormal parameter growth and logit divergence in late-stage training.

</details>


### [151] [Entropy-Regularized Adjoint Matching for Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06156)
*Abdelghani Ghanem,Mounir Ghogho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了最大熵伴随匹配（ME-AM）框架，通过引入镜面下降熵最大化目标和混合行为先验，解决了离线强化学习中Q-learning与伴随匹配（QAM）存在的流行度偏差和支持约束问题，在稀疏奖励连续控制任务上取得了优于SOTA的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Q-learning与伴随匹配（QAM）方法虽然稳定了策略优化，但受限于固定的行为分布，存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 流行度偏差，抑制了低密度高奖励动作；(2) 支持约束，限制了流形外探索。现有的残差高斯策略方案则重新引入了单峰分布的表达瓶颈。

Method: 提出最大熵伴随匹配（ME-AM）统一框架，在连续流形式中引入两个核心机制：(1) 镜面下降熵最大化目标，缓解流行度偏差以促进最优策略提取；(2) 混合行为先验，在数学上扩展几何支持以覆盖分布外的高奖励区域，同时保持生成向量场的绝对连续性。

Result: 在多样化的稀疏奖励连续控制环境基准测试中，ME-AM相较于先前的最先进方法展现出具有竞争力或更优越的性能表现。

Conclusion: ME-AM成功地在连续流形式内解决了离线强化学习中行为分布依赖带来的表达性限制问题，在保持生成向量场数学性质的同时实现了对高奖励区域的有效探索。

Abstract: Integrating expressive generative policies, such as flow-matching models, into offline reinforcement learning (RL) allows agents to capture complex, multi-modal behaviors. While Q-learning with Adjoint Matching (QAM) stabilizes policy optimization via the continuous adjoint method, it remains inherently bound to the fixed behavior distribution. This dependence induces a \textit{popularity bias} that can suppress high-reward actions in low-density regions, and creates a \textit{support binding} that restricts off-manifold exploration. Existing workarounds, such as appending \textit{residual} Gaussian policies, often re-introduce the expressivity bottlenecks associated with unimodal distributions. In this work, we propose \textit{Maximum Entropy Adjoint Matching} (ME-AM), a unified framework that addresses these limitations within the continuous flow formulation. ME-AM incorporates two mechanisms: (1) a Mirror Descent entropy maximization objective that mitigates the popularity bias to facilitate the extraction of optimal policies from offline datasets, and (2) a \textit{Mixture Behavior Prior} that mathematically broadens the geometric support to encompass out-of-distribution high-reward regions. By exploring this extended geometry, ME-AM identifies robust actions while preserving the absolute continuity of the generative vector field. Empirically, ME-AM demonstrates competitive or superior performance compared to prior state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods across a diverse suite of sparse-reward continuous control environments.

</details>


### [152] [One Algorithm, Two Goals: Dual Scoring for Parameter and Data Selection in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06166)
*Xinrui Chen,Liu Yang,Ou Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DualSFT算法，通过统一的梯度交互矩阵实现了参数和数据的一次性联合选择，在LLM微调中取得了优于传统分离方法的性能与成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM微调中，参数选择和数据选择通常使用独立的评分机制，这种分离导致了冗余开销且难以协调。当参数掩码和数据子集共同决定受限微调时，缺乏统一的框架来实现联合优化。

Method: 将参数和数据选择建模为共享验证目标的两个双层选择问题，推导出统一的局部响应代理评分规则。在一阶和二阶验证改进近似下，参数重要性和数据效用从单一梯度交互矩阵的列聚合和行聚合中产生，建立了联合提取两种信号的闭流行列对应关系。基于此结构提出DualSFT，一种一次性双评分算法，从共享梯度统计中生成参数掩码和数据子集。

Result: 在3B-9B参数的LLM上，单轴DualSFT变体在比较组内增强了目标任务性能和稳定性-可塑性权衡；完整DualSFT在匹配预算约束下，相比序列混合基线实现了更有利的联合约束权衡。

Conclusion: 通过统一的双层选择框架和共享梯度统计，DualSFT成功实现了参数与数据的协同选择，为高效LLM微调提供了新的范式，证明了联合优化优于传统的分离式方法。

Abstract: In Large Language Model (LLM) fine-tuning, parameter and data selection are common strategies for reducing fine-tuning cost, yet they are typically driven by separate scoring mechanisms. When a parameter mask and data subset jointly determine restricted fine-tuning, this separation incurs redundant overhead and makes coordinated selection difficult. We cast parameter and data selection as two bilevel selection problems under a common validation objective and derive a shared local response-surrogate scoring rule. Under first- and second-order validation-improvement approximations, parameter importance and data utility emerge as column-wise and row-wise aggregations of a single gradient interaction matrix, yielding a closed-form row-column correspondence for co-extracting both signals. Building on this structure, we propose DualSFT (Dual-Selection Fine-Tuning), a one-shot dual-scoring algorithm that produces a parameter mask and data subset from shared gradient statistics. On 3B-9B LLMs, single-axis DualSFT variants strengthen target-task performance and stability-plasticity trade-offs within their comparison groups, while full DualSFT yields a more favorable joint-constrained trade-off than sequential hybrid baselines under matched budgets.

</details>


### [153] [Mean Mode Screaming: Mean--Variance Split Residuals for 1000-Layer Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06169)
*Pengqi Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了深度扩散Transformer中存在的“均值模式尖叫（MMS）”崩溃现象，并提出了MV-Split残差方法成功解决了这一问题，实现了1000层极深网络的稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将扩散Transformer扩展到数百层时会出现结构性脆弱：网络可能进入一种隐蔽的、由均值主导的崩溃状态，导致token表示同质化并抑制中心化变化，即使训练表面上看起来稳定也会发生。

Method: 通过机制审计精确分解梯度为均值一致和中心化分量，提出均值-方差分裂（MV-Split）残差方法：将独立增益的中心化残差更新与泄漏式主干均值替换相结合。

Result: 在400层单流DiT上，MV-Split防止了导致基线崩溃的发散性坍塌，训练轨迹贴近基线崩溃前的表现，且在整个训练过程中显著优于LayerScale等token各向同性门控方法；成功实现了1000层DiT的边界规模验证。

Conclusion: MV-Split残差方法有效解决了深度DiT的结构性脆弱问题，使该架构在极深规模（1000层）下仍能保持稳定可训练性，为扩散模型的深度扩展提供了可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: Scaling Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) to hundreds of layers introduces a structural vulnerability: networks can enter a silent, mean-dominated collapse state that homogenizes token representations and suppresses centered variation. Through mechanistic auditing, we isolate the trigger event of this collapse as Mean Mode Screaming (MMS). MMS can occur even when training appears stable, with a mean-coherent backward shock on residual writers that opens deep residual branches and drives the network into a mean-dominated state. We show this behavior is driven by an exact decomposition of these gradients into mean-coherent and centered components, compounded by the structural suppression of attention-logit gradients through the null space of the Softmax Jacobian once values homogenize.
  To address this, we propose Mean-Variance Split (MV-Split) Residuals, which combine a separately gained centered residual update with a leaky trunk-mean replacement. On a 400-layer single-stream DiT, MV-Split prevents the divergent collapse that crashes the un-stabilized baseline; it tracks close to the baseline's pre-crash trajectory while remaining substantially better than token-isotropic gating methods such as LayerScale across the full schedule. Finally, we present a 1000-layer DiT as a scale-validation run at boundary scales, establishing that the architecture remains stably trainable at extreme depth.

</details>


### [154] [In-Context Black-Box Optimization with Unreliable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06187)
*Nicolas Samuel Blumer,Julien Martinelli,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FICBO方法，通过预训练一个反馈感知的Transformer模型，使其能够在测试时同时利用优化历史和廉价的辅助反馈信息进行黑盒优化，有效利用有价值反馈的同时对噪声或误导性反馈保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学和工程中的黑盒优化常伴随辅助反馈信息（如专家建议、模拟器等），但这些信息可能存在偏差或误导。现有的反馈感知贝叶斯优化方法通常仅处理单一任务，泛化能力有限；而上下文优化器通常假设优化历史是唯一信号，忽略了辅助反馈的利用。因此，需要一种能综合利用多源反馈并适应其可靠性的方法。

Method: 提出反馈感知的上下文黑盒优化（FICBO）框架。首先设计了一个结构化反馈先验，建模不同反馈源在访问权限、相关性及相对于真实目标的失真度方面的差异；随后基于此先验预训练一个反馈感知的Transformer模型。在测试时，该模型通过对比观测到的目标值与辅助信号，在上下文中动态估计各反馈源的可靠性，从而优化查询选择策略。

Result: 在合成任务和真实世界任务上的实验表明，FICBO能够有效利用有信息量的反馈源提升优化性能，同时对弱相关或误导性的反馈源保持鲁棒性，整体表现优于其他基线方法。此外，实验还展示了模型如何感知测试时的反馈源特性，验证了其可解释性。

Conclusion: FICBO成功地将上下文学习能力与多源反馈利用相结合，为黑盒优化问题提供了一种既能加速搜索又具备鲁棒性的解决方案，并在实验中展现了良好的性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Black-box optimization in science and engineering often comes with side information: experts, simulators, pretrained predictors, or heuristics can suggest which candidates look promising. This information can accelerate search, but it can also be biased, input-dependent, or misleading. Feedback-aware BO methods typically handle one task at a time, limiting their ability to generalize over multiple sources of feedback. In-context optimizers address cross-task adaptation, but usually assume that optimization history is the only available signal at test time. We study feedback-informed in-context black-box optimization (FICBO), where a pretrained optimizer conditions on both the observed history and cheap auxiliary feedback for the current candidate set. We introduce a structured feedback prior that models how feedback sources vary in their access, relevance, and distortion relative to the true objective, and use it to pretrain a feedback-aware transformer. At test time, the model estimates source reliability in context by comparing observed objective values with auxiliary signals, improving query selection. On synthetic and real-world tasks, FICBO effectively exploits informative feedback while remaining robust to weak or misleading sources, improving over other baselines. Empirical investigations further illustrate how the model perceives test-time sources, offering insights into its interpretability and decision-making process.

</details>


### [155] [Constrained Contextual Bandits with Adversarial Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06190)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Abhishek Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个简单、模块化的框架，将带有预算约束的上下文赌博问题归约为标准的无约束上下文赌博问题，首次在对抗性上下文设置下实现了改进的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究预算约束下的上下文赌博问题，特别是在对抗性上下文场景中。现有工作主要关注随机上下文，而本文旨在处理更一般的对抗性上下文情况，同时控制遗憾和预算约束违反程度。

Method: 基于Foster等人[2018]的SquareCB框架，提出一个利用在线回归预言机的框架，通过自适应定义代理奖励函数，将约束问题归约为标准的无约束上下文赌博问题。采用标准可实现性假设，关注持续设置（预算耗尽后算法继续运行）。

Result: 成功将预算约束问题转化为无约束问题，在对抗性上下文设置下获得了比以往工作更优的理论保证，并提供了高效算法和简洁透明的分析。

Conclusion: 该方法为预算约束上下文赌博问题提供了一个通用且有效的解决方案，扩展了现有理论的适用范围，具有简洁的理论分析和实用价值。

Abstract: We study budget-constrained contextual bandits with adversarial contexts, where each action yields a random reward and incurs a random cost. We adopt the standard realizability assumption: conditioned on the observed context, rewards and costs are drawn independently from fixed distributions whose expectations belong to known function classes. We focus on the continuing setting, in which the algorithm operates over the entire horizon even after the budget for cumulative cost is exhausted. In this setting, the objective is to simultaneously control regret and the violation of the budget constraint. Building on the seminal $\mathsf{SquareCB}$ framework of Foster et al. [2018], we propose a simple and modular framework that leverages online regression oracles to reduce the constrained problem to a standard unconstrained contextual bandit problem with adaptively defined surrogate reward functions. In contrast to prior works, which focus on stochastic contexts, our reduction yields improved guarantees for more general adversarial contexts, together with an efficient algorithm with a compact and transparent analysis.

</details>


### [156] [Bandit Learning in General Open Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06202)
*Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了开放系统中的赌博机学习问题，提出了新的复杂度概念（预训练度、稳定性），开发了具有可证明保证的global-UCB学习方法，并证明了后悔界依赖的紧密性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于开放系统赌博机学习的研究施加了实践中经常违反的结构假设。新到达的智能体会产生内生非平稳性；智能体模式决定了信息积累速度；新智能体使得后悔值随时间范围进一步增长。需要为一般开放系统建立统一的学习范式。

Method: 建立了具有一般动态的统一开放系统赌博机问题框架，包括异质性奖励和一般智能体模式。引入新概念：预训练度（量化智能体进入时携带的信息量）、稳定性（衡量新智能体对系统的影响）、全局动态后悔值（比较所有活跃智能体与变化最优臂的累积期望奖励）。开发了具有可证明保证的认证全局UCB学习方法。

Result: 后悔界揭示了进入不确定性通过预训练度线性进入系统；在稳定机制下，后悔值由识别持续最优臂所需时间以及智能体模式决定。通过困难实例的下界证明了这些依赖关系的紧密性。

Conclusion: 本文为一般开放系统赌博机学习提供了统一的理论框架，新提出的概念能够有效刻画系统内在复杂度，所开发的方法具有可证明的理论保证，且依赖关系已被证明是紧密的。

Abstract: Recent developments in digital platforms have highlighted the prevalence of open systems, where agents can arrive and depart over time. While bandit learning in open systems has recently received initial attention, existing work imposes structural assumptions that are frequently violated in practice. A learning paradigm for general open systems creates fresh challenges: newly arriving agents induce endogenous non-stationarity; agent patterns determine how quickly information accumulates; and new agents make regret scale further with the time horizon. To this end, we formulate a unified open-system bandit problem with general dynamics, including heterogeneous rewards and general agent patterns. We introduce new concepts to capture the inherent complexities: the \emph{pre-training degree} of new agents quantifies how much information an agent carries upon entry, \emph{stability} measures the impact of new agents on the system, and \emph{global dynamic regret} compares the cumulative expected reward of all active agents with that of the varying optimal arms. We develop certified global-UCB learning methodologies with provable guarantees. Our regret bounds reveal that entry uncertainty enters linearly via the pre-training degree, while in stable regimes, regret is governed by the time needed to identify a persistent optimal arm, as well as by the agent patterns. We further show that these dependencies are tight via lower bounds in hard instances.

</details>


### [157] [Federation of Experts: Communication Efficient Distributed Inference for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06206)
*Muhammad Shahir Abdurrahman,Chun Deng,Azalia Mirhoseini,Philip Levis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“专家联合”（Federation of Experts, FoE）的新型架构，通过将MoE块重构为多个集群并限制all-to-all通信在节点内部，显著降低了分布式LLM推理的通信开销和延迟（最高提升5.2倍），同时保持了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家模型虽然提高了大语言模型的计算效率，但在分布式推理场景中，专家间的Token嵌入通信（all-to-all通信）成为主要的性能瓶颈，限制了推理吞吐量和延迟表现。

Method: 提出FoE架构，将Transformer层中的MoE块重构为多个MoE集群。每个集群仅负责一个KV头，并在集群内部应用专家并行。集群间通过求和同步注意力后的残差，用于下一MoE块的路由与分发。该设计在单节点内消除了all-to-all通信，在多节点场景下将其限制在节点内部网络中。

Result: 在LongBench基准测试中，FoE显著提升了推理吞吐量并降低了延迟：端到端前向传播延迟降低高达5.2倍，TTFT降低3.62倍，TBT降低1.95倍，同时在与同等规模和训练配置的MoE模型相比，保持了相当的生成质量。

Conclusion: FoE架构有效解决了分布式MoE模型推理中的通信瓶颈问题，通过结构性重组显著提升了推理效率，且未牺牲模型性能，为大规模LLM的高效部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture of experts has emerged as the primary mechanism for making Large Language Models (LLMs) computationally efficient. However, in distributed settings, communicating token embeddings between experts is a significant bottleneck.
  We present the novel Federation of Experts (FoE) architecture. FoE restructures the MoE block of a transformer layer into multiple MoE clusters. Each cluster is responsible for only one of the KV heads and expert parallelism is applied between those experts. Between clusters, a sum synchronizes the post-attention residuals, which then drives routing and dispatch for the next MoE block. In a single-node setting, FoE completely eliminates all-to-all communication as all experts within a group are contained on the same GPU. In multi-node settings, FoE confines all-to-all communication to the intra-node fabric, thus significantly reducing communication overhead.
  An implementation of FoE finds that on LongBench, FoE significantly improves inference throughput and latency in both single-node and multi-node settings, reducing the end-to-end forward-pass latency by up to 5.2x, TTFT by 3.62x, and TBT by 1.95x. It does so while achieving comparable generation quality to a mixture of experts model of the same size and training configuration.

</details>


### [158] [Contrastive Identification and Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06211)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究极限下的对比式识别与生成问题，学习者观察无标记的对比样本对（仅知两样本标签不同），在无噪声情况下给出了可识别类的精确刻画、对比闭包维度及严格的层级关系；而在有限对抗扰动下，对比模型相比传统正例模型表现出惊人的鲁棒性反转——存在某些概念类仅能被对比模型鲁棒识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的极限学习模型（Gold的识别模型及Kleinberg等人的生成模型）主要关注正例或全标记数据。然而，许多自然的监督信号本质上是关系型的，编码样本间的关系而非单个样本的标签。本文旨在研究对比式（contrastive）数据展示下的极限学习问题，即学习者观察到无标记的对比样本对（仅知两样本标签不同），以填补这一研究空白。

Method: 1. 定义对比式数据展示：学习者观察到无序对{ x,y }满足h(x)≠h(y)，但不知道哪个是正例。
2. 在无噪声设置下，提出了**公共交叉图（common crossing graph）**作为统一的技术对象，用于编码成对歧义、族级生成障碍和损坏缺陷。
3. 引入**对比闭包维度（contrastive closure dimension）**作为组合维度刻画。
4. 在有限对抗扰动设置下，研究算法的鲁棒性。

Result: 无噪声设置：
1. 对比可识别类的精确刻画：Angluin [1980]的tell-tale条件的几何细化（一行式表述）。
2. 引入对比闭包维度，精确刻画了均匀对比生成并给出了紧的样本复杂度。
3. 建立严格层级：对比生成与文本识别互不可比。

有限对抗扰动设置：
存在概念类，存在单个与扰动预算无关的算法可从对比对中识别，但即便只有一个损坏观测，也无法从正例中识别——呈现出急剧的反转。

Conclusion: 本文开创了对比式极限学习的研究方向，揭示了对比模型与传统模型在无噪声和有噪声（有限对抗扰动）设置下的本质差异。特别是发现了在有限对抗扰动下，对比模型相比传统正例模型具有显著的鲁棒性优势，这与无噪声设置的层级关系形成鲜明对比。公共交叉图作为统一技术工具，成功刻画了三种不同层面的性质。

Abstract: In the classical identification in the limit model of Gold [1967], a stream of positive examples is presented round by round, and the learner must eventually recover the target hypothesis. Recently, Kleinberg and Mullainathan [2024] introduced generation in the limit, where the learner instead must eventually output novel elements of the target's support. Both lines of work focus on positive-only or fully labeled data. Yet many natural supervision signals are inherently relational rather than singleton, which encode relationships between examples rather than labels of individual ones. We initiate the study of contrastive identification and generation in the limit, where the learner observes a contrastive presentation of data: a stream of unordered pairs $\{x,y\}$ satisfying $h(x)\ne h(y)$ for an unknown target binary hypothesis $h$, but which element is positive is hidden from the learner. We first present three results in the noiseless setting: an exact characterization of contrastive identifiable classes (a one-line geometric refinement of Angluin [1980]'s tell-tale condition), a combinatorial dimension called contrastive closure dimension (a contrasitive analogue of the closure dimension in Raman et al. [2025]) and exactly characterizing uniform contrastive generation with tight sample complexity, and a strict hierarchy in which contrastive generation and text identification are mutually incomparable. We then prove a sharp reversal under finite adversarial corruption: there exist classes identifiable from contrastive pairs under any finite corruption budget by a single budget-independent algorithm, yet not identifiable from positive examples under even one corrupted observation. The unifying technical object is the common crossing graph, which encodes pairwise ambiguity, family-level generation obstructions, and corruption defects in a single coverage-and-incidence language.

</details>


### [159] [Playing the network backward: A Game Theoretic Attribution Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06212)
*Jakob Paul Zimmermann,Jim Berend,Georg Loho,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将反向归因方法重新构建为扩展网络图上的双人博弈，提供了统一的比较框架，并在ViT-B/16上验证了新方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反向归因方法（如梯度、LRP和特定于Transformer的规则）缺乏一个统一的框架来比较其底层的反向计算过程，这限制了方法间的理论比较和改进。

Method: 将反向归因重新构建为扩展网络图上的双人博弈，基于Gaubert和Vlassopoulos的ReLU网络博弈。在此框架下，梯度和完整的alpha-beta-LRP家族作为特定均衡下博弈轨迹的积分出现，归因图成为轨迹分布的投影而非主要对象。解释属性（如局部化焦点、输入噪声鲁棒性、稳定注意力路由）可通过博弈论概念（策略正则化、风险规避、扩展行动集）来指定。

Result: 在ViT-B/16模型上，所选择的alpha-beta-LRP自适应方法在所有考虑的局部化指标上均优于先前的特定于Transformer的反向方法。

Conclusion: 博弈论框架成功统一了反向归因方法的理论基础，使得可以从博弈论角度设计和优化归因方法，实验验证了该框架的实用价值。

Abstract: Attribution methods explain which input features drive a model's prediction, making them central to model debugging and mechanistic interpretability. Yet backward attribution methods, including gradients, LRP, and transformer-specific rules, lack a shared framework in which to compare the underlying backward calculations. We introduce such a framework by recasting backward attribution as a two-player game on an extended network graph, building on Gaubert and Vlassopoulos' ReLU Net Game. Gradients and the full alpha-beta-LRP family arise as integrals over game trajectories under specific equilibria, so attribution maps become projections of trajectory distributions rather than the primary object. Desired explanation properties, such as localisation focus, robustness to input noise, or stable attention routing, can be specified as game-theoretic concepts, including policy regularization, risk aversion, and extended action sets, and translate directly into novel adaptations of the well-known backward rules. On ViT-B/16, one such selected adaptation of alpha-beta-LRP outperforms prior transformer-specific backward methods across all considered localisation metrics.

</details>


### [160] [AffineLens: Capturing the Continuous Piecewise Affine Functions of Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06218)
*Yi Wei,Xuan Qi,Furao shen,Jian Zhao,Vittorio Murino,Cigdem Beyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AffineLens框架，用于精确计算和可视化分段仿射神经网络中的超平面排列和多面体结构，实现对仿射区域的准确枚举和分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络可解释性和表达能力分析通常依赖间接代理指标（如激活统计或理论上界），缺乏在现实架构和有界输入域下枚举和可视化诱导区域划分的实用、准确工具。

Method: 开发AffineLens统一框架：给定校准的输入多面体，识别与域相交的神经元诱导超平面子集，以逐层方式枚举生成的仿射子区域，返回可证明非空的最大CPA区域及其内部代表点；利用CPA网络在固定激活模式下的仿射限制特性，支持批归一化、池化、残差连接、MLP和卷积层等现代组件。

Result: 提供了区域划分和决策边界的可视化功能，实现定性检查与定量区域计数；通过系统实证研究比较不同架构的区域复杂度指标，揭示了设计选择如何影响学习函数的几何结构。

Conclusion: AffineLens填补了PANNs区域分析的工具空白，为研究架构表达能力提供了实用工具，使研究者能够从几何视角理解和比较神经网络的设计选择及其对学习函数的影响。

Abstract: Piecewise affine neural networks (PANNs) provide a principled geometric perspective on neural network expressivity by characterizing the input--output map as a continuous piecewise affine (CPA) function whose complexity is governed by the number, arrangement, and shapes of its affine regions. However, existing interpretability and expressivity analyses often rely on indirect proxies (e.g., activation statistics or theoretical upper bounds) and rarely offer practical, accurate tools for enumerating and visualizing the induced region partition under realistic architectures and bounded input domains. In this work, we present AffineLens, a unified framework for computing the hyperplane arrangements and polyhedral structures underlying PANNs. Given a calibrated (bounded) input polytope, AffineLens identifies the subset of neuron-induced hyperplanes that intersect the domain, enumerates the resulting affine sub-regions in a layer-wise manner, and returns provably non-empty maximal CPA regions together with interior representatives. The framework further provides visualizations of region partitioning and decision boundaries, enabling qualitative inspection alongside quantitative region counts. By exploiting the affine restriction property of CPA networks under fixed activation patterns, AffineLens supports a broad class of modern components, including batch normalization, pooling, residual connections, multilayer perceptrons, and convolutional layers. Finally, we use AffineLens to perform a systematic empirical study of architectural expressivity, comparing networks through region complexity metrics and revealing how design choices influence the geometry of learned functions.

</details>


### [161] [Memory Inception: Latent-Space KV Cache Manipulation for Steering LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06225)
*Andy Zeyi Liu,Michael Zhang,Ilana Greenberg,Adam Alnasser,Lucas Baker,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Memory Inception (MI)，一种无需训练的大语言模型引导方法，通过在特定层插入文本派生的KV键值对实现潜注意力空间引导。MI在人格引导任务中实现了最佳的控制-漂移权衡，支持对话中途行为调整，在结构化推理任务上超越可见提示并最高减少118倍KV存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引导方法存在权衡困境：指令提示法控制力强但需在每层缓存引导token且会干扰长交互；激活引导法紧凑但控制力弱且不支持大型结构化提醒。需要一种既能保持强控制力又节省存储空间、支持结构化引导的方法。

Method: MI将引导视为选择性KV分配问题，从文本派生键值(KV)库，仅在选定层注入潜注意力槽位，而非在整个提示缓存中实体化提醒内容。该方法无需训练，通过在潜注意力空间进行选择性KV分配实现模型引导。

Result: 在人格引导任务上，MI实现了最佳的整体控制-漂移权衡，与提示法相当且持续优于CAA；在可更新引导场景下，MI支持对话中途行为切换且无需重写可见记录，在Qwen3上实现最高后切换对齐度；在结构化推理任务(HARDMath和PHYSICS)上，MI在10/12个实验设置中超越可见提示，同时将内容匹配的KV存储减少高达118倍。

Conclusion: MI作为一种强大的引导方法，特别适用于引导内容持久、结构化或在可见记录中维护成本高的场景，成功平衡了控制强度与存储效率，为LLM引导提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Steering large language models (LLMs) is usually done by either instruction prompting or activation steering. Prompting often gives strong control, but caches guidance tokens at every layer and can clutter long interactions; activation steering is compact but typically weaker and does not support large structured reminders. We introduce memory inception (MI), a training-free method that steers in latent attention space by inserting text-derived key-value (KV) banks only at selected layers. Rather than materializing reminder content throughout the prompt cache, MI treats steering as selective KV allocation, injecting latent slots only where the model routes to them. On matched personality-steering tasks, MI gives the best overall control--drift trade-off, remaining competitive with prompting while consistently outperforming CAA. On updateable guidance, MI supports mid-conversation behavior shifts without rewriting the visible transcript, achieving the highest post-shift alignment on Qwen3. On structured reasoning, MI outperforms visible prompting on HARDMath and PHYSICS (10/12 subject$\times$mode cells), serving as proxies for structured reasoning in verifiable domains, while cutting content-matched KV storage by up to 118$\times$. These results position MI as a powerful steering method when guidance is persistent, structured, or expensive to keep in the visible transcript.

</details>


### [162] [Soft Deterministic Policy Gradient with Gaussian Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06228)
*Hyunjun Na,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Soft-DPG框架，通过高斯平滑的贝尔曼方程解决了传统DPG在稀疏或离散奖励下因依赖critic对动作的梯度而导致学习不稳定的问题，并实现了Soft DDPG算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的确定性策略梯度(DPG)在策略更新时依赖于critic函数对动作的可微性，但在实际控制问题中，稀疏或离散奖励导致Q函数不平滑，使得策略梯度定义不清，学习不稳定。

Method: 提出基于高斯平滑的平滑贝尔曼方程，定义新的动作值函数，推导出软确定性策略梯度(Soft-DPG)，消除了对critic动作梯度的显式依赖，确保梯度在非平滑Q函数下依然定义良好，并实现为深度强化学习算法Soft DDPG。

Result: 在标准连续控制基准测试及离散化奖励变体上的实验表明，Soft DDPG在密集奖励环境中保持竞争力，在大多数离散化奖励环境中显著优于标准DDPG，后者对不规则critic景观更为敏感。

Conclusion: Soft-DPG为非平滑奖励场景下的连续控制提供了理论完备且实用的解决方案，扩展了确定性策略梯度的适用范围，提升了算法在复杂奖励结构下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deterministic policy gradient (DPG) is widely utilized for continuous control; however, it inherently relies on the differentiability of the critic with respect to the action during policy updates. This assumption is violated in practical control problems involving sparse or discrete rewards, leading to ill-defined policy gradients and unstable learning. To address these challenges, we propose a principled alternative based on a smoothed Bellman equation formulated via Gaussian smoothing. Specifically, we define a novel action-value function based on a smoothed Bellman equation and derive the soft deterministic policy gradient (Soft-DPG). Our formulation eliminates explicit dependence on critic action-gradients and ensures that the gradient remains well-defined even for non-smooth Q-functions. We instantiate this framework into a deep reinforcement learning algorithm, which we call soft deep deterministic policy gradient (Soft DDPG). Empirical evaluations on standard continuous control benchmarks and their discretized-reward variants show that Soft DDPG remains competitive in dense-reward settings and provides clear gains in most discretized-reward environments, where standard DDPG is more sensitive to irregular critic landscapes.

</details>


### [163] [Band Together: Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination against Evasion-based Promotion Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06238)
*Guanmeng Xian,Ning Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态推荐系统中的逃避式推广攻击，提出了UAT-MC方法。该方法通过处理未知目标项问题并引入梯度对齐机制，有效提升了系统鲁棒性，同时保持了推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推荐系统虽然利用视觉和文本信号缓解了数据稀疏问题，但也更容易受到逃避式推广攻击。现有防御方法主要局限于单模态设置且多关注投毒式攻击，逃避式威胁的研究不足。本文识别出多用户推广设置下存在跨模态梯度失配问题，即视觉和文本扰动因不同用户群体的主导作用而优化方向不一致，削弱了攻击有效性并导致鲁棒训练低估最坏情况风险。

Method: 提出UAT-MC（带多模态协调的非目标对抗训练）方法。针对逃避式攻击中目标项未知的问题，将所有项视为潜在目标；引入梯度对齐机制显式纠正跨模态梯度失配，确保各模态间的扰动同步化，从而最大化用于鲁棒训练的对抗强度。

Result: 大量实验表明，UAT-MC显著提升了对推广攻击的鲁棒性，同时在防御-精度权衡下保持了可接受的推荐性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: UAT-MC有效解决了多模态推荐系统逃避式推广攻击的防御难题，通过创新的多模态协调机制提升了系统安全性，为实际应用提供了可行的防御方案。

Abstract: Multimodal recommender systems exploit visual and textual signals to alleviate data sparsity, but this also makes them more vulnerable to evasion-based promotion attacks. Existing defenses are largely limited to single-modal settings and mainly focus on poisoning-based threats, leaving evasion-based threats underexplored. In this work, we first identify a cross-modal gradient mismatch under the multi-user promotion setting, where visual and textual perturbations are optimized in inconsistent directions due to the dominance of distinct user groups. This phenomenon dilutes the attack effectiveness and leads robust training to underestimate worst-case risks. To address this issue, we propose Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination (UAT-MC). UAT-MC tackles the challenge of unknown targeted items in evasion-based attacks (as opposed to poisoning-based attacks) by treating all items as potential targets, and introduces a gradient alignment mechanism to explicitly correct this mismatch. This design ensures synchronized perturbations across modalities, thereby maximizing adversarial strength for robust training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UAT-MC significantly improves robustness against promotion attacks while maintaining acceptable recommendation performance under the defense-accuracy trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/gmXian/UAT-MC.

</details>


### [164] [When Graph Language Models Go Beyond Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06239)
*Masatsugu Yamada,Mahito Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 图语言模型在大规模数据下能超越单纯记忆并学习结构规律，主要在高频模式上表现良好，但低频模式仍存在覆盖不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前尚不清楚图语言模型是否真正学习到了结构规律，还是仅仅记住了训练图；现有的聚合保真度指标无法解决这一问题。

Method: 开发了一种校准诊断协议，结合频繁子图挖掘、图级自举基线以及三层频率分层，以区分记忆与结构对齐。

Result: 在五个TU基准测试中，小规模模型的对齐基本等同于逐字记忆；而在375万图的大规模下，逐字记忆急剧下降，但排序相关性仍接近上限。高频模式在各尺度下均被良好复现，而稀有模式覆盖较差。

Conclusion: 图语言模型在大规模下确实能获取超越记忆的结构规律，具有跨序列化方式的鲁棒性，但低频模式的生成能力仍受限制。

Abstract: It remains unclear whether graph language models learn structural regularities or merely memorize training graphs; this cannot be resolved by current aggregate fidelity metrics alone. We develop a calibrated diagnostic protocol that combines frequent subgraph mining, a graph-level bootstrap baseline, and three-level frequency stratification to disentangle memorization from structural alignment. Using this framework, we show that graph language models can acquire structural regularities beyond memorization at scale, primarily in the high-frequency regime. This is supported by the following empirical evidence: On five TU benchmarks, LLaMA-style graph language models reach high subgraph-rank correlation, yet their alignment is matched or exceeded by the memorization bootstrap in most cases. At small scale, under our bootstrap diagnostic, fidelity is largely indistinguishable from verbatim recall. In contrast, at large scale with 3.75M graphs, verbatim memorization drops sharply while rank correlation remains near ceiling. Crucially, in a separate fixed-subsample analysis, frequent subgraph mining restricted to the novel-only subset closely tracks the corresponding all-generation Spearman correlation, providing evidence that the alignment is not driven solely by verbatim recall. Across all scales, high-frequency patterns are well reproduced, while rare patterns remain poorly covered, and this deficit narrows only marginally as capacity increases. We observe the same scale-dependent crossover under two distinct graph serializations (canonical DFS code and action sequences), providing evidence of robustness in our analysis.

</details>


### [165] [Cumulative-Goodness Free-Riding in Forward-Forward Networks: Real, Repairable, but Not Accuracy-Dominant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06240)
*Amirhossein Yousefiramandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Forward-Forward训练中存在的“层搭便车”问题，提出了三种局部修正方案来缓解该问题。虽然这些方法显著改善了层分离指标，但对最终精度的提升有限，说明其他因素是限制性能的主因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Forward-Forward训练的累积优度变体中，研究者观察到后层可能继承已被前层部分分离的任务。本文旨在形式化这一“层搭便车”现象，并探究其对模型训练的影响及可能的解决方案。

Method: 1. 形式化定义“层搭便车”现象：在softplus FF准则下，到达第d块的类别判别梯度随前置块累积的正边际呈指数衰减。
2. 提出三种局部修正方案：逐块修正、硬度门控、深度缩放，这些方法不依赖反向传播梯度即可恢复当前层的分离度量。

Result: 1. 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，三种修正方案显著改善了层分离统计量，深层增益达4-45倍，且精度变化小于1个百分点。
2. Tiny ImageNet实验验证了所选逐块配置的泛化性，显示出层健康诊断与最终精度间的定性差距。
3. 校准实验表明，架构和数据增强选择对最终精度的影响大于所研究的训练规则修改。

Conclusion: 累积搭便车是一种真实存在且可修复的优化病理。然而，在所研究的FF训练规则、架构和数据集中，它并非限制可达精度的主导因素。架构和增强选择对最终精度的影响更为显著。

Abstract: Forward-Forward (FF) training allows each layer to learn from a local goodness criterion. In cumulative-goodness variants, however, later layers can inherit a task that earlier layers have already partially separated. We formalize this phenomenon as layer free-riding: under the softplus FF criterion, the class-discrimination gradient reaching block $d$ decays exponentially with the positive margin accumulated by preceding blocks. We then study three local remedies -- per-block, hardness-gated, and depth-scaled -- that recover current-layer separation measures without relying on backpropagated gradients. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, these remedies dramatically improve layer-separation statistics, with $4\times$--$45\times$ gains in deeper layers, while changing accuracy by less than one percentage point for non-degenerate training procedures. Tiny ImageNet provides a tougher cross-dataset check for our selected block-wise configuration and reveals the same qualitative gap between layer-health diagnostics and final accuracy. Calibration experiments further show that architecture and augmentation choices have a larger effect on final accuracy than the training-rule modifications studied here. Cumulative free-riding is therefore a real and repairable optimization pathology. Nonetheless, for the FF training rules, architectures, and datasets we study, it is not the dominant factor limiting achievable accuracy.

</details>


### [166] [Structure-Preserving Gaussian Processes Via Discrete Euler-Lagrange Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06246)
*Jan-Hendrik Ewering,Kathrin Flaßkamp,Niklas Wahlström,Thomas B. Schön,Thomas Seel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了拉格朗日高斯过程（LGPs），一种能够仅从稀疏位置数据学习动力学的新方法。该方法通过离散受迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程保持几何结构，克服了能量漂移问题，实现了物理一致性和稳定的长期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力学学习方法通常需要速度或动量信息，但在实际场景（如动作捕捉或视觉伺服）中往往只能获取位置测量数据。此外，传统方法可能导致系统能量的错误漂移，影响长期预测的稳定性和物理一致性。

Method: 基于离散受迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程和变分离散化方案，构建用于高斯过程调节的线性算子。该方法在设计上保持了Lagrange-d'Alembert原理的几何结构（在无外力情况下），使模型在本质上有界于哈密顿结构，从而避免能量漂移。核心创新在于仅使用离散位置快照即可学习动力学，无需速度或动量信息。

Result: 在多个合成和真实案例研究（包括具有迟滞特性的软体机器人）中验证了方法的有效性。实验结果表明：LGPs能够仅从稀疏位置数据学习物理一致的动力学模型；相比现有方法具有更高的数据效率；提供不确定性量化能力；实现稳定的长期预测，无能量漂移现象。

Conclusion: LGPs成功解决了从离散位置数据学习动力学的关键问题，通过保持几何结构确保了物理一致性和长期稳定性。该方法在数据效率、泛化能力和不确定性量化方面表现优异，特别适用于只能获取位置测量的实际应用场景，为动力学学习提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Lagrangian Gaussian Processes (LGPs) for probabilistic and data-efficient learning of dynamics via discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations. Importantly, the geometric structure of the Lagrange-d'Alembert principle, which governs the motion of dynamical systems, is preserved by construction in the absence of external forces. This allows learning physically consistent models that overcome erroneous drift in the system's energy, thereby providing stable long-term predictions. At the core of our approach lie linear operators for Gaussian process conditioning, constructed from discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations and variational discretization schemes. Thereby and unlike prior work, the method enables learning dynamics from discrete position snapshots, i.e., without access to a system's velocities or momenta. This is particularly relevant for a large class of practical scenarios where only position measurements are available, for instance, in motion capture or visual servoing applications. We demonstrate the data-efficiency and generalization capabilities of the LGPs in various synthetic and real-world case studies, including a real-world soft robot with hysteresis. The experimental results underscore that the LGPs learn physically consistent dynamics with uncertainty quantification solely from sparse positional data and enable stable long-term predictions.

</details>


### [167] [The Role of Node Features in Graph Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06250)
*Jan von Pichowski,Alžbeta Hrabošová,Ingo Scholtes,Christopher Blöcker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了图池化性能增益不稳定的根本原因：节点特征需与图拓扑良好对齐，且提出了特征质量评估指标，在满足该条件时池化能有效提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图池化在图分类任务中相比标准WL-1表达能力的GNN往往只能带来边际或不一致的增益，作者旨在研究并缩小这一性能差距。

Method: 通过分析节点特征与图拓扑之间的交互作用及其对池化目标的影响，形式化了节点特征实现有效池化的基本要求，并引入了特征质量的量化度量标准。

Result: 实验评估表明，当节点特征满足所提出的要求（即与图拓扑良好对齐）时，池化操作确实能带来益处并在合适数据集上提升性能。

Conclusion: 现有经验网络中常被忽视的“节点特征需与图拓扑对齐”这一条件是影响图池化有效性的关键因素，满足该条件可释放池化的潜力。

Abstract: Graph pooling is commonly applied in graph classification, yet its empirical gains over standard WL-1 expressive GNNs are often marginal or inconsistent. We study this gap by analysing the interaction between node features and graph topology and their effect on pooling objectives. Our analysis reveals that pooling operators require node features that are well-aligned with the graph's topology -- a condition often overlooked and not guaranteed in empirical networks. We formalise fundamental requirements for node features to enable effective pooling, and introduce a quantitative measure of feature quality. Our empirical evaluation shows that, when these requirements are satisfied, pooling can be beneficial and improve performance on appropriate datasets.

</details>


### [168] [The Weight Gram Matrix Captures Sequential Feature Linearization in Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06258)
*Taehun Cha,Daniel Beaglehole,Adityanarayanan Radhakrishnan,Donghun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了特征学习方程，通过权重Gram矩阵将权重更新与特征演化联系起来，揭示了深度网络通过顺序线性化表示来学习的机制，统一解释了神经崩溃等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度神经网络如何学习表示仍然是机器学习理论中的核心挑战，现有研究缺乏一个统一的框架来分析训练过程中特征的演化规律。

Method: 提出了一个以特征为中心的分析框架，引入特征学习方程（Feature Learning Equation），将权重Gram矩阵识别为捕获特征动力学的关键对象；定义了虚拟协方差（Virtual Covariance）来描述特征演化，并引入目标线性（Target Linearity）指标量化特征与目标之间的线性对齐程度。

Result: 通过分析训练过程和逐层动力学，发现深度网络学习过程会顺序地将表示转化为目标线性结构；该线性化视角统一解释了多个实证现象，包括神经崩溃（Neural Collapse）和生成模型中的线性插值现象。

Conclusion: 提出的特征中心框架和特征学习方程为理解神经网络训练动力学提供了新的理论视角，线性化观点能够统一解释多种经验现象，为深度学习理论提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Understanding how deep neural networks learn representations remains a central challenge in machine learning theory. In this work, we propose a feature-centric framework for analyzing neural network training by relating weight updates to feature evolution. We introduce a simple identity, the Feature Learning Equation, which identifies the weight Gram matrix as the key object capturing feature dynamics. This enables us to interpret gradient descent as implicitly inducing a hypothetical evolution of features, whose covariance structure - termed the Virtual Covariance - characterizes how representations evolve during training. Building on this perspective, we introduce Target Linearity, a measure quantifying the linear alignment between features and targets. By analyzing the training and layer-wise dynamics, we show that deep networks learn to sequentially transform representations toward target-linear structure. This linearization perspective provides a unified interpretation of several empirical phenomena, including Neural Collapse and linear interpolation in generative models.

</details>


### [169] [Trade-off Functions for DP-SGD with Subsampling based on Random Shuffling: Tight Upper and Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06259)
*Marten van Dijk,Murat Bilgehan Ertan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在$f$-DP框架下给出了基于随机洗牌的DP-SGD的紧确分析，得出了透明可解释的闭式界限；在一定条件下只需约$1.14\times 10^6$轮迭代即可达到有意义的隐私；提出新证明技术将$\delta$的依赖从线性$E$改进到$O(\sqrt{E})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于泊松子采样的$f$-DP分析只能给出非闭式的隐式公式，缺乏透明性和可解释性。作者希望利用随机洗牌子采样的特性，推导出紧确、闭式且易于理解和应用的隐私界限。

Method: 利用Berry-Esseen定理推导紧确的闭式界限；提出一种基于大数定律推广的新证明技术，分析多轮复合后的渐近性质，并将结果与泊松子采样情形进行对比。

Result: 在$σ\geq \sqrt{3/\ln M}$条件下，得到了紧确的闭式界限；对于$δ=1/100$和$σ=1$，约$M\approx 1.14\times 10^6$轮迭代即可实现有意义的差分隐私；多轮复合时，基于Berry-Esseen的方法$\delta$与$E$呈线性关系，新方法将依赖改进为$O(\sqrt{E})$，并证明当$E=c_M^2M$且$c_M\to 0$时，复合权衡函数一致收敛于理想随机猜测对角线$1-a$。

Conclusion: 随机洗牌相比泊松子采样能够提供透明可解释的紧确隐私界限；新证明技术突破了Berry-Esseen方法的限制，显著改善了多轮训练中$\delta$的依赖关系，为实际应用提供了更好的理论保障；显式收敛率的刻画仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: We derive a tight analysis of the trade-off function for Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) with subsampling based on random shuffling within the $f$-DP framework. Our analysis covers the regime $σ\geq \sqrt{3/\ln M}$, where $σ$ is the noise multiplier and $M$ is the number of rounds within a single epoch. Unlike $f$-DP analyses for Poisson subsampling, which yield non-closed implicit formulas that can be machine computed but are non-transparent, random shuffling admits a tight analysis yielding transparent and interpretable closed-form bounds. Our concrete bounds, derived via the Berry-Esseen theorem, are tight up to constant factors within the proof framework. We demonstrate worked parameter settings for a single epoch ($E=1$) with a corresponding trade-off function $\geq 1-a-δ$, that is, only $δ$ below the ideal random guessing diagonal $1-a$: For $δ= 1/100$ and $σ= 1$, roughly $M \approx 1.14\times 10^6$ rounds and $N \approx 1.14\times 10^7$ training samples suffice to achieve meaningful differential privacy. This is in contrast to recent negative results for the regime $σ\leq 1/\sqrt{2 \ln M}$. Our concrete bounds can be composed over multiple epochs leading to $δ$ having a linear in $E$ dependency, which restricts $E=O(\sqrt{M})$. To go beyond Berry--Esseen, we introduce a new proof technique based on a generalization of the law of large numbers that yields an asymptotic random guessing diagonal-limit result: if $E=c_M^2M$ with $c_M\to 0$, then the $E$-fold composed trade-off function satisfies $f^{\otimes E}(a)\to 1-a$ uniformly in $a\in[0,1]$ with $δ$ having only an $O(\sqrt{E})$ dependency. We compare this asymptotic regime with the corresponding Poisson subsampling asymptotic, and highlight the characterization of explicit convergence rates as an open question.

</details>


### [170] [Beyond Rigid Alignment: Graph Federated Learning via Dual Manifold Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06260)
*Wentao Yu,Bo Han,Jie Yang,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedGMC方法，通过双重流形校准机制解决图联邦学习中的语义和结构异构性问题，在保持全局共性的同时最大化本地个性化表示空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图联邦学习方法通过刚性对齐（模型参数或原型）解决异构性问题，但隐含依赖全局线性假设，使用单一全局一致的表示空间来概括本地数据分布，严重压缩了客户端的个性化表示空间，无法保留多样化的本地图分布。

Method: 提出联邦图流形校准（FedGMC）框架，从统一流形视角解决异构性：(1)语义异构性：服务器通过等距语义锚点构建几何最优语义流形，指导本地语义流形校准；(2)结构异构性：服务器通过构建全局结构模板建立全局结构流形，指导本地结构流形校准；(3)服务器通过聚合本地流形动态优化全局语义和结构流形。

Result: 在11个同质性和异质性图数据集上进行广泛实验，FedGMC有效平衡了全局共性和本地个性化，显著优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: FedGMC通过双重流形校准机制成功解决了图联邦学习中的语义和结构异构性挑战，避免了刚性对齐的局限性，在保持全局共性的同时最大化本地个性化表示空间，为图联邦学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Graph Federated Learning (GFL) enables collaborative representation learning across distributed subgraphs while preserving privacy. However, heterogeneity remains a critical challenge, as subgraphs across clients typically differ significantly in both semantics and structures. Existing methods address heterogeneity by enforcing the rigid alignment of model parameters or prototypes between clients and the server. However, these alignments implicitly rely on a restrictive global linearity assumption that summarizes local data distributions using a single and globally consistent representation space. This severely compresses the personalized representation space of clients and fails to preserve diverse local graph distributions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Federated Graph Manifold Calibration (FedGMC), a novel paradigm that tackles semantic heterogeneity and structural heterogeneity from a unified manifold perspective. Instead of enforcing rigid alignment, FedGMC introduces a dual manifold calibration mechanism that preserves global commonalities while maximizing the personalized representation space of local clients. Specifically, for semantic heterogeneity, the server constructs a geometrically optimal semantic manifold via equidistant semantic anchors, so as to guide the calibration of local semantic manifolds. For structural heterogeneity, the server constructs a global structural manifold by building global structural templates, so as to guide the calibration of local structural manifolds. Finally, the server dynamically refines both global semantic manifolds and structural manifolds by aggregating local manifolds. Extensive experiments on eleven homophilic and heterophilic graphs demonstrate that FedGMC effectively balances global commonality and local personalization, thereby significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [171] [Inference-Time Refinement Closes the Synthetic-Real Gap in Tabular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06261)
*Eugenio Lomurno,Filippo Balzarini,Francesco Benelle,Francesca Pia Panaccione,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TARDIS框架，通过在推理时优化预训练的表格数据扩散模型，实现了在下游任务中超越真实数据表现的效果，中位数提升达8.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的表格数据生成方法虽然接近但很少超越真实数据的效用，且以往工作主要通过训练时的架构改进、规模扩展和重新训练来缩小合成数据与真实数据的差距。推理时优化预训练模型输出的方案在表格数据合成领域尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出TARDIS推理时优化框架，基于冻结的预训练骨干网络，通过Tree-structured Parzen Estimator搜索分数级引导配置，并结合内部网格搜索进行后验样本选择和可选的软标签蒸馏步骤。核心创新为双向Chamfer优化（BCR），同时通过分数级梯度进行连续优化和通过批次排序进行离散优化，最小化合成样本与真实样本间的对称Chamfer泛函。

Result: 在15个二分类、多分类和回归基准测试中，TARDIS相比真实数据训练的模型获得中位数+8.6%的下游任务提升（95%置信区间[+3.3, +16.4]，Wilcoxon p=0.016，15个数据集中11个严格胜出），相比TabDiff骨干网络平均提升+12.9%（p<10^-4），同时在流形保真度、多样性和样本级隐私方面与骨干网络相当。单块消费级GPU上推理时优化仅需1至80分钟。

Conclusion: 推理时优化预训练表格数据扩散模型可以超越真实数据的效用，证明了BCR是主导的优化模式。该方法无需重新训练骨干网络，为缩小合成数据与真实数据差距提供了高效的新途径。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generators set the current state of the art for synthetic tabular data. These methods approach but rarely exceed real-data utility, and closing this synthetic-real gap has so far been pursued exclusively at training time, via architectural advances, scaling, and retraining of monolithic generators. The inference-time alternative, i.e., refining the outputs of a pre-trained backbone with parameters left untouched, has remained largely unexplored for tabular synthesis. We introduce TARDIS (Tabular generation through Refinement, Distillation, and Inference-time Sampling), an inference-time refinement framework that operates on a frozen pre-trained backbone, configured per dataset by a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator search over score-level guidance during reverse diffusion, with each trial's objective set by an inner grid search over post-hoc sample selectors and an optional soft-label distillation step. The search space encodes a single mathematical pattern we name Bidirectional Chamfer Refinement (BCR): the symmetric Chamfer functional between synthetic and real samples is minimized both continuously, via a score-level gradient, and discretely, via batch-ranking post-generation. The per-dataset search recovers BCR-aligned configurations on most datasets, evidence for BCR as the dominant refinement pattern. Across 15 binary, multiclass, and regression benchmarks TARDIS achieves a median +8.6% downstream-task improvement over models trained on real data (95% CI [+3.3, +16.4], Wilcoxon p=0.016, 11/15 strict wins) and improves over the TabDiff backbone on all 15 datasets (mean +12.9%, p<10^-4), matching the backbone on manifold fidelity, diversity, and sample-level privacy. Inference-time refinement of a pre-trained tabular diffusion backbone reaches and exceeds real-data utility in 1 to 80 minutes on a single consumer-grade GPU.

</details>


### [172] [Can Attribution Predict Risk? From Multi-View Attribution to Planning Risk Signals in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06264)
*Le Yang,Ruoyu Chen,Haijun Liu,Jiawei Liang,ShangQuan Sun,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端自动驾驶规划的分层归因框架，通过粗到细的区域归因策略识别关键视图和区域，并提取三种归因统计量作为规划风险的预测信号，在碰撞检测等任务上取得了AUROC 0.77的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型从多视角输入生成轨迹，但存在决策不透明和风险难以定位的问题。现有方法（辅助监控模型或文本解释）与规划过程解耦，无法揭示轨迹生成背后的视觉证据；而归因方法虽直接，但规划任务与图像分类不同，需处理六视角输入和连续多步轨迹预测，且归因图能否支持风险识别尚未被探索。

Method: 提出分层归因框架：1）以与原始轨迹的L2一致性为目标，设计粗到细的区域归因策略，在六视角全输入中搜索候选区域并在其中细化归因；2）提取三种归因统计量作为规划风险的预测信号：归因熵（衡量规划器对联合视觉空间的依赖集中度）、相机内空间方差（表征各视角内归因的分散程度）、跨相机基尼系数（量化六个相机间归因分布的不均匀性）。

Result: 在BridgeAD、UniAD和GenAD数据集上的实验表明：所提统计量与规划风险相关，与轨迹误差的Spearman相关系数为0.30±0.07，碰撞检测的AUROC为0.77±0.04；该信号在未见场景上泛化良好（性能衰减可忽略），且在替代归因基线下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 所提分层归因框架成功将视觉证据与轨迹生成关联，提取的归因统计量可作为规划风险的有效预测信号，为端到端自动驾驶的可解释性和风险识别提供了新途径。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models generate future trajectories from multi-view inputs, improving system integration but introducing opaque decisions and hard-to-localize risks. Existing methods either rely on auxiliary monitoring models or generate textual explanations, but are decoupled from the planning process and fail to reveal the visual evidence underlying trajectory generation. While attribution offers a direct alternative, planning differs from image classification by taking six-view camera images as input and predicting continuous multi-step trajectories, requiring attribution to capture both critical views and regions and their influence on outputs. Moreover, whether attribution maps can support risk identification remains underexplored. To address this, we propose a hierarchical attribution framework for end-to-end planning. Specifically, using L2 consistency with the original trajectory as the objective, we design a coarse-to-fine region attribution strategy that searches candidate regions across the full six-view input and refines attribution within them. We further extract three attribution statistics as predictive signals for planning risk, including attribution entropy to measure how concentrated the planner's reliance is over the joint visual space, within-camera spatial variance to characterize how spread out the attribution is within each view, and cross-camera Gini coefficient to quantify how unevenly attribution is distributed across the six cameras. Experiments on BridgeAD, UniAD, and GenAD show that these statistics correlate with planning risk, achieving Spearman correlations of $0.30 \pm 0.07$ with trajectory error and AUROC of $0.77 \pm 0.04$ for collision detection. The signal generalizes to held-out scenes with negligible degradation and remains stable under an alternative attribution baseline.

</details>


### [173] [A Flow Matching Algorithm for Many-Shot Adaptation to Unseen Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06272)
*Tyler Ingebrand,Ruihan Zhao,Kushagra Gupta,David Fridovich-Keil,Sandeep P. Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FP-FM算法，通过学习基函数来表示速度场，实现了在无需额外推理训练的情况下，仅凭目标分布的样本即可快速适应新分布，在精度和召回率上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在自然语言条件图像生成等任务上已取得成功，但如何仅从示例数据点（而非显式条件）出发实现模型对新分布的快速适应，仍是一个相对未被探索且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 提出Flow Matching的函数投影法（FP-FM）。该方法学习一组基函数来张成训练分布对应的速度场空间；在面对新目标分布时，仅需将速度场最小二乘投影到该基函数空间即可完成适应，无需在推理时进行额外训练。同时引入了多种变体（如时间依赖系数）以平衡表达能力与计算开销。

Result: 在合成数据集和图像数据集上的实验表明，FP-FM在精度和召回率方面相比基线方法有显著提升，尤其在未见过的分布上取得了尤为突出的性能增益。

Conclusion: FP-FM为生成模型提供了一种高效、无需推理时重训练的数据驱动适应范式，有效解决了从示例样本进行分布适应的难题。

Abstract: While generative modeling has achieved remarkable success on tasks like natural language-conditioned image generation, enabling model adaptation from example data points remains a relatively underexplored and challenging problem. To this end, we propose Function Projection for Flow Matching (FP-FM), an algorithm that directly conditions generation on samples from the target distribution. FP-FM learns basis functions to span the velocity fields corresponding to a set of training distributions, and adapts to new distributions by computing a simple least-squares projection onto this basis. This enables efficient generation of samples from diverse target distributions without additional training at inference time. We further introduce multiple variants of FP-FM that provide a trade-off in expressivity and compute by enriching the coefficient calculation, e.g., by making the coefficients dependent on time. FP-FM achieves greatly improved precision and recall relative to baselines across synthetic and image-based datasets, with especially strong gains on unseen distributions.

</details>


### [174] [When Labels Have Structure: Improving Image Classification with Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06274)
*April Chan,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HACE（层次感知交叉熵），一种替代标准交叉熵的即插即用损失函数，通过预测聚合和祖先标签平滑利用类别层次结构。在端到端训练和线性探测两种设置下，HACE在多个数据集和架构上均优于标准交叉熵，平均准确率提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失函数将所有误分类视为同等重要，忽略了类别层次结构中编码的语义距离信息。然而在实际应用中，不同类别之间的误分类应当具有不同的惩罚程度（例如将“狗”误分类为“猫”比误分类为“汽车”更可接受），因此需要将层次结构信息显式融入损失函数设计中。

Method: HACE由两个核心组件构成：（1）预测聚合：将模型的概率质量沿类别层次结构向上传播，确保父节点累积其子节点的置信度；（2）祖先标签平滑：将真实标签的信号沿着从真实类别到根节点的路径进行分布，从而在训练过程中引入层次结构信息。HACE作为标准交叉熵的直接替代，无需改变模型架构即可使用。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、FGVC Aircraft和NABirds三个数据集上进行了评估。在端到端训练设置下（涵盖卷积和注意力机制的6种架构），HACE在18个架构-数据集组合中的15个上取得准确率提升，平均增益为4.66%。在使用冻结DINOv2-Large特征的线性探测设置下，HACE在所有三个数据集上均超越所有竞争方法，相比最佳基线平均提升2.18%。

Conclusion: HACE证明了将类别层次结构显式融入损失函数的有效性，能够显著提升分类性能。作为一种即插即用的替代方案，HACE具有良好的通用性，可广泛应用于不同的模型架构和训练范式，为利用语义层次信息改进分类任务提供了简单而有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy is the default classification loss across virtually all of machine learning, yet it treats all misclassifications equally, ignoring the semantic distances that a class hierarchy encodes. We propose Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy (HACE), a drop-in replacement for standard cross-entropy that incorporates a known class hierarchy directly into the loss. HACE combines two components: prediction aggregation, which propagates the model's probability mass upward through the class hierarchy to ensure that parent nodes accumulate the confidence of their children; and ancestral label smoothing, which distributes the ground-truth signal along the path from the true class to the root. We evaluate HACE on CIFAR-100, FGVC Aircraft, and NABirds in two regimes: end-to-end training across six architectures spanning convolutional and attention-based designs, and linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features. In end-to-end training, HACE improves accuracy over standard cross-entropy in 15 out of 18 architecture--dataset pairs, with a mean gain of 4.66\%. In linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features, HACE outperforms all competing methods on all three datasets, with a mean improvement of 2.18\% over the next best baseline.

</details>


### [175] [PACE: Prune-And-Compress Ensemble Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06278)
*Fabian Akkerman,Julien Ferry,Théo Guyard,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PACE框架，通过交替进行主动生成新学习器和剪枝冗余学习器两个阶段，在保证对原始集成模型忠实度的前提下，实现了更高效的集成模型压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成模型虽然预测性能优越，但通常需要聚合大量弱学习器，这会导致部署困难、可解释性差以及难以进行鲁棒性验证等问题。现有的解决方案（剪枝和压缩）各自存在局限性，需要一种能够结合两者优势的新方法。

Method: 提出PACE框架，采用两阶段策略：第一阶段通过理论指导的主动生成过程创建新学习器以增强初始集成的多样性；第二阶段当无法找到相关新学习器时，对增强后的集成进行剪枝。整个过程允许对原始集成的忠实度进行精细控制。

Result: 实验结果表明，PACE方法在性能上优于现有的剪枝和压缩方法，同时能够提供有理论依据的忠实度保证控制。

Conclusion: PACE成功地将剪枝和压缩两种范式有机结合，在保持模型性能的同时显著减少了集成模型的规模，为集成模型的实际部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Ensemble models achieve state-of-the-art performance on prediction tasks, but usually require aggregating a large number of weak learners. This can hinder deployment, interpretability, and downstream tasks such as robustness verification. Remedies to this issue fall into two main camps: pruning, which discards redundant learners, and compression, which generates new ones from scratch. We introduce PACE, a framework that interleaves these paradigms in a two-phase strategy. First, new learners are actively generated via a theoretically grounded procedure to enhance the diversity of the initial ensemble. When no more relevant learners can be found, a second phase of pruning is performed on this enriched ensemble. During both operations, PACE allows fine control on the faithfulness to the original ensemble. Experiments show that our method outperforms prior pruning and compression methods while offering principled control of faithfulness guarantees.

</details>


### [176] [INEUS: Iterative Neural Solver for High-Dimensional PIDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06281)
*Jean-Loup Dupret,Davide Gallon,Patrick Cheridito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了INEUS，一种用于求解偏积分微分方程（PIDEs）的无网格迭代神经求解器。该方法通过单跳跃采样替代显式非局部跳跃积分计算，将PIDE求解转化为递归回归问题序列，在保持全局求解能力的同时显著提升了非局部项的处理效率，适用于高维问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在处理偏积分微分方程（PIDEs）时面临非局部跳跃积分计算成本高、需要对完整PIDE残差进行昂贵微分等问题，特别是在高维场景下计算效率低下。因此需要一种既能保持全局求解能力，又能高效处理非局部项的神经求解器。

Method: INEUS是一种无网格迭代神经求解器。核心方法包括：（1）用单跳跃采样替代非局部跳跃积分的显式评估；（2）将PIDE求解重构为递归回归问题序列；（3）避免对完整PIDE残差进行计算昂贵的微分操作；（4）在整个时空域上学习全局解。方法有针对线性PIDEs的基于收缩的收敛性证明支持。

Result: 数值实验表明，INEUS能够为各种高维线性和非线性算例提供精确且可扩展的解。方法在保持求解精度的同时，显著提升了计算效率，特别是在高维PDE和PIDE问题上表现优异。理论上有针对线性PIDEs的基于收缩的收敛性证明作为支撑。

Conclusion: INEUS成功地将PIDE求解转化为递归回归问题，通过单跳跃采样高效处理非局部项，避免了昂贵微分计算。该方法为高维PDEs和PIDEs提供了一种高效、精确且理论有保证的神经求解方案，在高维计算金融等领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce INEUS, a meshfree iterative neural solver for partial integro-differential equations (PIDEs). The method replaces the explicit evaluation of nonlocal jump integrals with single-jump sampling and reformulates PIDE solving as a sequence of recursive regression problems. Like Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), INEUS learns global solutions over the entire space-time domain, yet it offers a more efficient treatment of nonlocal terms and avoids the computationally expensive differentiation of full PIDE residuals. These features make INEUS particularly well suited for high-dimensional PDEs and PIDEs. Supported by a contraction-based convergence proof for linear PIDEs, our numerical experiments show that INEUS delivers accurate and scalable solutions for various high-dimensional linear and nonlinear examples.

</details>


### [177] [Attributions All the Way Down? The Metagame of Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06295)
*Hubert Baniecki,Przemyslaw Biecek,Fabian Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“元博弈”框架，通过计算Shapley值量化特征间的二阶交互效应（元归因），即特征j对特征i归因的方向性影响，并证明了其层级分解性质及与现有交互指标的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型解释方法（一阶归因）仅关注单个特征的重要性，忽略了特征归因之间的相互影响。为了深入理解模型的内部机制和特征间的复杂交互关系，需要一种能够量化特征间二阶交互效应的方法。

Method: 将归因方法本身视为一个合作博弈，计算特征j对特征i归因的Shapley值，定义为元归因φ_{j→i}(f)。理论上证明了归因可以层级分解为元归因，并将其确立为现有交互指标的方向性扩展。

Result: 在多种场景下验证了元博弈框架的有效性：(i)量化指令微调语言模型中的token交互；(ii)解释视觉-语言编码器中的跨模态相似性；(iii)解释多模态扩散Transformer中的文本到图像概念。

Conclusion: 元博弈框架为量化二阶交互效应提供了理论完备且通用的解决方案，能够揭示跨模态和多模态模型中复杂的特征交互关系，增强了模型可解释性分析的能力。

Abstract: We introduce the metagame, a conceptual framework for quantifying second-order interaction effects of model explanations. For any first-order attribution $φ(f)$ explaining a model $f$, we measure the directional influence of feature $j$ on the attribution of feature $i$, denoted as meta-attribution $\varphi_{j \to i}(f)$, by treating the attribution method itself as a cooperative game and computing its Shapley value. Theoretically, we prove that attributions hierarchically decompose into meta-attributions, and establish these as directional extensions of existing interaction indices. Empirically, we demonstrate that the metagame delivers insights across diverse interpretability applications: (i) quantifying token interactions in instruction-tuned language models, (ii) explaining cross-modal similarity in vision-language encoders, and (iii) interpreting text-to-image concepts in multimodal diffusion transformers.

</details>


### [178] [Region Seeding via Pre-Activation Regularization: A Geometric View from Piecewise Affine Nerual Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06300)
*Yi Wei,Xuan Qi,Furao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种区域播种正则化方法，通过在训练早期促使神经元切换面靠近数据点来增加局部仿射区域数量，从而提高深度网络的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的区域计数理论主要关注网络架构，缺乏对优化过程如何影响数据附近分区形状的指导。标准训练实际实现的区域细化远少于架构理论支持的数量，因此需要一种方法来引导优化过程在数据相关区域产生更多的仿射区域。

Method: 提出了充分条件：当神经元切换面充分接近数据点时，确保其与局部邻域相交，从而严格增加局部仿射区域计数。基于此理论，设计了一种即插即用的区域播种正则化器，在训练早期鼓励分区，同时允许后续任务驱动的细化主导训练后期。

Result: 实验表明，该正则化器通过精确枚举增加了实现的仿射区域数量，在玩具数据集上提升了整体性能，在ImageNet-1k上提高了早期阶段准确率并达到可比（或略好）的最终准确率。

Conclusion: 该方法为训练时播种数据相关分区提供了原则性依据，成功将理论洞察转化为实用的正则化技术，在保持或略微提升最终性能的同时改善了早期训练效果。

Abstract: Deep networks with continuous piecewise affine activations induce polyhedral partitions of the input space, making the number of realized affine regions a natural measure of expressive capacity and a key determinant of how well the model can approximate nonlinear target functions. In practice, standard training realizes far fewer region refinements in data-visited neighborhoods than the architecture could in principle support, while existing region-count theory is primarily architectural and offers little guidance on how optimization shapes the realized partition near the data. Our theory provides a sufficient condition under which bringing neuron switching surfaces sufficiently close to data points ensures their intersection with local neighborhoods, which in turn implies a strict increase in the local affine-region count, yielding a principled training-time handle for seeding data-relevant partitions early in optimization. Guided by these results, we propose a plug-and-play region-seeding regularizer that encourages early partitioning while allowing task-driven refinement to dominate later in training. Experiments show that the regularizer increases the number of realized affine regions via exact enumeration and improves overall performance on toy datasets, while also improving early-stage accuracy and achieving comparable (or slightly improved) final accuracy on ImageNet-1k for classical models.

</details>


### [179] [Molecules Meet Language: Confound-Aware Representation Learning and Chemical Property Steering in Transformer-VAE Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06303)
*Zakaria Elabid,Jan Andrzejewski,Bartosz Brzoza,Attila Cangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对分子生成模型潜在空间可能存在的伪相关现象，提出了一种混淆感知的评估方法，在控制SELFIES语法特征干扰后，证实了部分化学性质在潜在空间中确实存在可利用的全局调控方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子生成模型通常假设潜在空间具有有意义的几何结构，但观察到的性质可预测性可能仅反映了序列层面的捷径（如字符串长度等伪相关），而非真实的化学组织结构。因此，需要区分潜在空间中的真实化学信号与表示层面的混淆因素。

Method: 1. 训练一个基于SELFIES的无监督自回归Transformer-VAE模型。
2. 冻结模型参数，使用RDKit描述符拟合线性探针，并将探针权重作为候选的全局导向方向。
3. 引入混淆感知评估框架，包含残差化分析、混淆方向对齐分析以及解码分子遍历，以剥离SELFIES长度、分支/环标记、token熵等非化学干扰因素的影响。

Result: 研究发现SELFIES的长度、分支标记、环标记和token熵在潜在空间中被强编码。在控制这些混淆因素后，证实了cLogP、FractionCSP3、HeavyAtomCount、TPSA、BertzCT和HBA等性质具有稳健的单调导向能力。非线性探针进一步揭示：部分性质存在稳定的全局导向方向，而另一些性质则更适合用局部潜在梯度来描述。

Conclusion: 在纠缠的分子潜在空间中，确实可以涌现出化学上有意义的导向方向，但这必须建立在对分子表示层面的混淆因素进行严格控制、并通过解码后的分子进行验证的基础上。该研究强调了分子生成模型评估中混淆控制的必要性。

Abstract: Molecular generative models often assume meaningful latent geometry, but apparent property predictability can reflect sequence-level shortcuts rather than chemical organization. We study this issue in an unsupervised autoregressive Transformer-VAE trained on SELFIES. After training, we freeze the model, fit linear probes to RDKit descriptors, and use the probe weights as candidate global steering directions. To separate chemical signal from SELFIES artifacts, we introduce a confound-aware evaluation based on residualization, confound-direction alignment analysis, and decoded-molecule traversal. This is necessary because SELFIES length, branch tokens, ring tokens, and token entropy are strongly encoded in the latent space. Under this confound-aware evaluation, we find robust monotonic steering for cLogP, FractionCSP3, HeavyAtomCount, TPSA, BertzCT, and HBA.
