*Murat Bilgehan Ertan,Xiaochen Zhu,Phuong Ha Nguyen,Marten van Dijk,Srinivas Devadas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PACZero，一种在互信息为零（I=0）的极端隐私条件下仍能保持可用效用的零阶大语言模型微调方法，在SST-2上达到88.99%准确率，接近非私有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私（DP）框架在高隐私 regime（ε<1）下需要无限噪声才能抵抗成员推理攻击（MIA），导致效用严重下降。研究动机是在严格的隐私约束（I(S*;Y)=0，即MIA后验成功率被限制在先验水平）下实现可用的模型微调效用。

Method: 基于PAC Privacy框架，提出两种零阶优化机制：1）利用子集聚合梯度的符号量化，在候选子集对更新方向达成一致时，释放的符号条件互信息为零；2）PACZero-MI通过精确校准预算化互信息；3）PACZero-ZPL在分歧步骤通过均匀硬币翻转实现I=0。在OPT-1.3B和OPT-6.7B上进行LoRA和全参数微调实验。

Result: 在SST-2数据集上，OPT-1.3B全参数微调在I=0条件下达到88.99±0.91%准确率，仅比非私有MeZO基线（91.1%）低2.1个百分点。在OPT-1.3B和OPT-6.7B上均获得了具有竞争力的SST-2准确率和非平凡的SQuAD F1分数。这是首个在ε<1高隐私 regime下产生可用效用的方法。

Conclusion: PACZero成功在互信息为零的极端隐私条件下实现了可用的模型效用，填补了现有DP方法在高隐私 regime下效用严重损失的空白，为隐私保护的大语言模型微调提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce PACZero, a family of PAC-private zeroth-order mechanisms for fine-tuning large language models that delivers usable utility at $I(S^*; Y_{1:T})=0$. This privacy regime bounds the membership-inference attack (MIA) posterior success rate at the prior, an MIA-resistance level the DP framework matches only at $\varepsilon=0$ and infinite noise. All DP-ZO comparisons below are matched at the MIA posterior level. The key insight is that PAC Privacy charges mutual information only when the release depends on which candidate subset is the secret. Sign-quantizing subset-aggregated zeroth-order gradients creates frequent unanimity, steps at which every candidate subset agrees on the update direction; at these steps the released sign costs zero conditional mutual information. We propose two variants that span the privacy-utility trade-off: PACZero-MI (budgeted MI via exact calibration on the binary release) and PACZero-ZPL ($I=0$ via a uniform coin flip on disagreement steps). We evaluate on SST-2 and SQuAD with OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B in both LoRA and full-parameter tracks. On SST-2 OPT-1.3B full fine-tuning at $I=0$, PACZero-ZPL reaches ${88.99\pm0.91}$, within $2.1$pp of the non-private MeZO baseline ($91.1$ FT). No prior method produces usable utility in the high-privacy regime $\varepsilon<1$, and PACZero-ZPL obtains competitive SST-2 accuracy and nontrivial SQuAD F1 across OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B at $I=0$.

</details>


### [217] [Is One Layer Enough? Understanding Inference Dynamics in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06510)
*Amir Rezaei Balef,Mykhailo Koshil,Katharina Eggensperger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对基于Transformer的表格基础模型（TFMs）进行了大规模的层间机制研究，发现了显著的深度冗余，并据此设计了一个仅使用原模型20%参数的循环单层模型，实现了相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于Transformer的表格基础模型在小到中型表格预测基准任务中占据主导地位，但其推理机制在很大程度上仍未被探索。为了填补这一空白，本文旨在深入理解这些模型的内部工作机制。

Method: 对6个最先进的表格上下文学习模型进行了大规模的层间机制研究。分析了预测如何在深度上涌现，识别了推理的不同阶段，并揭示了潜在空间的动态特性。基于发现的深度冗余和迭代优化特性，设计了一个概念验证的循环单层模型。

Result: 研究发现多个模型存在显著的深度冗余，表明在推理阶段存在重叠计算的迭代优化过程。基于此设计的循环单层模型仅使用原模型20%的参数，却实现了可比拟的性能。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型的推理机制与语言模型不同，存在深度冗余。可以通过循环利用单层结构来大幅压缩模型参数而不显著牺牲性能，为未来高效表格模型的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models (TFMs) dominate small to medium tabular predictive benchmark tasks, yet their inference mechanisms remain largely unexplored. We present the first large-scale mechanistic study of layerwise dynamics in 6 state-of-the-art tabular in-context learning models. We explore how predictions emerge across depth, identify distinct stages of inference and reveal latent-space dynamics that differ from those of language models. Our findings indicate substantial depthwise redundancy across multiple models, suggesting iterative refinement with overlapping computations during inference stages. Guided by these insights, we design a proof-of-concept, looped single-layer model that uses only 20% of the original model's parameters while achieving comparable performance. The code is available at https://github.com/amirbalef/is_one_layer_enough.

</details>


### [218] [Efficient Techniques for Data Reconstruction, with Finite-Width Recovery Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06519)
*Edward Tansley,Roy Makhlouf,Estelle Massart,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的数据重建优化框架，证明了在随机特征模型下可以高概率恢复训练数据，并提出了一种高效的子空间感知重建算法，在合成数据集和CIFAR-10上表现优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对训练好的神经网络的数据重建攻击能够恢复训练数据，对隐私安全构成严重威胁，尤其是在训练数据包含敏感信息的情况下。因此，需要深入理解数据重建的理论基础并设计高效的攻击算法。

Method: 提出基于初始参数和训练参数的统一优化框架；在随机特征模型下，利用PAC式界限证明有限宽度网络的数据重建理论；针对低维子空间数据，提出通过第一层权重变化近似数据子空间的高效重建算法，仅需最后层权重即可实现重建。

Result: 证明了在足够大的网络宽度下，随机特征模型可高概率实现训练数据重建；当数据位于低维子空间时，网络宽度要求可放宽，界限依赖于子空间维度；在合成数据集和CIFAR-10上的实验表明，子空间感知方法优于传统的全空间重建技术。

Conclusion: 本研究为神经网络数据重建攻击提供了理论保证，并提出了一种高效的子空间感知重建方法，显著降低了搜索空间维度和网络宽度要求，为隐私风险评估提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Data reconstruction attacks on trained neural networks aim to recover the data on which the network has been trained and pose a significant threat to privacy, especially if the training dataset contains sensitive information. Here, we propose a unified optimization formulation of the data reconstruction problem based on initial and trained parameter values, incorporating state-of-the-art proposals. We show that in the random feature model, this formulation provably leads to training data reconstruction with high probability, provided the network width is sufficiently large; this unprecedented finite-width result uses PAC-style bounds. Furthermore, when the data lies in a low-dimensional subspace, we show that the network width requirement for successful reconstruction can be relaxed, with bounds depending on the subspace dimension rather than the ambient dimension. For general neural network models and unknown data orientations, we propose an efficient reconstruction algorithm that approximates the low-dimensional data subspace through the change in the first-layer weights during training and uses only the last-layer weights for reconstruction, thus reducing the search space dimension and the required network width for high-quality reconstructions. Our numerical experiments on synthetic datasets and CIFAR-10 confirm that our subspace-aware reconstruction approach outperforms standard full-space techniques.

</details>


### [219] [Agentic AIs Are the Missing Paradigm for Out-of-Distribution Generalization in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06522)
*Xin Wang,Haibo Chen,Wenxuan Liu,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张基础模型的分布外（OOD）问题是结构上独特的挑战，无法仅靠以模型为中心的范式解决；基于Agent的系统通过感知、策略选择、外部行动和闭环验证四个结构属性，能够突破模型中心方法固有的参数覆盖天花板，是解决FM-OOD问题的必要补充范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在开放世界部署中面临分布外（OOD）现象，包括知识边界、能力上限、组合性偏移和开放式任务变化，这与传统OOD研究设定在本质上不同。此外，现代基础模型的预训练和后训练分布往往仅被部分观测。现有以模型为中心的范式无法有效应对这些挑战，因此需要一种新的范式来解决FM-OOD问题。

Method: 1. 提出阶段感知的形式化方法，适应部分观测的多阶段训练分布；2. 证明参数覆盖天花板定理：存在实际相关的输入，任何以模型为中心的方法（训练时或测试时）都无法在容忍度ε内处理；3. 刻画智能体OOD系统的四个结构属性——感知、策略选择、外部行动和闭环验证，并证明其能严格扩展可达集超越天花板；4. 回应七个反驳论点，承认其中两个，并概述研究议程。

Result: 证明了以模型为中心的方法存在固有的参数覆盖天花板，存在某些输入无法被处理；展示了智能体系统通过四个结构属性能够严格扩展可达集，突破这一天花板；论证了智能体范式与模型中心范式是互补关系，而非替代关系。

Conclusion: 基础模型的OOD问题是结构上独特的挑战，不能在现有的模型中心范式内解决。智能体系统构成了解决这一问题的缺失范式。FM-OOD的研究进展需要明确承认智能体范式作为一等研究方向，与模型中心方法形成互补，共同推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) are increasingly deployed in open-world settings where distribution shift is the rule rather than the exception. The out-of-distribution (OOD) phenomena they face -- knowledge boundaries, capability ceilings, compositional shifts, and open-ended task variation -- differ in kind from the settings that have shaped prior OOD research, and are further complicated because the pretraining and post-training distributions of modern FMs are often only partially observed. Our position is that OOD for foundation models is a structurally distinct problem that cannot be solved within the prevailing model-centric paradigm, and that agentic systems constitute the missing paradigm required to address it. We defend this claim through four steps. First, we give a stage-aware formalization of OOD that accommodates partially observed multi-stage training distributions. Second, we prove a parameter coverage ceiling: there exist practically relevant inputs that no model-centric method (training-time or test-time) can handle within tolerance $\varepsilon$, for reasons intrinsic to parameter-based representation. Third, we characterize agentic OOD systems by four structural properties -- perception, strategy selection, external action, and closed-loop verification -- and show that they strictly extend the reachable set beyond the ceiling. Fourth, we respond to seven counterarguments, conceding two, and outline a research agenda. We do not claim that agentic methods subsume model-centric ones; we argue that the two are complementary, and that progress on FM-OOD requires explicit recognition of the agentic paradigm as a first-class research direction.

</details>


### [220] [On the Implicit Reward Overfitting and the Low-rank Dynamics in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06523)
*Hao Ye,Jisheng Dang,Junfeng Fang,Bimei Wang,Yizhou Zhang,Ning Lv,Wencan Zhang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过周期性秩-1替换实验发现RLVR存在隐式奖励过拟合现象，并揭示了RLVR通过优化特定奇异谱和秩-1组件来增强推理能力的内在机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明RLVR增强的推理能力主要集中在秩-1组件中，但尚不清楚其内在机制。本文旨在深入探究RLVR如何塑造模型参数，揭示其训练特性和潜在的过拟合问题。

Method: 基于RLVR推理能力集中于秩-1组件的观察，采用周期性秩-1替换方法进行实验分析，研究模型在训练过程中的奖励变化和测试性能关系。

Result: 发现RLVR存在隐式奖励过拟合现象：模型训练时奖励较低但仍能在测试集上获得满意性能。揭示了RLVR训练的三个特性：(1)有效的秩-1组件仅保留数学推理能力；(2)RLVR通过优化特定奇异谱实现功能，线性层奇异值呈重尾分布；(3)秩-1组件对应的左奇异向量在训练中表现出更强的对齐趋势。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了RLVR塑造模型参数的内在机制，本质上是优化采样效率，为改进现有RL范式和实现持续学习提供了潜在见解。

Abstract: Recent extensive research has demonstrated that the enhanced reasoning capabilities acquired by models through Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) are primarily concentrated within the rank-1 components. Predicated on this observation, we employed Periodic Rank-1 Substitution and identified a counterintuitive phenomenon: RLVR may exhibit implicit reward overfitting to the training dataset. Specifically, the model can achieve satisfactory performance on the test set even when its rewards remain relatively low during the training process. Furthermore, we characterize three distinct properties of RL training: (1) The effective rank-1 component in RLVR don't maintain other model knowledge except mathematical reasoning capability. (2) RLVR fundamentally functions by optimizing a specific singular spectrum. The distribution of singular values of almost all linear layers in RLVR-trained model behaves like heavy-tailed distribution. (3) the left singular vectors associated with rank-1 components demonstrate a stronger alignment tendency during training, which echoes the discovery that RLVR is optimizing sampling efficiency in essence. Taken together, our findings and analysis further reveal how RLVR shapes model parameters and offer potential insights for improving existing RL paradigms or other training paradigms to implement continual learning.

</details>


### [221] [Diffusion-Based Posterior Sampling: A Feynman-Kac Analysis of Bias and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06538)
*Matias G. Delgadino,Sebastien Motsch,Advait Parulekar,William Porteous,Sanjay Shakkottai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了扩散后验采样器的偏差分析理论框架，揭示了DPS和STSL的偏差来源，并通过PDE和路径期望刻画了采样不足与过采样区域，为设计稳定变体提供了理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的后验采样器在逆问题中应用广泛，但其理论行为尚不清楚：即使有精确的先验分数，输出仍存在偏差，且在低温区域离散化不稳定，亟需理论分析来理解偏差来源并指导改进。

Method: 引入连接真实后验与标准高斯的代理路径，比较其与采样器路径的差异；推导密度比满足的抛物型PDE，反应项衡量累积偏差；利用Feynman-Kac表示将Radon-Nikodym修正表达为显式路径期望。

Result: 对DPS：修正为Ornstein-Uhlenbeck路径期望，揭示过/欠采样区域；对STSL：重新解释为辅助漂移，使DPS反应项的空间变化部分平坦化；刻画了提前引导停止策略对低温不稳定性的缓解作用。

Conclusion: 该框架系统性地刻画了采样器偏差，解释了现有修正方法的机制，并为设计稳定的后验采样器变体提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Diffusion-based posterior samplers use pretrained diffusion priors to sample from measurement- or reward-conditioned posteriors, and are widely used for inverse problems. Yet their theoretical behavior remains poorly understood: even with exact prior scores, their outputs are biased, and in low-temperature regimes their discretizations can become unstable. We characterize this bias by introducing a tractable surrogate path connecting the true posterior to a standard Gaussian and comparing it to the sampler's path. Their density ratio satisfies a parabolic PDE whose reaction term measures the accumulated bias. A Feynman-Kac representation then expresses the Radon-Nikodym correction as an explicit path expectation, identifying which posterior regions are over- or under-sampled.
  We apply this framework to DPS and STSL, a related sampler. For DPS, the correction is an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck path expectation coupling the data conditional covariance with the reward curvature, revealing where DPS over- or under-samples. Next, we reinterpret STSL as an auxiliary drift that steers trajectories toward low-uncertainty regions, flattening the spatially varying part of the DPS reaction term. Finally, we characterize early guidance-stopping, a common mitigation for low-temperature instabilities caused by forward-Euler integration of the vector field. Together, these results clarify sampler bias, explain existing correctives, and guide stable variant designs.

</details>


### [222] [Hedging Memory Horizons for Non-Stationary Prediction via Online Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06541)
*Yutong Wang,Yannig Goude,Qiwei Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为MELO的模型无关在线预测方法，通过在多个遗忘因子下对基础预测器进行指数加权最小二乘（EWLS）自适应专家聚合，有效解决了分布漂移下的预测问题，在法国COVID-19封锁期间的电力负荷预测中，RMSE相比基线降低34.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布漂移的在线预测场景中，预测器面临双重挑战：在平稳期保持稳定，在漂移期快速适应，且最佳适应窗口长度（记忆长度）无法预知。传统方法难以同时满足稳定性和适应性的需求。

Method: MELO（Memory-hedged Exponentially Weighted Least-Squares Online aggregation）是一种模型无关的聚合方法：(1) 对任意非预期基础预测器池，在多个遗忘因子下构建指数加权最小二乘（EWLS）自适应专家；(2) 使用MLpol（一种无参数在线聚合规则）对原始预测和EWLS自适应预测进行聚合；(3) 采用轻量级的递归更新，无需模型重训练。

Result: 在理论层面，在有界条件下建立了确定性oracle不等式，证明MELO能够与最佳原始预测器以及最佳有界时变仿射组合竞争，仅承受路径长度相关的追踪成本和次线性聚合开销。在实证方面，针对COVID-19封锁期间法国国家电力负荷预测（不使用机制指示器、封锁日期或政策协变量），MELO相比仅使用MLpol的基线方法RMSE降低34.7%，且优于使用外部COVID政策响应协变量的TabICL参考方法。

Conclusion: MELO成功解决了在线预测中稳定性和适应性之间的权衡问题，无需预知适应记忆长度或进行模型重训练，在分布漂移场景下实现了卓越的预测性能，为实际应用中的在线预测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We study online prediction under distribution shift, where inputs arrive chronologically and outcomes are revealed only after prediction. In this setting, predictors must remain stable in quiet regimes yet adapt when regimes shift, and the right adaptation memory is unknown in advance. We propose MELO (Memory-hedged Exponentially Weighted Least-Squares Online aggregation), a model-agnostic method that hedges across adaptation scales: it wraps any non-anticipating base-predictor pool with exponentially weighted least-squares (EWLS) adaptation experts at multiple forgetting factors, and aggregates raw and EWLS-adapted forecasts with MLpol, a parameter-free online aggregation rule. Under boundedness conditions, we establish deterministic oracle inequalities showing that it competes with both the best raw predictor and the best bounded, time-varying affine combinations of the base predictions, up to a path-length-dependent tracking cost and a sublinear aggregation overhead. We evaluate MELO on French national electricity-load forecasting through the COVID-19 lockdown using no regime indicators, lockdown dates, or policy covariates. MELO reduces overall RMSE by 34.7\% relative to base-only MLpol and achieves lower overall RMSE than a TabICL reference supplied with an external COVID policy-response covariate. Moreover, MELO requires only lightweight per-step recursive updates without model retraining.

</details>


### [223] [Sequential Design of Genetic Circuits Under Uncertainty With Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06552)
*Michal Kobiela,Diego A. Oyarzún,Michael U. Gutmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的序列优化框架，通过预先训练的摊销方法同时处理生物分子反应的内在随机性和实验室条件差异，实现了无需显式参数推断的快速自适应遗传电路设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统设计面临两种不确定性来源：生物分子反应的内在随机性和实验室/实验条件的变异性。现有的贝叶斯方法虽然通过迭代实验-推断-优化循环处理不确定性，但每次实验后都需要计算昂贵的推断和优化步骤，导致设计延迟。

Method: 采用基于强化学习策略的方法，结合微分方程或马尔可夫跳跃过程模拟器模型。提出摊销方法，在可能的参数分布上预先训练策略，避免在设计周期中进行显式参数推断，实现基于观察的即时适应。

Result: 在异源基因表达模型和抑制振荡器电路模型上验证了该方法，证明其能够高效处理分子噪声和跨实验室变异性，有效优化遗传电路设计。

Conclusion: 该框架成功克服了现有方法中计算瓶颈，实现了在双重不确定性下的高效遗传电路设计，为合成生物学中的实验设计提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: The design of biological systems is hindered by uncertainty arising from both intrinsic stochasticity of biomolecular reactions and variability across laboratory or experimental conditions. In this work, we present a sequential framework to optimize genetic circuits under both forms of uncertainty. By employing simulator models based on differential equations or Markov jump processes alongside a reinforcement learning (RL) policy-based approach, our method suggests experiments that adapt to unknown laboratory conditions while accounting for inherent stochasticity. While previous Bayesian methods address uncertainty through iterative experiment-inference-optimization cycles, they typically require computationally expensive inference and optimization steps after each experimental round, leading to delays. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose an amortized approach trained up-front across a distribution of possible uncertain parameters. This strategy sidesteps the need for explicit parameter inference during the design cycle, enabling immediate, observation-based adaptation. We demonstrate our framework on models for heterologous gene expression and a repressilator circuit, showing that it efficiently handles both molecular noise and cross-laboratory variability.

</details>


### [224] [Diverse Sampling in Diffusion Models with Marginal Preserving Particle Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06553)
*Gal Vinograd,Idan Achituve,Ethan Fetaya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了EDDY，一种无需额外训练的引导机制，通过无散度动力学的对称性原理，在保持生成质量的同时有效提升了扩散模型和流匹配模型的样本多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型和流匹配模型在生成样本时往往缺乏多样性，而传统的多样性提升方法通常会牺牲生成质量或需要额外的训练成本。作者旨在寻找一种既能提升样本多样性，又能严格保持边际分布质量且无需额外训练的方法。

Method: 利用Fokker-Planck方程的对称性，通过引入核函数构建的反对称成对矩阵场来产生漂移扰动。这种漂移扰动改变了粒子轨迹，但保持了演化的边际分布不变。通过这种无散度动力学（divergence-free dynamics），在联合粒子层面促进多样性，同时保持每个粒子的边际分布。针对计算开销大的情况（如文本到图像生成），提出了实用的近似方案。

Result: 在合成分布和文本到图像生成任务上的实验表明，EDDY相比常用基线方法，在保持强分布保真度（distributional fidelity）的同时，有效提升了生成样本的多样性。

Conclusion: EDDY成功实现了在无需额外训练的前提下，通过数学原理上严谨的无散度动力学设计，有效解决了扩散模型生成样本多样性不足的问题，为生成模型的质量-多样性权衡提供了一个新的解决方案。

Abstract: We present EDDY (Exact-marginal Diversification via Divergence-free dYnamics), a guidance mechanism for diffusion and flow matching models that promotes diversity among samples generated while maintaining quality. EDDY exploits symmetries of the Fokker-Planck equation, using drift perturbations that change particle trajectories while preserving the evolving marginal distribution. We instantiate this principle through kernel-based anti-symmetric pairwise matrix fields, constructed from the repulsive directions. The resulting divergence-free dynamics promote diversity at the joint particle level while preserving each particle's marginal distribution without any additional training. As computing the guidance can be computationally expensive in cases such as text-to-image generation with perceptual embeddings, we propose practical approximations as an effective and efficient solution. Experiments on synthetic distributions and text-to-image generation show that EDDY improves diversity while maintaining strong distributional fidelity compared to common baselines.

</details>


### [225] [Optimal Counterfactual Search in Tree Ensembles: A Study Across Modeling and Solution Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06561)
*Awa Khouna,Youssouf Emine,Julien Ferry,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CPCF方法，利用约束规划为树集成模型计算最优反事实解释，在可扩展性和综合性能上优于MaxSAT和MILP方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法可能产生次优解，导致建议的变更远超实际所需，且启发式错误会对不同个体产生不公平的影响，因此需要计算满足合理性和可操作性约束的最优反事实解释。

Method: 提出CPCF约束规划方法，将数值特征编码为分裂阈值诱导的区间域，离散特征保持原生有限域表示，形成紧凑的有限域公式；同时扩展MaxSAT方法以支持软投票集成，并与MILP方法进行比较。

Result: 在十个数据集和三种树集成类型的实验中，CP方法整体性能最佳；不同范式各有优势：CP最通用，MaxSAT适合硬投票集成，MILP在中等分裂层级的摊销推理设置中仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 研究为树集成模型的最优反事实解释提供了有效的约束规划解决方案，并为不同数学规划范式的适用场景提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Trust in counterfactual explanations depends critically on whether their recommended changes are truly minimal: suboptimal explanations may vastly overshoot the actual changes needed to alter a decision, and heuristic errors can affect individuals unevenly, giving some users relevant recourse while assigning others unnecessarily costly recommendations. Consequently, we study the problem of computing optimal counterfactual explanations for tree ensembles under plausibility and actionability constraints. This is a combinatorial problem: for a fixed model, counterfactual search boils down to selecting consistent branching decisions and threshold-defined regions under a distance objective. We exploit this structure through CPCF, a constraint programming (CP) formulation in which numerical features are encoded as interval domains induced by split thresholds, while discrete features retain native finite-domain representations. This yields a compact finite-domain formulation that supports multiple distance objectives without continuous split-boundary search. We then place CPCF in a broader comparison across mathematical programming paradigms: we extend a maximum Boolean satisfiability (MaxSAT) formulation, originally designed for hard-voting random forests, to soft-voting ensembles, and compare against the current state-of-the-art mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) optimal approach. Across ten datasets and three types of tree ensembles, we analyze scalability, anytime performance, and sensitivity to distance metrics. We observe that CP achieves the best overall performance. More importantly, our results identify regimes in which the specific strengths of each paradigm make it best suited: CP is most versatile overall, MaxSAT handles hard-voting ensembles particularly well, and MILP remains competitive in amortized inference settings with a moderate number of split levels.

</details>


### [226] [Feature Dimensionality Outweighs Model Complexity in Breast Cancer Subtype Classification Using TCGA-BRCA Gene Expression Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06562)
*Meena Al Hasani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了模型复杂度和特征选择对乳腺癌亚型分类的影响，发现逻辑回归在处理高维基因表达数据时表现最稳定，尤其是在少数类别的识别上优于随机森林和SVM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用基因表达数据准确分类乳腺癌亚型对临床诊断和治疗至关重要，但此类数据集具有高维度和小样本量的特点，给机器学习模型带来了巨大挑战。

Method: 基于TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，使用不同数量（50至20,518个）的高变基因训练逻辑回归、随机森林和支持向量机（SVM）模型，并采用分层5折交叉验证，以准确率和宏F1分数评估性能。

Result: 虽然所有模型均达到了较高的整体准确率，但宏F1分析揭示了亚型级别性能的显著差异。逻辑回归在各亚型间表现最稳定且均衡，对罕见类别的检测能力有所提升；随机森林尽管整体准确率高，但在少数亚型上表现不佳；SVM则表现出对特征维度的敏感性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在高维生物分类任务中，模型简洁性、合适的评估指标（如宏F1）以及特征选择至关重要，简单的模型（如逻辑回归）可能比复杂模型更具优势。

Abstract: Accurate classification of breast cancer subtypes from gene expression data is critical for diagnosis and treatment selection. However, such datasets are characterized by high dimensionality and limited sample size, posing challenges for machine learning models.
  In this study, we evaluate the impact of model complexity and feature selection on subtype classification performance using TCGA-BRCA gene expression data. Logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machine (SVM) models were trained using varying numbers of highly variable genes (50 to 20,518). Performance was evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation and assessed with accuracy and macro F1 score. While all models achieved high accuracy, macro F1 analysis revealed substantial differences in subtype-level performance. Logistic regression demonstrated the most stable and balanced performance across subtypes, including improved detection of rare classes. Random forest underperformed on minority subtypes despite strong overall accuracy, while SVM showed sensitivity to feature dimensionality. These findings highlight the importance of model simplicity, evaluation metrics, and feature selection in high-dimensional biological classification tasks.

</details>


### [227] [Criticality and Saturation in Orthogonal Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06563)
*Max Guillen,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上解释了正交权重初始化网络在有限宽度修正下的稳定性，填补了该领域的长期空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知正交权重初始化相比独立同分布（i.i.d.）高斯初始化能提升训练性能，且先前实证研究发现前者在大深度下的有限宽度张量表现稳定，但缺乏相应的理论解释。

Method: 推导了正交初始化下网络统计量有限宽度展开中张量的逐层递推关系；扩展了适用于i.i.d.情形的费曼图方法，使其对所有阶数的1/宽度有效；解析证明了递推关系再现了具有消失不动点激活函数下的张量稳定性。

Result: 成功推导出了正交初始化网络的显式逐层递推关系，理论上证明了其有限宽度张量在大深度下的稳定性，并通过数值求解递推关系与蒙特卡洛模拟验证了理论结果的一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限宽度正交权重初始化非线性网络的稳定性提供了坚实的理论基础，解释了其优于i.i.d.初始化的原因，完善了相关理论体系。

Abstract: It has been known for a long time that initializing weight matrices to be orthogonal instead of having i.i.d. Gaussian components can improve training performance. This phenomenon can be analyzed using finite-width corrections, where the infinite-width statistics are supplemented by a power series in $1/\mathrm{width}$. In particular, recent empirical results by Day et al. show that the tensors appearing in this treatment stabilize for large depth, as opposed to the tensors of i.i.d.-initialized networks. In this article, we derive explicit layer-wise recursion relations for the tensors appearing in the finite-width expansion of the network statistics in the case of orthogonal initializations. We also provide an extension of recently-introduced Feynman diagrams for the corresponding recursions in the i.i.d.-case which are valid to all orders in $1/\mathrm{width}$. Finally, we show explicitly that the recursions we derive reproduce the stability of the finite-width tensors which was observed for activation functions with vanishing fixed point. This work therefore provides a theoretical explanation for the stability of nonlinear networks of finite width initialized with orthogonal weights, closing a long-standing gap in the literature. We validate our theoretical results experimentally by showing that numerical solutions of our recursion relations and their analytical large-depth expansions agree excellently with Monte-Carlo estimates from network ensembles.

</details>


### [228] [SNAPO: Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization for Optimal Control via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06570)
*Dmitri Goloubentsev,Natalija Karpichina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SNAPO框架，将神经网络策略嵌入可微模拟器中，通过一次反向传播同时完成策略训练和大量敏感性计算，显著降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的序列决策问题（如天然气存储、养老金投资组合再平衡、制药反应器链）需要在不确定性下进行决策。传统的动态规划方法随状态维度呈指数增长，而黑箱强化学习训练缓慢且无法提供敏感性分析。因此，需要一种既能处理高维状态又能高效计算敏感性的方法。

Method: 提出SNAPO（Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization）框架。该方法将神经网络策略嵌入已知且可微分的模拟器中，用平滑近似替代硬约束，利用伴随方法在一次反向传播中计算目标函数对所有策略参数和输入的精确梯度。

Result: 在三个领域验证了SNAPO的有效性：天然气存储（训练不到一分钟，365个远期曲线敏感性无额外成本）；养老金资产负债管理（敏感性计算速度提升6.5-200倍）；制药制造（通过4单元过程链计算交叉单元敏感性，5次伴随传递在74.5毫秒内产生20个ICH Q8监管敏感性）。所有敏感性均由训练策略的同一反向传播过程产生，计算成本与一次反向传播相当，与敏感性数量无关。

Conclusion: SNAPO成功结合了可微分模拟和伴随方法的优点，解决了传统方法无法兼顾高效训练和敏感性分析的问题，为高维序列决策问题提供了一种高效的解决方案，具有广泛的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Many real-world problems require sequential decisions under uncertainty: when to inject or withdraw gas from storage, how to rebalance a pension portfolio each month, what temperature profile to run through a pharmaceutical reactor chain. Dynamic programming solves small instances exactly but scales exponentially in state dimensions. Black-box reinforcement learning handles high-dimensional states but trains slowly and produces no sensitivities. We introduce SNAPO (Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization), a framework that embeds a neural policy inside a known, differentiable simulator, replaces hard constraints with smooth approximations, and computes exact gradients of the objective with respect to all policy parameters and all inputs in a single adjoint pass. We demonstrate SNAPO on three domains: natural gas storage (training in under a minute, 365 forward curve sensitivities at no additional cost per sensitivity), pension fund asset-liability management (6.5x-200x sensitivity speedup over bump-and-revalue, scaling with the number of risk factors), and pharmaceutical manufacturing (cross-unit sensitivities through a 4-unit process chain, with 20 ICH Q8 regulatory sensitivities from 5 adjoint passes in 74.5 milliseconds). All sensitivities are produced by the same backward pass that trains the policy, at a cost proportional to one reverse pass regardless of how many sensitivities are computed.

</details>


### [229] [Directional Consistency as a Complementary Optimization Signal: The GONO Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06575)
*Victor Daniel Gera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现深度学习优化中方向对齐与损失收敛可以解耦，提出基于梯度方向一致性的GONO优化器，在保持理论收敛性的同时有效处理振荡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到优化器可以表现出近乎完美的方向一致性（连续梯度余弦相似度cc_t趋近于1），但损失值却保持高位或缓慢下降。这一现象表明现有优化器（如Adam、SGD、RMSprop）缺乏利用梯度方向时间一致性的显式机制，仅依赖基于幅值的信号，无法区分平台期、鞍点和真正的收敛。

Method: 提出GONO（Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer），基于cc_t自适应调整Adam的动量系数β_1：在方向一致时放大动量，在振荡时抑制动量。理论证明GONO匹配Adam的O(1/√T)收敛率，且当信号无信息时精确退化为Adam。

Result: cc_t在振荡检测中达到F1=1.00（梯度范数仅为0.45）；GONO在MNIST（98.15%）、CIFAR-10（43.14%）和ResNet-18（75.44%）上与AdamW保持竞争力，确立了方向对齐作为理论严谨、实际可行的优化信号。

Conclusion: 方向对齐与损失收敛的解耦现象为优化器设计提供了新视角，基于方向一致性的自适应机制能够有效补充传统的幅值信号，为深度学习优化提供了理论上grounded且实用的改进方向。

Abstract: We identify and formalize an underexplored phenomenon in deep learning optimization: directional alignment and loss convergence can be decoupled. An optimizer can exhibit near-perfect directional consistency (cc_t -> 1, measured via consecutive gradient cosine similarity) while the loss remains high or decreases slowly. This observation reveals that existing optimizers such as Adam, SGD, and RMSprop lack explicit mechanisms to exploit temporal consistency in gradient directions, relying instead on magnitude-based signals that fail to distinguish plateaus, saddle points, and genuine convergence. Motivated by this, we introduce GONO (Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer), which adapts Adam's momentum coefficient beta_1 based on cc_t: amplifying momentum under directional consistency and suppressing it during oscillation. We prove GONO matches Adam's O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence rate and reduces exactly to Adam when the signal is uninformative. Empirically, cc_t achieves oscillation detection with F1=1.00 (vs. 0.45 for gradient norm), and GONO remains competitive with AdamW on MNIST (98.15%), CIFAR-10 (43.14%), and ResNet-18 (75.44%), establishing directional alignment as a theoretically grounded, practically actionable optimization signal. Code: https://github.com/victordaniel/gono-optimizer

</details>


### [230] [On the Safety of Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06576)
*Xiaoguang Guo,Zehong Wang,Ziming Li,Shawn Spitzel,Soonwoo Kwon,Tianyi Ma,Yanfang Ye,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRL-Safety基准，从五个安全轴全面评估图表示学习方法的可靠性，发现安全表现取决于表示设计与受压图因素的交互，而非仅由方法类别决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图表示学习（GRL）评估主要关注纯净迁移、适应性和任务覆盖，但在实际部署中，图信号、图上下文、标签支持、结构群体或预测证据可能受到压力，GRL方法的可靠性尚不明确。

Method: 引入GRL-Safety多轴安全评估基准，在标准化评估条件下，对包括拓扑嵌入、监督GNN、自监督图模型和图基础模型在内的12种代表性方法进行评估，覆盖25个图数据集。评估涵盖五个安全轴：腐蚀鲁棒性、分布外（OOD）泛化、类别不平衡、公平性和可解释性，采用分轴和分子条件报告而非单一聚合分数。

Result: 分析得出三个跨轴洞察：（1）安全行为由表示设计与受压图因素的交互塑造，而非仅由方法家族决定；（2）基础时代的方法在特定轴上表现优异，而非全面安全优势；（3）即使是最佳评估方法，某些部署场景仍存在困难，揭示了能力缺口。

Conclusion: GRL方法的安全表现复杂且依赖于设计、数据与压力因素的交互，未来研究需要超越模型选择，开发新的鲁棒性、适应或训练目标以应对现实部署挑战。

Abstract: Graph representation learning (GRL) has evolved from topology-only graph embeddings to task-specific supervised GNNs, and more recently to reusable representations and graph foundation models (GFMs). However, existing evaluations mainly measure clean transfer, adaptation, and task coverage. It remains unclear whether GRL methods stay reliable when deployment stresses affect graph signals, graph contexts, label support, structural groups, or predictive evidence. We introduce GRL-Safety, a multi-axis safety evaluation benchmark for GRL. GRL-Safety evaluates twelve representative methods, spanning topology-only embedding methods, supervised GNNs, self-supervised graph models, and GFMs, on twenty-five graph datasets under standardized evaluation conditions while preserving method-native adaptation. The evaluation covers five safety axes: corruption robustness, OOD generalization, class imbalance, fairness, and interpretation, with per-axis and sub-condition reporting rather than a single aggregate score. Our analysis yields three cross-axis insights that can inspire future research. First, safety behavior is shaped by the interaction between representation design and the stressed graph factor, rather than by method family alone. Second, foundation-era methods show axis-specific strengths rather than broad safety dominance. Third, several deployment regimes remain difficult even for the best evaluated method, revealing capability gaps that require new robustness, adaptation, or training objectives beyond model selection. The benchmark, evaluation protocols, and code are available at: https://github.com/GXG-CS/GRL-Safety.

</details>


### [231] [PairAlign: A Framework for Sequence Tokenization via Self-Alignment with Applications to Audio Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06582)
*Adhiraj Banerjee,Vipul Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PairAlign框架，通过序列级自对齐实现紧凑的音频符号化，将音频转换为离散符号序列，在保持编辑距离相似性的同时显著减少标记数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频标记器依赖量化、聚类或编解码器重建，采用局部分配方式，难以直接优化序列一致性、紧凑性、长度控制、终止和编辑相似性。音频需要学习类似于语言中标记的接口，以便进行比较、记忆、检索和推理等操作。

Method: PairAlign将标记化视为条件序列生成：编码器将语音映射到连续条件，自回归解码器从BOS生成标记，学习标记身份、顺序、长度和EOS位置。通过两个内容保持视图的交叉配对，训练每个视图序列在另一视图表示下具有高似然性，同时使用无关示例提供竞争序列。该方法从VQ风格标记化开始，结合EMA教师目标、交叉配对教师强制、前缀损坏、似然对比和长度控制进行优化。

Result: 在3秒语音上，PairAlign学习了紧凑、非退化的序列，具有广泛的词汇使用和强跨视图一致性。在TIMIT检索任务中，保持编辑距离搜索的同时将存档标记数减少55%。连续扫描探测显示局部重叠低于密集几何标记器，但具有更强的长度控制和100ms偏移下的有界编辑轨迹。

Conclusion: PairAlign成功实现了序列-符号预测学习器，类似于JEPA风格目标，从一个视图预测抽象目标作为学习的可变长度符号序列而非连续潜变量，为音频符号表示提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Many operations on sensory data -- comparison, memory, retrieval, and reasoning -- are naturally expressed over discrete symbolic structures. In language this interface is given by tokens; in audio, it must be learned. Existing audio tokenizers rely on quantization, clustering, or codec reconstruction, assigning tokens locally, so sequence consistency, compactness, length control, termination, and edit similarity are rarely optimized directly.
  We introduce PairAlign, a framework for compact audio tokenization through sequence-level self-alignment. PairAlign treats tokenization as conditional sequence generation: an encoder maps speech to a continuous condition, and an autoregressive decoder generates tokens from BOS, learning token identity, order, length, and EOS placement. Given two content-preserving views, each view's sequence is trained to be likely under the other's representation, while unrelated examples provide competing sequences. This gives a scalable surrogate for edit-distance preservation while discouraging many-to-one collapse.
  PairAlign starts from VQ-style tokenization and refines it with EMA-teacher targets, cross-paired teacher forcing, prefix corruption, likelihood contrast, and length control.
  On 3-second speech, PairAlign learns compact, non-degenerate sequences with broad vocabulary usage and strong cross-view consistency. On TIMIT retrieval, it preserves edit-distance search while reducing archive token count by 55%. A continuous-sweep probe shows lower local overlap than a dense geometric tokenizer, but stronger length control and bounded edit trajectories under 100 ms shifts. PairAlign is a sequence-symbolic predictive learner: like JEPA-style objectives, it predicts an abstract target from another view as a learned variable-length symbolic sequence, not a continuous latent.

</details>


### [232] [Distributionally-Robust Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06585)
*Vinit Ranjan,Jisun Park,Bartolomeo Stellato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布鲁棒的超参数学习方法，用于一阶凸优化算法。通过最小化Wasserstein分布鲁棒的性能估计问题（PEP），在算法参数（如步长）上实现优化。该方法在学习优化（L2O）与最坏情况最优算法设计之间建立了统一的框架，并在实验中表现出优越的样本外性能和可证明的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的“学习优化”（L2O）方法在特定数据集上学习超参数（如步长），虽然在样本内表现优异，但在面对新问题实例时缺乏理论保证和鲁棒性；相反，基于性能估计问题（PEP）的最坏情况设计虽然鲁棒，但可能过于保守。为了在二者之间取得平衡，作者提出了一种分布鲁棒的超参数学习方法，旨在通过最小化Wasserstein分布鲁棒的PEP，学习既能适应数据分布又具备鲁棒性的算法参数。

Method: 作者构建了一个Wasserstein分布鲁棒的性能估计问题（PEP）框架。给定一个问题实例的数据集，最小化该鲁棒PEP以学习算法参数。当鲁棒半径趋于零时，方法退化为经典的L2O；当半径增大时，方法趋近于最坏情况下的最优算法设计。求解过程采用随机梯度下降（SGD），每一步通过微分内部的半定规划（SDP）解来计算梯度。这种端到端的优化框架允许算法参数直接通过求解内部优化问题来调整。

Result: 理论方面，作者证明了高概率界限：学习到的算法的真实风险至多为样本内L2O最优值加上一个随样本量增大而收缩的余量，且不劣于最坏情况的PEP界。实验方面，在无约束二次最小化、LASSO和线性规划基准测试中，学习到的算法在样本外表现优异，兼具可证明的鲁棒性，超越了最坏情况最优和普通L2O基准算法。

Conclusion: 本研究成功地将分布鲁棒优化引入超参数学习，为一阶凸优化算法提供了一种在性能和鲁棒性之间可调节权衡的框架。该方法不仅在理论上提供了风险界限的保证，还在多种优化问题上展示了卓越的实验性能，为设计既数据自适应又具备鲁棒性的优化算法开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We propose a distributionally robust approach to learning hyperparameters for first-order methods in convex optimization. Given a dataset of problem instances, we minimize a Wasserstein distributionally robust version of the performance estimation problem (PEP) over algorithm parameters such as step sizes. Our framework unifies two extremes: as the robustness radius vanishes, we recover classical learning to optimize (L2O); as it grows, we recover worst-case optimal algorithm design via PEP. We solve the resulting problem with stochastic gradient descent, differentiating through the solution of an inner semidefinite program at each step. We prove high-probability bounds showing that the true risk of the learned algorithm is at most the in-sample L2O optimum plus a slack that shrinks with the sample size, and is no worse than the worst-case PEP bound. On unconstrained quadratic minimization, LASSO, and linear programming benchmarks, our learned algorithms achieve strong out-of-sample performance with certifiable robustness, outperforming both worst-case optimal and vanilla L2O baselines.

</details>


### [233] [Towards Metric-Faithful Neural Graph Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06588)
*Jyotirmaya Shivottam,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将双Lipschitz几何性质的编码器（FSW-GNN）作为即插即用组件，显著提升了神经网络图编辑距离（GED）估计的性能，证明了编码器几何特性是图匹配架构的重要设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图编辑距离（GED）是衡量图结构相似性的基础指标，但属于NP难问题。现有的神经图匹配架构在近似GED方面虽有进展，但编码器几何特性在神经GED估计中的作用尚不清楚。研究旨在建立理论框架，理解并利用编码器几何特性来提升GED估计质量。

Method: 建立了连接编码器几何特性与GED估计质量的理论框架。针对两类神经GED估计器（图相似性预测器和基于对齐的方法）：在固定图集合上，利用双随机度量$d_{\mathrm{DS}}$与GED的可比性，证明了图级双Lipschitz编码器能产生受控的GED代理并改善排序稳定性；对于基于匹配的估计器，节点级双Lipschitz几何特性可传播到编码器诱导的对齐代价中。实例化为FSW-GNN——一种双Lipschitz WL等价编码器，作为代表性神经GED架构的即插即用替代组件。

Result: 在代表性基线和基准数据集上，几何感知变体显著改善了GED预测和排序指标。对未经训练编码器的忠实性案例研究，连同消融实验和迁移实验，支持了性能提升源于改进的表示几何特性这一观点。

Conclusion: 编码器几何特性是神经图匹配的有用设计原则。通过采用具有双Lipschitz几何特性的编码器，可以在不改变架构主体的情况下有效提升GED估计性能，为未来图匹配神经网络的设计提供了理论指导和实践验证。

Abstract: Graph Edit Distance (GED) is a fundamental, albeit NP-hard, metric for structural graph similarity. Recent neural graph matching architectures approximate GED by first encoding graphs with a Graph Neural Network (GNN) and then applying either a graph-level regression head or a matching-based alignment module. Despite substantial architectural progress, the role of encoder geometry in neural GED estimation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework that connects encoder geometry to GED estimation quality for two broad classes of neural GED estimators: graph similarity predictors and alignment-based methods. On fixed graph collections, where the doubly-stochastic metric $d_{\mathrm{DS}}$ is comparable to GED, we show that graph-level bi-Lipschitz encoders yield controlled GED surrogates and improved ranking stability; for matching-based estimators, node-level bi-Lipschitz geometry propagates to encoder-induced alignment costs and the resulting optimized alignment objective. We instantiate this perspective using FSW-GNN, a bi-Lipschitz WL-equivalent encoder, as a drop-in replacement in representative neural GED architectures. Across representative baselines and benchmark datasets, the resulting geometry-aware variants significantly improve GED prediction and ranking metrics. A faithfulness case study of untrained encoders, together with ablations and transfer experiments, supports the view that these gains arise from improved representation geometry, positioning encoder geometry as a useful design principle for neural graph matching.

</details>


### [234] [BRICKS: Compositional Neural Markov Kernels for Zero-Shot Radiation-Matter Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06591)
*Richard Hildebrandt,Evangelos Kourlitis,Baran Hashemi,Manuel Bünstorf,Thierry Meyer,Nikola Boskov,Michael Kagan,Dan Rosenbaum,Sanmay Ganguly,Lukas Heinrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于组合式神经代理的辐射-物质相互作用模拟方法，利用混合离散-连续Transformer模型实现下一粒子预测，支持零样本大规模模拟并显著提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射-物质相互作用模拟是粒子物理、核工程、空间工程和医学物理等领域的关键任务。传统机理模拟器计算成本高、不可微分且难以提供可处理的似然估计，限制了其在下游应用中的灵活性。研究者希望利用粒子相互作用的局部性和马尔可夫性质，构建可组合、可微分的高效神经代理模型。

Method: 提出下一粒子预测核，采用基于黎曼流匹配的混合离散-连续Transformer模型，在乘积流形上建模。模型能够生成变大小类型化的粒子集合和辐射副作用，这些是入射粒子与材料体相互作用的结果。该预测核可组合使用，实现对未见大规模材料分布的零样本模拟。

Result: 单核执行在GPU上相比CPU绑定的机理模拟器实现了显著加速。在核级别评估中，模型表现出良好的预测稳定性，在多轮自回归展开中保持稳定。发布了包含2000万事件的辐射-物质相互作用数据集供研究使用。

Conclusion: 该方法成功构建了可微分且具有可处理似然估计的辐射-物质相互作用神经代理模型，为下游应用提供了新工具。组合式设计使模型具备零样本泛化能力，显著提升了计算效率，为跨领域的辐射模拟任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce a new strategy for compositional neural surrogates for radiation-matter interactions, a key task spanning domains from particle physics through nuclear and space engineering to medical physics. Exploiting the locality and the Markov nature of particle interactions, we create a \emph{next-particle prediction} kernel using hybrid discrete-continuous transformer models based on Riemannian Flow Matching on product manifolds. The model generates variable-sized typed sets of particles and radiation side effects that are the result of the interaction of an incident particle with a material volume. The resulting kernel can be composed to simulate unseen large-scale material distributions in a zero-shot manner. Unlike mechanistic simulators, our model is designed to be differentiable, provides tractable likelihoods for future downstream applications. A significant computational speed-up on GPU compared to CPU-bound mechanistic simulation is observed for single-kernel execution. We evaluate the model at the kernel level and demonstrate predictive stability over multi-round autoregressive rollouts. We additionally release a novel 20M-event radiation-matter interaction dataset for further research.

</details>


### [235] [Weight-Decay Turns Transformer Loss Landscapes Villani: Functional-Analytic Foundations for Optimization and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06599)
*Abhijit Das,Sayantan Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从功能分析角度证明了带$L^2$正则化的Transformer损失函数满足Villani强制性能量函数准则，推导出了显式的对数Sobolev和Poincaré常数，为大规模语言模型中权重衰减的理论作用提供了严格数学证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重衰减在大语言模型中被广泛用作正则化器，但其在塑造Transformer损失景观中的精确作用在理论上尚未得到充分探索。现有研究缺乏对标准Transformer目标函数（带$L^2$正则化的交叉熵损失）的严格数学刻画，特别是关于其收敛性和泛化性质的理论保证。

Method: 1. 从功能分析角度证明正则化损失$\mathcal{F}$满足Villani准则：无穷可微、至少二次增长、具有高斯可积尾部，并满足微分增长条件$-Δ\mathcal{F} + \tfrac{1}{s}\|\nabla\mathcal{F}\|^{2} \to \infty$；2. 基于此结构推导显式的对数Sobolev和Poincaré常数$C_{\mathrm{LS}} \leq λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}$；3. 引入可扩展的Villani诊断函数$Ψ_s(θ)$并使用Hutchinson迹探针在超过100M参数的模型上高效估计。

Result: 1. 建立了正则化强度$λ$和模型维度$d$与有限时间收敛保证的联系；2. 推导出随$λ$增加而收紧的PAC-Bayesian泛化界；3. 在GPT-Neo-125M上的实验证实了$Ψ_s$的二次增长、Hessian谱膨胀和与对数Sobolev分析一致的指数收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了权重衰减不仅在经验上改善泛化能力，还为深度学习中的快速Langevin混合和理论基础的曲率感知优化建立了必要的数学条件，为大语言模型的正则化理论提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: Weight decay is widely used as a regularizer in large language models, yet its precise role in shaping Transformer loss landscapes remains theoretically underexplored. This paper provides the first rigorous functional-analytic characterization of the standard Transformer objective--cross-entropy loss with $L^2$ regularization--by proving it satisfies Villani's criteria for coercive energy functions. Specifically, we show that the regularized loss $\mathcal{F}$ is infinitely differentiable, grows at least quadratically, has Gaussian-integrable tails, and satisfies the differential growth condition $-Δ\mathcal{F} + \tfrac{1}{s}\|\nabla\mathcal{F}\|^{2} \to \infty$ as $\|θ\| \to \infty$ for all $s>0$. From this structure, we derive explicit log-Sobolev and Poincaré constants $C_{\mathrm{LS}} \leq λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}$, linking the regularization strength $λ$ and model dimension $d$ to finite-time convergence guarantees for noisy stochastic gradient descent and PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds that tighten with increasing $λ$. To validate our theory, we introduce a scalable Villani diagnostic $Ψ_s(θ) = -Δ\mathcal{F} + s^{-1}\|\nabla \mathcal{F}\|^2$ and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson trace probes in models with over 100M parameters. Experiments on GPT-Neo-125M across Penn Treebank and WikiText-103 confirm the predicted quadratic growth of $Ψ_s$, spectral inflation of the Hessian, and exponential convergence behavior consistent with our log-Sobolev analysis. These results demonstrate that weight decay not only improves generalization empirically but also establishes the mathematical conditions required for fast Langevin mixing and theoretically grounded curvature-aware optimization in deep learning.

</details>


### [236] [How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06605)
*Shai Feldman,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DAPRO框架，这是首个用于多轮LLM交互事件时间预测的动态预算分配方法，提供了更紧的无分布有限样本覆盖保证，并在代理任务成功、越狱攻击、有毒内容生成等场景中表现优于静态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估多轮对话中LLM性能（如越狱成功或任务完成）计算成本高昂，关键事件往往在多次交互后才出现且可能很罕见。现有conformal survival框架采用静态预算分配效率低下，且依赖事件时间与审查时间的条件独立性假设。

Method: 提出Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization (DAPRO)框架，实现动态预算分配。理论上证明了满足预算约束，提供无分布、有限样本覆盖保证，无需条件独立性假设。核心贡献是新型覆盖界，其尺度与平均审查权重的平方根而非最坏情况权重成比例，理论上比先前工作更紧。还可用于在有限计算资源下获得无偏、低方差的人群级评估指标估计。

Result: 在代理任务成功、对抗性越狱、有毒内容生成和RAG幻觉等场景上，使用Llama 3.1和Qwen 2.5等LLM进行全面实验。DAPRO持续实现接近名义水平的覆盖率，方差低于静态基线，同时满足预算约束。

Conclusion: DAPRO为多轮LLM交互的性能评估提供了理论上可靠且实践高效的动态预算分配方案，突破了静态方法的局限性，为有限计算资源下的LLM评估提供了新的理论保证和实用工具。

Abstract: Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events -- e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent -- often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare, and under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved.
  Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that is inefficient in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce \emph{Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization} (DAPRO), the first theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions.
  We prove that DAPRO satisfies the budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches.
  A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight rather than the worst-case weight, yielding provably tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased, low-variance estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources.
  Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, adversarial jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations using LLMs such as Llama 3.1 and Qwen 2.5 demonstrate that DAPRO consistently achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while satisfying the budget constraint.

</details>


### [237] [Transformers Efficiently Perform In-Context Logistic Regression via Normalized Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06609)
*Chenyang Zhang,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了能执行上下文逻辑回归的多层Transformer，揭示了其通过归一化梯度下降实现上下文学习的机制，并提供了训练收敛性和分布外泛化性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer强大的上下文学习（ICL）能力通常被认为源于其在上下文上隐式执行特定算法的能力。为深入理解这一机制，本文旨在研究具有softmax注意力的Transformer如何在线性分类数据上执行上下文学习。

Method: 首先，构建一类多层Transformer，使其能执行上下文逻辑回归，且每一层精确执行一步归一化梯度下降；其次，通过监督训练单层自注意力层（以一步梯度下降为监督信号）并循环应用该层，得到所需的循环模型；最后，给出理论证明。

Result: 成功构建了具有理论保证的Transformer模型，证明了单层自注意力层的训练收敛性，以及循环模型的分布外（OOD）泛化能力，从理论上展示了softmax Transformer如何有效作为上下文学习器。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了对上下文学习机制的理论理解，明确展示了softmax Transformer如何通过模拟梯度下降算法来有效地执行上下文学习任务。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities. The strong ICL performance of transformers is commonly believed to arise from their ability to implicitly execute certain algorithms on the context, thereby enhancing prediction and generation. In this work, we investigate how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification data. We first construct a class of multi-layer transformers that can perform in-context logistic regression, with each layer exactly performing one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. Then, we show that our constructed transformer can be obtained through (i) training a single self-attention layer supervised by one-step gradient descent, and (ii) recurrently applying the trained layer to obtain a looped model. Training convergence guarantees of the self-attention layer and out-of-distribution generalization guarantees of the looped model are provided. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of ICL mechanism by showcasing how softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners.

</details>


### [238] [SoftSAE: Dynamic Top-K Selection for Adaptive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06610)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftSAE，通过动态Top-K选择机制实现基于输入复杂度的自适应稀疏度，解决了传统SAE固定稀疏度无法适应数据流形局部固有维度变化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TopK SAE架构对所有输入强制执行固定的活跃特征数量K，忽略了现实世界数据流形具有变化的局部固有维度这一特性。简单输入只需少量特征，复杂输入需要更多特征，固定K会导致简单情况引入噪声或复杂情况遗漏重要结构。

Method: 提出SoftSAE，引入可微分的Soft Top-K算子来学习依赖于输入的稀疏度水平k，使模型能够根据每个输入的复杂度动态调整活跃特征数量，从而更好地匹配数据结构。

Result: 实验结果表明，SoftSAE不仅能够发现有意义的特征，还能为每个概念选择正确数量的特征，解释长度能够反映输入中的信息量。

Conclusion: 通过允许基于输入复杂度的自适应特征选择，SoftSAE成功解决了固定稀疏度的局限性，在稀疏自编码器的可解释性研究中取得了进展。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool in mechanistic interpretability, helping to analyze internal representations in both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs). By decomposing polysemantic activations into sparse sets of monosemantic features, SAEs aim to translate neural network computations into human-understandable concepts. However, common architectures such as TopK SAEs rely on a fixed sparsity level. They enforce the same number of active features (K) across all inputs, ignoring the varying complexity of real-world data. Natural data often lies on manifolds with varying local intrinsic dimensionality, meaning the number of relevant factors can change significantly across samples. This suggests that a fixed sparsity level is not optimal. Simple inputs may require only a few features, while more complex ones need more expressive representations. Using a constant K can therefore introduce noise in simple cases or miss important structure in more complex ones. To address this issue, we propose SoftSAE, a sparse autoencoder with a Dynamic Top-K selection mechanism. Our method uses a differentiable Soft Top-K operator to learn an input-dependent sparsity level k. This allows the model to adjust the number of active features based on the complexity of each input. As a result, the representation better matches the structure of the data, and the explanation length reflects the amount of information in the input. Experimental results confirm that SoftSAE not only finds meaningful features, but also selects the right number of features for each concept. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/.

</details>


### [239] [The Structural Origin of Attention Sink: Variance Discrepancy, Super Neurons, and Dimension Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06611)
*Siquan Li,Kaiqi Jiang,Jiacheng Sun,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从机制层面揭示了LLM中注意力汇现象源于自注意力的值聚合过程引发的方差差异，并通过FFN层中超神经元的激活被放大，最终提出了head-wise RMSNorm架构修改来稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管注意力汇现象（attention sink）在大语言模型中普遍存在，即初始token不成比例地占据注意力分数，但其结构起源仍然难以捉摸。本文旨在为这一现象提供机制层面的解释。

Method: 首先追溯注意力汇的根源到自注意力中固有的值聚合过程，该过程会导致系统性的方差差异。进一步证明这种差异被FFN层中超神经元的激活急剧放大，特别是通道稀疏的下投影触发了首token表示的维度差异。通过两个受控干预验证因果链：(i)通过注意力掩码修改隔离聚合效应，(ii)放大目标token表示的方差。

Result: 两个干预实验均能在任意位置复现注意力汇现象，验证了提出的因果链。提出的head-wise RMSNorm架构修改能够在预训练期间稳定值聚合输出，恢复各位置间的统计平衡显著加速了收敛。

Conclusion: 本文为注意力汇现象提供了机制解释，将其归因于值聚合过程的方差差异和FFN中超神经元的放大效应，为系统控制汇的形成提供了基础，并证明了通过架构修改恢复统计平衡可以有效加速训练收敛。

Abstract: Despite the prevalence of the attention sink phenomenon in Large Language Models (LLMs), where initial tokens disproportionately monopolize attention scores, its structural origins remain elusive. This work provides a \textit{mechanistic explanation} for this phenomenon. First, we trace its root to the value aggregation process inherent in self-attention, which induces a systematic variance discrepancy. We further demonstrate that this discrepancy is drastically amplified by the activation of super neurons within Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layers. Specifically, the channel-sparse down-projections trigger a dimension disparity of the first-token representation, necessitating the formation of attention sinks as a structural anchor. Then, we validate this causal chain through two controlled interventions: (i) isolating the aggregation effect via attention mask modifications and (ii) amplifying the variance of targeted token representations. Both interventions can replicate attention sinks at arbitrary positions. Our mechanistic understanding offers a foundation for the systematic control of sink formation. Finally, as a proof of concept, we propose \textit{head-wise RMSNorm}, an architectural modification that stabilizes value aggregation outputs during pre-training. Our experiments demonstrate that restoring statistical parity across positions significantly accelerates convergence.

</details>


### [240] [Online Bayesian Calibration under Gradual and Abrupt System Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06612)
*Yang Xu,Chiwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BRPC框架，通过分离校准参数更新与差异函数更新解决了在线贝叶斯校准中的参数-差异混淆问题，并结合重启机制处理突变，在非平稳环境下显著提升了校准精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典贝叶斯校准存在参数与差异函数混淆问题，且通常假设平稳数据生成过程，无法适应现代数字孪生应用中系统随时间演化、出现渐变漂移和突变的情况；数据同化方法虽支持序贯更新，但未显式建模系统偏差，在突变下效果有限。

Method: 提出贝叶斯递归投影校准(BRPC)：将投影校准扩展到在线设置，通过无差异函数的粒子更新校准参数，结合条件高斯过程更新差异函数，在保持可识别性的同时实现偏差感知的自适应；集成重启机制检测突变并重置校准过程。

Result: 在合成数据和工厂仿真基准测试中，BRPC在渐变场景下提升了校准精度；结合重启机制的BRPC在突变场景下进一步提升了鲁棒性和预测性能，优于滑动窗口贝叶斯校准和数据同化基线方法。

Conclusion: BRPC框架成功实现了在线贝叶斯校准，在渐变和突变两种非平稳场景下均表现出优越性能，为数字孪生应用中的动态系统校准提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Bayesian model calibration is central to digital twins and computer experiments, as it aligns model outputs with field observations by estimating calibration parameters and correcting systematic model bias. Classical Bayesian calibration introduces latent parameters and a discrepancy function to model bias, but suffers from parameter--discrepancy confounding and is typically formulated as an offline procedure under a stationary data-generating assumption. These limitations are restrictive in modern digital twin applications, where systems evolve over time and may exhibit gradual drift and abrupt regime shifts. While data assimilation methods enable sequential updates, they generally do not explicitly model systematic bias and are less effective under abrupt changes. We propose Bayesian Recursive Projected Calibration (BRPC), an online Bayesian calibration framework for streaming data under simulator mismatch and nonstationarity. BRPC extends projected calibration to the online setting by separating a discrepancy-free particle update for calibration parameters from a conditional Gaussian process update for discrepancy, preserving identifiability while enabling bias-aware adaptation under gradual system evolution. To handle abrupt changes, BRPC is integrated with restart mechanisms that detect regime shifts and reset the calibration process. We establish theoretical guarantees for both components, including tracking performance under gradual evolution and false-alarm and detection behavior for restart mechanisms. Empirical studies on synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks show that BRPC improves calibration accuracy under gradual changes, while restart-augmented BRPC further improves robustness and predictive performance under abrupt regime shifts compared to sliding-window Bayesian calibration and data assimilation baselines.

</details>


### [241] [When and Why SignSGD Outperforms SGD: A Theoretical Study Based on $\ell_1$-norm Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06615)
*Hongyi Tao,Dingzhi Yu,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入$\ell_1$-范数平稳性、$\ell_\infty$-光滑性和可分离噪声模型，证明了基于符号的优化器（SignSGD和Muon）在稀疏噪声下相比SGD能将复杂度降低$d$倍（$d$为问题维度），并在124M参数GPT-2预训练中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于符号的优化算法（如SignSGD和Muon）在大模型训练中表现优异，但现有理论无法解释其为何优于SGD。核心障碍在于标准光滑和有限方差条件下，SGD在$\ell_2$-范数度量下已达到极小极大最优，这从根本上排除了符号方法在标准设定下的复杂度优势。

Method: 作者采用与符号更新坐标特性更匹配的问题几何结构：使用$\ell_1$-范数平稳性、$\ell_\infty$-光滑性和可分离噪声模型替代标准假设。在此框架下推导SignSGD的匹配上下界，并通过比较SignSGD的上界与SGD的下界来量化优势。进一步将框架扩展至矩阵域，分析Muon优化器。

Result: 在稀疏噪声条件下，SignSGD相比SGD将复杂度降低$d$倍（$d$为问题维度）。Muon优化器在矩阵域扩展中保持相同的最优维度缩放特性。在124M参数GPT-2模型预训练实验中，理论预测的SignSGD更快收敛速度得到验证。

Conclusion: 通过改变问题几何设定（$\ell_1$平稳性、$\ell_\infty$光滑性、可分离噪声），成功为符号优化器建立了理论优势，证明了其在特定问题类（特别是稀疏噪声场景）中优于SGD的根本原因，为大模型训练中符号优化器的实际成功提供了理论解释。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD and Muon, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in training large foundation models. Despite this empirical success, we still lack a theoretical understanding of when and why these sign-based methods outperform vanilla SGD. The core obstacle is that under standard smoothness and finite variance conditions, SGD is known to be minimax optimal for finding stationary points measured by $\ell_2$-norms, thereby fundamentally precluding any complexity gains for sign-based methods in standard settings. To overcome this barrier, we analyze sign-based optimizers leveraging $\ell_1$-norm stationarity, $\ell_\infty$-smoothness, and a separable noise model, which can better capture the coordinate-wise nature of signed updates. Under this distinct problem geometry, we derive matched upper and lower bounds for SignSGD and explicitly characterize the problem class in which SignSGD provably dominates SGD. Specifically, we compare the \emph{upper bound of SignSGD} with the \emph{lower bound of SGD}, illustrating that SignSGD effectively reduces the complexity by a factor of $d$ under \emph{sparse noise}, where $d$ is the problem dimension. Furthermore, we elevate this framework to the matrix domain, providing an equivalent optimal lower bound for the Muon optimizer, proving that extending the sign operator to matrices preserves this optimal scaling with dimensionality. Finally, we bridge our theoretical bounds to practice, demonstrating that the theoretical superiority of SignSGD accurately predicts its faster convergence during the pretraining of a 124M parameter GPT-2 model.

</details>


### [242] [Hybrid Quantum-Classical GANs for the Generation of Adversarial Network Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06629)
*Prateek Paudel,Nitin Jha,Abhishek Parakh,Mahadevan Subramaniam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合量子-经典生成对抗网络（QC-GAN）框架，利用变分量子生成器生成能够绕过入侵检测系统（IDS）的对抗性网络流量，展示了量子机器学习在网络安全攻击中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的生成对抗网络（GANs）已被用于生成攻击入侵检测系统的对抗性网络流量，但存在需要大量高维数据集、模式崩溃和计算开销大等缺陷。为了克服这些限制并探索量子计算在网络安全领域的应用潜力，作者提出了混合量子-经典方案。

Method: 提出混合量子-经典生成对抗网络（QC-GAN）框架，使用变分量子生成器替代经典生成器。该方法将潜向量（隐藏特征）编码为量子态而非经典噪声向量，声称能获得更具表达力的潜在表示并降低计算开销。使用经典判别器在真实数据集（UNSW-NB15）和生成的假网络流量上进行训练，采用经典IDS模型（随机森林分类器和基于卷积神经网络的分类器）评估生成流量的攻击效果，并研究了硬件噪声对攻击的影响。

Result: 所提出的QC-GAN框架能够生成可以绕过经典入侵检测系统（如随机森林和CNN分类器）的对抗性网络流量，验证了量子机器学习在生成高级攻击流量方面的有效性，同时分析了硬件噪声对攻击性能的影响。

Conclusion: 本研究突出了量子机器学习作为生成高级攻击流量和压力测试经典IDS的手段的可能性，强调了建立量子弹性防御系统的必要性，为未来网络安全防御提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Classical generative adversarial networks (GANs) have been applied to generate adversarial network traffic capable of attacking intrusion detection systems, but they suffer from shortcomings such as the need for large amounts of high-dimensional datasets, mode collapse, and high computational overhead. In this work, we propose a hybrid quantum-classical GAN (QC-GAN) framework where a variational quantum generator is used to generate synthetic network traffic flows mimicking malicious traffic using latent representations. Instead of sampling classical noise vectors, we encode the latent vector (the hidden features) as a quantum state, which is the basis for claiming more expressive latent representations and reducing computational overhead. A classical discriminator will be trained on real-world datasets (UNSW-NB15) and the proposed QC-GAN-generated fake network flows. In this configuration, the generator aims to minimize the discriminator's ability to distinguish real from fake traffic, while the discriminator aims to maximize its classification accuracy, in an iterative manner. In our attack model, we assume that the attacker is a state actor with access to limited quantum computing power, whereas the discriminator is chosen to be classical, as will likely be the case for most end users and organizations. We test the generated flows using classical intrusion detection system (IDS) models, such as a random forest classifier and a convolutional neural network-based classifier, for their ability to bypass the detection process. This work aims to highlight the possibilities of quantum machine learning as a means of generating advanced attack flows and stress testing classical IDS. Lastly, we further evaluate how hardware-based noise affects these attacks to offer a new perspective on IDS, highlighting the need for a quantum resilient defense system.

</details>


### [243] [Crafting Reversible SFT Behaviors in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06632)
*Yuping Lin,Pengfei He,Yue Xing,Yingqian Cui,Jiayuan Ding,Subhabrata Mukherjee,Hui Liu,Zhen Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LCDD方法将SFT诱导的行为压缩到稀疏的“载体”子网络中，并通过SFT-Eraser实现推理时可逆的行为控制，证明了结构而非触发器设计是实现行为抑制的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SFT行为解释方法（如电路归因）仅能事后识别与行为相关的稀疏子网络，但相关性不等于因果必要性，无法在推理时选择性控制SFT诱导的行为。作者希望找到一种方法，能够将SFT诱导的行为显式压缩到稀疏且机制必要的子网络（载体）中，从而在不修改权重的情况下实现可控的行为抑制。

Method: 提出两个核心方法：(a) LCDD（损失约束双下降）通过联合优化路由掩码和模型权重，在显式效用预算下构建稀疏载体；(b) SFT-Eraser通过激活匹配优化软提示，对提取的载体通道进行干预以逆转SFT诱导的行为。

Result: 在多个模型家族的安全、固定响应和风格行为上，LCDD生成的稀疏载体能够保持目标行为，同时在SFT-Eraser触发时实现强逆转。消融实验表明，相同的触发优化在标准SFT模型上失败，证实稀疏结构是逆转的必要前提，而非触发器设计。

Conclusion: 学习到的载体对行为具有因果必要性，为系统性地定位和选择性抑制部署模型中的SFT诱导行为提供了新方向，证明了结构化约束而非事后相关性分析是实现可控行为干预的关键。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) induces new behaviors in large language models, yet imposes no structural constraint on how these behaviors are distributed within the model. Existing behavior interpretation methods, such as circuit attribution approaches, identify sparse subnetworks correlated with SFT-induced behaviors post-hoc. However, such correlations do not imply *causal necessity*, limiting the ability to selectively control SFT-induced behaviors at inference time. We pursue an alternative by asking: can an SFT-induced behavior be deliberately compressed into a sparse, mechanistically necessary subnetwork, termed a *carrier*, while remaining controllable at inference time without weight modification? We propose (a) **Loss-Constrained Dual Descent (LCDD)**, which constructs such carriers by jointly optimizing routing masks and model weights under an explicit utility budget, and (b) **SFT-Eraser**, a soft prompt optimized via activation matching on extracted carrier channels, to reverse the SFT-induced behavior. Across safety, fixed-response, and style behaviors on multiple model families, LCDD yields sparse carriers that preserve target behaviors while enabling strong reversion when triggered by SFT-Eraser. Ablations further establish that the sparse structure is the key precondition for reversal: the same trigger optimization fails on standard SFT models, confirming that structure rather than trigger design is the operative factor. These results provide direct evidence that the learned carriers are causally necessary for the behaviors, pointing to a new direction for systematically localizing and selectively suppressing SFT-induced behaviors in deployed models.

</details>


### [244] [Concept-Based Abductive and Contrastive Explanations for Behaviors of Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06640)
*Ronaldo Canizales,Divya Gopinath,Corina Păsăreanu,Ravi Mangal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于概念的溯因和对比解释方法，通过概念擦除程序建立高层概念与模型预测之间的因果关系，能够识别导致模型决策的最小概念集合，并支持单张图像和具有特定行为的图像集合的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于概念的解释方法要么无法建立概念与模型预测之间的因果联系，要么表达能力有限仅能推断单一概念的因果解释；而形式化的溯因和对比解释方法虽然能计算因果相关的最小输入特征集，但仅考虑像素级等低层特征。因此需要一种能结合两者优势的方法。

Method: 提出基于概念的溯因和对比解释概念，通过概念擦除程序建立因果关系，设计算法枚举所有最小解释集合，并通过适当的聚合方法将解释应用于单张图像和具有用户指定共同行为的图像集合。

Result: 在多个模型、数据集和行为上进行了评估，证明了该方法在计算有用且用户友好的解释方面的有效性，能够理解模型在单张图像和图像集合上的预测行为。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地将基于概念的解释与因果推理相结合，不仅能解释单个样本的模型预测，还能解释模型在特定行为模式下的决策机制，为深度神经网络提供了高层、可理解且具有因果性的解释框架。

Abstract: *Concept-based explanations* offer a promising approach for explaining the predictions of deep neural networks in terms of high-level, human-understandable concepts. However, existing methods either do not establish a causal connection between the concepts and model predictions or are limited in expressivity and only able to infer causal explanations involving single concepts. At the same time, the parallel line of work on *formal abductive and contrastive explanations* computes the minimal set of input features causally relevant for model outcomes but only considers low-level features such as pixels. Merging these two threads, in this work, we propose the notion of *concept-based abductive and contrastive explanations* that capture the minimal sets of high-level concepts causally relevant for model outcomes. We then present a family of algorithms that enumerate all minimal explanations while using *concept erasure* procedures to establish causal relationships. By appropriately aggregating such explanations, we are not only able to understand model predictions on individual images but also on collections of images where the model exhibits a user-specified, common *behavior*. We evaluate our approach on multiple models, datasets, and behaviors, and demonstrate its effectiveness in computing helpful, user-friendly explanations.

</details>


### [245] [Edge-specific signal propagation on mature chromophore-region 3D mechanism graphs for fluorescent protein quantum-yield prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06644)
*Yuchen Xiong,Swee Keong Yeap,Steven Aw Yoong Kit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种以发色团为中心的机制图算法，通过3D残基图和通道-信号-区域传播来预测荧光蛋白的量子产率，在531个蛋白基准测试中优于ESM-C和SaProt等基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白语言模型和发射带平均值仅能捕捉全局趋势，无法建模局部物理信号如何作用于特定发色团区域，因此需要一种能够显式建模局部物理信号的方法。

Method: 将PDB结构转换为类型化3D残基图，配准到成熟CRO状态，划分为酚盐、桥和咪唑啉酮区域，通过通道-信号-区域传播提取121个富集特征，使用非身份特征的ExtraTrees回归进行预测。

Result: 在531个蛋白基准测试中达到R=0.772±0.008、MAE=0.131±0.002，优于ESM-C(R=0.734)和SaProt(R=0.731)；在远程同源性桶(<50%相似度)中优势明显(R=0.697 vs 0.575,0.408)；亮蛋白筛选Top-K表现最强。

Conclusion: 特征具有内在可解释性，发现了不同波段蛋白的带特异性机制：GFP类蛋白的芳香堆积和夹钳不对称、红光蛋白的电荷/夹钳平衡、远红光蛋白的柔性风险/大体积接触特征。

Abstract: Fluorescent protein quantum yield (QY) is governed by the mature chromophore and its three-dimensional microenvironment rather than sequence identity alone. Protein language models and emission-band averages capture global trends, but do not model how local physical signals act on specific chromophore regions.
  We present a chromophore-centred mechanism graph algorithm for QY prediction. Each PDB structure is converted into a typed 3D residue graph, registered to a mature-CRO state, partitioned into phenolate, bridge and imidazolinone regions, and transformed by channel-signal-region propagation. The representation contains 121 enrichment features; after removing identity shortcuts, 52 non-identity features are used for band-specific ExtraTrees regression. Because each feature encodes a contact channel, seed signal and target CRO region, interpretation is intrinsic rather than post hoc. On a 531-protein benchmark, the method achieved the best random-CV performance among model-based baselines (R = 0.772 +/- 0.008, MAE = 0.131 +/- 0.002), exceeding Band mean (R = 0.632), ESM-C (R = 0.734) and SaProt (R = 0.731), and ranked first in bright screening (Bright P@5 = 0.704). Under homology control, the advantage was clearest in the remote bucket (<50% similarity; R = 0.697 versus 0.633, 0.575 and 0.408), with the strongest overall bright/dark Top-K screening. Stable selected features recovered band-specific mechanisms: aromatic packing and clamp asymmetry in GFP-like proteins, charge/clamp balance in Red proteins, and flexibility-risk/bulky-contact features in Far-red proteins.
  Source code, feature tables and evaluation scripts are available from the first author upon request. Contact: yuchenak05@gmail.com

</details>


### [246] [Inductive Venn-Abers and related regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06646)
*Ivan Petej,Vladimir Vovk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Venn-Abers预测器从二分类和有界回归推广到无界回归场景，通过结合共形预测元素，并在实验中验证了该方法在大训练集下能略微提升预测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Venn-Abers预测器虽然具有理想的统计有效性，但应用范围受限，仅适用于二分类问题，近期虽扩展至有界回归，但仍无法处理更普遍的无界回归问题。因此需要将其推广到无界回归场景以扩大应用范围。

Method: 通过引入共形预测（conformal prediction）元素，将Venn-Abers预测器扩展至无界回归场景；从Venn-Abers回归器推导点回归器，并通过仿真和实证研究评估其预测效率。

Result: 实验研究表明，在大规模训练集上，从Venn-Abers回归器推导的点回归器相比标准回归器在预测效率上有一定程度的提升。

Conclusion: 成功将Venn-Abers预测器推广到无界回归问题，在保持原有方法有效性优势的同时，在大训练集场景下获得了预测效率的改善，扩展了该方法的应用范围。

Abstract: Venn-Abers predictors are probabilistic predictors that enjoy appealing properties of validity, but their major limitation is that they are applicable only to the case of binary classification, with a recent extension to bounded regression. We generalize them to the case of unbounded regression, which requires adding an element of conformal prediction. In our simulation and empirical studies we investigate the predictive efficiency of point regressors derived from Venn-Abers regressors and argue that they somewhat improve the predictive efficiency of standard regressors for larger training sets.

</details>


### [247] [When No Benchmark Exists: Validating Comparative LLM Safety Scoring Without Ground-Truth Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06652)
*Sushant Gautam,Finn Schwall,Annika Willoch Olstad,Fernando Vallecillos Ruiz,Birk Torpmann-Hagen,Sunniva Maria Stordal Bjørklund,Leon Moonen,Klas Pettersen,Michael A. Riegler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在缺乏标注基准的情况下进行语言模型安全比较评分的方法（Benchmarkless Comparative Safety Scoring），通过工具效度链确保评分有效性，并展示了其在挪威公共部门采购中的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际部署场景需要在相关语言、行业或监管领域尚无标注基准的情况下，对候选语言模型进行安全性比较。现有方法依赖标注数据，无法满足这一实际需求。

Method: 将问题形式化为无基准比较安全评分，定义了场景化审计可被解释为部署证据的契约条件（固定场景包、评分标准、审计员、评判者、采样配置和重运行预算）。由于缺乏标签，采用工具效度链替代真实一致性验证，包括：对受控的安全-消融对比的响应性、目标驱动方差优于审计员和评判者伪影、跨重运行的稳定性。在SimpleAudit工具中实现该方法，并在挪威安全数据包上验证。

Result: 在挪威安全数据包验证中，安全目标与消融目标分离的AUROC值介于0.89至1.00之间；目标身份是主要方差成分（η²≈0.52）；严重性分布约在10次重运行后稳定。将相同链条应用于Petri工具表明两者均可适用，但差异源于上游的声明契约执行和部署适配。挪威公共部门采购案例显示，更安全的模型取决于场景类别和风险度量方式。

Conclusion: 评分必须在固定契约条件下才有效，评分结果、匹配增量、关键比率、不确定性以及所使用的审计员和评判者应一并报告，而不能简化为单一排名。不同场景类别和风险度量可能导致不同的安全性结论。

Abstract: Many deployments must compare candidate language models for safety before a labeled benchmark exists for the relevant language, sector, or regulatory regime. We formalize this setting as benchmarkless comparative safety scoring and specify the contract under which a scenario-based audit can be interpreted as deployment evidence. Scores are valid only under a fixed scenario pack, rubric, auditor, judge, sampling configuration, and rerun budget. Because no labels are available, we replace ground-truth agreement with an instrumental-validity chain: responsiveness to a controlled safe-versus-abliterated contrast, dominance of target-driven variance over auditor and judge artifacts, and stability across reruns.
  We instantiate the chain in SimpleAudit, a local-first scoring instrument, and validate it on a Norwegian safety pack. Safe and abliterated targets separate with AUROC values between 0.89 and 1.00, target identity is the dominant variance component ($η^2 \approx 0.52$), and severity profiles stabilize by ten reruns. Applying the same chain to Petri shows that it admits both tools. The substantial differences arise upstream of the chain, in claim-contract enforcement and deployment fit. A Norwegian public-sector procurement case comparing Borealis and Gemma 3 demonstrates the resulting evidence in practice: the safer model depends on scenario category and risk measure. Consequently, scores, matched deltas, critical rates, uncertainty, and the auditor and judge used must be reported together rather than collapsed into a single ranking.

</details>


### [248] [Optimizer-Model Consistency: Full Finetuning with the Same Optimizer as Pretraining Forgets Less](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06654)
*Yuxing Liu,Jianyu Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在全量微调(SFT)阶段，使用与预训练相同的优化器（保持优化器-模型一致性）能获得更优的学习-遗忘权衡，表现优于其他优化器甚至LoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究优化器选择对大语言模型微调性能的影响，特别是预训练与微调阶段优化器一致性如何影响模型对新任务的学习能力和对预训练知识的保留能力（遗忘问题）。

Method: 通过控制实验和理论分析，探究优化器对激活的正则化效应及其对预训练检查点周围损失景观的影响；分析SFT阶段权重更新结构如何降低知识遗忘；对比Muon和AdamW优化器在推理任务上的表现，并通过合成语言建模实验验证Muon的机械记忆倾向。

Result: 1）优化器通过激活的正则化效应塑造模型，形成不同的损失景观；2）使用相同优化器可使权重更新遵循特定结构以减少预训练知识的遗忘；3）Muon在推理任务微调中表现较差，源于其强烈的机械记忆倾向，不利于小数据量下的模式获取。

Conclusion: 提出了“优化器-模型一致性”现象，证明保持预训练和微调阶段优化器一致的重要性；揭示了优化器影响模型学习的内在机制；为LLM训练实践提供了优化器选择的理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: Optimizers play an important role in both pretraining and finetuning stages when training large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we present an observation that full finetuning with the same optimizer as in pretraining achieves a better learning-forgetting tradeoff, i.e., forgetting less while achieving the same or better performance on the new task, than other optimizers and, possibly surprisingly, LoRA, during the supervised finetuning (SFT) stage. We term this phenomenon optimizer-model consistency. To better understand it, through controlled experiments and theoretical analysis, we show that: 1) optimizers can shape the models by having regularization effects on the activations, leading to different landscapes around the pretrained checkpoints; 2) in response to this regularization effect, the weight update in SFT should follow some specific structures to lower forgetting of the knowledge learned in pretraining, which can be obtained by using the same optimizer. Moreover, we specifically compare Muon and AdamW when they are employed throughout the pretraining and SFT stages and find that Muon performs worse when finetuned for reasoning tasks. With a synthetic language modeling experiment, we demonstrate that this can come from Muon's strong tendency towards rote memorization, which may hurt pattern acquisition with a small amount of data, as for SFT.

</details>


### [249] [Why Global LLM Leaderboards Are Misleading: Small Portfolios for Heterogeneous Supervised ML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06656)
*Jai Moondra,Ayela Chughtai,Bhargavi Lanka,Swati Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现现有LLM排行榜基于全局Bradley-Terry排名存在严重误导性，由于用户意见的异质性，排名几乎没有区分度。本文提出(λ, ν)-投资组合框架，通过小规模模型集合覆盖不同用户群体，大幅提升预测覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM排行榜基于成对人类反馈进行全局排名，但这种排名可能掩盖了用户群体的异质性。研究者观察到在Arena数据集中，近2/3的决定性选票相互抵消，顶级模型间胜率差异极小（最多0.53），这表明全局排名可能不可靠。需要深入分析这种失败的原因并寻找解决方案。

Method: 1. 分析Arena平台约89K条多语言比较数据（116种语言，52个LLM），识别全局BT排名的失败原因；2. 提出(λ, ν)-投资组合框架，定义为满足预测误差≤λ且覆盖≥ν比例用户的小规模模型集合；3. 将问题形式化为集合覆盖问题的变体，利用底层集合系统的VC维度提供理论保证；4. 开发算法从数据中恢复多个局部BT排名构成投资组合。

Result: 1. 全局排名失败源于语言、任务和时间维度的强结构性意见异质性；2. 按语言分组后投票一致性大幅提升，ELO分数差异扩大两个数量级；3. Arena数据上仅需5个不同BT排名即可在较低λ值下覆盖超过96%的选票（全局排名仅覆盖21%）；4. 6个LLM的投资组合覆盖票数是全局排名Top-6的两倍；5. 在COMPAS数据集上成功检测数据盲点。

Conclusion: 全局LLM排名具有误导性，"全球噪声"实际上是多个一致但相互冲突的子群体的混合。通过识别这些子群体并构建(λ, ν)-投资组合，可以用少量模型有效覆盖多样化用户需求，为模型选择和公平性评估提供更可靠的方法。

Abstract: Ranking LLMs via pairwise human feedback underpins current leaderboards for open-ended tasks, such as creative writing and problem-solving. We analyze ~89K comparisons in 116 languages from 52 LLMs from Arena, and show that the best-fit global Bradley-Terry (BT) ranking is misleading. Nearly 2/3 of the decisive votes cancel out, and even the top 50 models according to the global BT ranking are statistically indistinguishable (pairwise win probabilities are at most 0.53 within the top 50 models). We trace this failure to strong, structured heterogeneity of opinions across language, task, and time. Moreover, we find an important characteristic - *language* plays a key role. Grouping by language (and families) increases the agreement of votes massively, resulting in two orders of magnitude higher spread in the ELO scores (i.e., very consistent rankings). What appears as global noise is in fact a mixture of coherent but conflicting subpopulations.
  To address such heterogeneity in supervised machine learning, we introduce the framework of $(λ, ν)$-portfolios, which are small sets of models that achieve a prediction error at most $λ$, "covering" at least a $ν$ fraction of users. We formulate this as a variant of the set cover problem and provide guarantees using the VC dimension of the underlying set system. On the Arena data, our algorithms recover just 5 distinct BT rankings that cover over 96% of votes at a modest $λ$, compared to the 21% coverage by the global ranking. We also provide a portfolio of 6 LLMs that cover twice as many votes as the top-6 LLMs from a global ranking. We further construct portfolios for a classification problem on the COMPAS dataset using an ensemble of fairness-regularized classification models and show that these portfolios can be used to detect blind spots in the data, which might be of independent interest to policymakers.

</details>


### [250] [Verifier-Backed Hard Problem Generation for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06660)
*Yuhang Lai,Jiazhan Feng,Yee Whye Teh,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了VHG框架，通过在传统的设置者-求解者二元对抗中引入独立的验证者，构建三方博弈自博弈机制，有效解决了大模型在生成科学和数学难题时面临的无效问题和奖励黑客问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然在解决科学和数学问题方面表现出色，但在生成有效、具有挑战性和新颖的问题方面存在困难，而这是推进LLM训练和实现自主科学研究的关键。现有的问题生成方法要么依赖昂贵的人类专家参与，要么采用简单的自博弈范式，后者常因奖励黑客问题导致生成无效问题。

Method: 提出VHG（Verifier-Enhanced Hard Problem Generation）框架，基于三方自博弈机制。在传统的设置者-求解者二元对立中集成一个独立的验证者，将设置者的奖励约束为由问题有效性（由验证者评估）和问题难度（由求解者评估）共同决定。实现了两种验证者变体：硬符号验证者和软LLM验证者。

Result: 在不定积分任务和通用数学推理任务上的评估表明，VHG在所有基线方法上均有显著优势，大幅超越现有方法。验证了引入独立验证者能有效约束奖励机制，生成更有效且具有挑战性的问题。

Conclusion: 通过引入独立验证者构建三方博弈机制，VHG成功解决了传统自博弈方法中的奖励黑客问题，能够生成更有效且更具挑战性的数学问题，为LLM训练和自主科学研究提供了重要支持。
