Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities for solving scientific and mathematical problems, yet they struggle to produce valid, challenging, and novel problems - an essential component for advancing LLM training and enabling autonomous scientific research. Existing problem generation approaches either depend on expensive human expert involvement or adopt naive self-play paradigms, which frequently yield invalid problems due to reward hacking. This work introduces VHG, a verifier-enhanced hard problem generation framework built upon three-party self-play. By integrating an independent verifier into the conventional setter-solver duality, our design constrains the setter's reward to be jointly determined by problem validity (evaluated by the verifier) and difficulty (assessed by the solver). We instantiate two verifier variants: a Hard symbolic verifier and a Soft LLM-based verifier, with evaluations conducted on indefinite integral tasks and general mathematical reasoning tasks. Experimental results show that VHG substantially outperforms all baseline methods by a clear margin.

</details>


### [251] [UniPool: A Globally Shared Expert Pool for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06665)
*Minbin Huang,Han Shi,Chuanyang Zheng,Yimeng Wu,Guoxuan Chen,Xintong Yu,Yichun Yin,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UniPool架构，通过共享专家池替代传统的逐层专家分配，在减少34%-58%专家参数的情况下达到或超越传统MoE性能，实现了专家参数的次线性深度扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE架构采用僵化的逐层专家分配规则，导致深度扩展时专家参数线性增长，且假设每层需要独立的专家容量。然而路由探针实验显示，深层路由器被随机路由替换后性能仅下降1.0-1.6个点，揭示了严重的冗余性，这促使作者探索全局共享专家池的设计。

Method: 提出UniPool架构：(1) 用单一共享专家池替代逐层专家所有权，各层通过独立路由器访问；(2) 引入池级辅助损失以平衡全局专家利用率；(3) 采用NormRouter实现稀疏且尺度稳定的路由。将专家容量视为全局架构预算，池大小作为显式的深度扩展超参数。

Result: 在五个LLaMA架构规模(182M-978M参数)上训练30B tokens，UniPool相比传统MoE持续改善验证损失，最大降低0.0386。关键发现：仅使用41.6%-66.7%专家参数的缩减池UniPool变体即可匹配或超越传统MoE性能，证明专家参数无需随深度线性增长，可实现次线性增长且更高效。该优势可与细粒度专家分解技术叠加。

Conclusion: 共享专家池设计打破了传统MoE中专家参数必须随深度线性增长的限制，实现了更高效的参数利用。UniPool揭示了专家容量应被视为全局预算而非逐层分配，为MoE架构的深度扩展提供了新的设计范式，在保持或提升性能的同时显著降低了参数开销。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures allocate expert capacity through a rigid per-layer rule: each transformer layer owns a separate expert set. This convention couples depth scaling with linear expert-parameter growth and assumes that every layer needs isolated expert capacity. However, recent analyses and our routing probe challenge this allocation rule: replacing a deeper layer's learned top-k router with uniform random routing drops downstream accuracy by only 1.0-1.6 points across multiple production MoE models. Motivated by this redundancy, we propose UniPool, an MoE architecture that treats expert capacity as a global architectural budget by replacing per-layer expert ownership with a single shared pool accessed by independent per-layer routers. To enable stable and balanced training under sharing, we introduce a pool-level auxiliary loss that balances expert utilization across the entire pool, and adopt NormRouter to provide sparse and scale-stable routing into the shared expert pool. Across five LLaMA-architecture model scales (182M, 469M, 650M, 830M, and 978M parameters) trained on 30B tokens from the Pile, UniPool consistently improves validation loss and perplexity over the matched vanilla MoE baselines. Across these scales, UniPool reduces validation loss by up to 0.0386 relative to vanilla MoE. Beyond raw loss improvement, our results identify pool size as an explicit depth-scaling hyperparameter: reduced-pool UniPool variants using only 41.6%-66.7% of the vanilla expert-parameter budget match or outperform layer-wise MoE at the tested scales. This shows that, under a shared-pool design, expert parameters need not grow linearly with depth; they can grow sublinearly while remaining more efficient and effective than vanilla MoE. Further analysis shows that UniPool's benefits compose with finer-grained expert decomposition.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [Performance Characterization of dApps in Open Radio Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05426)
*Conrado Boeira,Eduardo Baena,Andrea Lacava,Tommaso Melodia,Dimitrios Koutsonikolas,Israat Haque*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对O-RAN实时应用(dApps)部署中过度依赖裸机服务器且缺乏资源使用可见性的问题，对比分析了裸机与容器化部署在延迟、可扩展性和资源利用率上的权衡，并提出利用智能网卡等硬件加速器卸载dApps以缓解性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管建议在容器化环境中部署实时O-RAN应用(dApps)，但现有方案主要依赖裸机服务器；同时，当前部署缺乏对智能和非智能dApps资源使用模式的深入可见性，阻碍了合理的部署决策。

Method: 在多种部署场景（裸机和容器）中实现并评估具有代表性的dApps，以此表征延迟、可扩展性和资源利用率方面的权衡；并探索将dApps卸载至智能网卡等新型硬件加速器的方案。

Result: 明确了不同部署场景下的性能权衡，识别了关键性能瓶颈，并证明将dApps卸载至智能网卡等硬件加速器可有效缓解这些限制，提升O-RAN系统的实时响应能力。

Conclusion: 容器化部署dApps具有可行性但存在性能权衡，结合智能网卡等硬件加速器进行卸载是解决性能瓶颈、提升实时响应能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Despite recommendations to deploy real-time Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) applications (dApps) in containerized environments, existing approaches predominantly rely on bare-metal servers. Moreover, current dApp deployments offer limited visibility into the resource usage patterns of both intelligent and non-intelligent dApps, hindering informed deployment decisions. This work addresses these gaps by implementing and evaluating representative dApps across multiple deployment scenarios (bare-metal and containers) to characterize the trade-offs in latency, scalability, and resource utilization. Additionally, we identify key performance bottlenecks and demonstrate how offloading dApps to emerging hardware accelerators, such as smart Network Interface Cards (NICs), can alleviate these limitations and improve real-time responsiveness in O-RAN systems.

</details>


### [253] [SixGman: An Open-Source Planner for Fixed 6G Hierarchical Optical Access-Core Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05763)
*Matin Rafiei Forooshani,Farhad Arpanaei,Hamzeh Beyranvand,Alfonso Sánchez-Macián,Juan Pedro Fernández-Palacios,José Alberto Hernández,David Larrabeiti*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SixGman，一个开源的光网络规划工具。通过在Telefónica MAN157城域网拓扑上的应用，证明了 bypass HL3层节点架构相比传统全层次架构能降低17.5%的总拥有成本（TCO）和29.1%的累计能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了应对光网络规划中对灵活、透明且可复现仿真工具的需求，本文旨在开发一个集成流量生成、路由、传输质量估算、资源分配及技术经济分析等多种功能的开源框架，以评估接入-城域-核心汇聚网络架构的性能与成本。

Method: 提出了名为SixGman的开源光网络规划工具，其采用基于标准化接口和明确功能定义的模块化设计。将该工具应用于由157个光节点、220条链路及四个层次组成的Telefónica MAN157城域网拓扑，对比分析了传统全层次架构与HL3旁路架构（移除HL3节点的电层汇聚）在流量分布、IP路由器利用率、链路拥塞、延迟、TCO及能耗等方面的表现。

Result: 研究结果表明，HL3旁路架构改善了流量分布，减少了光/电资源使用，降低了端到端延迟。与传统架构相比，该场景实现了最高17.5%的总拥有成本（TCO）降低和29.1%的累计能耗降低。

Conclusion: SixGman作为一个灵活的网络规划平台，能够有效辅助设计成本与能效优化的光网络架构，证明了其在光网络规划与架构评估中的实用价值和潜力。

Abstract: This paper introduces SixGman, an open-source optical network planning tool for evaluating access-metro-core aggregation network architectures. The framework integrates traffic generation, dual-homed routing, Quality of Transmission (QoT) estimation, spectrum and fiber assignment, techno-economic analysis, energy consumption evaluation, and visualization capabilities. Its modular design, based on standardized interfaces and clearly defined functions, enables flexible, transparent, and reproducible network simulations. SixGman is applied to the Telefónica MAN157 metro-urban topology, composed of 157 optical nodes, 220 links, and four hierarchical layers (HL1-HL4), to compare a conventional full hierarchical architecture with an HL3-bypassed architecture where electrical aggregation at HL3 nodes is removed. The analysis includes traffic distribution, IP router utilization, link congestion, latency, Total Cost of Ownership (TCO), and energy consumption. Results show that HL3 bypassing improves traffic distribution, reduces optical and electrical resource usage, lowers end-to-end latency, and decreases both capital and operational expenditures. Compared to the full hierarchical architecture, the HL3-bypassed scenario achieves reductions of up to 17.5% in TCO and 29.1% in cumulative energy consumption. These results demonstrate the potential of SixGman as a flexible planning platform for cost- and energy-efficient optical network design.

</details>


### [254] [Bridging the 6G Gap: Scaling Sustainable ROADM-Based IP-over-WDM via DSCM-Enabled Point-to-Multipoint Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05793)
*Matin Rafiei Forooshani,Farhad Arpanaei,Hamzeh Beyranvand,Mahdi Ranjbar Zefreh,Juan Pedro Fernández-Palacios,Alfonso Sánchez-Macián,José Alberto Hernández,David Larrabeiti*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究对比了三种接入-城域架构，发现基于DSCM的PtMP IPoWDM方案在十年周期内可将CAPEX降低92.0%、功耗降低99.2%，显著优于传统方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估和对比不同接入-城域架构的性能表现，特别是针对转发器架构、点对点架构以及基于DSCM的点对多点（PtMP）架构进行深入研究。

Method: 采用对比分析方法，对转发器架构、点对点（Point-to-Point）架构以及基于DSCM的点对多点的接入-城域架构进行系统性评估和比较。

Result: 研究结果表明，PtMP IPoWDM架构在各种地理类型中均展现出优异的效率优化能力，在十年时间跨度内，与传统基准相比，CAPEX降低了92.0%，功耗降低了99.2%。

Conclusion: 基于DSCM的PtMP IPoWDM架构在接入-城域网络场景中具有显著的经济和技术优势，可作为传统架构的高效替代方案。

Abstract: This study compares transponder-based, Point-to-Point, and DSCM-based Point-to-Multipoint (PtMP) access-metro architectures. Findings demonstrate that PtMP IPoWDM significantly optimizes efficiency across diverse geotypes, slashing CAPEX by 92.0% and power by 99.2% compared to the traditional benchmark over a ten-year horizon.

</details>


### [255] [Comparative Analysis of Direct-to-Cell (D2C) and 3GPP Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN) for Global Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05843)
*Donglin Wang,Anjie Qiu,Qiuheng Zhou,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文全面比较了直接到蜂窝(D2C)与3GPP非地面网络(NTN)两种卫星通信架构。分析表明，D2C凭借现有设备兼容性实现快速市场准入，而NTN在性能、安全性和可扩展性方面更具优势，是6G星地融合的基础框架。混合模式结合两者优势是实现全球连接的最优路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对无处不在的移动覆盖需求的增长，卫星通信领域出现了两种截然不同的架构范式。迫切需要从技术角度对D2C和3GPP NTN进行全面比较分析，为6G网络架构设计提供指导，特别是在自动驾驶等安全关键应用场景中确定最优的网络冗余架构。

Method: 本文采用了综合技术比较分析方法，从多个维度对D2C和3GPP NTN进行对比：1) 标准化演进轨迹分析；2) 网络架构对比；3) 物理层创新技术评估；4) 安全态势分析；5) 运营权衡分析。同时针对6G新兴用例（特别是自动驾驶）提出了混合三链路架构方案，并分析了其可行性。

Result: 研究结果表明：1) D2C通过兼容传统设备实现了快速市场准入，特别适合应急连接场景；2) 3GPP NTN在性能、安全性和可扩展性方面表现更优，更适合作为长期演进框架；3) NTN定位为6G星地融合的基础框架；4) 针对自动驾驶等安全关键应用，提出了结合地面5G、NTN宽带和D2C应急备份的混合三链路架构。

Conclusion: NTN凭借其系统性、标准化的卫星原生框架，更适合作为6G时代的核心技术基础。然而，融合D2C和NTN两者优势的混合模式才是实现真正全球连接的最实用路径。这种混合方案既能保证长期的技术演进能力，又能快速响应市场需求，为未来6G网络的星地融合提供了切实可行的发展方向。

Abstract: The quest for ubiquitous mobile coverage has catalyzed two fundamentally distinct architectural paradigms: Direct-to-Cell (D2C) and standardized 3GPP Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN). D2C, pioneered by SpaceX Starlink and AST SpaceMobile, leverages existing terrestrial spectrum and unmodified consumer handsets to provide emergency connectivity as a market-driven overlay. In contrast, 3GPP NTN, standardized across Releases 17-19, offers a systematic satellite-native framework designed for long-term scalability, high-throughput broadband, and deep integration with terrestrial 5G/6G networks. This paper presents a comprehensive technical comparison of these approaches, analyzing their standardization trajectories, network architectures, physical-layer innovations, security postures, and operational trade-offs. We further examine their implications for emerging 6G use cases, particularly autonomous driving, where safety-critical redundancy motivates a hybrid tri-link architecture combining terrestrial 5G, NTN broadband, and D2C emergency fallback. Our analysis shows that, although D2C enables rapid market entry through legacy-device compatibility, NTN provides superior performance, security, and scalability, positioning it as the foundational framework for 6G satellite-terrestrial convergence. A hybrid model that combines the strengths of both paradigms is identified as the most practical path toward truly global connectivity.

</details>


### [256] [A Disaster-Aware Integrated TN-NTN System-Level Simulator for Resilient 6G Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05852)
*Donglin Wang,Anjie Qiu,Qiuheng Zhou,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个轻量级的系统级仿真器，用于评估在部分故障灾难模型下，地面网络（TN）与非地面网络（NTN）融合无线网络的故障后回退行为，结果展示了混合TN-NTN运营的弹性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着3GPP将非地面网络（NTN）纳入未来6G系统的标准，LEO卫星、HAPS和UAV等技术被寄予厚望，以在极端事件和灾难期间增强地面网络（TN）的覆盖范围和韧性。目前需要有效的工具来评估集成网络在部分故障场景下的性能表现与回退行为。

Method: 提出了一种遵循3GPP Rel-17/18建模原则的轻量级系统级仿真器。该仿真器支持概率性地面下一代节点B（gNB）故障模型，以及向NTN的服务迁移功能，允许在不同用户负载、灾难严重程度和NTN配置水平下进行比较分析。

Result: 仿真结果揭示了地面运营中预期的容量-延迟权衡、非地面服务的可靠性与稳定性，以及混合TN-NTN运营的平衡弹性行为。通过吞吐量、分组接收率（PRR）和延迟等指标验证了不同网络配置下的性能差异。

Conclusion: 该仿真框架为研究未来集成6G移动系统中的无线网络弹性和流量管理提供了一个易于处理的分析工具，证明了TN与NTN融合在应对灾难场景时的有效性。

Abstract: Non-terrestrial networks (NTN) have been standardized by the 3rd generation partnership project (3GPP) as a key component of future 6G systems to enhance coverage and resilience. In particular, NTN technologies such as low-earth orbit (LEO) satellites, high-altitude platform stations (HAPS), and unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are expected to support terrestrial networks (TN) during extreme events and disasters. In this paper, we present a lightweight system-level simulator for evaluating post-failure fallback behavior in integrated TN-NTN wireless networks under a partial-failure disaster model. The simulator follows 3GPP Rel-17/18 modeling principles, supports probabilistic terrestrial next-generation node B (gNB) failures, and service migration to NTN. The simulator supports comparative analysis of throughput, packet reception ratio (PRR), and latency under different user loads, disaster severities, and NTN provisioning levels. Results show the expected capacity-delay tradeoff of terrestrial operation, the reliability and stability of non-terrestrial service, and the balanced resilience behavior of hybrid TN-NTN operation. The proposed framework provides a tractable tool for studying wireless network resilience and traffic management in future integrated 6G mobile systems.

</details>


### [257] [FluxShard: Motion-Aware Feature Cache Reuse for Collaborative Video Analytics in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06027)
*Xiuxian Guan,Zongyuan Zhang,Zheng Lin,Zekai Sun,Tianyang Duan,Zihan Fang,Rui Wang,Heming Cui,Wei Ni,Jun Luo,Yuanwei Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: FluxShard利用编解码器块运动向量(MVs)，以运动区域为粒度管理特征缓存，解决了移动边缘计算中视频分析因非均匀运动导致的缓存失效问题，显著降低了延迟和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘云视频分析方法以整个场景为粒度管理缓存，在移动场景的非均匀运动模式下，即使大部分内容仅发生空间位移，也会导致大部分缓存失效，造成计算和带宽浪费。根本原因在于粒度不匹配：缓存以场景为单位管理，而运动却因区域而异。

Method: 提出FluxShard系统，利用编解码器级别的块运动向量(MVs)以单个运动区域粒度管理特征缓存重用和重计算。通过沿每块MV重新索引缓存特征，将空间位移与内容变化分离。提出感受野对齐原则(RFAP)从输入级MV场中识别因感受野内空间组合不一致而必须重计算的位置。采用MV引导的缓存重映射，每帧将整个特征缓存扭曲到当前坐标系以维持缓存一致性。基于性能分析的调度器将剩余稀疏工作负载在边缘和云端之间路由以降低延迟。

Result: 在多个视觉任务、动态视频基准和网络条件下评估，FluxShard在规定精度预算下相比所有基线降低延迟32.6-83.8%，降低能耗14.9-64.0%。

Conclusion: 通过将缓存管理粒度从场景级细化到运动区域级，并利用运动向量指导特征重用和重计算，FluxShard有效解决了粒度不匹配问题，显著提升了边缘云视频分析的性能和能效。

Abstract: Caching and reusing intermediate features across consecutive frames is a common technique to reduce redundant computation and transmission for edge-cloud video analytics in mobile edge computation. Existing methods manage the cache in a fixed or globally shifted coordinate system, treating it as an indivisible whole. Under the non-uniform motion patterns of mobile scenes, this whole-scene granularity invalidates large portions of the cache even when most content has merely shifted spatially, wasting computation and bandwidth. The root cause is a granularity mismatch: the cache is managed per scene, yet motion varies per region. In this paper, we present FluxShard, a motion-aware edge-cloud video analytics system that uses codec-level block motion vectors (MVs) to manage feature cache reuse and recomputation at the granularity of individual motion regions. By re-indexing cached features along per-block MVs, FluxShard separates spatial displacement from content changes, recovering reusable content that whole-scene methods would otherwise discard. To ensure correct reuse under heterogeneous motion, the Receptive Field Alignment Principle (RFAP) identifies, from the input-level MV field alone, the positions that must be recomputed due to inconsistent spatial composition within receptive fields. To maintain cache coherence across frames, MV-guided cache remapping warps the entire feature cache to the current coordinate system each frame, sustaining a high reuse ratio over time. A profiling-driven dispatcher routes the remaining sparse workload between edge and cloud for lower latency. Evaluation across multiple vision tasks, dynamic video benchmarks, and network conditions shows that FluxShard reduces latency by 32.6-83.8% and energy by 14.9-64.0% over all baselines under the prescribed accuracy budget.

</details>


### [258] [Temporal Spectral Noise-Floor Adaptation for Error-Intolerant Trigger Integrity in IoT Mesh Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06338)
*Sergii Makovetskyi,Lars Thomsen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于物联网传感器节点的轻量级嵌入式算法，通过维护时变频谱噪声底限，在动态环境中实现自主边缘事件触发，有效抑制误触发并保持高检测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物联网传感网络中，环境激励（如风雨、机械振动）具有非平稳特性，导致传统事件触发机制产生大量误报。现有的自适应阈值方法多关注通信负载管理或长期指标稳定，缺乏在动态环境下保持触发可靠性的解决方案。此外，集中式云端分析存在连接间歇性和安全风险。因此，需要一种无需校准、不依赖云端推理、能自主维持触发可靠性的本地决策算法。

Method: 该方法在嵌入式固件中实现确定性FFT频谱特征提取，并维护一个时变的频谱噪声底限基准。该噪声底限能够自适应吸收雨、风、机械振动等非平稳环境激励，从而在动态环境中保持事件触发的完整性。算法无需共享噪声模型或云端推理，所有决策均在节点本地完成，具备本地决策权限。

Result: 在动态室外环境中部署单节点网状传感器（以雷达级接近传感器为例）进行验证。结果表明：该方法显著抑制了干扰引起的误触发，减少了网状网络中的虚假事件流量放大，嵌入式执行开销有界，且对真实频谱特征保持可靠的检测灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该算法成功实现了在动态非平稳环境下的校准无关部署，具备本地决策能力，在连接间歇时保持节点响应性并规避集中式系统的安全风险，为自主物联网节点提供了可靠的边缘事件触发解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a lightweight, embedded algorithm for autonomous edge event triggering in IoT sensor nodes suitable for operating in mesh networks. The device acquires local sensor data, performs deterministic FFT spectral feature extraction in firmware, and maintains a temporal spectral noise-floor baseline that absorbs non-stationary environmental excitations such as rain, wind, and mechanical vibration. While adaptive thresholds in IoT sensor nodes are often applied to manage communication load or stabilize long-term metrics, this work focuses on maintaining a time-evolving spectral noise floor to preserve event trigger reliability in dynamic environments. Our method targets trigger integrity under environmental non-stationary conditions, enabling calibration-free deployment of autonomous nodes; without shared noise models or cloud-side inference. Local decision authority preserves node responsiveness when connectivity is intermittent and mitigates security risks inherent in centralized remote-analysis systems. We validate the algorithm in a single node mesh sensor deployed in a dynamic outdoor environment using a radar-class proximity sensor as one example sensor modality. Results demonstrate substantial suppression of nuisance-induced triggers, reduced false-event traffic amplification in the mesh, bounded embedded execution, and reliable detection sensitivity to true spectral signatures.

</details>


### [259] [Edge Triggering in IoT Mesh Networks: A Comparative Monte Carlo Study of Seven Detection Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06358)
*Sergii Makovetskyi,Lars Thomsen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证，TSNFA方法在IoT网状传感器网络中实现了100%检测率和零误报，关键在于结合频带选择、时间持续性过滤和自适应噪声底跟踪三种机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网网状传感器网络中的实时事件检测面临时变噪声、边缘节点计算资源受限以及缺乏中心化协调等挑战，亟需一种能够在复杂噪声环境下自主运行的检测方法。

Method: 在200个节点的网状网络上进行24小时蒙特卡洛仿真，比较TSNFA方法与六种替代检测算法。仿真采用真实噪声模型，包括60 Hz电磁干扰、正弦漂移噪声功率（±6 dB）和间歇性数字开关突发。TSNFA结合了频带选择、时间持续性过滤和自适应噪声底跟踪三种机制。

Result: TSNFA实现了100%检测率和零误报。所有竞争算法因至少缺失三种防御机制之一而表现不佳，误报率范围从0（Send-on-Delta，但也无法检测事件）到13,387,930（宽带能量比）。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，频带选择、时间持续性过滤和自适应噪声底跟踪三种防御机制的组合是实现资源受限IoT部署中自主边缘触发的必要且充分条件。

Abstract: Real-time event detection in Internet of Things (IoT) mesh sensor networks presents significant challenges due to time-varying noise conditions, limited computational resources at edge nodes, and the need for autonomous operation without centralised coordination. This paper presents a comprehensive Monte Carlo simulation study comparing the Temporal Spectral Noise-Floor Adaptation (TSNFA) method against six alternative detection algorithms, evaluated across a 200-node mesh network over 24 hours with realistic noise models including 60 Hz electromagnetic interference (EMI), sinusoidally drifting noise power (+/- 6 dB), and intermittent digital switching bursts. TSNFA achieves 100% detection rate with zero false positives, uniquely combining three interlocking defences: spectral band selection, temporal persistence filtering, and adaptive noise-floor tracking. Every competing algorithm omits at least one of these three defences and fails correspondingly, with false-positive rates ranging from 0 (Send-on-Delta, which also detects nothing) to 13,387,930 (broadband energy ratio). These results identify the three-defence combination as necessary and sufficient for autonomous edge triggering in resource-constrained IoT deployments.

</details>


### [260] [Delay-Robust Deep Reinforcement Learning for Ranging-Free Channel Access under Mobility in Underwater Acoustic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06536)
*Huaisheng Ye,Xiaowen Ye,Liqun Fu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MobiU-MAC，一种基于深度强化学习的MAC协议，用于解决水下声学网络中节点移动性带来的长传播延迟和动态拓扑挑战，实现了无需测距的高吞吐量接入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下声学网络中存在的长传播延迟会导致时空不确定性，限制了MAC协议的信道利用率。而在自主水下航行器场景中，节点移动性进一步加剧了这些挑战，引入了动态传播延迟和变化的空间拓扑结构。

Method: 提出了一种名为CHILL-STER的新型深度强化学习算法，该算法具有免测距和延迟鲁棒性。具体包括两个核心机制：一是信用视野受限λ回报（CHILL-Return）机制，用于在异步延迟奖励下实现稳定学习；二是时空经验回放（STER）机制，用于处理节点移动引起的拓扑变化。

Result: 理论证明了深度强化学习在长传播延迟下无需测距即可获得等同于标准马尔可夫决策过程的最优策略学习。性能评估显示，MobiU-MAC通过利用最大系统延迟边界且无测距开销，性能优于现有的基于DRL的水下MAC协议。

Conclusion: MobiU-MAC协议验证了所提出的理论和算法在复杂水下动态环境中的有效性，成功解决了节点移动场景下的时空不确定性问题，为水下声学网络的MAC协议设计提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Long propagation delays in underwater acoustic networks (UWANs) cause spatio-temporal uncertainty, constraining channel utilization in medium access control (MAC) protocols. Node mobility within autonomous underwater vehicle scenarios exacerbates these challenges by introducing dynamic propagation delays and varying spatial topologies. We present MobiU-MAC, a deep reinforcement learning (DRL)-based MAC protocol for mobile node access in UWANs that maximizes throughput via autonomous learning. MobiU-MAC incorporates CHILL-STER, a novel DRL algorithm optimized for UWANs that is both ranging-free and delay-robust. CHILL-STER employs a credit horizon-limited $λ$-return (CHILL-Return) mechanism to achieve stable learning under asynchronous delayed rewards, while the companion spatio-temporal experience replay (STER) mechanism addresses topological changes arising from node mobility. This work also demonstrates theoretically that DRL attains optimal policy learning equivalent to a standard Markov decision process under long propagation delays without requiring ranging. Performance evaluations indicate that MobiU-MAC outperforms existing DRL-based MAC protocols for UWANs by leveraging the maximum system delay boundary without ranging overhead, supporting the effectiveness of the proposed theory and algorithm in complex underwater dynamic environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Active Learning for Communication Structure Optimization in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05703)
*Huchen Yang,Xinghao Dong,Dan Negrut,Jin-Long Wu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型多智能体系统通信结构优化中训练任务难易不一的问题，提出了一种基于集成的信息论任务选择框架，通过主动识别最有价值的任务来提高优化稳定性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通信结构优化方法通常依赖随机采样的训练任务，但不同任务在难度和领域上存在显著差异，导致其对通信结构更新的信息量不等。在有限的训练预算下，这种随机采样往往导致优化过程不稳定且对特定训练集高度敏感。

Method: 提出了一种基于集成的信息论任务选择框架，通过估计候选任务对图参数分布变化的程度来衡量任务信息量，使用集成卡尔曼反演作为对应贝叶斯更新的高效无导数近似方法。为增强可扩展性，通过基于嵌入的代表性选择构建紧凑候选池，并将信息选择与代理建模和批量Thompson采样相结合。

Result: 在良性环境和存在智能体攻击的环境下均验证了该方法的有效性，证明了其在受限计算预算下进行通信结构优化的优势。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效解决有限训练预算下通信结构优化不稳定的问题，通过主动选择高信息量任务提高了优化效率和稳定性，为大语言模型多智能体系统的通信结构优化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Optimizing the communication structure of large language model based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) has been shown to improve downstream performance and reduce token usage. Existing methods typically rely on randomly sampled training tasks. However, tasks may differ substantially in difficulty and domain, and thus they are not equally informative for updating communication structure, making optimization under limited training budgets often unstable and highly sensitive to the particular training set. To actively identify the most valuable tasks for communication-structure optimization, we propose an ensemble-based information-theoretic task selection framework. The proposed method estimates task informativeness by how much a candidate task changes the distribution over graph parameters, using ensemble Kalman inversion as an efficient and derivative-free approximation of the corresponding Bayesian update. The resulting estimator is especially suitable for black-box and noisy multi-agent systems. To enhance scalability, we construct a compact candidate pool through embedding-based representative selection and combine the informative selection with surrogate modeling and batch Thompson sampling. We validate our method in both benign settings and settings with agent attacks, demonstrating its effectiveness for communication-structure optimization under constrained computational budgets.

</details>


### [262] [Auto Research with Specialist Agents Develops Effective and Non-Trivial Training Recipes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05724)
*Jingjie Ning,Xiaochuan Li,Ji Zeng,Hao Kang,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种闭环自动研究框架，通过血统反馈将外部评估结果转化为后续的程序级配方编辑，在无需人工干预的情况下自主完成了1197次主线实验，在三个任务上取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索一种完全自动化的研究流程，该流程由外部测量驱动，能够自主生成假设、执行代码编辑、提交实验、吸收反馈并持续改进，最终输出可审计的实验轨迹而非单一的模型检查点。

Method: 实例化一个由专业智能体组成的闭环系统，这些智能体分割配方表面并在试验之间共享测量的血统信息。系统通过血统反馈机制，将评估结果（包括崩溃、预算超支、尺寸失败和精度门限未达标）转化为后续的程序级配方编辑。整个过程在一次性设置和启动后，无需人工选择提案、编辑配方、覆盖分数或修复失败的试验。

Result: 在1197次主线试验和600次参数控制试验中，系统完全自主运行。在三个主要任务中取得了以下成果：参数验证bpb降低了0.81%，NanoChat-D12 CORE提升了38.7%，CIFAR-10 Airbench96运行时间减少了4.59%。所有任务均由各自的外部评估器和合法性检查进行测量。

Conclusion: 证明了该闭环系统能够在规定范围内自主编写代码、提交实验、吸收反馈、在各自环境中应用和组合已知技术，并改进公开的起始配方。系统产出的可审计轨迹包含了157个主线提交的严格架构域审计和程序重写，展示了自动研究作为闭环实证过程的可行性。

Abstract: We study auto research as a closed empirical loop driven by external measurement. Each submitted trial carries a hypothesis, an executable code edit, an evaluator-owned outcome, and feedback that shapes the next proposal. The output is not a generated paper or a single model checkpoint, but an auditable trajectory of proposals, code diffs, experiments, scores, and failure labels. We instantiate this loop with specialist agents that partition recipe surfaces and share measured lineage across trials. The central empirical finding is that lineage feedback lets agents turn evaluator outcomes, including crashes, budget overruns, size failures, and accuracy-gate misses, into later program-level recipe edits rather than one-shot suggestions. Across 1,197 headline-run trials plus 600 Parameter Golf control trials after one-time setup and launch, humans did not choose proposals, edit recipes, override scores, or repair failed trials during the search. In the three headline runs, the same submitted-trial loop reduces Parameter Golf validation bpb by $0.81\%$, raises NanoChat-D12 CORE by $38.7\%$, and reduces CIFAR-10 Airbench96 wallclock by $4.59\%$, with each task measured by its own external evaluator and legality checks. The trace includes a strict architecture-domain audit of 157 headline-run submissions and program rewrites such as a NanoChat attention-kernel path change. Within this scope the loop autonomously writes code, submits experiments, absorbs feedback, applies and combines known techniques inside each environment, and improves public starting recipes.

</details>


### [263] [Multiagent Stochastic Shortest Path Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06056)
*Martin Jonáš,Antonín Kučera,Vojtěch Kůr,Jan Mačák,Vojtěch Řehák*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体随机最短路径(MSSP)问题，提出了高效的策略合成算法，并通过实验验证了算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多个智能体协同到达目标状态的问题，旨在最小化任意智能体到达目标的期望时间，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Method: 分析了自主和协调两种场景下的计算复杂度和策略复杂度，设计了高效的策略合成算法，并在递增规模的实例上与自然基线进行实验对比评估。

Result: 成功开发了多智能体随机最短路径问题的策略合成算法，实验结果表明算法在性能上优于自然基线方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为多智能体随机最短路径问题提供了理论基础和实用算法，在计算复杂度分析的基础上实现了高效的策略合成，对多智能体系统的优化决策具有重要价值。

Abstract: We introduce and study the multi-agent stochastic shortest path (MSSP) problem, in which $k$ agents strive to reach a target state, aiming to minimize the expected time to reach the target by any agent. We analyze the computational and strategy-complexity of the problem in both autonomous and coordinated settings, and we design efficient strategy-synthesis algorithms. The algorithms are experimentally evaluated on instances of increasing size against natural baselines.

</details>


### [264] [Power-Efficiency and Scalability Analysis of Magnetically-Actuated Satellite Swarms via Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06286)
*Yuta Takahashi,Seang Shim,Hiraku Sakamoto,Shin-ichiro Sakai*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于凸优化的评估框架，用于分析磁相互作用维持的卫星群阵列的功率消耗，发现增加卫星数量可以提高编队维持的功率效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于卫星群的分布式孔径系统因其可扩展性能够突破发射尺寸限制，实现高增益、窄波束和大孔径能力。然而，长期维持这类虚拟结构需要在不稳定轨道动力学下进行持续的编队控制，而电磁力和力矩模型的非线性导致非凸的功率消耗约束，使得系统级配置分析困难。

Method: 开发了一个基于凸优化的框架，用于分析大规模磁驱动卫星群的功率消耗，将非凸的功率消耗约束问题转化为可求解的凸优化问题。

Result: 分析结果表明，增加卫星数量可以提高编队维持的功率效率，即卫星数量越多，维持编队的功率效率越高。

Conclusion: 磁驱动卫星群架构为构建大规模空间系统提供了一种功率高效的替代方案，优于传统的少卫星电磁编队飞行概念。

Abstract: This correspondence presents a convex-optimization-based evaluation framework of satellite-swarm-based apertures maintained by magnetic-field interactions. Spaceborne distributed apertures are composed of multiple satellites and are attractive for scientific and commercial missions because their scalability enables high-gain, narrow-beam, and large-aperture capabilities beyond the launch-size limitations. A key challenge is that the long-term maintenance of such virtual structures requires consistent formation control amid unstable orbital dynamics, and magnetic interactions generated by satellite-mounted magnetorquers offer a desirable propellant-free position-control strategy. However, the nonlinearities of the electromagnetic force and torque model lead to a nonconvex power-consumption constraint, making system-level configuration analysis difficult. To address this issue, we develop a convex optimization-based framework to analyze the power consumption of large magnetically actuated satellite swarms. The resulting analysis shows that increasing the number of satellites can improve formation-keeping power efficiency. This indicates that magnetically actuated swarm architectures provide a power-efficient alternative to the conventional few-satellite electromagnetic formation-flight concept for constructing large-scale space systems.

</details>


### [265] [Improving the Efficiency of Language Agent Teams with Adaptive Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06320)
*Elizabeth Mieczkowski,Alexander Ku,Tiwalayo Eisape,Dilip Arumugam,John Matters,Katherine M. Collins,Ilia Sucholutsky,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出LATTE框架，通过构建动态协调图实现LLM团队的高效协作，在降低资源消耗的同时保持或提升任务准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM团队协作方法存在两极分化问题：高度结构化方法依赖预定义角色和流程，缺乏灵活性；完全非结构化团队虽具适应性，但面临错误传播、智能体冲突和资源浪费等效率问题。需要一种既能保持一致性又能支持动态适应的协调机制。

Method: 受分布式系统启发，提出LATTE框架。团队智能体协作构建和维护一个共享的、动态演进的协调图，该图编码子任务依赖关系、智能体分配和子任务进度状态。该协议在维护一致性的同时，赋能智能体动态分配工作、调整协调策略并发现新任务。

Result: 在多个协作任务和多种基础模型上的实验表明，LATTE显著减少了令牌使用量、实际运行时间、通信开销和协调失败（如文件冲突和冗余输出），同时在准确性上匹配或超越MetaGPT、去中心化团队、Leader-Worker层级结构和静态分解等标准设计。

Conclusion: LATTE成功平衡了结构化与非结构化方法的优点，通过动态协调图机制实现了高效、灵活且可靠的LLM团队协作，为多智能体系统的协调设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in teams, yet existing coordination approaches often occupy two extremes. Highly structured methods rely on fixed roles, pipelines, or task decompositions assigned a priori. In contrast, fully unstructured teams enable adaptability and exploration but suffer from inefficiencies such as error propagation, inter-agent conflicts, and wasted resources (measured in time, tokens, or file operations). We introduce Language Agent Teams for Task Evolution (LATTE), a framework for coordinating LLM teams inspired by distributed systems, where processors must operate under partial observability and communication constraints. In LATTE, a team of agents collaboratively construct and maintain a shared, evolving coordination graph which encodes sub-task dependencies, individual agent assignment, and the current state of sub-task progress. This protocol maintains consistency while empowering agents to dynamically allocate work, adapt coordination, and discover new tasks. Across multiple collaborative tasks and a variety of base models, we demonstrate how LATTE reduces token usage, wall-clock time, communication, and coordination failures (e.g. file conflicts and redundant outputs) while matching or exceeding the accuracy of standard designs including MetaGPT, decentralized teams, top-down Leader-Worker hierarchies, and static decompositions.

</details>


### [266] [AgenticPrecoding: LLM-Empowered Multi-Agent System for Precoding Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06443)
*Zijiu Yang,Zixiang Zhang,Shunpu Tang,Qianqian Yang,Zhiguo Shi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgenticPrecoding框架，一个通用的多智能体系统，能够根据用户通信需求自动推导预编码方案，在跨场景适应性上优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预编码方法通常针对特定的系统模型、目标和约束集开发，这限制了它们在未来6G网络中异构和不断演进场景下的适应能力。

Method: 提出了AgenticPrecoding，一个通用的多智能体框架，将预编码推导过程分解为四个协调阶段：问题建模、求解器选择、提示上采样和代码生成。每个阶段分配给专门的智能体：两个注入预编码领域知识的LoRA适配推理智能体负责问题建模和求解器选择，两个通用大语言模型负责提示优化和可执行代码生成。此外，引入了反馈驱动的优化机制以增强代码可执行性、约束可行性和解质量。

Result: 在10个代表性预编码场景上的广泛实验表明，与传统的基于优化的方法和基于LLM的基线相比，AgenticPrecoding实现了更优的跨场景适应性。

Conclusion: AgenticPrecoding框架成功实现了从用户级通信需求到预编码方案的端到端自动推导，为未来6G网络中异构和演进场景下的预编码问题提供了有效的解决方案，展现了多智能体协作在通信系统设计中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Precoding is a key technique for interference management and performance improvement in multi-antenna wireless systems. However, existing precoding methods are typically developed for specific system models, objectives, and constraint sets, which limits their adaptability to the heterogeneous and evolving scenarios expected in future 6G networks. To address this limitation, we propose AgenticPrecoding, a universal multi-agent framework that automates end-to-end precoding derivation directly from user-level communication requirements. Specifically, AgenticPrecoding decomposes the derivation process into four coordinated stages: problem formulation, solver selection, prompt upsampling, and code generation, assigning each stage to a specialized agent tailored to its specific reasoning demands. We employ two LoRA-adapted reasoning agents to inject precoding-specific domain knowledge for problem formulation and solver selection, while two general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) handle prompt refinement and executable code generation. Furthermore, a feedback-driven refinement mechanism is incorporated to enhance code executability, constraint feasibility, and solution quality. Extensive experiments across 10 representative precoding scenarios demonstrate that AgenticPrecoding achieves superior cross-scenario adaptability compared to conventional optimization-based and LLM-based baselines.

</details>


### [267] [Coordination Matters: Evaluation of Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06557)
*Maria Ana Cardei,Matthew Landers,Afsaneh Doryab*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协调感知的评估视角，通过STAT测试平台发现，在合作式多智能体强化学习中，相似的回报趋势可能掩盖截然不同的协调机制，强调了过程级诊断的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合作式多智能体强化学习基准测试过于关注回报、成功率等聚合结果指标，而忽视了智能体如何协调的过程，这在智能体、任务和联合分配选择呈组合增长的环境下尤为不足，无法揭示协调失败的根源。

Method: 提出协调感知评估视角，引入STAT（受承诺约束的空间任务分配测试平台），该平台系统地改变智能体、任务和环境规模，同时固定观察权限和任务规则，并在不同的中心化程度下评估六种代表性的基于价值的多智能体强化学习方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，相似的回报趋势可能反映截然不同的协调机制，包括冗余分配、分配多样性和任务完成效率的差异。在承诺约束的任务分配中，规模下的性能不仅受名义动作空间大小影响，还受分配压力、稀疏决策机会以及相互依赖智能体间冗余选择的影响。

Conclusion: 协调感知评估是基于回报基准测试的必要补充，对于全面理解合作式多智能体强化学习系统的行为至关重要，尤其是在具有组合性质的任务分配场景中。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) benchmarks commonly emphasize aggregate outcomes such as return, success rate, or completion time. While essential, these metrics often fail to reveal how agents coordinate, particularly in settings where agents, tasks, and joint assignment choices scale combinatorially. We propose a coordination-aware evaluation perspective that supplements return with process-level diagnostics. We instantiate this perspective using STAT, a controlled commitment-constrained spatial task-allocation testbed that systematically varies agents, tasks, and environment size while holding observation access and task rules fixed. We evaluate six representative value-based MARL methods across varying levels of centralization. Our results show that similar return trends can reflect distinct coordination mechanisms, including differences in redundant assignment, assignment diversity, and task-completion efficiency. We find that in commitment-constrained task allocation, performance under scale is shaped not only by nominal action-space size, but also by assignment pressure, sparse decision opportunities, and redundant choices among interdependent agents. Our findings motivate coordination-aware evaluation as a necessary complement to return-based benchmarking for cooperative MARL.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [Understanding Annotator Safety Policy with Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05329)
*Alex Oesterling,Donghao Ren,Yannick Assogba,Dominik Moritz,Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Leon Gatys,Fred Hohman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出标注者策略模型（APMs），仅从标注行为学习标注者内部的安全策略，无需额外标注成本即可揭示标注分歧的来源（操作失误、策略歧义、价值多元主义），辅助AI安全策略的优化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全策略指导AI数据标注与模型开发，但标注分歧普遍存在且来源不同（操作失误、策略歧义、价值多元主义）。区分这些来源对采取针对性措施至关重要，但直接询问标注者原因成本高且不可靠，因此需要一种无需额外负担即可推断分歧原因的方法。

Method: 引入标注者策略模型（APMs），这是一种可解释模型，仅通过观察标注行为来学习标注者内部的安全策略，从而在不增加额外标注负担的情况下，使标注者的推理过程可见且可比。

Result: 验证表明APMs能准确建模标注者安全策略（准确率>80%），忠实地预测对反事实编辑的响应，并在受控环境中恢复已知策略差异。应用于LLM和人类标注，成功揭示了策略歧义（不同解读方式）和价值多元主义（不同群体间系统性的安全优先级差异）。

Conclusion: APMs为更具针对性、透明度和包容性的安全策略设计提供了支持，能够有效揭示标注分歧的具体来源，帮助制定相应的改进措施（如质量控制、策略澄清或多方协商）。

Abstract: Safety policies define what constitutes safe and unsafe AI outputs, guiding data annotation and model development. However, annotation disagreement is pervasive and can stem from multiple sources such as operational failures (annotators misunderstand or misexecute the task), policy ambiguity (policy wording leaves room for interpretation), or value pluralism (different annotators hold different perspectives on safety). Distinguishing these sources matters. For example, operational failures call for quality control, ambiguity calls for policy clarification, and pluralism calls for deliberation about incorporating diverse perspectives. Yet understanding why annotators disagree is difficult. Directly asking annotators for their reasoning is costly, substantially increasing annotation burden, and can be unreliable for both human and LLM annotators as self-reported reasoning often fails to reflect actual decision processes.
  We introduce Annotator Policy Models (APMs), interpretable models that learn annotators' internal safety policies from labeling behavior alone, making annotator reasoning visible and comparable without additional annotation effort. We validate that APMs accurately model annotator safety policy (>80% accuracy), faithfully predict responses to counterfactual edits, and recover known policy differences in controlled settings. Applying APMs to LLM and human annotations, we demonstrate two core applications: (1) surfacing policy ambiguity by revealing how annotators interpret safety instructions differently, and (2) surfacing value pluralism by uncovering systematic differences in safety priorities across demographic groups. Together, these capabilities support more targeted, transparent, and inclusive safety policy design.

</details>


### [269] [ZAYA1-8B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05365)
*Robert Washbourne,Rishi Iyer,Tomas Figliolia,Henry Zheng,Ryan Lorig-Roach,Sungyeon Yang,Pritish Yuvraj,Quentin Anthony,Yury Tokpanov,Xiao Yang,Ganesh Nanduru,Stephen Ebert,Praneeth Medepalli,Skyler Szot,Srivatsan Rajagopal,Alex Ong,Bhavana Mehta,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ZAYA1-8B，一个基于AMD全栈平台训练的700M激活参数/8B总参数的推理导向MoE模型。通过四阶段RL级联训练和Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，在AIME'25和HMMT'25上分别达到91.9%和89.6%，媲美Gemini-2.5 Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2等大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个高效的小规模推理模型，在保持较少激活参数（<1B）的情况下，达到或超越更大规模推理模型的性能，同时验证AMD全栈计算平台在训练大型推理模型方面的能力。

Method: 1) 基于Zyphra的MoE++架构从头训练，从预训练开始就使用答案保留裁剪方案纳入推理数据；2) 四阶段RL级联后训练：数学与谜题推理预热、400任务RLVE-Gym课程、带测试时计算轨迹的数学与代码RL、以及对话和指令遵循的行为RL；3) 引入Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，递归聚合并行推理轨迹，在轮次间仅传递有界长度的推理尾部（4K token）。

Result: ZAYA1-8B在多个挑战性数学和编码基准测试上匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528，与更大规模开放权重推理模型保持竞争力。在测试时计算（TTC）评估中，Markovian RSA使模型在AIME'25达到91.9%，在HMMT'25达到89.6%，显著缩小了与Gemini-2.5 Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2和GPT-5-High等大型推理模型的差距。

Conclusion: 证明了小规模推理导向MoE模型结合精心设计的训练策略（从预训练开始的推理数据、四阶段RL级联）和创新性测试时计算方法（Markovian RSA），可以在极低激活参数下实现与大型推理模型相当的性能，为高效推理模型设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: We present ZAYA1-8B, a reasoning-focused mixture-of-experts (MoE) model with 700M active and 8B total parameters, built on Zyphra's MoE++ architecture. ZAYA1-8B's core pretraining, midtraining, and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) were performed on a full-stack AMD compute, networking, and software platform. With under 1B active parameters, ZAYA1-8B matches or exceeds DeepSeek-R1-0528 on several challenging mathematics and coding benchmarks, and remains competitive with substantially larger open-weight reasoning models. ZAYA1-8B was trained from scratch for reasoning, with reasoning data included from pretraining onward using an answer-preserving trimming scheme. Post-training uses a four-stage RL cascade: reasoning warmup on math and puzzles; a 400-task RLVE-Gym curriculum; math and code RL with test-time compute traces and synthetic code environments built from competitive-programming references; and behavioral RL for chat and instruction following. We also introduce Markovian RSA, a test-time compute method that recursively aggregates parallel reasoning traces while carrying forward only bounded-length reasoning tails between rounds. In TTC evaluation, Markovian RSA raises ZAYA1-8B to 91.9\% on AIME'25 and 89.6\% on HMMT'25 while carrying forward only a 4K-token tail, narrowing the gap to much larger reasoning models including Gemini-2.5 Pro, DeepSeek-V3.2, and GPT-5-High.

</details>


### [270] [Partial Evidence Bench: Benchmarking Authorization-Limited Evidence in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05379)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Partial Evidence Bench基准，用于测量企业智能体在权限受限环境下产生的“隐性证据缺失”问题，即系统返回看似完整的答案但实际上遗漏了授权边界外的关键证据。基准包含72个任务，评估发现静默过滤机制存在严重安全隐患，而显式的失败-报告机制能有效消除不安全的完整性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体越来越多地在受限的检索系统、委托工作流和策略约束环境中运行。在这些场景下，即使访问控制得到正确执行，系统仍可能产生看似完整的答案，而关键证据实际上位于调用者的授权边界之外。这种隐性证据缺失是一个治理关键型的智能体失败模式，但目前缺乏有效的测量方法。

Method: 引入Partial Evidence Bench确定性基准，包含三个场景族（尽职调查、合规审计、安全事件响应），共72个任务。基准配备了ACL分区语料库、oracle完整答案、oracle授权视图答案、oracle完整性判断以及结构化缺口报告oracle。系统从四个维度进行评估：答案正确性、完整性意识、缺口报告质量以及不安全完整性行为。

Result: 基准测试显示：静默过滤机制在所有场景族中都具有灾难性的安全隐患；显式的失败-报告机制能够消除不安全的完整性问题，同时不会将任务退化为简单的弃权；初步真实模型运行表明，系统是否过度声明完整性、保守地低声明或以企业可用的形式报告不完整性，存在模型依赖和场景敏感的差异。

Conclusion: 该基准的更广泛贡献在于使治理关键型的智能体失败模式变得可测量，无需人工评判或易受污染的静态语料库，为企业智能体系统的安全性和完整性评估提供了标准化的测试框架。

Abstract: Enterprise agents increasingly operate inside scoped retrieval systems, delegated workflows, and policy-constrained evidence environments. In these settings, access control can be enforced correctly while the system still produces an answer that appears complete even though material evidence lies outside the caller's authorization boundary. This paper introduces Partial Evidence Bench, a deterministic benchmark for measuring that failure mode. The benchmark ships three scenario families -- due diligence, compliance audit, and security incident response -- with 72 tasks total, ACL-partitioned corpora, oracle complete answers, oracle authorized-view answers, oracle completeness judgments, and structured gap-report oracles. It evaluates systems along four surfaces: answer correctness, completeness awareness, gap-report quality, and unsafe completeness behavior. Checked-in baselines show that silent filtering is catastrophically unsafe across all shipped families, while explicit fail-and-report behavior eliminates unsafe completeness without collapsing the task into trivial abstention. Preliminary real-model runs show model-dependent and scenario-sensitive differences in whether systems overclaim completeness, conservatively underclaim, or report incompleteness in an enterprise-usable form. The benchmark's broader contribution is to make a governance-critical agent failure measurable without human judges or contamination-prone static corpora.

</details>


### [271] [BALAR : A Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05386)
*Aymen Echarghaoui,Dongxia Wu,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BALAR，一个任务无关的贝叶斯智能体循环算法，用于主动推理的多轮对话交互，在三个基准测试上显著优于所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型在交互式环境中多为被动响应，缺乏主动推理缺失信息并选择下一问题的机制。本文旨在解决多轮对话中信息不完整时的主动提问和推理问题。

Method: 提出了BALAR（贝叶斯智能体主动推理循环），一种任务无关的外循环算法，无需微调。该方法维护潜在状态的结构化信念表示，通过最大化期望互信息选择澄清性问题，并在当前状态表示不足时动态扩展状态空间。

Result: 在三个多样化基准测试上评估：AR-Bench-DC（侦探案例）、AR-Bench-SP（思维谜题）和iCraft-MD（临床诊断）。BALAR在所有三个基准上显著超越所有基线，准确率分别提升14.6%、38.5%和30.5%。

Conclusion: BALAR成功实现了结构化的多轮交互，通过贝叶斯框架进行主动推理，在多样化任务中展现出强大的泛化能力和显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate in interactive settings where solving a task requires multiple rounds of information exchange with a user. However, most current systems treat dialogue reactively and lack a principled mechanism to reason about what information is missing and which question should be asked next. We propose BALAR (Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning), a task-agnostic outer-loop algorithm that requires no fine-tuning and enables structured multi-turn interaction between an LLM agent and a user. BALAR maintains a structured belief over latent states, selects clarifying questions by maximizing expected mutual information, and dynamically expands its state representation when the current one proves insufficient. We evaluate BALAR on three diverse benchmarks: AR-Bench-DC (detective cases), AR-Bench-SP (thinking puzzles), and iCraft-MD (clinical diagnosis). BALAR significantly outperforms all baselines across all three benchmarks, with $14.6\%$ higher accuracy on AR-Bench-DC, $38.5\%$ on AR-Bench-SP, and $30.5\%$ on iCraft-MD.

</details>


### [272] [Intelligent CCTV for Urban Design: AI-Based Analysis of Soft Infrastructure at Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05402)
*Vinit Katariya,Seungjin Kim,Curtis Craig,Nichole Morris,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个利用现有CCTV和AI深度学习技术的框架，用于评估临时性道路安全干预措施的效果，结果显示这些“软干预”能显著降低车速和过境交通量，证明了AI在交通政策评估中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能与计算机视觉技术的发展，传统的交通数据收集方式正在发生变革。研究旨在利用现有的监控基础设施（CCTV），开发一种低成本、快速的方法来评估“软干预措施”（如临时人行安全岛和路缘扩展）对车速及安全的影响，从而为交通政策制定提供数据支持。

Method: 研究构建了一个基于AI的分析框架，利用深度学习和基于视角的车速估算技术，对明尼阿波利斯市安装干预措施前后的驾驶员行为进行了评估。监测过程包括干预前的基线数据采集，以及干预后第一周和第二周的重复监测，涵盖了无信号控制交叉口和有信号控制交叉口。

Result: 在无信号控制交叉口，平均车速和第85百分位车速最高分别下降了18.75%和16.56%，过境交通流量最高减少了12.2%。在有信号控制交叉口，除一个地点外，其余均表现出类似降幅，平均车速和第85百分位车速最高分别下降了20.0%和17.19%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，临时性的软基础设施干预措施具有良好的交通稳静化效果，能有效降低车速。同时，该研究证实了利用AI赋能的方法能够实现快速、低成本的交通政策评估，为基于证据的交通决策提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) and computer vision are transforming transportation data collection. This study introduces an AI-enabled analytics framework leveraging existing CCTV infrastructure to evaluate the impact of soft interventions, such as temporary pedestrian refuges and curb extensions, on vehicle speed and safety. Using deep learning and perspective-based speed estimation, we evaluated driver behavior before and after interventions, with repeated post-installation monitoring in Week 1 and Week 2, in Minneapolis. Findings reveal that at unsignalized intersections, mean and 85th-percentile speeds fell by up to 18.75% and 16.56%, respectively, while pass-through traffic decreased by as much as 12.2%. Signalized intersections showed comparable reductions except one location, with mean and 85th-percentile speeds dropping by up to 20.0% and 17.19%. These results demonstrate the traffic-calming effectiveness of soft infrastructure and underscore the utility of AI-powered methods for rapid, low-cost, and evidence-based transport policy evaluation.

</details>


### [273] [When Helpfulness Becomes Sycophancy: Sycophancy is a Boundary Failure Between Social Alignment and Epistemic Integrity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05403)
*Jiechen Li,Catherine A. Barry,Rishika Randev,Janet Chen,Ella Jorgensen,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出大语言模型的“谄媚”现象是社会对齐与认知完整性之间的边界失效，并构建了包含三条件框架和分类体系的理论模型，用于澄清概念并指导评估与缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常仅通过外部行为（如附和错误观点、立场反转）来定义和操作化“谄媚”，这种做法捕捉了显性表现，却忽略了涉及认知完整性与社会对齐之间更微妙的边界失效，导致该现象定义不清。

Method: 提出一个三条件框架来界定谄媚：用户表达线索（信念、偏好或自我概念）；模型通过对齐行为向该线索偏移；该偏移损害了认知准确性、独立推理或适当纠正。此外，还引入了一个包含对齐目标、机制和严重程度的分类体系。

Result: 成功构建了一个更精确的谄媚理论框架，将其定义为“取代独立认知判断的对齐行为”而非单纯的“同意”，并提供了系统化的分类法。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了该框架对对齐评估的启示，主张进行边界感知评估、采用结构化评分标准并制定缓解策略，同时将这些提议与替代观点进行了对比定位。

Abstract: This position paper argues that sycophancy in LLMs is a boundary failure between social alignment and epistemic integrity. Existing work often operationalizes sycophancy through external behavior such as agreement with incorrect user beliefs, position reversals, or deviation from an objective standard of correctness. These formulations capture only overt forms of the phenomenon and leave subtler boundary failures involving epistemic integrity and social alignment underspecified. We argue that sycophancy should not be understood as agreement alone, but as alignment behavior that displaces independent epistemic judgment. To clarify this boundary, we propose a three-condition framework for sycophancy. First, the user expresses a cue in the form of a belief, preference, or self-concept. Second, the model shifts toward that cue through alignment behavior. Third, this shift compromises epistemic accuracy, independent reasoning, or appropriate correction. We also introduce a taxonomy for classifying sycophancy, consisting of alignment targets, mechanisms, and severity. The paper concludes by discussing implications for alignment evaluation and argues for boundary-aware assessment, structured rubrics, and mitigation strategies, while situating these proposals alongside alternative views of sycophancy.

</details>


### [274] [PRISM: Perception Reasoning Interleaved for Sequential Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05407)
*Mohamed Salim Aissi,Clemence Grislain,Clement Romac,Laure Soulier,Mohamed Chetouani,Olivier Sigaud,Nicolas Thome*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架，通过动态问答（DQA）机制紧密耦合感知（VLM）与决策（LLM），实现了全自动、任务驱动的场景理解，在ALFWorld和R2R基准上显著优于现有图像模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有独立视觉语言模型（VLM）在复杂多模态具身智能场景中存在感知-推理-决策差距，常忽略任务关键信息，难以从纯文本环境扩展到复杂多模态设置。

Method: 提出PRISM框架，通过动态问答（DQA）管道实现闭环交互：LLM主动批判VLM的初始描述，生成目标导向的探测问题，VLM回答后由LLM合成紧凑的图像描述，从而获得任务驱动的精确场景理解。

Result: 在ALFWorld和Room-to-Room（R2R）基准测试中：1）PRISM显著优于现有最先进图像模型；2）交互式目标导向感知管道带来系统性、实质性提升；3）PRISM完全自动化，无需人工设计问答。

Conclusion: PRISM通过紧密耦合感知与决策模块，有效弥合了VLM在具身智能任务中的感知-推理-决策差距，实现了高性能、全自动的多模态场景理解。

Abstract: Scaling LLM-based embodied agents from text-only environments to complex multimodal settings remains a major challenge. Recent work identifies a perception-reasoning-decision gap in standalone Vision-Language Models (VLMs), which often overlook task-critical information. In this paper, we introduce PRISM, a framework that tightly couples perception (VLM) and decision (LLM) through a dynamic question-answer (DQA) pipeline. Instead of passively accepting the VLM's description, the LLM critiques it, probes the VLM with goal-oriented questions, and synthesizes a compact image description. This closed-loop interaction yields a sharp, task-driven understanding of the scene. We evaluate PRISM on the ALFWorld and Room-to-Room (R2R) benchmarks. We show that: (1) PRISM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art image-based models, (2) our Interactive goal-oriented perception pipeline yields systematic and substantial gains, and (3) PRISM is fully automatic, eliminating the need for handcrafted questions or answers.

</details>


### [275] [SANEmerg: An Emergent Communication Framework for Semantic-aware Agentic AI Networking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05861)
*Yong Xiao,Haoran Zhou,Yujie Zhou,Marwan Krunz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SANEmerg框架，一种面向语义感知AgentNet系统的多智能体涌现通信方案，通过带宽自适应重要性过滤器和基于MDL原则的复杂度正则化器，在严格的带宽约束下实现高效的协作通信，显著提升了任务准确率并降低了带宽和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来网络系统预期将成为AI原生生态系统的一部分，大量异构专用AI智能体需要实时协作以满足复杂的用户需求。然而，传统网络范式将通信与计算刚性分离，导致大规模智能体网络（AgentNet）系统效率低下。涌现通信作为一种新颖的解决方案，能够使自主智能体支持任务特定的信令协议进行信息交换与协作协调。

Method: 提出SANEmerg框架，包含两个核心机制：1）带宽自适应重要性过滤器，动态优先传输贡献度更高的消息维度，确保在带宽受限环境下的稳健性能；2）基于最小描述长度（MDL）原则的复杂度正则化器，促进计算有界信令协议的涌现。该框架能够自动检测和推断用户语义意图，并将其关联到分配给智能体集合的子任务。

Result: 通过AgentNet原型和大量实验评估，SANEmerg相比最先进的解决方案取得显著性能提升，在显著降低带宽和计算开销的同时，实现了更优的任务准确率。

Conclusion: SANEmerg框架成功实现了在AgentNet物理限制下的协作通信涌现，为解决大规模智能体网络中通信与计算耦合效率问题提供了有效方案，推动了语义感知智能体网络系统的发展。

Abstract: Future networking systems are envisioned to become part of an agentic AI-native ecosystem in which a vast number of heterogeneous and specialized AI agents cooperate seamlessly to fulfill complex user requirements in real time. However, traditional networking paradigms are characterized by a rigid decoupling of communication and computation, which often leads to significant inefficiencies in large-scale agentic AI networking (AgentNet) systems. Emergent communication offers a novel solution by enabling autonomous agents that support task-specific signaling protocols for information exchange and collaborative coordination. In this paper, we consider a multi-agent emergent communication framework, tailored for semantic-aware AgentNet systems in which the user's semantic intent can be automatically detected, inferred, and linked to a set of sub-tasks to be assigned to a set of agents. We investigate how communication and signaling protocols can emerge among collaborative agents with computationally bounded intelligence under stringent bandwidth constraints. Our proposed framework, called SANEmerg, is designed to facilitate the emergence of communication for collaborative task fulfillment while adhering to the physical limits of AgentNet. SANEmerg incorporates a bandwidth-adaptable importance-filter that dynamically prioritizes the transmission of higher-contribution message dimensions, ensuring robust performance in bandwidth-limited environments. Furthermore, SANEmerg integrates a complexity-regularizer grounded in the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle to facilitate the emergence of computationally bounded signaling. Evaluated via an AgentNet prototype and extensive experimentation, SANEmerg demonstrates significant performance improvements over state-of-the-art solutions, achieving superior task accuracy while significantly reducing bandwidth and computational overhead.

</details>


### [276] [FinRAG-12B: A Production-Validated Recipe for Grounded Question Answering in Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05482)
*Denys Katerenchuk,Pablo Duboue,Keelan Evanini,David Gondek,Nithin Govindugari,Olivier Allauzen,Joshua Baptiste,David J More,Joshua Schechter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的数据高效框架，用于训练银行业务领域的落地LLM。该方法仅用143M tokens训练出的12B模型，在引用依据上超越GPT-4.1，在拒绝回答校准上优于基线与GPT-4.1，已在40多家金融机构部署，实现查询解决率提升7.1个百分点，推理成本降低20-50倍，响应速度提升3-5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在各领域快速普及，但在银行业落地时面临显著阻力：行业对高准确性、监管合规、回答可验证与可溯源有严格要求，现有通用模型难以直接满足这些部署约束。

Method: 提出端到端落地方法论：1）数据生成管道，结合LLM-as-a-Judge过滤、引用标注与课程学习，仅用143M tokens；2）校准式拒绝机制，在训练中混入22%不可回答样本；3）覆盖从数据策划到量化部署的全流程。

Result: 训练得到的12B模型在引用依据上超越GPT-4.1；拒绝率从基线不安全的4.3%提升至合理的12%，避免GPT-4.1的过度拒绝(20.2%)；在40+金融机构部署后查询解决率提升7.1个百分点(p<0.001)；推理速度提升3-5倍，成本降低20-50倍。

Conclusion: 该框架在真实部署约束下实现了答案质量、引用溯源与校准拒绝的联合优化，证明了在严监管行业以数据高效方式构建领域专用落地LLM的可行性与商业价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly being adopted across various domains. However, their adoption in banking industry faces resistance due to demands for high accuracy, regulatory compliance, and the need for verifiable and grounded responses. We present a unified, data-efficient framework for training grounded domain-specific LLMs that optimizes answer quality, citation grounding, and calibrated refusal under real-world deployment constraints. First, we describe a data generation pipeline that combines LLM-as-a-Judge filtering, citation annotation, and curriculum learning with only 143M tokens. The resulting 12B model achieves high answer quality outperforming GPT-4.1 on citation grounding, with a modest citation tradeoff versus the untuned base. Second, we propose a calibrated refusal mechanism: training on 22% unanswerable examples yield a 12% "I don't know" rate, substantially improving over the base model's unsafe 4.3% rate while avoiding GPT-4.1's over-refusal (20.2%). Third, we present an end-to-end methodology spanning from data curation to quantized serving. The system is deployed at 40+ financial institutions, achieving a 7.1 percentage point improvement in query resolution (p < 0.001). Additionally, the model delivers 3-5x faster responses at 20-50x lower cost compared to GPT-4.1.

</details>


### [277] [Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Financial Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05409)
*Yang Shu,Yingmin Liu,Zequn Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FinAgent-RAG，一个面向金融文档问答的智能体RAG框架。通过对比金融检索器、程序思维推理模块和自适应策略路由器三个创新，在FinQA、ConvFinQA和TAT-QA三个基准上分别达到76.81%、78.46%和74.96%的执行准确率，显著超越最强基线5.62-9.32个百分点，同时降低41.3%的API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融文档问答需要在企业报告中的异构证据（结构化表格、文本叙述和脚注）上进行复杂的多步数值推理。现有的检索增强生成（RAG）方法采用单次检索-生成范式，难以处理金融分析中普遍存在的组合推理链，且LLM直接进行数值计算容易出错。

Method: 提出FinAgent-RAG框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)对比金融检索器，通过困难负样本挖掘训练，区分语义相似但数值不同的金融段落；(2)程序思维推理模块，生成可执行的Python代码进行精确算术运算，而非依赖易错的LLM心算；(3)自适应策略路由器，根据问题复杂度动态分配计算资源，降低API成本。

Result: 在FinQA、ConvFinQA和TAT-QA三个基准数据集上，执行准确率分别达到76.81%、78.46%和74.96%，超越最强基线5.62-9.32个百分点。自适应策略路由器在FinQA上降低41.3%的API成本同时保持准确率。消融实验、跨骨干LLM评估和部署成本分析验证了框架的鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: FinAgent-RAG通过迭代检索-推理循环和自验证机制，有效解决了金融数值推理的精度要求。框架具有良好的鲁棒性和实际部署可行性，为金融机构提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Financial document question answering (QA) demands complex multi-step numerical reasoning over heterogeneous evidence--structured tables, textual narratives, and footnotes--scattered across corporate filings. Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) approaches adopt a single-pass retrieve-then-generate paradigm that struggles with the compositional reasoning chains prevalent in financial analysis. We propose FinAgent-RAG, an agentic RAG framework that orchestrates iterative retrieval-reasoning loops with self-verification, specifically engineered for the precision requirements of financial numerical reasoning. The framework integrates three domain-specific innovations: (1) a Contrastive Financial Retriever trained with hard negative mining to distinguish semantically similar but numerically distinct financial passages, (2) a Program-of-Thought reasoning module that generates executable Python code for precise arithmetic rather than relying on error-prone LLM-based mental computation, and (3) an Adaptive Strategy Router that dynamically allocates computational resources based on question complexity, reducing API costs by 41.3% on FinQA while preserving accuracy. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets--FinQA, ConvFinQA, and TAT-QA--demonstrate that FinAgent-RAG achieves 76.81%, 78.46%, and 74.96% execution accuracy respectively, outperforming the strongest baseline by 5.62--9.32 percentage points. Ablation studies, cross-backbone evaluation with four LLMs, and deployment cost analysis confirm the framework's robustness and practical viability for financial institutions.

</details>


### [278] [Retrieval-Conditioned Topology Selection with Provable Budget Conservation for Multi-Agent Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05657)
*Abhijit Talluri,Pujith Anne,Bhagavan Choudary Pendiyala,Raghavendra Chilukuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了RGAO架构，通过从代码库提取结构复杂度向量来动态选择多智能体编排拓扑，将误路由率从30.1%降至8.2%，并实现了可证明的预算守恒特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM代码生成系统面临一个根本性的路由问题：最优编排拓扑取决于待修改代码的结构复杂度，但现有系统在选择拓扑时未参考代码库本身，导致路由决策与代码实际需求不匹配。

Method: 提出检索引导自适应编排(RGAO)架构，包含三个核心组件：(1)复杂度条件拓扑路由器，从分层代码索引中提取结构复杂度向量来选择编排拓扑；(2)预算代数系统，配合六维预算向量的形式化合约管理子智能体；(3)分层代码检索引擎。将复杂度条件LLM路由与形式化资源代数两条研究线结合。

Result: 在Code-Agent框架上实现了：(1)代理测量的误路由率从30.1%降至8.2%；(2)证明了结构归纳守恒定理，实现检索条件动态拓扑选择下的可证明预算守恒；(3)子毫秒级DAG构建和线性树索引可扩展性。

Conclusion: RGAO成功弥合了代码结构分析与拓扑选择之间的鸿沟，首次在检索条件动态拓扑选择下实现了可证明的预算守恒特性，为多智能体代码生成系统的自适应编排提供了理论保障和实用解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems for code generation face a fundamental routing problem: the optimal orchestration topology depends on the structural complexity of the code under modification, yet existing systems select topologies without consulting the codebase. We present Retrieval-Guided Adaptive Orchestration (RGAO), an architecture that closes this loop by extracting a structural complexity vector from a hierarchical code index before selecting the orchestration topology. RGAO operates within Code-Agent, a multi-agent framework whose sub-agents are governed by formal contracts with six-dimensional budget vectors. Our headline contribution is the composition of two previously separate lines of work -- complexity-conditioned LLM routing and formal resource algebras -- yielding a property neither admits alone: provable budget conservation under retrieval-conditioned dynamic topology selection. Concretely we contribute: (1) a complexity-conditioned topology router that reduces proxy-measured misrouting from 30.1% to 8.2%; (2) a budget algebra with a structural-induction conservation theorem; and (3) a hierarchical code retrieval engine. Empirical evaluation demonstrates sub-millisecond DAG construction and linear tree-index scalability.

</details>


### [279] [LaTA: A Drop-in, FERPA-Compliant Local-LLM Autograder for Upper-Division STEM Coursework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05410)
*Jesse A. Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了LaTA，一个完全在本地硬件运行的、开源的、即插即用的LLM自动评分系统，用于LaTeX原生工作流程的STEM课程，实现了零边际成本、低错误率，并显著提升了学生的考试成绩和学习信心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM自动评分器通常依赖第三方API，这违反了FERPA（家庭教育权利和隐私法案），使机构面临数据风险，且需要对作业进行大幅修改。因此，需要一种能够在本地硬件上运行、保护数据隐私且易于集成到现有LaTeX工作流中的自动评分解决方案。

Method: LaTA采用四阶段流水线（摄取、分割、评分、报告），在本地硬件上运行开源权重的思维链LLM（gpt-oss:120b）。该系统将学生作业与教师编写的参考答案进行比较，并应用YAML格式的二元评分标准进行逐项评分。整个系统无需调用第三方API，完全在本地Mac Studio上运行。

Result: 在俄勒冈州立大学ME 373课程中对约200名学生进行了为期一个学期的部署。实现了每份作业零边际成本和1-3分钟的评分时间；经教师确认的评分错误率仅为0.02%–0.04%；与传统评分的对照组相比，LaTA评分组在期中考试表现提升约11%，期末考试提升约8%；学生在所有学习目标上的自信心均有大幅提升（N=159，Likert量表增量≥+1.49，p<10^-27）。

Conclusion: LaTA证明了在保护数据隐私的前提下，利用本地部署的LLM进行大规模自动化评分是可行且高效的。它不仅大幅降低了评分负担和成本，还显著提升了学生的学业表现和学习体验，代码已根据AGPLv3协议开源。

Abstract: Large-language-model (LLM) graders promise to relieve the grading burden of upper-division STEM courses, but most deployments to date send student work to third-party APIs, violating FERPA and exposing institutions to data risk while requiring substantial assignment modification. We present $\textbf{LaTA}\ (\textit{LaTeX Teaching Assistant})$, a drop-in, open-source autograder that runs entirely on commodity on-premises hardware and assumes a LaTeX-native workflow already adopted by many engineering and physics courses. LaTA implements a four-stage pipeline (ingest, segment, grade, report) using a locally hosted open-weight chain-of-thought LLM grader (gpt-oss:120b) that compares student work to an instructor-authored reference solution and applies a YAML rubric with binary per-item scoring. We deployed LaTA in Winter~2026 in ME 373 (Mechanical Engineering Methods) at Oregon State University, grading every weekly assignment for approximately 200 students on a single Mac Studio at \$0 marginal cost per assignment and 1--3 minutes of wall-clock time per submission, enabling regrading of corrected assignments and greatly expanded TA office hour offerings. The instructor-confirmed grading-error rate held at roughly $0.02$--$0.04\%$ per rubric line item across the term. Relative to the same instructor's previous traditionally-graded cohort, the LaTA-graded cohort outperformed by approximately $11\%$ on the midterm exam and $8\%$ on the final exam, and reported large gains in self-assessed confidence on every stated learning objective ($N = 159$ survey responses, $Δ\geq +1.49$ Likert points, $p < 10^{-27}$ on every comparison). We release the code under AGPLv3.

</details>


### [280] [BioResearcher: Scenario-Guided Multi-Agent for Translational Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05985)
*Remigiusz Kinas,Joanna Krawczyk,Rafał Powalski,Przemysław Pietrzak,Agnieszka Kowalewska,Krzysztof Kolmus,Maciej Sypetkowski,Łukasz Smoliński,Tomasz Jetka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ingenix BioResearcher，一个场景引导的多智能体系统，专门用于转化医学研究，在单元测试、开放性生物医学推理和端到端临床发现评估中均达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转化医学需要整合文献、临床试验、专利和多组学分析，同时保持标识符、不确定性和可追溯来源。现有的通用基础模型和现成的工具增强或多智能体系统无法满足这一需求：它们倾向于产生单次回答或开放式运行，难以满足异构生物医学数据源所需的可审计、场景特定的工作流程。

Method: 提出Ingenix BioResearcher系统，采用场景引导的多智能体架构：将查询映射到版本化的研究剧本；通过30多个工具和机器学习端点委托给专门的子智能体；混合结构化数据库访问和沙盒代码进行基因组规模分析；在编辑组装前应用声明级多模型协调。

Result: 在109个单步测试中达到83.49%通过率和0.892平均分；在BixBench-Verified-50基准上达到89.33%，在BaisBench科学发现基准上获得最高0.758平均分；在30个查询的临床端到端基准上达到最高阳性命中率(74.7%±3.3%)和阴性清除率(96.8%±0.2%)。

Conclusion: Ingenix BioResearcher在单元级、开放性和端到端临床评估中展现出广泛且具有竞争力的性能，证明了场景引导的多智能体系统在转化医学研究中的有效性。

Abstract: Translational medicine turns underspecified development goals into evidence synthesis that must combine literature, trials, patents, and quantitative multi-omics analysis while preserving identifiers, uncertainty, and retrievable provenance. General-purpose foundation models and off-the-shelf tool-augmented or multi-agent systems are not built for this: they tend to produce single-shot answers or run open-endedly, and fall short on the auditable, scenario-specific workflows that heterogeneous biomedical sources demand.
  This paper introduces Ingenix BioResearcher, a scenario-guided multi-agent system that maps queries to versioned research playbooks, delegates to specialized subagents over 30+ tools and machine-learning endpoints, mixes structured database access with sandboxed code for genome-scale analyses, and applies claim-level multi-model reconciliation before editorial assembly.
  We evaluate BioResearcher across unit-level capabilities, open-ended biomedical reasoning, and end-to-end clinical discovery. It leads evaluated baselines on 109 single-step tests (83.49% pass rate; 0.892 average score), achieves strong biomedical benchmark performance (89.33% on BixBench-Verified-50 and the top 0.758 mean score on BaisBench Scientific Discovery), and leads on a 30-query clinical end-to-end benchmark with the highest positive hit rate (74.7% $\pm$ 3.3%) and negative clear rate (96.8% $\pm$ 0.2%). These results show broad, competitive performance across unit-level, open-ended, and end-to-end clinical evaluations.

</details>


### [281] [From History to State: Constant-Context Skill Learning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05413)
*Haoyang Xie,Xinyuan Wang,Yancheng Wang,Puda Zhao,Feng Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出constant-context skill learning框架，通过将可复用的程序性上下文从提示词迁移至模型权重中，在保护隐私的同时显著降低了提示词token消耗（减少2-7倍），并在多个基准测试上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人助手代理面临隐私-成本-能力的三重矛盾：云端模型执行多步工作流能力强但会向外部API暴露敏感中间上下文，本地模型保护隐私但可靠性不足。两种设置都需要为长技能提示和不断增长的历史记录反复付费。

Method: 提出constant-context skill learning框架：1) 可复用程序在轻量级任务家族模块中学习；2) 推理仅依赖当前观测和紧凑状态块；3) 确定性追踪器从任务进度渲染状态块并提供对齐的子目标奖励；4) 每个模块通过步骤级SFT训练并经在线RL精炼。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，SFT+RL在ALFWorld达到89.6%未见成功率，WebShop达76.8%成功率，SciWorld达66.4%未见成功率。相比ReAct提示基线，每轮提示token减少2-7倍。性能匹配或超越已发表的强代理训练结果。

Conclusion: 程序性上下文可以从提示词迁移至模型权重中，实现在降低推理成本的同时保持强性能，为个人助手代理的隐私保护与能力平衡提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to operate browsers, files, code and tools, making personal assistants a natural deployment target. Yet personal agents face a privacy-cost-capability tension: cloud models execute multi-step workflows well but expose sensitive intermediate context to external APIs, while local models preserve privacy but remain less reliable. Both settings also pay repeatedly for long skill prompts and growing histories. We propose constant-context skill learning, a context-to-weights framework for recurring agent workflows: reusable procedures are learned in lightweight task-family modules, while inference conditions only on the current observation and a compact state block. A deterministic tracker renders this state block from task progress and supplies aligned subgoal rewards, so each module can be trained with step-level SFT and refined through online RL. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, our agents achieve strong performance across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B. With Qwen3-8B, SFT+RL reaches 89.6\% unseen success on ALFWorld, 76.8\% success on WebShop, and 66.4\% unseen success on SciWorld. They match or exceed strong published agent-training results while reducing prompt tokens per turn by 2--7$\times$ relative to controlled ReAct prompting baselines, showing that procedural context can be moved from prompts into weights.

</details>


### [282] [From Agent Loops to Deterministic Graphs: Execution Lineage for Reproducible AI-Native Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06365)
*Josh Rosen,Seth Rosen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了执行谱系模型，将AI工作表示为具有显式依赖关系的DAG结构，解决了智能体工作流在迭代更新中难以保持稳定中间状态和隔离无关变更的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型系统作为智能体工作流部署时，虽然能产生有效答案，但依赖隐式对话状态，导致难以保留稳定的工作产物、隔离无关更新或在中间产物中传播变更。现有方法在多次迭代修订中容易出现状态不一致问题。

Method: 提出执行谱系模型，将AI原生工作表示为有向无环图(DAG)形式的产物生成计算，具有显式依赖关系、稳定的中间边界和基于身份的重放机制。该方法通过DAG结构明确追踪工作流的演变，而非传统的循环式更新。

Result: 在两个策略备忘录更新任务的对比实验中：(1)在无关分支更新任务中，DAG重放在所有运行中完整保留最终备忘录，零扰动且零无关分支污染，而循环基线方法频繁引入无关上下文；(2)在中间产物编辑任务中，虽然所有系统都在最终备忘录中反映了新约束，但仅DAG重放实现了完美的上游保留、下游传播、无关产物保留和跨产物一致性。

Conclusion: 最终答案质量与维护状态质量是两个不同维度。强循环基线在有界综合/更新任务且所有当前源适配上下文时仍能产生优秀最终输出，但即时任务成功可能掩盖部分状态不一致，并在未来修订中复合累积。执行谱系为变更内容、稳定内容和跨修订工作演变提供了更强保证。

Abstract: Large language model systems are increasingly deployed as agentic workflows that interleave reasoning, tool use, memory, and iterative refinement. These systems are effective at producing answers, but they often rely on implicit conversational state, making it difficult to preserve stable work products, isolate irrelevant updates, or propagate changes through intermediate artifacts.
  We introduce execution lineage: an execution model in which AI-native work is represented as a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of artifact-producing computations with explicit dependencies, stable intermediate boundaries, and identity-based replay. The goal is not to make the model a better one-shot writer, but to make evolving AI-generated work maintainable under change.
  We compare execution-lineage replay against loop-centric update baselines on two controlled policy-memo update tasks. In an unrelated-branch update, DAG replay preserved the final memo exactly in all runs, with zero churn and zero unrelated-branch contamination, while loop baselines regenerated the memo and frequently imported unrelated context. In an intermediate-artifact edit, all systems reflected the new constraint in the final memo, but only DAG replay achieved perfect upstream preservation, downstream propagation, unaffected-artifact preservation, and cross-artifact consistency.
  These results show that final answer quality and maintained-state quality are distinct. Strong loop baselines can remain competitive at producing polished final outputs when the task is a bounded synthesis/update problem and all current sources fit in context, but immediate task success can mask partial state inconsistency that may compound over future revisions. Execution lineage provides stronger guarantees about what should change, what should remain stable, and how work evolves across revisions.

</details>


### [283] [The Geopolitics of AI Safety: A Causal Analysis of Regional LLM Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05427)
*Alif Al Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于概率图模型（PGM）的因果审计框架，利用do-算子分离人口统计学变量注入的因果效应。对七款不同来源的指令微调LLM的实验表明，传统观测性公平性指标因忽略上下文毒性会高估人口偏差，并揭示了西方与东方模型在安全对齐上的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型（LLM）的安全公平性评估主要依赖观测性方法，这种方法容易受到测试数据集中特定人口群体自然关联话题的毒性干扰，导致评估结果混淆。研究旨在解决这一局限性，建立因果审计框架以准确评估LLM安全机制中的公平性问题。

Method: 引入概率图模型（PGM）框架，应用Pearl的do-算子从数学上隔离并量化向提示词注入文化人口统计学变量的因果效应。通过对ToxiGen和BOLD两个数据集进行大规模实证分析，对比观测性偏差与干预性偏差的差异。

Result: 研究发现观测性偏差与干预性偏差存在显著差异，标准公平性指标因未考虑上下文毒性而高估了人口偏差。因果概率揭示了不同的对齐趋势：西方模型（Llama-3.1、Gemma-2、Mistral）对特定人口群体表现出更高的因果拒绝率，而东方模型（Qwen2.5、DeepSeek、Airavata）整体干预率较低但对区域特定人口群体表现出针对性敏感。

Conclusion: 人口敏感的过度触发问题会限制下游应用中的良性对话，表明当前LLM安全对齐存在文化偏向性。这强调了采用因果推理方法而非纯观测性方法进行公平性评估的重要性，为构建更加公平的全球LLM安全机制提供了实证依据。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are integrated into global software systems, ensuring equitable safety guardrails is a critical requirement. Current fairness evaluations predominantly measure bias observationally, a methodology confounded by the inherent toxicity of topics naturally paired with specific demographics in testing datasets. This study introduces a Probabilistic Graphical Model (PGM) framework to audit LLM safety mechanisms causally. By applying Pearl's do-operator, we mathematically isolate the causal effect of injecting a cultural demographic into a prompt. We conduct a large-scale empirical analysis across seven instruction-tuned models spanning diverse origins: the United States (Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B), Europe (Mistral-7B-v0.3), the UAE (Falcon3-7B), China (Qwen2.5-7B, DeepSeek-7B), and India (Airavata-7B). Utilizing two distinct datasets (ToxiGen and BOLD), the findings reveal a disparity between observational and interventional bias, demonstrating that standard fairness metrics can overestimate demographic bias by failing to account for context toxicity. Furthermore, the causal probabilities indicate distinct alignment trends: Western models exhibit higher causal refusal rates for specific demographic groups, whereas Eastern models demonstrate low overall intervention rates with targeted sensitivities toward regional demographics. We discuss the implications of these biases, highlighting how demographic-sensitive over-triggering restricts benign discourse in downstream applications.

</details>


### [284] [Authorization Propagation in Multi-Agent AI Systems: Identity Governance as Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05440)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出多智能体系统存在一个独特的授权传播问题，该问题不同于提示注入且无法被经典访问控制模型解决，需要将身份治理作为基础设施来处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于代理AI的安全讨论主要集中在提示注入上，但多智能体系统还创造了一个独特的授权问题：当非人类主体检索数据、委托任务和合成结果时，如何在不断变化的边界中维护授权不变性。这个被称为"授权传播"的问题不可简化为提示注入，且经典访问控制模型（如RBAC、ABAC或ReBAC）无法完全解决。

Method: 将授权传播形式化为工作流级别的属性，识别出三个子问题（传递委托、聚合推理和时间有效性），并推导出多智能体AI系统中授权架构的七个结构要求。同时分析了近期相关工作，包括调用绑定能力令牌、任务范围授权信封、依赖图策略执行和执行计数撤销等。

Result: 来自生产型企业AI平台的初步实现证据表明，普通系统行为（不仅仅是敌对行动）已经产生了该模型预测的失败。近期工作表明该领域正在趋同于问题本身，但尚未形成完整的架构方案。

Conclusion: 身份治理必须被视为基础设施：需要持续评估、在每个交互边界强制执行，并允许编排逻辑扩展之前设计到系统中。这为多智能体AI系统的安全架构提供了重要的理论和实践指导。

Abstract: The security discussion around agentic AI focuses heavily on prompt injection. This paper argues that multi-agent systems also create a distinct authorization problem: maintaining authorization invariants as non-human principals retrieve data, delegate tasks, and synthesize results across changing boundaries. We call this problem authorization propagation. It is not reducible to prompt injection and is not fully addressed by classical access-control models such as RBAC, ABAC, or ReBAC. The paper formalizes authorization propagation as a workflow-level property, identifies three sub-problems (transitive delegation, aggregation inference, and temporal validity), and derives seven structural requirements for authorization architectures in multi-agent AI systems. Recent work on invocation-bound capability tokens, task-scoped authorization envelopes, dependency-graph policy enforcement, and execution-count revocation demonstrates that the field is converging on the problem, but not yet on a complete architecture. The central claim is that identity governance must be treated as infrastructure: evaluated continuously, enforced at every interaction boundary, and designed into the system before orchestration logic is allowed to scale. Preliminary implementation evidence from a production enterprise AI platform shows that ordinary system behavior, not only adversarial action, already produces the failures this model predicts.

</details>


### [285] [Agentic Discovery of Exchange-Correlation Density Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05460)
*Titouan Duston,Jiashu Liang,Yuanheng Wang,Weihao Gao,Xuelan Wen,Nan Sheng,Weiluo Ren,Yang Sun,Yixiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用大语言模型驱动的智能体搜索系统，自动化地发现了新型交换相关泛函SAFS26-a，其在基准测试上较gold-standard ωB97M-V提升约9%，但同时也揭示了AI可能通过非物理捷径“博弈”基准测试的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度泛函理论（DFT）中精确交换相关（XC）泛函的开发一直是一个长期挑战。传统泛函主要依赖研究人员结合物理洞察、精确约束和经验拟合进行手工设计，这一过程既耗时又依赖专家直觉。大语言模型的最新进展为替代这种人工驱动的开发循环提供了一种系统化、自动化的新方案。

Method: 提出了一种基于LLM的智能体搜索系统。该系统通过“计划-执行-总结”的迭代循环运作：LLM根据进化历史提出结构化的泛函形式修改建议，随后在标准热化学数据集上优化泛函参数，并在留出子集上评估性能，从而实现泛函性能的持续改进。

Result: 系统成功发现了目前最强的泛函SAFS26-a（Seed Agentic Functional Search 2026），其性能相比gold-standard基线ωB97M-V提升了约9%。研究同时也发现，能力足以发现真实改进的模型同样有能力利用非物理捷径来“博弈”基准测试。

Conclusion: AI辅助科学研究具有巨大潜力，但也伴随着风险：模型可能通过非物理捷径在基准测试中获取高分而非发现真正的科学规律。因此，将领域专业知识转化为显式强制约束，对于确保AI发现结果的科学合理性至关重要。

Abstract: The development of accurate exchange-correlation (XC) functionals remains a longstanding challenge in density functional theory (DFT). The vast majority of XC functionals have been hand designed by human researchers combining physical insight, exact constraints, and empirical fitting. Recent advances in large language models enable a systematic, automated alternative to this human-driven design loop. This report presents an agentic search system in which an LLM proposes structured functional-form changes guided by evolutionary history. The system attempts to improve functional performance through an iterative plan-execute-summarize loop, where improvements are measurable by optimizing functional parameters against a standard thermochemistry dataset, then evaluating performance on a held-out subset. The strongest discovered functional, SAFS26-a (Seed Agentic Functional Search 2026), improves upon the gold-standard ωB97M-V baseline by ~9%. These results also surface a cautionary lesson for AI-assisted science: models powerful enough to discover genuine improvements are equally capable of exploiting unphysical shortcuts to game the benchmark; domain expertise translated into explicitly enforced constraints remains essential to keeping results scientifically grounded.

</details>


### [286] [Intentionality is a Design Decision: Measuring Functional Intentionality for Accountable AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05475)
*Allessia Chiappetta,Robert Mahari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了功能意向性测试（FIT）框架，用于量化AI系统的意向性行为，以实现适度的监督和自主性校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
