Building on this insight, we introduce HyperLens, a high-resolution probe designed to trace confidence trajectories and quantify the cognitive effort during inference. Across LLMs and datasets, HyperLens reveals a consistent divergence in confidence trajectories that separates complex from simple tasks. We abstract this pattern into a quantitative cognitive effort metric. Our analysis reveals a fundamental principle: complex tasks consistently require higher cognitive effort. Finally, we provide a mechanistic diagnosis of a common side effect of standard Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT): it can reduce cognitive effort and consequently degrade performance on in-domain tasks.

</details>


### [320] [Best Arm Identification in Generalized Linear Bandits via Hybrid Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05745)
*Qirun Zeng,Xuchuang Wang,Jiayi Shen,Xutong Liu,Fang Kong,Jinhang Zuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义线性老虎机中固定置信度最佳臂识别问题，提出了一种融合绝对奖励和相对比较混合反馈的Track-and-Stop算法，并给出了样本复杂度的理论上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最佳臂识别研究大多仅关注单一反馈类型（绝对奖励或相对比较），而在实际应用中，决策者往往可以同时获取多种类型的反馈信息。为了更高效地利用异质反馈信息，降低样本复杂度，亟需构建能统一处理混合反馈的算法框架。

Method: 作者提出了一种基于似然比的置信序列，在自协和性假设下构建了显式的椭球置信集，从而统一了异质的广义线性观测；基于此，设计了混合Track-and-Stop算法，通过跟踪联合动作空间上的极小极大最优设计自适应地分配查询；还将框架扩展到了成本感知场景，考虑不同反馈模态的异质获取成本。

Result: 证明了所提算法具有δ-正确性，并给出了停止时间的高概率上界；实验结果表明，相较于基准方法，该算法显著提高了样本效率。

Conclusion: 本文构建了一个统一的混合反馈建模框架，算法在理论和实验上均显示出优越性，为复杂反馈场景下的序贯决策问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: We study fixed-confidence best arm identification in generalized linear bandits under a hybrid feedback model: at each round, the learner may query either (i) absolute reward feedback from a single arm or (ii) relative (dueling) feedback from an arm pair, both governed by generalized linear models. We introduce a likelihood-ratio--based confidence sequence that unifies heterogeneous generalized linear observations and yields an explicit ellipsoidal confidence set under a self-concordance assumption. Building on this confidence set, we propose a hybrid Track-and-Stop algorithm that adaptively allocates queries by tracking a minimax-optimal design over a joint action space of arms and pairs. We establish $δ$-correctness and provide high-probability upper bounds on the stopping time. We further extend the framework to a cost-aware setting that accounts for heterogeneous acquisition costs across feedback modalities. Empirical experiments demonstrate that the proposed algorithms significantly improve sample efficiency over baseline methods.

</details>


### [321] [Evaluating Explainability in Safety-Critical ATR Systems: Limitations of Post-Hoc Methods and Paths Toward Robust XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05748)
*Vanessa Buhrmester,David Muench,Dimitri Bulatov,Michael Arens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对安全关键自动目标识别（ATR）系统中的可解释人工智能（XAI）方法进行了系统性评估，发现当前广泛使用的后解释方法存在关键缺陷，不足以支持安全关键部署，需向更鲁棒、因果基础和物理信息驱动的解释方法发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习系统在安全关键环境中的部署需求增加，单纯的高预测性能已不足够。在自动目标识别（ATR）领域，处理图像、视频、雷达和多传感器数据的模型决策必须具备可解释性、可靠性和可验证性，以确保系统安全。

Method: 本文提出了面向保障的解释性评估框架，建立了分类体系，从可解释性、鲁棒性、抗操纵脆弱性、验证与确认适用性四个关键维度，对显著性方法、注意力机制方法、代理方法及检测感知扩展等主要XAI范式进行系统性评估。

Result: 研究识别出当前后解释方法存在系统性局限，包括虚假解释、扰动不稳定性、视觉可信输出导致的过度信任等关键失效模式。这些发现表明，广泛使用的XAI技术可能不足以满足安全关键部署的要求。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了超越视觉合理性解释的必要性，需要发展支持可靠决策和系统级保障的方法。论文讨论了对ATR系统的启示，并提出了向更鲁棒、因果基础和物理信息驱动的解释方法发展的方向。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) is increasingly rec ognized as essential for deploying machine learning systems in safety critical environments. In Automatic Target Recognition (ATR), where models operate on image, video, radar, and multisensor data, high pre dictive performance alone is insufficient. Model decisions must also be interpretable, reliable, and suitable for validation. This paper presents a structured evaluation of explainability methods in the context of safety-critical ATR systems: We identify major XAI paradigms, including saliency-based, attention-based, and surrogate ap proaches, as well as recent detection-aware extensions. Based on this, we formalize explainability as an assurance-oriented assessment problem, introduce a taxonomy, and assess these methods with respect to four key dimensions: interpretability, robustness, vulnerability to manipula tion, and suitability for validation and verification. The analysis identifies systematic limitations of current post-hoc explanation methods. In par ticular, we derive critical failure modes such as spurious explanations, instability under perturbations, and overtrust induced by visually con vincing outputs. These findings indicate that widely used XAI techniques may be insufficient for safety-critical deployment. Finally, we discuss implications for ATR systems and outline directions toward more robust, causally grounded, and physically informed explain ability methods. Our results emphasize the need to move beyond visually plausible explanations toward approaches that support reliable decision making and system-level assurance.

</details>


### [322] [Confidence is the key: how conformal prediction enhances the generative design of permeable peptides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05770)
*Laura van Weesep,Sunay Chankeshwara,Leonardo De Maria,Florian David,Ola Engkvist,Gökçe Geylan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次将共形预测（CP）与强化学习引导的生成模型相结合，用于设计具有渗透性的环肽，通过不确定性感知的预测器提高了优化过程的可靠性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在利用强化学习进行分子设计时，预测模型的适用域有限，可能导致生成的分子落在预测器适用域之外，从而使预测不可靠，引导设计进入高回报但高不确定性的化学空间。这一问题在环肽设计中尤为突出，尽管环肽具有治疗潜力，但其渗透性优化仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出了一种基于强化学习的生成框架，采用不确定性感知的渗透性预测器作为评分组件。引入共形预测（CP）作为不确定性量化方法，根据用户定义的置信水平评估设计，以应对新颖化学结构带来的预测不确定性。

Result: 实验表明，奖励具有CP信息预测的生成肽段不仅提高了肽优化过程的可靠性和效率，还有效抑制了在预测器适用域之外的探索，避免了不可靠的设计建议。

Conclusion: 该方法成功弥合了预测不确定性与强化学习引导探索之间的差距，首次展示了生成模型与共形预测的结合，为分子设计提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative models coupled with reinforcement learning (RL), such as REINVENT and PepINVENT, have emerged as a powerful framework for de novo molecular design. During the ideation process these generative frameworks utilize various predictive models as part of the optimization objectives. However, the utility of the predictive models can be limited by their domain of applicability. When RL is used to explore the chemical space with predictive models, it can suggest molecules that lie outside the predictor's domain of applicability. As a result, the predictions may become less reliable, potentially steering designs into high reward but also high uncertainty chemical spaces. This is particularly pronounced for cyclic peptides which show therapeutic promise due to their modifiability and large interaction surfaces but are understudied compared to small molecules. While passive membrane permeation in cyclic peptides has attracted interest, identifying optimal permeable designs remains challenging yet crucial for targeting intracellular sites. We present an RL-guided generative framework that designs permeable cyclic peptides using an uncertainty-aware permeability predictor as the scoring component. To address predictive uncertainty, especially impacted by novel chemistry, we integrate conformal prediction (CP) as our uncertainty quantification method. CP assesses designs based on the calibrated model under a user-defined confidence level. We demonstrate that rewarding generated peptides with CP-informed predictions improves both reliability and efficiency of peptide optimization process. This also discourages exploration outside the predictor's applicability domain. This approach bridges the gap between predictive uncertainty and RL-guided exploration, showing how generative modelling and conformal prediction can be combined for the first time.

</details>


### [323] [CircuitFormer: A Circuit Language Model for Analog Topology Design from Natural Language Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05773)
*Md Touhidul Islam,Sujan Kumar Saha,Farimah Farahmandi,Mark Tehranipoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了目前最大的模拟电路数据集，并提出了一种新型电路图分词器CKT，基于此训练的CircuitFormer模型以240倍更少的参数量在电路生成任务上显著超越了现有开源大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟电路设计自动化是EDA领域的长期难题。尽管基于Transformer的大语言模型在软件代码生成上取得成功，但应用于模拟硬件设计面临两大瓶颈：(1) 缺乏包含自然语言描述与网表对应的模拟电路设计数据集；(2) 通用分词器（如BPE）无法有效捕捉电路固有的图结构特征。

Method: 1) 构建了包含31,341对网表-自然语言描述的模拟电路数据集，覆盖所有主要电路类别；2) 提出Circuit Tokenizer (CKT)，一种新型电路图分词器，通过显式挖掘频繁子电路来编码网表连接性，将词汇表增长与电路复杂度解耦，实现常数级O(1)复杂度；3) 基于优化后的分词方法，训练了511M参数的编码器-解码器Transformer模型CircuitFormer。

Result: CKT相比标准BPE在电路拓扑表示上表现更优，序列长度减少57%，使用固定512大小的词汇表实现了2.3倍的更优压缩比。CircuitFormer模型在所有主要模拟电路类别上实现了100%的语法正确率和83%的功能成功率，分别以240倍更少的参数量超越最先进开源LLM 10%和14%。

Conclusion: 通过构建专用数据集和针对电路图结构设计的分词器，本文有效解决了LLM应用于模拟电路设计的关键瓶颈，为模拟电路设计自动化提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案，并公开了数据集以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Automating analog circuit design remains a longstanding challenge in Electronic Design Automation (EDA). While Transformer-based Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized software code generation, their application to analog hardware design is hindered by two critical limitations: (i) the scarcity of analog design datasets containing natural language description of a design and its corresponding netlist, and (ii) the inefficiency of general-purpose tokenizers (e.g., Byte Pair Encoding (BPE)) in capturing the inherent graph structure of circuits. To bridge this gap, first, we curate the largest annotated dataset of analog circuit netlists to date, comprising 31,341 netlist-natural language description pairs across all major circuit classes. Furthermore, we propose Circuit Tokenizer (CKT), a novel circuit graph tokenizer designed to encode netlist connectivity by explicitly mining frequent subcircuits. In terms of scalability, CKT overcomes the bottleneck of prior circuit graph serialization methods where vocabulary size scales linearly with maximum number of components in the dataset, n_max, (O(n_max)); instead, CKT decouples vocabulary growth from circuit complexity, achieving a constant O(1) complexity. Empirically, CKT outperforms standard BPE on circuit topology representation, reducing sequence length by 57% and achieving a 2.3x superior compression ratio using a compact, fixed vocabulary of size 512. Leveraging this optimized tokenization, we train a circuit-specific language model, CircuitFormer, a 511M parameter encoder-decoder transformer. Our model achieves 100% syntactic correctness and an 83% functional success rate across all major analog circuit categories, outperforming state-of-the-art open-source LLMs by 10% and 14%, respectively, while requiring 240x fewer parameters. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/touhid314/cktformer-dataset.

</details>


### [324] [HEDP: A Hybrid Energy-Distance Prompt-based Framework for Domain Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05776)
*Yu Feng,Zhen Tian,Haoran Luo,Xie Yu,Diancheng Cheng,Haoyue Zheng,Shuai Lyu,Ping Zong,Lianyuan Li,Xin Ge,Yifan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HEDP框架，通过引入能量正则化损失和混合能量-距离加权机制，有效解决了领域增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，在未见过的领域上实现了2.57%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域增量学习场景要求模型在不重新训练的情况下持续适应新数据领域，但领域偏移往往导致严重的性能下降和灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 受亥姆霍兹自由能启发，提出了混合能量-距离提示(HEDP)框架。该方法引入能量正则化损失来增强领域表示的可分性，并设计了混合能量-距离加权机制，融合基于能量和基于距离的线索来改进领域选择和泛化能力。

Result: 在包括CORe50在内的多个基准测试上的实验表明，HEDP在未见过的领域上实现了2.57%的准确率提升，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘并增强了开放世界适应性。

Conclusion: HEDP是一个有效的领域增量学习框架，能够有效缓解灾难性遗忘问题并提升模型在开放世界场景下的适应性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Domain Incremental Learning is a critical scenario that requires models to continuously adapt to new data domains without retraining. However, domain shifts often cause severe performance degradation. To address this, we propose Hybrid Energy-Distance Prompt, a domain-incremental framework inspired by Helmholtz free energy. HEDP introduces an energy regularization loss to enhance the separability of domain representations and a hybrid energy-distance weighted mechanism that fuses energy-based and distance-based cues to improve domain selection and generalization. Experiments on multiple benchmarks, including CORe50, show that HEDP achieves superior performance on unseen domains with a 2.57\% accuracy gain, effectively mitigating catastrophic forgetting and enhancing open-world adaptability. Our code is \href{https://github.com/dannis97500/HEDP/}{available here}.

</details>


### [325] [Von Neumann Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05780)
*Shekhar S. Chandra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出冯·诺依曼神经元和冯·诺依曼网络（VNN），基于细胞阵列和扩散过程的计算系统，构建出一种架构自适应、参数更高效且性能更优的新型神经网络，并证明其具有计算通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受冯·诺依曼在20世纪中期提出的细胞阵列计算系统启发，该系统旨在作为人脑的简化模型，其中每个细胞具有有限的状态集并预测可通过扩散过程建模。本研究试图将这一理论在现代深度学习环境中实现，探索是否能够构建具有可学习专门角色的类脑计算系统。

Method: 基于细胞拓扑结构和扩散签名，构建了冯·诺依曼神经元和相应的冯·诺依曼网络（VNNs）的数学框架。该方法扩展了神经算子理论，通过细胞阵列上的卷积来学习格林函数，使得网络架构仅依赖于输入和输出在细胞阵列上的结构和位置，从而实现自工程化设计。

Result: 实验表明，基于VNN的多层感知器在基础任务上优于等效的深度学习变体，同时具有更高的参数效率。VNN能够学习新类型的任务，包括构建现代计算机通用的冯·诺依曼（硬件）架构向细胞的扩展，证明了其在细胞层面解决和扩展计算架构的能力。

Conclusion: 研究不仅证明VNN属于更一般的计算系统——细胞机且具有计算通用性，还展示了其在性能、参数效率和任务适应性方面的优势。这为探索基于细胞阵列的新型计算架构和神经形态计算提供了新的机遇。

Abstract: In the mid-twentieth century, mathematician and polymath John von Neumann created a computational system on an array of cells as a simple model of the human brain, where each cell had one of a finite set of roles or states that he predicted would be modelled by a diffusion process. In this work, we show that such a system, when developed in a modern deep learning setting, enables the construction of an artificial neuron having specialized roles that can be learnt. We refer to this neuron as the Von Neumann neuron, and the resulting neural network from such neurons result in a self-engineered design whose architecture is only dependent on the structure and locations of its inputs and outputs on this cellular array. The mathematical framework for these Von Neumann Networks (VNNs) is also constructed and shows that they are based on the extension of neural operators and the learning of Green's functions with convolutions on a cellular topology having a diffusion signature. We also prove that these VNNs are part of a more general computational system called Cellular Machines that are computationally universal. Initial experiments show that VNN based multi-layered perceptrons outperform their equivalent deep learning variant on basic tasks, while being more parameter efficient and are capable of learning new types of tasks. This includes the ability to solve for and construct an extension of the Von Neumann (hardware) architecture common to all modern computers to cells and suggests new opportunities that could be explored.

</details>


### [326] [Sheet as Token: A Graph-Enhanced Representation for Multi-Sheet Spreadsheet Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05811)
*Yiming Lei,Yiqi Wang,Yujia Zhang,Bo Guan,Depei Zhu,Chunhui Wang,Zhuonan Hao,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Sheet as Token框架，将每个工作表视为统一语义单元，通过图增强方法实现多表电子表格检索，显著优于传统分块方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作簿级别的电子表格理解对基于语言模型的数据分析智能体日益重要，但面临挑战：相关信息通常分散在多个工作表中，且具有异构的模式、布局和隐式关系。现有检索增强方法将电子表格分解为行、列或块以提高可扩展性，但这种以块为中心的表示会将工作表分割成孤立的文本片段，削弱全局表级语义。

Method: 提出Sheet as Token框架，将每个工作表视为统一语义单元。具体方法包括：1）从表名、列标题、代表性值和布局特征中提取模式感知记录，将每个工作表编码为紧凑的密集令牌；2）设计图检索器，利用语义、查询条件、模式一致性和形状兼容性关系，在表令牌上构建特定于查询的候选图；3）通过多阶段图变换器组合这些通道，检索支持性表集。

Result: 在构建的多表电子表格语料库上的实验表明：表级令牌化能够学习稳定的表示，图增强的跨表推理在有限额外图端计算的情况下，相比浅层图基线改进了列表式检索性能。

Conclusion: 表级令牌化是可扩展多表电子表格理解的有前景的抽象方法，为未来研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Workbook-scale spreadsheet understanding is increasingly important for language-model-based data analysis agents, but remains challenging because relevant information is often distributed across multiple sheets with heterogeneous schemas, layouts, and implicit relationships. Existing retrieval-augmented approaches typically decompose spreadsheets into rows, columns, or blocks to improve scalability; however, such chunk-centric representations can fragment worksheets into isolated text spans and weaken global sheet-level semantics. We propose Sheet as Token, a graph-enhanced framework that treats each worksheet as a unified semantic unit for multi-sheet spreadsheet retrieval. Our method extracts schema-aware records from sheet names, column headers, representative values, and layout features, and encodes each worksheet into a compact dense token. Given a natural-language query, a Graph Retriever constructs a query-specific candidate graph over sheet tokens using semantic, query-conditioned, schema-consistency, and shape-compatibility relations, and composes these channels through a multi-stage graph transformer to retrieve supporting sheet sets. Experiments on a constructed multi-sheet spreadsheet corpus show that sheet-level tokenization learns stable representations, and that graph-enhanced cross-sheet reasoning improves listwise retrieval over a shallow graph baseline with limited additional graph-side computation. These results suggest that sheet-level tokenization is a promising abstraction for scalable multi-sheet spreadsheet understanding.

</details>


### [327] [Long-Horizon Q-Learning: Accurate Value Learning via n-Step Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05812)
*Armaan A. Abraham,Lucy Xiaoyang Shi,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了长视野Q学习（LQL）算法，通过利用铰链损失惩罚违反最优性紧致约束的行为，在不增加额外网络和计算开销的情况下，有效缓解了时序差分学习中的误差累积问题，在多种基准测试中表现优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略、基于价值的强化学习方法（如Q学习）虽然能够从任意经验中学习，但在长视野任务中，自举过程使得学习变得脆弱：后期状态的估计误差会通过时序差分更新向后传播，并随时间复合累积，导致算法性能下降。

Method: 本文提出了长视野Q学习（LQL）。该方法基于最优性紧致观察：任何已实现的动作序列都是最优策略期望收益的下界，因此早期采取最优行为不应比先跟随观测动作几步再切换到最优行为更差。LQL将此不等式转化为一种实用的稳定机制，通过铰链损失惩罚违反这些界限的行为。重要的是，LQL利用计算TD误差时已产生的网络输出来计算这些惩罚，不需要辅助网络，也不需要相对于Q学习的额外前向传播。

Result: 在一系列在线和离线转在线基准测试中，将LQL与多种最先进的方法结合，结果显示LQL在相似的运行时间内始终优于1步TD和n步TD学习。

Conclusion: LQL通过引入有原则的误差累积防范机制，成功提升了Q学习在长视野任务中的稳定性和性能，且计算高效，为强化学习中的价值函数估计提供了一种有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Off-policy, value-based reinforcement learning methods such as Q-learning are appealing because they can learn from arbitrary experience, including data collected by older policies or other agents. In practice, however, bootstrapping makes long-horizon learning brittle: estimation errors at later states propagate backward through temporal-difference (TD) updates and can compound over time. We propose long-horizon Q-learning (LQL), which introduces a principled backstop against compounding error when learning the optimal action-value function. LQL builds on a prior optimality tightening observation: any realized action sequence lower-bounds what the optimal policy can achieve in expectation, so acting optimally earlier should not be worse than following the observed actions for several steps before switching to optimal behavior. Our contribution is to turn this inequality into a practical stabilization mechanism for Q-learning by using a hinge loss to penalize violations of these bounds. Importantly, LQL computes these penalties using network outputs already produced for the TD error, requiring no auxiliary networks and no additional forward passes relative to Q-learning. When combined with multiple state-of-the-art methods on a range of online and offline-to-online benchmarks, LQL consistently outperforms both 1-step TD and n-step TD learning at similar runtime.

</details>


### [328] [AGPO: Asymmetric Group Policy Optimization for Verifiable Reasoning and Search Ads Relevance at JD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05826)
*Yang Xu,Kun Yao,Yiming Deng,Zheng Fang,Kai Ming Ting,Ming Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出非对称组策略优化(AGPO)方法，通过负主导强化策略和组优势机制，有效缓解RLVR导致的模型推理边界收缩问题，在数学基准和工业应用中取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可验证奖励强化学习(RLVR)方法虽然提高了采样效率，但会导致模型推理能力边界相比基础模型收缩，在大样本规模下基础模型反而能达到更高的覆盖率。作者旨在解决这一边界收缩问题，保持模型的探索能力。

Method: 提出非对称组策略优化(AGPO)：1) 采用负主导强化策略抑制错误推理路径，保持基础模型的探索能力；2) 引入组优势机制，根据组内方差缩放正更新，使模型聚焦于稀有正确路径同时抑制平凡路径的更新。

Result: 在五个数学基准测试上达到最先进准确率，并持续提升大规模下的pass@$k$性能；在搜索广告相关性优化的工业应用中，有效提升数据标注质量，显著改善下游学生模型的性能。

Conclusion: AGPO通过非对称的强化策略成功缓解了RLVR的边界收缩问题，在保持采样效率的同时扩展了模型的推理能力边界，为大规模语言模型的强化学习训练提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has demonstrated notable success in enhancing the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs). However, recent studies reveal that while current RLVR methods improve sampling efficiency towards correct paths, they do not elicit fundamentally new reasoning patterns. Instead, the reasoning capability boundary of trained models often narrows compared to their base models, with base models achieving higher coverage at large sample sizes. In this work, we propose Asymmetric Group Policy Optimization (AGPO) to counteract this boundary shrinkage. AGPO adopts a negative-dominant reinforcement strategy to suppress incorrect reasoning paths, maintaining the base model's exploration capacity. For positive reinforcement, AGPO adopts a group advantage mechanism, which scales positive updates based on intra-group variance, allowing the model to focus on rare correct paths while suppressing updates from trivial paths. Our experiments on five mathematical benchmarks demonstrate that AGPO achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while consistently improving pass@$k$ performance at scale. In a large-scale industrial application for search ads relevance optimization, AGPO effectively enhances the quality of the data annotation, leading to substantial performance gains in downstream student models.

</details>


### [329] [MolRecBench-Wild: A Real-World Benchmark for Optical Chemical Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05832)
*Haote Yang,Hui Wang,Chen Zhu,Jingchao Wang,Linye Li,Hongbin Lai,Huijie Ao,Yongxuan Lyu,Jiang Wu,Jiaxing Sun,Lua Chen,Yuanyuan Cao,Ruijie Zhang,Shengxin Lu,Lijun Wu,Bin Wang,Conghui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MOSAIC难度分析框架、MolRecBench-Wild现实世界基准及CARBON表示语言，对18个OCSR模型进行评估，发现现有模型在真实学术文献上性能严重下降，揭示了专利基准与现实应用间的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前光学化学结构识别（OCSR）系统在面对真实科学文献图像时仍不可靠，主要归因于视觉复杂性和化学语义的复杂性，现有基准无法充分反映现实世界的挑战，亟需建立更全面的评估体系。

Method: 1）构建MOSAIC双维度难度框架，定义37个细粒度标签以刻画视觉干扰和化学语义挑战；2）基于该框架构建MolRecBench-Wild基准数据集，包含从820篇近期化学论文中提取的5029个结构；3）提出CARBON表示语言，支持表达价态变化、图标基团等非标准化学语义；4）采用双轨评估协议，同时支持CARBON和SMILES输出格式。

Result: 在MolRecBench-Wild上对18个具备OCSR能力的模型进行全面实验，结果显示模型性能严重下降，暴露出既往专利基准与真实学术场景之间存在巨大差距。

Conclusion: 现有OCSR模型在面对真实世界化学文献时表现不佳，亟需针对视觉干扰和化学语义复杂性进行改进；所提出的框架、基准和表示语言为后续研究提供了更真实、全面的评估工具。

Abstract: Optical Chemical Structure Recognition (OCSR) aims to translate molecular diagrams in scientific literature into machine-readable formats, but current systems remain unreliable on real-world images due to substantial visual and chemical complexity. We introduce MOSAIC, a dual-dimensional difficulty framework with 37 fine-grained labels that jointly characterize visual interference and chemical semantic challenges in molecular diagrams. Based on this framework, we construct MolRecBench-Wild, a benchmark of 5,029 structures from 820 recent chemistry papers, covering the full difficulty spectrum observed in real publications. To enable faithful semantic evaluation beyond SMILES and MolFile, we propose CARBON, a representation language capable of expressing valence variations, icon-based groups, and other non-standard chemical semantics. We further adopt a dual-track evaluation protocol supporting both CARBON and SMILES outputs for broad model compatibility. Comprehensive experiments over 18 OCSR-capable models reveal severe performance degradation on MolRecBench-Wild, exposing a large gap between previous patent benchmarks and real-world academic scenarios.

</details>


### [330] [On the Role of Language Representations in Auto-Bidding: Findings and Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05833)
*Guanyu Zhu,Jining Luan,Hanwen Du,Xinyu Fang,Sibo Xu,Ersheng Ni,Hongji Li,Jincheng Fang,Ronghao Chen,Huacan Wang,Xuanqi Lan,Yongxin Ni,Yiqi Sun,Youhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemBid是一个自动竞价框架，通过将LLM编码的语义信息作为token注入到数值竞价轨迹中，解决了传统数值方法难以显式表达高层意图的问题，在多场景下显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动竞价方法依赖紧凑的数值状态表示，虽然能隐式捕捉投放动态，但难以显式表示和控制高层意图、反馈演变和策略指导；同时，大语言模型（LLMs）擅长编码语义信息，但其如何以及何时能辅助自动竞价尚不清晰。

Method: 提出了SemBid框架，核心是将LLM编码的语义信息在token级别注入到离线竞价轨迹中。引入三种语义输入：任务、历史和策略。将这些语义作为token与数值轨迹token拼接，通过自注意力机制进行语义-数值的精细整合，而非简单拼接。

Result: 在初步系统研究中发现：(1) LLM嵌入包含竞价相关线索但无法替代数值特征；(2) 只有通过精细的语义-数值整合而非简单拼接才能获得提升。SemBid在多样化场景和预算机制下均优于离线RL和生成序列建模的竞争基线，在整体性能、约束满足和鲁棒性方面表现更稳定。

Conclusion: SemBid成功实现了语义信息与数值精度的有效融合，提升了自动竞价的可控性和跨目标泛化能力，验证了LLM在自动竞价领域的应用价值，证明了token级别的语义-数值整合是一种有效的技术路径。

Abstract: Auto-bidding is a crucial task in real-time advertising markets, where policies must optimize long-horizon value under delivery constraints (e.g., budget and CPA). Existing methods for auto-bidding rely on compact numerical state representations: while they can implicitly capture delivery dynamics, they offer limited support for explicitly representing and controlling high-level intent, evolving feedback, and operator-style strategic guidance in real campaigns. Meanwhile, Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a powerful method for encoding semantic information, it remains unclear when LLMs help and how to integrate them without sacrificing numerical precision. Through systematic preliminary studies, we find that (1) LLM embeddings contain bidding-relevant cues yet cannot replace numerical features, and (2) gains emerge only with careful semantic--numeric integration rather than naive concatenation. Motivated by these findings, we propose \textit{SemBid}, a novel auto-bidding framework that injects LLM-encoded semantics into offline bidding trajectories at the token level. SemBid introduces three semantic inputs: \textit{Task}, \textit{History}, and \textit{Strategy}. It injects these semantics as tokens alongside numerical trajectory tokens and uses self-attention to integrate them, improving controllability and generalization across objectives. Across diverse scenarios and budget regimes, SemBid outperforms competitive baselines from offline RL and generative sequence modeling, with more consistent gains in overall performance, constraint satisfaction, and robustness. Our code is available at: \href{https://github.com/AlanYu04/SemBid-KDD2026}{\textcolor{blue}{here}}.

</details>


### [331] [Taklif.AI: LLM-Powered Platform for Interest-Based Personalized College Assignments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05842)
*Zaki Kurdya,Mohammed Zuqlam,Salem Amassi,Shady Telbany,Motaz Saad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Taklif.AI平台，利用大语言模型（LLM）根据学生的课外兴趣和文化背景自动生成个性化作业，初步测试显示84%的参与者认为个性化功能有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育工作者在创建能够适应学生多样化兴趣和认知能力的引人入胜的个性化作业方面面临重大挑战。传统的“一刀切”作业往往导致学生参与度下降，并增加抄袭等不道德行为。现有AI教育平台仅基于学业成绩指标进行个性化，忽视了课外兴趣和文化背景。

Method: 提出了Taklif.AI平台，采用AWS无服务器架构和Next.js，使用Llama 3.3 70B作为主要LLM（通过LiteLLM进行多提供商负载均衡），利用LangChain进行提示编排。通过结构化的提示工程管道和输入输出护栏，将学生的课外兴趣和文化背景纳入作业生成过程。

Result: 初步用户接受度测试包含68名参与者（65名学生和3名教育工作者），结果显示反响积极，84%的参与者认为个性化功能有益。

Conclusion: 讨论了平台当前的能力和局限性，并概述了对学习成果进行严格实证评估的未来方向。

Abstract: Educators face significant challenges in creating engaging, personalized assignments that accommodate students' diverse interests and cognitive abilities. Traditional one-size-fits-all assignments frequently lead to decreased student engagement and increased reliance on unethical practices such as plagiarism. To address these challenges, we present Taklif.AI, a platform that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to automatically generate personalized assignments tailored to individual student interests. Unlike existing AI-powered educational platforms that personalize based on academic performance metrics alone, Taklif.AI incorporates students' extracurricular interests and cultural contexts into the assignment generation process through a structured prompt engineering pipeline with input and output guardrails. The platform employs a serverless architecture on AWS with Next.js, using Llama 3.3 70B as the primary LLM via LiteLLM for multi-provider load balancing and LangChain for prompt orchestration. We describe the system architecture, the prompt design methodology, and the guardrails framework that ensures output quality. Preliminary user acceptance testing with 68 participants (65 students and 3 educators) indicates positive reception, with 84% of participants rating the personalization feature as beneficial. We discuss the platform's current capabilities and limitations, and outline directions for rigorous empirical evaluation of learning outcomes.

</details>


### [332] [AirQualityBench: A Realistic Evaluation Benchmark for Global Air Quality Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05854)
*Xing Xu,Xu Wang,Yudong Zhang,Huilin Zhao,Zhengyang Zhou,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AirQualityBench，一个全球多污染物空气质量预测基准数据集，旨在解决现有评估协议忽视真实监测网络条件（如不均匀覆盖、结构化缺失、异质污染物尺度等）的问题，实验表明在预处理数据集上的优异表现并不能可靠地迁移至全球碎片化的监测流中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空气质量预测模型通常在区域、预处理和归一化的数据集上进行评估，通过移除或人工填补缺失观测来简化比较，但这掩盖了真实监测网络中的主导条件：不均匀的全球覆盖、结构化的数据缺失、异质的污染物尺度以及部署成本，因此需要建立一个更贴近现实的评估基准。

Method: 引入AirQualityBench全球多污染物基准，包含2021-2025年间3720个监测站的逐小时观测数据，涵盖六种主要污染物，保留原始观测掩码；不通过插补构建稠密张量，而是将数据缺失作为预测问题的一部分，并在逆变换到物理浓度尺度后报告有效未来观测的误差。

Result: 在该统一协议下评估代表性时空模型发现，在经过清洗处理的数据集上的强劲表现并不能可靠地迁移到全球、碎片化的监测流中，暴露了现有模型在真实场景下的局限性。

Conclusion: AirQualityBench作为一个现实的测试平台，可用于评估可扩展、掩码感知和物理可解释的空气质量预测模型，推动该领域向更贴近实际应用的方向发展。

Abstract: Air-quality forecasting models are commonly evaluated on regional, preprocessed, and normalized datasets, where missing observations are removed or artificially completed. Such protocols simplify comparison but hide the conditions that dominate real monitoring networks: uneven global coverage, structured missingness, heterogeneous pollutant scales, and deployment cost. We introduce \textbf{AirQualityBench}, a global multi-pollutant benchmark designed to evaluate forecasting models under these realistic conditions. The benchmark contains hourly observations from 3,720 monitoring stations over 2021--2025, covers six major pollutants, and preserves provider-native observation masks. Rather than imputing a dense data tensor, AirQualityBench exposes missingness as part of the forecasting problem and reports errors on valid future observations after inverse transformation to physical concentration scales. Evaluating representative spatio-temporal models under this unified protocol shows that strong performance on sanitized datasets does not reliably transfer to global, fragmented monitoring streams. AirQualityBench therefore serves as a realistic testbed for scalable, mask-aware, and physically interpretable air-quality forecasting. All benchmark data, code, evaluation scripts, and baseline implementations are available at \href{https://github.com/Star-Learning/AirQualityBench}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [333] [XDecomposer: Learning Prior-Free Set Decomposition for Multiphase X-ray Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05866)
*Hanyu Gao,Bin Cao,Yunyue Su,Tong-Yi Zhang,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了XDecomposer框架，首次实现了无需先验信息的混合物XRD图谱分解与物相鉴定，解决了多相粉末衍射分析这一长期瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的合成过程常产生复杂的混合物，而现有的基于表示的晶体检索和生成方法主要假设单相输入，在多相环境中失效，导致多相粉末X射线衍射(PXRD)分析仍是结构鉴定的基本瓶颈。

Method: 将多相衍射分析形式化为集合预测问题，模型在统一架构中推断无序的相分辨组分集合、混合比例和对应的结构表示。采用相位查询驱动的分解机制，结合衍射一致的物理重建，在保持晶体学保真度的同时实现准确的源分离。

Result: 在模拟和实验数据集上的广泛实验表明，XDecomposer显著提高了重建精度和相识别能力，并在各种化学系统中保持对未见混合物的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 为数据驱动的、源解析的多相XRD分析提供了实用途径，减少了对先验引导的迭代相匹配的长期依赖。

Abstract: Multiphase powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) analysis remains a fundamental bottleneck in structure identification, as real-world synthesis often produces complex mixtures whose constituent phases (components) cannot be reliably disentangled. While recent advances in representation-based crystal retrieval and generation suggest the possibility of inferring structures directly from PXRD, existing approaches largely assume single-phase inputs and break down in multiphase settings. Here, we present XDecomposer, a prior-free framework for joint decomposition and identification of multiphase XRD patterns without requiring candidate phase lists, structural templates, or prior knowledge of phase number. We formulate multiphase diffraction analysis as a set prediction problem, where the model infers an unordered set of phase-resolved components, their mixture proportions, and corresponding structural representations within a unified architecture. A phase-query-driven decomposition mechanism, together with diffraction-consistent physical reconstruction, enables accurate source separation while preserving crystallographic fidelity. Extensive experiments on both simulated and experimental datasets show that XDecomposer substantially improves reconstruction accuracy and phase identification across diverse chemical systems, while maintaining strong generalization to unseen mixtures. These results provide a practical route toward data-driven, source-resolved multiphase XRD analysis and reduce long-standing dependence on prior-guided iteratively phase matching. The code is openly available at https://github.com/Licht0812/XDecomposer

</details>


### [334] [Agentic, Context-Aware Risk Intelligence in the Internet of Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05878)
*Basel Magableh,OmniRisk Research*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向价值互联网(IoV)的组合风险原语架构，包含预测引擎、Bittensor验证子网、情绪融合引擎、智能体引擎和API风险场景引擎五个核心组件，并通过Solana链上的流动性压力实验和预测路由器校准实验验证了其可部署性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 价值互联网(IoV)是一个异构、部分可信的网络环境，其主要边际风险是复合风险（包括路由风险、情绪风险、流动性风险和系统政策承诺风险），而非单一链的属性问题。现有风险原语无法有效应对这一复合风险场景，因此需要设计一种新的风险原语架构来适应这种机制。

Method: 提出了由五个引擎组成的组合风险原语架构：(1)价格、流动性、波动率和路由健康的预测引擎；(2)去中心化并对预测输出进行经济评分的Bittensor验证子网；(3)融合文本、链上流动和灰色文献信息的情绪融合引擎；(4)在宪法约束和角色约束下行动的智能体引擎；(5)将预测转换为预承诺行动程序的API风险和场景引擎，采用蒙特卡洛场景生成方法。

Result: 通过两个实证研究验证了架构的有效性：在Solana上进行的27小时政策约束流动性压力响应实验，以及报告了明确的类别不平衡诚实的168小时预测路由器校准弧。验证者损失分解已形式化表述且具有可证伪性。案例研究表明该架构具有可部署性。

Conclusion: 所提出的五引擎组合风险原语架构能够有效应对价值互联网中的复合风险问题，实验验证了其在实际部署中的可行性，为IoV环境下的风险管理提供了新的技术路径和理论框架。

Abstract: The Internet of Value (IoV) is a heterogeneous, partially-trusted network in which the dominant marginal risk is composite (route, sentiment, liquidity, and the policy a system is willing to commit to) rather than a property of any single chain. We argue that a risk primitive adequate for this regime is a composition of five engines: a prediction engine over price, liquidity, volatility, and route health; a Bittensor verification subnet that decentralises and economically scores prediction outputs; a sentiment-fusion engine over text, on-chain flow, and grey-literature feeds; an agentic engine under constitutional, role-bound action constraints; and an API-risk and scenario engine that converts forecasts into pre-committed action programs in the sense of Monte-Carlo scenario generation. We anchor the architecture in two empirical artefacts: a 27-hour policy-constrained liquidity stress-response experiment on Solana, and a 168-hour prediction-router calibration arc reported with explicit class-imbalance honesty. The case study supports deployability; the validator-loss decomposition is stated formally and is falsifiable.

</details>


### [335] [Null Space Constrained Contrastive Visual Forgetting for MLLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05909)
*Yuhang Wang,Zhenxing Niu,Haoxuan Ji,Guangyu He,Linlin Zhang,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的机器遗忘方法，通过冻结LLM骨干网络并微调视觉模块，利用对比视觉遗忘机制和零空间约束，在遗忘目标视觉知识的同时有效保留非目标视觉知识和所有文本知识，并支持持续遗忘场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘的核心挑战在于平衡目标知识的移除与非目标知识的保留。在多模态大语言模型中，这一挑战更为严峻，因为视觉与文本模态的知识紧密交织，传统方法难以在遗忘特定视觉知识时不损害其他知识。

Method: 提出了一种针对MLLM的遗忘框架：1) 冻结LLM骨干网络，仅通过微调视觉模块实现遗忘；2) 设计对比视觉遗忘（CVF）机制，将目标视觉概念的表示引导至特征空间中的特定区域，以分离目标与非目标视觉知识；3) 识别保留知识的零空间，将遗忘过程约束在该空间内，以减轻对保留知识的损害；4) 将方法扩展至持续遗忘场景，处理顺序到达的遗忘请求。

Result: 在多个多样化基准测试上的广泛实验表明，该方法在有效遗忘目标视觉知识与稳健保留非目标视觉知识及文本知识之间取得了强有力的平衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功解决了MLLM中机器遗忘的模态纠缠问题，通过对比遗忘机制和零空间约束实现了高效的视觉知识遗忘与知识保留的平衡，并具备持续遗忘能力，为多模态模型的遗忘任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The core challenge of machine unlearning is to strike a balance between target knowledge removal and non-target knowledge retention. In the context of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), this challenge becomes even more pronounced, as knowledge is further divided into visual and textual modalities that are tightly intertwined. In this paper, we introduce an MLLM unlearning approach that aims to forget target visual knowledge while preserving non-target visual knowledge and all textual knowledge. Specifically, we freeze the LLM backbone and achieve unlearning by fine-tuning the visual module. First, we propose a Contrastive Visual Forgetting (CVF) mechanism to separate target visual knowledge from retained visual knowledge, guiding the representations of target visual concepts toward appropriate regions in the feature space. Second, we identify the null space associated with retained knowledge and constrain the unlearning process within this space, thereby significantly mitigating degradation in knowledge retention. Third, beyond static unlearning scenarios, we extend our approach to continual unlearning, where forgetting requests arrive sequentially. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves a strong balance between effective forgetting and robust knowledge retention.

</details>


### [336] [PREFER: Personalized Review Summarization with Online Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05911)
*Millend Roy,Agostino Capponi,Vineet Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种在线学习框架，通过迭代反馈机制生成个性化产品评论摘要，解决了现有系统无法适应用户偏好差异和演变的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电商评论摘要系统生成通用静态摘要，忽略了用户对产品特性关注点的差异及其随交互时间的演变，且评论数量庞大导致用户难以获取相关信息。

Method: 提出在线学习框架，针对未知潜在偏好生成个性化摘要，通过迭代地从生成的摘要中直接获取用户反馈来逐步优化对用户偏好的理解。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews'23数据集上的控制仿真实验表明，在线偏好学习提高了摘要与目标用户兴趣的契合度，同时保持了摘要质量。

Conclusion: 该在线学习框架能够有效学习用户潜在偏好，实现个性化评论摘要生成，为电商平台的用户体验改进提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Product reviews significantly influence purchasing decisions on e-commerce platforms. However, the sheer volume of reviews can overwhelm users, obscuring the information most relevant to their specific needs. Current e-commerce summarization systems typically produce generic, static summaries that fail to account for the fact that (i) different users care about different product characteristics, and (ii) these preferences may evolve with interactions. To address the challenge of unknown latent preferences, we propose an online learning framework that generates personalized summaries for each user. Our system iteratively refines its understanding of user preferences by incorporating feedback directly from the generated summaries over time. We provide a case study using the Amazon Reviews'23 dataset, showing in controlled simulations that online preference learning improves alignment with target user interests while maintaining summary quality.

</details>


### [337] [Wisteria: A Unified Multi-Scale Feature Learning Framework for DNA Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05913)
*Weihua Wang,Haoji Li,Feilong Bao,Lei Yang,Guanglai Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Wisteria模型，通过整合多尺度特征学习，有效解决了DNA语言模型中局部模体与全局依赖关系的联合建模问题，在下游任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DNA语言模型主要关注长距离token交互，但往往忽视了局部模体与全局依赖之间的相互作用。作者旨在解决这一局限性，提出一个能统一建模局部和全局特征的框架，以更好地破译基因组的调控规律和语义。

Method: 提出了Wisteria模型，在Mamba架构基础上进行增强。具体包括：引入门控空洞卷积捕捉局部模体和调控模式；使用门控多层感知机精细化全局依赖；引入基于傅里叶的注意力机制，支持频域建模、周期性扩展和长度泛化。

Result: 在包含短程和长程依赖的四种实验设置下，Wisteria在下游基准测试中表现出强大的性能，优于现有的竞争性DNA语言模型基线。

Conclusion: Wisteria成功地在统一框架内结合了局部和全局依赖建模，证明了多尺度特征学习在基因组序列分析中的有效性。

Abstract: DNA language model aims to decipher the regulatory grammar and semantic of genomes by capturing long range dependencies in DNA sequences. Existing methods emphasize long range token interactions but often ignore the interplay between local motifs and global dependencies. In this paper, we propose Wisteria, a genomic language model that integrates multi scale feature learning within a unified framework for DNA sequence. Specifically, Wisteria augments the Mamba based architecture with gated dilated convolutions to capture local motifs and regulatory patterns, while gated multilayer perceptrons refine global dependencies. We further introduce a Fourier based attention mechanism to support frequency domain modeling, periodic extension and length generalization. Across four experimental settings with both short and long range dependencies, Wisteria demonstrates strong performance on downstream benchmarks against competitive DNA language model baselines. These results indicate that Wisteria effectively unifies local and global dependency modeling for multi scale genomic sequence analysis.

</details>


### [338] [Intentmaking and Sensemaking: Human Interaction with AI-Guided Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05921)
*Alex Bäuerle,Adam Connors,Alexander Novikov,Adam Zsolt Wagner,Ngân Vũ,Fernanda Viegas,Martin Wattenberg,Lucas Dixon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过专家用户研究，识别出“意图构建”（intentmaking）这一AI辅助科学发现的关键工作流程，强调用户与AI系统进行目标迭代定义的重要性，主张设计超越问答模式的协作式AI工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人工智能为科学发现提供了强大工具，但有效利用这些系统所需的交互范式仍未被充分探索，现有的问答模式难以满足复杂科研工作的需求。

Method: 开展了一项形成性用户研究，邀请11位专家数学家使用进化编码智能体AlphaEvolve解决其专业领域的难题，并对交互过程进行分析与表征。

Result: 识别并刻画了“意图构建”这一独特工作流程，即用户通过与系统主动交互来发现、定义和优化实验目标的迭代过程；将其构建为“感知构建”（sensemaking）的自然延伸，提出了用户在探索过程中反复进行意图构建与感知构建的循环模型。

Conclusion: 研究结果建议科学发现AI工具的设计应超越现有的问答模式，将其视为协作工具而非不透明的黑盒助手，从而更好地支持用户的迭代式目标探索与结果理解。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence offers powerful new tools for scientific discovery, but the interaction paradigms required to effectively harness these systems remain underexplored. In this paper, we present findings from a formative user study with 11 expert mathematicians who used AlphaEvolve, an evolutionary coding agent, to tackle advanced problems in their fields of expertise. We identify and characterize a distinct workflow we term intentmaking, the iterative process of discovering, defining, and refining one's experimental goals through active system interaction. We frame this as a natural extension to sensemaking, the cognitive process of building an understanding of complex or novel data. We suggest that users enter a cycle of intentmaking (defining and updating their experiment) and sensemaking (interpreting the results) which repeats many times during the course of an investigation. Our documentation of these themes suggests an approach to designing AI tools for scientific discovery that goes beyond the existing question/answer model of many current systems, treating them as collaborative instruments rather than opaque black-box assistants.

</details>


### [339] [Which Are the Low-Resource Languages of the Semantic Web?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05929)
*Ndeye-Emilie Mbengue,Pierre Monnin,Miguel Couceiro,Fabien Gandon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对链接开放数据知识图谱（LOD KGs）中缺乏低资源语言定量定义的问题，提出了一个多层次语言分类方法论，并基于DBpedia、BabelNet和Wikidata给出了低、中、高资源语言的形式化定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴数字技术加剧了高资源与低资源语言在开放获取数据（OAD）之间的差距，导致许多社区被排除在全球数字化转型之外。多语言链接开放数据知识图谱（LOD KGs）具备通过跨语言迁移缩小这一差距的潜力，但在该领域中，尚未建立明确的低资源语言定量定义。

Method: 提出一种分析方法，研究语言在LOD KGs中的分布情况。基于三个主要知识图谱（DBpedia、BabelNet和Wikidata），构建了一个初步的多层次语言分类体系，并据此给出了低、中、高资源语言的形式化定义。

Result: 建立了一个基于DBpedia、BabelNet和Wikidata的初步多层次语言分类体系，并利用该分类成功提出了低资源、中等资源和高资源语言的形式化定义，该定义可用于后续筛选跨语言迁移的候选语言。

Conclusion: 该研究为LOD KGs语境下的语言资源分类提供了形式化基础，有助于识别适合进行跨语言迁移的语言对，从而在未来缓解高资源与低资源语言之间的数字鸿沟。

Abstract: Emerging digital technologies are exacerbating the existing divide in Open Access Data (OAD) between high-and low-resource languages, excluding many communities from the global digital transformation. Multilingual Linked Open Data Knowledge Graphs (LOD KGs) could contribute to mitigating this divide through cross-lingual transfer; however, no clear quantitative definition of low-resource languages has yet been established in the context of LOD KGs. In this poster, we present a methodology to analyze the distribution of languages across LOD KGs and propose a preliminary multi-level categorization based on DBpedia, BabelNet, and Wikidata. This categorization is leveraged to bring a formal definition of low-, high-, and medium-resource languages that could be later leveraged to select cross-lingual transfer candidates.

</details>


### [340] [In Data or Invisible: Toward a Better Digital Representation of Low-Resource Languages with Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05931)
*Ndeye-Emilie Mbengue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该博士提案旨在解决链接开放数据知识图谱中高资源语言与低资源语言之间的覆盖差距，通过分析语言分布特征，研究基于语言邻近性和类比推理的跨语言迁移策略，以提升多语言知识图谱补全性能和语言覆盖度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴数字技术加剧了开放获取数据中高资源语言与低资源语言之间的现有鸿沟，导致许多社区被排除在全球数字化转型之外，亟需解决链接开放数据知识图谱中语言覆盖不平衡的问题。

Method: 首先识别描述LOD中语言分布的关键变量（如各语言版维基百科文章数量、LOD知识图谱中语言标签实体数量），并在DBpedia、BabelNet和Wikidata三大多语言LOD知识图谱上进行分析；进而研究跨语言迁移候选选择对多语言知识图谱补全任务的影响，探索基于语言邻近性和语言间对齐标注可用性的策略，以及利用语言相似性/差异性进行类比推理以识别跨语言对应关系。

Result: 对三大主要多语言LOD知识图谱的语言表征和分布提供了分析洞察；提出了通过语言邻近性和类比推理来改善知识图谱补全性能并增强LOD语言覆盖的研究计划。

Conclusion: 本研究有望通过跨语言迁移和类比推理方法提升多语言知识图谱补全的效果，从而缩小LOD中的语言覆盖差距，促进低资源语言社区参与全球数字化转型。

Abstract: Emerging digital technologies are exacerbating the existing divide in Open Access Data (OAD) between high-and low-resource languages, excluding many communities from participating in the global digital transformation. In this PhD proposal, we aim to address this gap, focusing on the language coverage of Linked Open Data knowledge graphs (LOD KGs). First, we identify key variables that characterize language distribution in LOD, including the number of Wikipedia articles per language edition and the number of language-tagged entities in LOD KGs. These variables are analyzed across three major multilingual LOD KGs, DBpedia, BabelNet, and Wikidata, providing insights into the representation and distribution of languages within LOD. Building on this analysis, we intend to study the impact of cross-lingual transfer candidate selection on the task of multilingual KG completion. In particular, we plan to investigate strategies based on linguistic proximity and the availability of curated annotated alignments between languages. Language proximity also motivates us to explore the benefits of analogical reasoning that relies on (dis)similarities and has not yet been investigated to identify correspondences across languages to improve KG completion performance and enhance language coverage in LOD.

</details>


### [341] [ICU-Bench:Benchmarking Continual Unlearning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05938)
*Yuhang Wang,Wenjie Mei,Junkai Zhang,Guangyu He,Zhenxing Niu,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ICU-Bench，这是首个面向多模态大语言模型的持续遗忘基准，包含医疗报告和劳动合同等隐私敏感文档数据，揭示了现有遗忘方法在持续隐私删除场景下的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在大规模数据集上的训练引发了严重的隐私问题，使得机器遗忘技术变得必要。然而现有基准主要关注静态或短序列设置，无法有效评估实际部署中持续的隐私删除请求。

Method: 构建了ICU-Bench持续多模态遗忘基准，包含来自医疗报告和劳动合同两个文档域的1000个隐私敏感档案、9500张图像、16000个问答对和100个遗忘任务。同时引入了新的持续遗忘评估指标，用于综合分析遗忘有效性、历史遗忘保留、效用保持和稳定性。

Result: 通过在ICU-Bench上对代表性遗忘方法进行大量实验，发现现有方法在持续设置中普遍表现不佳，在平衡遗忘质量、效用保持和长任务序列可扩展性方面存在明显局限。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明迫切需要专门为持续隐私删除设计的多模态遗忘方法，现有方法无法满足实际部署中持续隐私删除的需求。

Abstract: Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress across many domains, their training on large-scale multimodal datasets raises serious privacy concerns, making effective machine unlearning increasingly necessary. However, existing benchmarks mainly focus on static or short-sequence settings, offering limited support for evaluating continual privacy deletion requests in realistic deployments. To bridge this gap, we introduce ICU-Bench, a continual multimodal unlearning benchmark built on privacy-critical document data. ICU-Bench contains 1,000 privacy-sensitive profiles from two document domains, medical reports and labor contracts, with 9,500 images, 16,000 question-answer pairs, and 100 forget tasks. Additionally, new continual unlearning metrics are introduced, facilitating a comprehensive analysis of forgetting effectiveness, historical forgetting preservation, retained utility, and stability throughout the continual unlearning process. Through extensive experiments with representative unlearning methods on ICU-Bench, we show that existing methods generally struggle in continual settings and exhibit clear limitations in balancing forgetting quality, utility preservation, and scalability over long task sequences. These findings highlight the need for multimodal unlearning methods explicitly designed for continual privacy deletion.

</details>


### [342] [MAS-Algorithm: A Workflow for Solving Algorithmic Programming Problems with a Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05949)
*Yuliang Xu,Xiang Xu,Yao Wan,Hu Wei,Tong Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MAS-Algorithm，一种受竞技程序员和算法工程师实践启发的多智能体工作流框架，用于算法问题求解。该框架将端到端求解过程分解为模块化阶段，在自建基准测试上实现了6.48%的平均通过率提升，远超参数高效微调的0.89%提升，并在LiveCodeBench-Pro上获得4.72%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的算法问题求解方法主要依赖模型中心策略（如架构修改和数据扩展），成本高昂且可解释性有限；而使用外部工具或提示技术的方法通常零散且缺乏统一框架。因此需要一种既能实现结构化推理，又能集成工具并灵活协调的系统性解决方案。

Method: 提出MAS-Algorithm，一种系统性的多智能体工作流框架。该框架受竞技程序员和算法工程师的实践启发，将端到端求解过程分解为模块化阶段，实现了结构化推理、工具集成和智能体间的灵活协调。设计强调严谨性和可扩展性，使其能够泛化到多种问题类型。

Result: 在自建基准测试上，多个Qwen系列模型均获得一致的改进，平均通过率提升6.48%，而相同数据上的参数高效微调仅带来0.89%的边际改进。在LiveCodeBench-Pro上获得4.72%的提升，并在其他准确性和效率指标上取得一致改进。消融研究表明单个智能体可贡献高达27.7%的改进。

Conclusion: MAS-Algorithm展示了在推进AI驱动的算法推理方面的强大潜力。该框架不仅带来了显著的性能提升，还通过全面分析深入理解了工作流内的推理过程，包括错误模式和跨场景行为，为AI算法推理领域提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Algorithmic problem solving serves as a rigorous testbed for evaluating structured reasoning in AI coding systems, as it directly reflects a model's ability to perform structured reasoning in complex scenarios.Existing approaches predominantly rely on model-centric strategies, such as architectural modifications and data scaling, which are costly and offer limited interpretability. Alternative methods leveraging external tools or prompting techniques (e.g., chain-of-thought) are often fragmented and lack a unified framework. In this paper, we propose MAS-Algorithm, a systematic multi-agent workflow for algorithmic problem solving inspired by the practices of competitive programmers and algorithm engineers. Our framework decomposes the end-to-end solving process into modular stages, enabling structured reasoning, tool integration, and flexible coordination among agents. The design emphasizes both rigor and extensibility, allowing it to generalize across diverse problem types.Experimental results on a self-constructed benchmark demonstrate consistent improvements across multiple Qwen series models, achieving an average gain of 6.48% in acceptance rate. In contrast, parameter-efficient fine-tuning on the same data yields only a marginal improvement of 0.89%. We further observe a 4.72% gain on LiveCodeBench-Pro, along with consistent improvements across additional accuracy and efficiency metrics.Beyond performance gains, we conduct comprehensive analyses to better understand the reasoning process within the workflow, including error patterns and cross-scenario behaviors. We further perform customized replacement and ablation studies to explore the upper bound of the framework, showing that individual agents can contribute improvements of up to 27.7%. These results highlight the strong potential of MAS-Algorithm for advancing AI-driven algorithmic reasoning.

</details>


### [343] [HaM-World: Soft-Hamiltonian World Models with Selective Memory for Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05951)
*Haoyun Tang,Haodong Cui,Keyao Xu,Kun Wang,Zhandong Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HaM-World结构化世界模型，通过引入历史条件记忆和几何组织结构解决长视野规划不稳定问题，在DeepMind Control Suite任务上达到最高Avg. AUC (117.9, +9.5%)，在12种OOD扰动下均取得最高回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在规划视野增长或动力学分布偏移时，想象的rollout变得不稳定。作者认为这种不稳定性反映了规划器面向的潜在状态中缺失两个结构：用于近似马尔可夫完备性的历史条件记忆，以及分离配置、动量和任务语义的几何组织结构。

Method: 提出HaM-World (HMW)结构化世界模型，将潜在状态分解为规范(q, p)子空间和上下文子空间c。使用Mamba选择性状态空间记忆作为历史条件输入。(q, p)通过能量导出的哈密顿向量场加可学习残差/控制动力学演化，c捕获语义、耗散和非保守因素。这为规划器提供单一潜在状态，用于动力学预测、奖励/价值估计、想象rollout和CEM动作搜索。

Result: 在四个DeepMind Control Suite任务上达到最高Avg. AUC (117.9, +9.5%)；长视野rollout误差降至强基准模型的45%；在{3,5,7} MSE单元中赢得11/12。在涵盖动力学偏移、动作延迟和观测掩码的12种OOD扰动下，每个条件均达到最高回报，Finger Spin平均OOD回报增益10.2%，Reacher Easy为13.6%。机制诊断显示有界的无动作哈密顿能量漂移、策略rollout下的结构化能量变化，以及一致的控制诱导能量转移。

Conclusion: HaM-World通过引入历史条件记忆和几何组织结构（规范-上下文分解）成功解决了世界模型的长视野规划不稳定问题。实验结果表明该方法在标准任务和OOD条件下均显著优于基线，机制诊断验证了软哈密顿动力学设计的有效性，证明了结构化潜在表示对模型规划的重要性。

Abstract: World models enable model-based planning through learned latent dynamics, but imagined rollouts become unstable as the planning horizon grows or the dynamics distribution shifts. We argue that this instability reflects two missing structures in planner-facing latents: history-conditioned memory for approximate Markov completeness, and geometric organization that separates configuration, momentum, and task semantics. We propose HaM-World (HMW), a structured world model that decomposes the latent state into a canonical (q, p) subspace and a context subspace c, while using Mamba selective state-space memory as the history-conditioned input to the same latent dynamics. Within this interface, (q, p) evolves through an energy-derived Hamiltonian vector field plus learnable residual/control dynamics, while c captures semantic, dissipative, and non-conservative factors. This gives the planner a single latent state shared by dynamics prediction, reward/value estimation, imagined rollouts, and CEM action search. On four DeepMind Control Suite tasks, HaM-World reaches the highest Avg. AUC (117.9, +9.5%), reduces long-horizon rollout error to 45% of a strong baseline model, and wins 11/12 k in {3,5,7} MSE cells. Under 12 OOD perturbations spanning dynamics shifts, action delay, and observation masking, HaM-World achieves the highest return in every condition, with average OOD-return gains of 10.2% on Finger Spin and 13.6% on Reacher Easy. Mechanism diagnostics further show bounded action-free Hamiltonian-energy drift, structured energy variation under policy rollouts, and coherent control-induced energy transfer, supporting the intended Soft-Hamiltonian dynamics design.

</details>


### [344] [Temporal Smoothness Doubly Robust Learning for Debiased Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05958)
*Peilin Zhan,Wei Chen,Weilin Chen,Shuyi Pan,Ruichu Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TSDR框架，通过双重稳健估计解决知识追踪中的选择偏差问题，并引入时间平滑性正则化来降低方差，在多个基准测试中持续提升现有最先进方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识追踪(KT)依赖于选择性观测的教育日志数据，而练习推荐和学生选择的非随机特性会导致严重的选择偏差。现有大多数KT方法忽略此问题，在观测日志上使用标准经验风险进行训练，导致有偏的掌握度估计并在后续推荐中累积误差。

Method: 提出时间平滑双重稳健(TSDR)框架：1) 引入双重稳健(DR)公式，整合倾向模型和误差插补模型，理论上保证只要任一模型准确即可实现无偏性；2) 推导泛化界明确刻画估计器方差的影响，识别时间平滑性为控制方差的关键因素；3) 通过平滑正则化联合优化KT预测器和插补模型，在保持DR无偏性保证的同时有效降低方差。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准测试上的实验表明，TSDR能够持续增强各种最先进的KT骨干模型的性能，验证了有原则的偏差校正在KT中的重要作用。

Conclusion: 本文成功将因果推断中的双重稳健估计引入知识追踪领域，通过理论分析识别并解决了序列设置下估计器方差累积导致的训练不稳定问题，为KT中的偏差校正提供了理论保证和实用解决方案。

Abstract: Knowledge Tracing (KT) is fundamental to intelligent education systems, yet relies on educational logs that are selectively observed. The non-random nature of exercise recommendations and student choices inevitably induces severe selection bias. Most existing KT methods neglect this issue, training on observed logs using standard empirical risk, which yields biased mastery estimates and accumulates errors in subsequent recommendations. To address this, we introduce a doubly robust (DR) formulation for KT that integrates a propensity model with an error imputation model, theoretically guaranteeing unbiasedness if either model is accurate. Beyond unbiasedness, in the sequential setting of KT, we identify that the estimator's performance is compromised by variance-dependent stochastic deviations that accumulate over time, thereby causing training instability and limiting performance. To mitigate this, we derive a generalization bound that explicitly characterizes the impact of estimator variance and identifies temporal smoothness as a key factor in controlling it. Building on these theoretical insights, we propose the Temporal Smoothness Doubly Robust (TSDR) framework. TSDR jointly optimizes the KT predictor and the imputation model with a smoothness regularizer, effectively reducing variance while preserving the unbiasedness guarantee of DR. Experiments on multiple real-world benchmarks demonstrate that TSDR consistently enhances various state-of-the-art KT backbones, underscoring the vital role of principled bias correction in KT.

</details>


### [345] [TheraAgent: Self-Improving Therapeutic Agent for Precise and Comprehensive Treatment Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05963)
*Junkai Li,Yunghwei Lai,Tianyi Zhu,Zheng Long Lee,Weizhi Ma,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TheraAgent框架，通过迭代生成-评判-优化的流程替代单次生成，显著提升了治疗方案的准确性、完整性和安全性，并在实验中取得了最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医疗大语言模型主要依赖单次输出，缺乏显式的验证过程，导致生成的治疗方案粗糙、不完整且存在潜在的安全风险，无法满足制定治疗计划所需的复杂推理与优化需求。

Method: 提出了TheraAgent智能体框架，采用迭代的“生成-评判-优化”流程模拟人类专家的推理过程。同时引入了TheraJudge评估模块作为评判组件，在推理循环中强制执行临床标准，将粗糙的草案逐步转化为精确、全面且安全的治疗方案。

Result: 在HealthBench基准测试中达到了最优（SOTA）性能，在准确性和完整性指标上领先。在专家评估中，针对医生的胜率达到86%，在针对性（Targeting）和伤害控制（Harm Control）方面表现优越。TheraJudge与HealthBench评估结果的高度一致性验证了框架的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了将单次生成转变为迭代优化流程的有效性，能够生成更高质量的治疗方案，且其集成的评估模块具有高度可靠性，为临床决策支持提供了更安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Formulating a treatment plan is inherently a complex reasoning and refinement task rather than a simple generation problem. However, existing large language models (LLMs) mainly rely on one-shot output without explicit verification, which may result in rough, incomplete, and potentially unsafe treatment plans. To address these limitations, we propose TheraAgent, an agentic framework that replaces one-shot generation with an iterative generate-judge-refine pipeline. By mirroring the actual reasoning process of human experts who iteratively revise treatment plans, our framework progressively transforms coarse and incomplete drafts into precise, comprehensive, and safer therapeutic regimens. To facilitate the critical judge component, we introduce TheraJudge, a treatment-specific evaluation module integrated into the inference loop to enforce clinical standards. Experiments show TheraAgent achieves state-of-the-art results on HealthBench, leading in Accuracy and Completeness. In expert evaluations, it attains an 86% win rate against physicians, with superior Targeting and Harm Control. Moreover, the highly agreement between TheraJudge and HealthBench evaluations confirms the reliability of our framework.

</details>


### [346] [BehaviorGuard: Online Backdoor Defense for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05977)
*Yinbo Yu,Xueyu Yin,Jiadai Wang,Chunwei Tian,Sai Xu,Qi Zhu,Daoqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BehaviorGuard，首个针对深度强化学习的在线后门检测与防御框架，通过监测动作分布的行为漂移，在无需逆向工程触发器或高昂微调的情况下，实现了对单智能体和多智能体场景的有效防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度强化学习后门防御方法依赖于奖励异常来逆向工程触发器，并使用模型微调来移除后门。然而，复杂的触发器模式破坏了这些方法的鲁棒性，且微调成本高昂，限制了实际应用价值。为了解决这些局限性，作者将防御重心从触发器本身转移到了与触发器无关的后门输出行为上。

Method: 作者发现无论攻击类型如何，被植入后门的策略都会导致动作分布发生一致的偏移，以确保后门被可靠激活，这种变化在高分位数区域和分布尾部留下了可检测的痕迹。基于此，论文设计了一种新的度量标准来捕捉动作分布中的行为漂移，从而在运行时识别并抑制后门动作。该方法不依赖于触发器的逆向工程或模型微调。

Result: BehaviorGuard在多种基准测试和不同的后门攻击场景下进行了评估。实验结果表明，该方法在有效性和效率方面均持续优于现有的先验方法。这是首个能够同时应对单智能体和多智能体深度强化学习后门攻击的在线防御方案。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了通过关注动作分布的行为漂移，可以实现对深度强化学习后门的有效在线检测与缓解。BehaviorGuard框架为DRL系统提供了一种高效、鲁棒且实用的防御手段，克服了传统方法对复杂触发器模式敏感和计算成本高的问题。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a serious threat to deep reinforcement learning (DRL). Current defenses typically rely on reward anomalies to reverse-engineer triggers and model finetuning to remove backdoors. However, complex trigger patterns undermine their robustness, and fine-tuning entails high costs, limiting practical utility. Therefore, we shift defense concerns to trigger-agnostic backdoor output behaviors and propose BehaviorGuard, an online behavior-based backdoor detection and mitigation framework for DRL. Specifically, we find that regardless of attacks, backdoored policies induce consistent shifts in action distributions to ensure reliable activation, leaving detectable traces in high-quantile regions and distribution tails, even in the absence of triggers. Based on this, we design a novel metric that captures behavioral drift in action distributions to identify and suppress backdoor actions at runtime. To our knowledge, this is the first online backdoor defense that counters attacks both in single- and multi-agent DRL. Evaluated across diverse benchmarks with different backdoor attacks, BehaviorGuard consistently surpasses prior methods in both efficacy and efficiency.

</details>


### [347] [TACT: Mitigating Overthinking and Overacting in Coding Agents via Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05980)
*Yuan Sui,Yulin Chen,Yibo Li,Xue Jiang,Yufei He,Yihong Dong,Xiaoxin He,Tianyu Gao,Bryan Hooi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TACT方法，通过识别和调控语言模型代理在长轨迹任务中的“代理漂移”现象（包括过度思考和过度行动两种失效模式），在残差流层面进行干预，从而提升代理在软件工程任务中的表现和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型代理在处理复杂软件工程任务的长轨迹过程中，经常会出现性能下降的现象（定义为“代理漂移”），主要表现为两种重复性失效模式：过度思考（反复推理已有信息）和过度行动（在不整合新观察的情况下频繁调用工具），这严重影响了代理的可靠性和效率。

Method: 作者提出TACT（Think-Act Calibration via activation Steering）方法：首先将轨迹步骤标注为过度思考、过度行动或校准状态，发现这些状态的隐藏表示在残差流中沿两个“漂移轴”线性可分；然后在测试时将激活投影到这些轴上，并将漂移的激活拉回到校准区域。

Result: 实验表明，TACT在SWE-bench Verified、Terminal-Bench 2.0和CLAW-Eval基准测试中均优于未引导的基线：在Qwen3.5-27B上平均解决率提升+5.8个百分点，在Gemma-4-26B-A4B-it上提升+4.8个百分点，同时将解决步骤减少高达26%。

Conclusion: 该研究将代理漂移定义为残差流中可引导的方向，证明了TACT作为可靠长视距代理的有效控制手段，为提升语言模型代理在复杂任务中的稳定性提供了新思路。

Abstract: When language model agents tackle complex software engineering tasks, they often degrade over long trajectories, which we define as *agent drift*. We focus on two recurring failure modes *overthinking* and *overacting*, i.e., where the agent repeatedly reasons over information it already has, and where it issues tool calls without integrating recent observations or acquiring new evidence. In this paper, we introduce TACT (Think-Act Calibration via activation Steering), to detect and mitigate agent drift in the residual stream before it surfaces as a behavioral failure. In specific, we label trajectory steps as overthinking, overacting, or calibrated, and find that their hidden states can separate linearly along two *drift axes*, pointing from calibrated behavior toward each failure mode (AUC $\approx$ 0.9). To mitigate agent drift, we project each step's activation onto these axes at test time and pull drifted ones back toward the calibrated region. Experiments show that TACT outperforms unsteered baselines across SWE-bench Verified, Terminal-Bench 2.0, and CLAW-Eval, lifting average resolve rate by $+5.8$ pp on Qwen3.5-27B and $+4.8$ pp on Gemma-4-26B-A4B-it while cutting steps-to-resolve by up to $26\%$. These gains frame agent drift as a steerable direction in the residual stream, and position TACT as a viable handle for reliable long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [348] [Strat-LLM: Stratified Strategy Alignment for LLM-based Stock Trading with Real-time Multi-Source Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06024)
*Wenliang Huang,Zengyi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Strat-LLM框架，基于分层策略对齐原则评估LLM交易代理。在A股和美股市场的实盘测试中发现：推理型模型适合自由模式，标准模型需严格模式风控；对齐效用具有市场状态依赖性；中等规模模型表现最佳；标准LLM易陷入高胜率陷阱，需通过深度推理或严格外部约束缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM交易代理基准测试往往忽视了架构推理能力与策略一致性之间的相互作用。随着大语言模型逐渐演变为自主交易代理，亟需一个能够消除前视偏差、整合多源异构数据的框架来系统评估不同架构模型在实盘交易中的表现与风险特征。

Method: 提出Strat-LLM框架，基于分层策略对齐原则，在2025年全年的实盘前向环境中运行。框架整合异构数据（时序价格、实时新闻、年度报告）以消除前视偏差。设计了三种运行模式：Free Mode（自由模式）、Guided Mode（引导模式）和Strict Mode（严格模式），通过压力测试系统评估不同架构和规模的LLM在A股与美股市场的表现。

Result: 在A股和美股市场的大量压力测试中发现：（1）重推理模型在自由模式下通过内部逻辑达到峰值效用，标准模型则需严格模式作为关键风控锚点；（2）对齐效用具有状态依赖性，自由和引导模式在上涨行情中捕捉动量，严格模式在下跌行情中缓解回撤；（3）中等规模模型（35B）在严格约束下表现最佳，超大规模模型（122B）在刚性规则下承受对齐税但在引导模式下获得性能溢价；（4）标准LLM常陷入高胜率陷阱，以牺牲总收益为代价优化小额收益，只能通过深度推理或严格外部约束缓解。

Conclusion: Strat-LLM框架揭示了LLM交易代理中架构推理与策略一致性的复杂关系：模型规模、推理能力与约束模式需根据市场状态动态匹配。中等规模模型在严格约束下具备最优保真度，而推理能力是突破高胜率陷阱的关键。研究为LLM在金融交易领域的实际部署提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are evolving into autonomous trading agents, yet existing benchmarks often overlook the interplay between architectural reasoning and strategy consistency. We propose Strat-LLM, a framework grounded in Stratified Strategy Alignment. Operating in a live-forward setting throughout 2025, it integrates heterogeneous data including sequential prices, real-time news, and annual reports to eliminate look-ahead bias. Extensive stress tests on A-share and U.S. markets reveal: (1) reasoning-heavy models achieve peak utility in Free Mode via internal logic, whereas standard models require Strict Mode as a vital risk anchor; (2) alignment utility is regime-dependent, with Free and Guided modes capturing momentum in uptrending markets, while Strict Mode mitigates drawdowns in downtrends; (3) mid-scale models (35B) show optimal fidelity under strict constraints, whereas ultra-large models (122B) suffer an alignment tax under rigid rules but gain a performance premium in Guided Mode; (4) standard LLMs often fall into a high win-rate trap, optimizing for small gains at the expense of total returns, which can only be mitigated through deep reasoning or strict external guardrails. Project details are available at https://Strat-LLM.github.io.

</details>


### [349] [Pathways to AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06029)
*Gordon Fletcher,Saomai Vu Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从批判性软件研究视角出发，通过分析生成式AI的关键发展路径、决策节点和替代方案，探讨当前商业AI发展的偶然性与可塑性，并对AGI概念提出质疑，最终提出可行的社会技术发展建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI研究往往预设当前AI发展路径的必然性，忽视了社会、政治、经济因素与商业AI发展的紧密关联。作者希望打破这种技术决定论视角，通过批判性分析揭示AGI概念与定义上的问题，并识别发展过程中的关键决策节点与替代可能性。

Method: 采用批判性软件研究视角，围绕五个核心问题展开分析：追溯生成式AI工具的主导发展路径；识别关键杠杆节点与失败路径；比较前沿专有模型、开放权重模型、特定领域主权模型三种基础模型轨迹的差异；分析从关键节点分叉的替代项目的成败原因；基于历史分析提出面向AGI相邻能力的社会技术发展方案。

Result: 揭示了当前主导生成式AI工具的形成并非必然，而是特定社会政治经济条件下的产物；识别出多个关键决策节点（杠杆节点）及其下游影响；发现不同基础模型路径存在显著差异；部分替代项目虽从关键节点分叉，但因各种原因成功、停滞、失败或被吸收；AGI在概念和定义层面存在根本性困难。

Conclusion: 当前商业AI发展路径具有偶然性和可塑性，AGI概念本身存在根本性问题。通过理解历史发展中的关键决策节点和替代可能性，可以设计满足透明性、治理性、福祉和可持续商业模式要求的社会技术发展项目，朝向AGI相邻能力方向推进。

Abstract: Our focus are five related questions that stem from a critical software studies perspective. Underpinning this view is the acknowledged need to avoid assumptions regarding the inevitability of the current situation relating to AI. What we need to see is the closeness of the linkage between current commercial AI development and our prevailing social, political and economic circumstances. This does mean that the perspectives presented here are done so critically and conditionally. Most importantly, Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) is seen as being problematic both conceptually and definitionally. This conditioning of any view regarding AGI does lead the discussion in specific directions and to certain conclusions regarding the future. However, adopting this perspective enables the work to offer some final recommendations. We set out to ask the following questions, 1. What are the critical pathways that produced the current dominant generative AI tools (capabilities, product forms, adoption patterns)? 2. Which decision points acted as leverage nodes (small changes that had large downstream effects), and which dead ends reveal alternative possibilities that did not become dominant? 3. How do pathways differ across three foundational-model trajectories such as the frontier proprietary models, open-weight models or specific domain and sovereign models? 4. Which alternative projects branched from key leverage nodes, what is their current state, and why did some succeed, stall, fail or become absorbed? 5. Based on this analysis, what socio-technical development programmes could plausibly move toward AGI-adjacent capability while meeting requirements for transparency, moderation, wellbeing and sustainable business models?

</details>


### [350] [Novelty-based Tree-of-Thought Search for LLM Reasoning and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06040)
*Leon Hamm,Zlatan Ajanovic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于新颖性（novelty）的剪枝方法来改进思维树推理，通过衡量新节点相对于搜索树中已有节点的独特性来修剪分支，从而在保持性能的同时降低整体token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管思维链、思维树和强化学习等方法提升了LLM在推理和规划任务中的表现，但这些方法仍然脆弱，在许多领域未能达到人类水平，且常面临高昂的时间和token成本。受宽度优先搜索在规划领域成功的启发，作者希望将新颖性概念迁移到语言领域以改进思维树推理。

Method: 提出了一种可度量的新颖性概念，用于描述新生成的节点（思维）相对于搜索树中已见过节点的独特性。该方法通过提示LLM并利用预训练中嵌入的通用知识来估计新颖性，将此度量作为剪枝标准来修剪分支、缩小搜索范围。虽然每个状态需要更多提示，但通过剪枝减少树的整体规模可以降低总token成本。

Result: 在多个基于语言的规划和通用推理基准测试上进行了方法验证和对比，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于新颖性的剪枝方法能够有效改进思维树推理，在保持推理质量的同时降低计算成本，为LLM推理优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Although advances such as chain-of-thought, tree-of-thought or reinforcement learning have improved the performance of LLMs in reasoning and planning tasks, they are still brittle and have not achieved human-level performance in many domains, and often suffer from high time and token costs. Inspired by the success of width-based search in planning, we explore how the concept of novelty can be transferred to language domains and how it can improve tree-of-thought reasoning. A tree of thoughts relies on building possible "paths" of consecutive ideas or thoughts. These are generated by repeatedly prompting an LLM. In our paper, a measurable concept of novelty is proposed that describes the uniqueness of a new node (thought) in comparison to nodes previously seen in the search tree. Novelty is estimated by prompting an LLM and making use of embedded general knowledge from pre-training. This metric can then be used to prune branches and reduce the scope of the search. Although this method introduces more prompts per state, the overall token cost can be reduced by pruning and reducing the overall tree size. This procedure is tested and compared using several benchmarks in language-based planning and general reasoning.

</details>


### [351] [Visual Fingerprints for LLM Generation Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06054)
*Amal Alnouri,Andreas Hinterreiter,Christina Humer,Furui Cheng,Marc Streit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种可视化方法，将大语言模型的输出建模为语言选择的集合，并生成“视觉指纹”来比较不同生成条件下模型行为的分布差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的输出受提示词、系统指令、模型参数和架构等“生成条件”的复杂交互影响，这些条件会以不同方式导致输出偏差。理解不同生成条件如何塑造模型行为对于提示设计和模型评估至关重要，但由于文本生成的随机性和开放性，这一直是一个挑战。

Method: 研究者将模型响应当作语言选择（包括内容、表达和结构）的集合，利用自然语言处理管道提取这些选择，并统计其在重复样本中的分布。随后，将这些分布可视化为“视觉指纹”，从而实现对不同条件下模型倾向的直接、分布级别的比较。

Result: 通过四个使用场景的演示，研究展示了视觉指纹能够揭示大语言模型行为中一致的模式，这些模式通过单个响应或聚合指标很难观察到。

Conclusion: 该方法为理解和比较大语言模型在不同生成条件下的行为模式提供了一种有效的可视化分析手段，能够辅助研究人员发现传统方法难以捕捉的行为偏差和一致性特征。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) outputs arise from complex interactions among prompts, system instructions, model parameters, and architecture. We refer to specific configurations of these factors as generation conditions, each of which can bias outputs in various ways. Understanding how different generation conditions shape model behaviors is essential for tasks such as prompt design and model evaluation, yet it remains challenging due to the stochastic and open-ended nature of text generation. We present an approach to visually compare LLM outputs across generation conditions by modeling responses as collections of linguistic choices, including content, expression, and structure. We extract these choices using natural language processing pipelines and represent their distributions across repeated samples. We then visualize these distributions as visual fingerprints, enabling direct, distribution-level comparison of condition-specific tendencies. Through four usage scenarios, we demonstrate how visual fingerprints reveal consistent patterns in LLM behavior that are difficult to observe through individual responses or aggregate metrics.

</details>


### [352] [Safety Certification is Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06087)
*Oliver Schön,Licio Romao,Sadegh Soudjani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核嵌入的框架，将安全认证视为轨迹数据上的分类问题，直接估计T步安全概率，避免了动态规划递归方法中的误差累积问题，能够处理非马尔可夫动态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用轨迹数据估计转移概率，并通过动态规划（DP）递归计算安全概率。这种递归会导致安全概率估计中的误差累积，随着认证时长T的增加，会导致下界失效，无法提供有意义的安全保证。

Method: 提出了一种核嵌入框架，将安全认证问题转化为轨迹数据上的分类问题。该方法直接估计T步安全概率，无需递归计算。该框架将现有方法（如障碍证书、鲁棒马尔可夫模型）作为特例包含在内，并能够突破其局限性。

Result: 所提出的直接估计器在认证时长增加时保持稳定，且适用于非马尔可夫动态系统。相比之下，基于DP的认证器在这些情况下会失效。仿真实验在神经控制四旋翼飞行器上验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 核嵌入框架成功避免了跨时段的误差累积，实现了对非马尔可夫系统的安全认证，为动态系统安全认证提供了一种更稳健和通用的解决方案。

Abstract: The goal of this paper is certifying safety of dynamical systems subject to uncertainty. Existing approaches use trajectory data to estimate transition probabilities, and compute safety probabilities recursively via dynamic programming (DP). This recursion may lead to compounding errors in the certified safety probability, thus collapsing to a vacuous lower bound for growing horizons $T$. We propose a kernel embedding framework that treats safety certification as a classification problem on trajectory data, directly estimating the $T$-step safety probability without recursion. We show that the framework subsumes well-established approaches from the literature (e.g., barrier certificates, robust Markov models) as special cases, and allows us to go beyond their limitations. As the main consequence, it bypasses compounding error across the horizon and enables certification for systems with non-Markovian dynamics. We demonstrate that direct estimators remain stable independent of the certification horizon and in the non-Markovian setting, whilst DP-based certificates silently go unsound -- confirmed in simulation on a neural-controlled quadrotor.

</details>


### [353] [Shallow Prefill, Deep Decoding: Efficient Long-Context Inference via Layer-Asymmetric KV Visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06105)
*Jungsuk Oh,Hyeseo Jeon,Hyunjune Ji,Kyongmin Kong,Jay-Yoon Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SPEED方法，通过仅在底层保留长上下文提示词的KV状态，在保持模型性能的同时显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解码器语言模型在长上下文推理中成本高昂，因为长提示词需要在预填充阶段处理、缓存每一层，并在自回归解码阶段重复参与注意力计算。现有方法仅降低上层提示词KV状态的存储或构建成本，但未从根本上解决计算开销问题。

Method: 提出"浅层预填充、深度解码"(SPEED)阶段非对称KV可见性策略：仅在模型下层具体化非锚点提示词token的KV状态，而上层完全移除这些预填充token的可见性，同时保持解码阶段token的完整深度。使用最小化的句首锚点机制。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B指令调优研究中，SPEED仅使用75%的层处理预填充token，在OLMES风格基准测试上达到51.2分（基线51.4分），同时TTFT提升33%，TPOT提升22%，在128K上下文时活跃KV内存减少25.0%。层级诊断表明该截断保留了完整深度模型的主要提示词选择和表示稳定区域。

Conclusion: 长上下文提示词token无需始终作为完整深度的KV缓存对象存在，只要解码阶段token保持完整深度，即可在保持模型质量的同时大幅降低推理成本。这一发现为长上下文推理效率优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Long-context inference in decoder-only language models is costly because long prompts are processed during Prefill, cached at every layer, and repeatedly attended to during autoregressive Decode. We introduce \emph{Shallow Prefill, dEEp Decode} (SPEED), a phase-asymmetric KV-visibility policy that materializes non-anchor prompt-token KV states only in lower layers while keeping Decode-phase tokens full-depth. Unlike previous approaches that make upper-layer prompt KV states cheaper to store or construct, SPEED removes prefill tokens from the upper-layer Decode visibility set altogether. With a minimal BoS anchor, this simple change preserves broad benchmark quality while reducing long-context cost. In a controlled Llama-3.1-8B instruction-tuning study, SPEED using only 75\% of layers for prefill tokens reaches 51.2 average score on OLMES-style benchmarks, compared with 51.4 for the full-depth baseline, while improving TTFT by 33\%, TPOT by 22\%, and reducing active KV memory by 25.0\% at 128K context. Layer-wise diagnostics suggest that this cutoff retains the main prompt-selection and representation-stabilization regions of the full-depth model. These results show that long-context prompt tokens need not always persist as full-depth KV-cache objects when Decode-phase tokens remain full-depth.

</details>


### [354] [On Time, Within Budget: Constraint-Driven Online Resource Allocation for Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06110)
*Xinglin Wang,Zishen Liu,Shaoxiong Feng,Peiwen Yuan,Yiwei Li,Jiayi Shi,Yueqi Zhang,Chuyi Tan,Ji Zhang,Boyuan Pan,Yao Hu,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了蒙特卡洛组合规划（MCPP）方法，用于在预算和截止期限约束下，最大化智能体工作流的成功完成概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体系统优化工作多关注平均效率提升，而实际部署常面临严格的预算和时间约束，研究目标从平均效率优化转变为在明确约束下最大化工作流成功完成的概率。

Method: 将问题建模为有限视界随机在线分配问题，提出MCPP方法，通过模拟工作流执行直接估计约束下的完成概率，并在观测到结果后进行重新规划，实现模型和并行样本的动态分配。

Result: 在CodeFlow和ProofFlow数据集上的实验表明，MCPP在广泛的预算-截止期限约束范围内，相比强基线方法一致性地提高了约束下的完成概率。

Conclusion: MCPP作为一种轻量级闭环规划器，能够有效解决约束驱动的智能体工作流资源分配问题，在实际部署场景中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly solve complex user requests by executing orchestrated workflows, where subtasks are assigned to specialized models or tools and coordinated according to their dependencies. While recent work improves agent efficiency by optimizing the performance--cost--latency frontier, real deployments often impose concrete requirements: a workflow must be completed within a specified budget and before a specified deadline. This shifts the goal from average efficiency optimization to maximizing the probability that the entire workflow completes successfully under explicit budget and deadline constraints. We study \emph{constraint-driven online resource allocation for agentic workflows}. Given a dependency-structured workflow and estimates of success rates and generation lengths for each subtask--model pair, the executor allocates models and parallel samples across simultaneously executable subtasks while managing the remaining budget and time. We formulate this setting as a finite-horizon stochastic online allocation problem and propose \emph{Monte Carlo Portfolio Planning} (MCPP), a lightweight closed-loop planner that directly estimates constrained completion probability through simulated workflow executions and replans after observed outcomes. Experiments on CodeFlow and ProofFlow demonstrate that MCPP consistently improves constrained completion probability over strong baselines across a wide range of budget--deadline constraints.

</details>


### [355] [CrossCult-KIBench: A Benchmark for Cross-Cultural Knowledge Insertion in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06115)
*Zhen Zeng,Leijiang Gu,Feng Li,Jing Yu,Zenglin Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了跨文化知识插入任务及评估基准CrossCult-KIBench，并提出了基线方法MCKI。实验发现现有方法难以在有效适应特定文化的同时保持对其他文化的原有表现，揭示了开发文化感知MLLM的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)主要基于英语中心化数据训练，在跨文化场景中经常生成文化不当或错位的响应。为了缓解这一问题，需要一种方法能够使模型适应特定文化语境，同时保持其在其他文化中的原有行为。

Method: 1. 提出跨文化知识插入任务，专注于在保留非目标文化原有行为的同时将模型适配到特定文化语境；2. 构建CrossCult-KIBench评估基准，包含9,800个图像标注案例，涵盖英语、中文和阿拉伯语三种语言-文化群体的49个文化相关视觉场景，支持单次插入和顺序插入两种设置；3. 提出记忆条件知识插入(MCKI)基线方法，该方法利用冻结的MLLM表示从外部记忆中检索相关文化知识，并将匹配条目作为条件提示前置。

Result: 在CrossCult-KIBench上的大量实验表明，当前方法难以在有效文化适应与行为保持之间取得平衡，突显了开发文化感知MLLM的关键挑战。现有方法在实现针对特定文化的有效适应的同时，难以避免对非目标文化产生非预期的副作用。

Conclusion: 本研究强调了开发更具文化适应性和负责任的MLLMs的重要研究方向，为后续研究提供了评估基准和方法论基础，推动了跨文化AI系统的发展。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), trained primarily on English-centric data, frequently generate culturally inappropriate or misaligned responses in cross-cultural settings. To mitigate this, we introduce the task of cross-cultural knowledge insertion, which focuses on adapting models to specific cultural contexts while preserving their original behavior in other cultures. To facilitate research in this area, we introduce CrossCult-KIBench, a comprehensive evaluation benchmark for assessing both the effectiveness of knowledge insertion and its unintended side effects on non-target cultures. The benchmark includes 9,800 image-grounded cases covering 49 culturally relevant visual scenarios across English, Chinese, and Arabic language-culture groups. It supports evaluation in both single-insert and sequential-insert settings. We also propose Memory-Conditioned Knowledge Insertion (MCKI) as a baseline method. MCKI retrieves relevant cultural knowledge from an external memory using frozen MLLM representations, prepending matched entries as conditional prompts when applicable.
